# Jeremiah

ירמיהו

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah)

התראות מפני חורבן ירושלים, דרישה לחזרה בתשובה ודחייתה על ידי רוב העם, עינויים ורדיפות שספג ירמיהו בעקבות נבואותיו.

Warnings of Jerusalem’s destruction and demands for repentance, largely rejected by the people, some of whom torture and persecute him.

Sections: Chapter-Verse

Location: Tanakh/Prophets

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Miqra according to the Masorah (Segments: 1364, License: CC-BY-SA)

Version (En): The Koren Jerusalem Bible (Segments: 1364, License: CC-BY-NC)


## Chapter 1

###### Jeremiah 1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:1)


דִּבְרֵ֥י יִרְמְיָ֖הוּ בֶּן־חִלְקִיָּ֑הוּ מִן־הַכֹּֽהֲנִים֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בַּעֲנָת֔וֹת בְּאֶ֖רֶץ בִּנְיָמִֽן׃


The words of Yirmeyahu the son of Ḥilqiyyahu, of the priests who were in ῾Anatot in the land of Binyamin:


###### Jeremiah 1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:2)


אֲשֶׁ֨ר הָיָ֤ה דְבַר־יְהֹוָה֙ אֵלָ֔יו בִּימֵ֛י יֹאשִׁיָּ֥הוּ בֶן־אָמ֖וֹן מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֑ה בִּשְׁלֹשׁ־עֶשְׂרֵ֥ה שָׁנָ֖ה לְמׇלְכֽוֹ׃


to whom the word of the Lord came in the days of Yoshiyyahu the son of Amon king of Yehuda, in the thirteenth year of his reign.


###### Jeremiah 1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:3)


וַיְהִ֗י בִּימֵ֨י יְהוֹיָקִ֤ים בֶּן־יֹאשִׁיָּ֙הוּ֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֔ה עַד־תֹּם֙ עַשְׁתֵּ֣י עֶשְׂרֵ֣ה שָׁנָ֔ה לְצִדְקִיָּ֥הוּ בֶן־יֹאשִׁיָּ֖הוּ מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֑ה עַד־גְּל֥וֹת יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם בַּחֹ֥דֶשׁ הַחֲמִישִֽׁי׃{פ}


It came also in the days of Yehoyaqim the son of Yoshiyyahu king of Yehuda to the end of the eleventh year of Żidqiyyahu the son of Yoshiyyahu king of Yehuda, to the carrying away of Yerushalayim captive in the fifth month.


###### Jeremiah 1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:4)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


Then the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Jeremiah 1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:5)


בְּטֶ֨רֶם (אצורך) [אֶצׇּרְךָ֤] בַבֶּ֙טֶן֙ יְדַעְתִּ֔יךָ וּבְטֶ֛רֶם תֵּצֵ֥א מֵרֶ֖חֶם הִקְדַּשְׁתִּ֑יךָ נָבִ֥יא לַגּוֹיִ֖ם נְתַתִּֽיךָ׃


Before I formed thee in the belly I knew thee; and before thou didst come out of the womb I sanctified thee, and I ordained thee a prophet to the nations.


###### Jeremiah 1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:6)


וָאֹמַ֗ר אֲהָהּ֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה הִנֵּ֥ה לֹֽא־יָדַ֖עְתִּי דַּבֵּ֑ר כִּי־נַ֖עַר אָנֹֽכִי׃{ס}


Then said I, Ah, Lord God! behold, I cannot speak: for I am a child.


###### Jeremiah 1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:7)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר יְהֹוָה֙ אֵלַ֔י אַל־תֹּאמַ֖ר נַ֣עַר אָנֹ֑כִי כִּ֠י עַֽל־כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֤ר אֶֽשְׁלָחֲךָ֙ תֵּלֵ֔ךְ וְאֵ֛ת כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֥ר אֲצַוְּךָ֖ תְּדַבֵּֽר׃


But the Lord said to me, Say not, I am a child: for thou shalt go to all to whom I shall send thee, and whatever I command thee thou shalt speak.


###### Jeremiah 1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:8)


אַל־תִּירָ֖א מִפְּנֵיהֶ֑ם כִּֽי־אִתְּךָ֥ אֲנִ֛י לְהַצִּלֶ֖ךָ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


Do not be afraid of them: for I am with thee to deliver thee, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:9)


וַיִּשְׁלַ֤ח יְהֹוָה֙ אֶת־יָד֔וֹ וַיַּגַּ֖ע עַל־פִּ֑י וַיֹּ֤אמֶר יְהֹוָה֙ אֵלַ֔י הִנֵּ֛ה נָתַ֥תִּי דְבָרַ֖י בְּפִֽיךָ׃


Then the Lord put out his hand, and touched my mouth. And the Lord said to me, Behold, I have put my words in thy mouth.


###### Jeremiah 1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:10)


רְאֵ֞ה הִפְקַדְתִּ֣יךָ**׀** הַיּ֣וֹם הַזֶּ֗ה עַל־הַגּוֹיִם֙ וְעַל־הַמַּמְלָכ֔וֹת לִנְת֥וֹשׁ וְלִנְת֖וֹץ וּלְהַאֲבִ֣יד וְלַהֲר֑וֹס לִבְנ֖וֹת וְלִנְטֽוֹעַ׃{פ}


See, I have this day set thee over the nations and over the kingdoms, to root out, and to pull down, and to destroy, and to throw down, to build, and to plant.


###### Jeremiah 1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:11)


וַיְהִ֤י דְבַר־יְהֹוָה֙ אֵלַ֣י לֵאמֹ֔ר מָה־אַתָּ֥ה רֹאֶ֖ה יִרְמְיָ֑הוּ וָאֹמַ֕ר מַקֵּ֥ל שָׁקֵ֖ד אֲנִ֥י רֹאֶֽה׃


Moreover the word of the Lord came to me, saying, What dost thou see, Yirmeyahu? And I said, I see a rod of an almond tree.


###### Jeremiah 1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:12)


וַיֹּ֧אמֶר יְהֹוָ֛ה אֵלַ֖י הֵיטַ֣בְתָּ לִרְא֑וֹת כִּֽי־שֹׁקֵ֥ד אֲנִ֛י עַל־דְּבָרִ֖י לַעֲשֹׂתֽוֹ׃{ס}


Then the Lord said to me, Thou hast seen well: for I will hasten my word to perform it.


###### Jeremiah 1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:13)


וַיְהִ֨י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֤ה׀אֵלַי֙ שֵׁנִ֣ית לֵאמֹ֔ר מָ֥ה אַתָּ֖ה רֹאֶ֑ה וָאֹמַ֗ר סִ֤יר נָפ֙וּחַ֙ אֲנִ֣י רֹאֶ֔ה וּפָנָ֖יו מִפְּנֵ֥י צָפֽוֹנָה׃


And the word of the Lord came to me a second time, saying, What dost thou see? And I said, I see a boiling pot; and its face is from the north.


###### Jeremiah 1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:14)


וַיֹּ֥אמֶר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלָ֑י מִצָּפוֹן֙ תִּפָּתַ֣ח הָרָעָ֔ה עַ֥ל כׇּל־יֹשְׁבֵ֖י הָאָֽרֶץ׃


Then the Lord said to me, Out of the north the evil shall break forth upon all the inhabitants of the land.


###### Jeremiah 1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:15)


כִּ֣י**׀** הִנְנִ֣י קֹרֵ֗א לְכׇֽל־מִשְׁפְּח֛וֹת מַמְלְכ֥וֹת צָפ֖וֹנָה נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה וּבָ֡אוּ וְֽנָתְנוּ֩ אִ֨ישׁ כִּסְא֜וֹ פֶּ֣תַח**׀** שַׁעֲרֵ֣י יְרוּשָׁלַ֗͏ִם וְעַ֤ל כׇּל־חוֹמֹתֶ֙יהָ֙ סָבִ֔יב וְעַ֖ל כׇּל־עָרֵ֥י יְהוּדָֽה׃


For, lo, I will call all the families of the kingdoms of the north, says the Lord; and they shall come, and they shall set everyone his throne at the entrance of the gates of Yerushalayim, and against all its walls round about, and against all the cities of Yehuda.


###### Jeremiah 1:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:16)


וְדִבַּרְתִּ֤י מִשְׁפָּטַי֙ אוֹתָ֔ם עַ֖ל כׇּל־רָעָתָ֑ם אֲשֶׁ֣ר עֲזָב֗וּנִי וַֽיְקַטְּרוּ֙ לֵאלֹהִ֣ים אֲחֵרִ֔ים וַיִּֽשְׁתַּחֲו֖וּ לְמַעֲשֵׂ֥י יְדֵיהֶֽם׃


And I will utter my judgments against them regarding all their wickedness, in that they have forsaken me, and have burned incense to other gods, and worshipped the works of their own hands.


###### Jeremiah 1:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:17)


וְאַתָּה֙ תֶּאְזֹ֣ר מׇתְנֶ֔יךָ וְקַמְתָּ֙ וְדִבַּרְתָּ֣ אֲלֵיהֶ֔ם אֵ֛ת כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֥ר אָנֹכִ֖י אֲצַוֶּ֑ךָּ אַל־תֵּחַת֙ מִפְּנֵיהֶ֔ם פֶּֽן־אֲחִתְּךָ֖ לִפְנֵיהֶֽם׃


Thou therefore gird up thy loins, and arise, and speak to them all that I command thee: do not be dismayed at them, lest I dismay thee before them.


###### Jeremiah 1:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:18)


וַאֲנִ֞י הִנֵּ֧ה נְתַתִּ֣יךָ הַיּ֗וֹם לְעִ֨יר מִבְצָ֜ר וּלְעַמּ֥וּד בַּרְזֶ֛ל וּלְחֹמ֥וֹת נְחֹ֖שֶׁת עַל־כׇּל־הָאָ֑רֶץ לְמַלְכֵ֤י יְהוּדָה֙ לְשָׂרֶ֔יהָ לְכֹהֲנֶ֖יהָ וּלְעַ֥ם הָאָֽרֶץ׃


For, behold, I have made thee this day a fortified city, and an iron pillar, and walls of brass against the whole land, against the kings of Yehuda, against its princes, against its priests, and against the people of the land.


###### Jeremiah 1:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/1:19)


וְנִלְחֲמ֥וּ אֵלֶ֖יךָ וְלֹא־י֣וּכְלוּ לָ֑ךְ כִּי־אִתְּךָ֥ אֲנִ֛י נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֖ה לְהַצִּילֶֽךָ׃{פ}


And they shall fight against thee; but they shall not prevail against thee; for I am with thee, says the Lord, to deliver thee.



## Chapter 2

###### Jeremiah 2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/2:1)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


Moreover the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Jeremiah 2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/2:2)


הָלֹ֡ךְ וְֽקָרָ֩אתָ֩ בְאׇזְנֵ֨י יְרוּשָׁלַ֜͏ִם לֵאמֹ֗ר כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה זָכַ֤רְתִּי לָךְ֙ חֶ֣סֶד נְעוּרַ֔יִךְ אַהֲבַ֖ת כְּלוּלֹתָ֑יִךְ לֶכְתֵּ֤ךְ אַֽחֲרַי֙ בַּמִּדְבָּ֔ר בְּאֶ֖רֶץ לֹ֥א זְרוּעָֽה׃


Go and cry in the ears of Yerushalayim, saying, Thus says the Lord; I remember in thy favour, the devotion of thy youth, thy love as a bride, when thou didst go after me in the wilderness, in a land that was not sown.


###### Jeremiah 2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/2:3)


קֹ֤דֶשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ לַיהֹוָ֔ה רֵאשִׁ֖ית תְּבוּאָתֹ֑ה כׇּל־אֹכְלָ֣יו יֶאְשָׁ֔מוּ רָעָ֛ה תָּבֹ֥א אֲלֵיהֶ֖ם נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


Israel is holy to the Lord, the firstfruits of his increase: all that devour him shall be held guilty; evil shall come upon them, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/2:4)


שִׁמְע֥וּ דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה בֵּ֣ית יַעֲקֹ֑ב וְכׇֽל־מִשְׁפְּח֖וֹת בֵּ֥ית יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


Hear the word of the Lord, O house of Ya῾aqov, and all the families of the house of Yisra᾽el.


###### Jeremiah 2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/2:5)


כֹּ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה מַה־מָּצְא֨וּ אֲבוֹתֵיכֶ֥ם בִּי֙ עָ֔וֶל כִּ֥י רָחֲק֖וּ מֵעָלָ֑י וַיֵּ֥לְכ֛וּ אַחֲרֵ֥י הַהֶ֖בֶל וַיֶּהְבָּֽלוּ׃


Thus says the Lord, What iniquity have your fathers found in me, that they have gone far from me, and have walked after vanity, and are become themselves worthless?


###### Jeremiah 2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/2:6)


וְלֹ֣א אָֽמְר֔וּ אַיֵּ֣ה יְהֹוָ֔ה הַמַּעֲלֶ֥ה אֹתָ֖נוּ מֵאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרָ֑יִם הַמּוֹלִ֨יךְ אֹתָ֜נוּ בַּמִּדְבָּ֗ר בְּאֶ֨רֶץ עֲרָבָ֤ה וְשׁוּחָה֙ בְּאֶ֙רֶץ֙ צִיָּ֣ה וְצַלְמָ֔וֶת בְּאֶ֗רֶץ לֹא־עָ֤בַר בָּהּ֙ אִ֔ישׁ וְלֹא־יָשַׁ֥ב אָדָ֖ם שָֽׁם׃


Neither did they say, Where is the Lord who brought us up out of the land of Miżrayim, who led us through the wilderness, through a land of deserts and of pits, through a land of drought, and of the shadow of death, through a land that no man passed through, and where no man dwelt?


###### Jeremiah 2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/2:7)


וָאָבִ֤יא אֶתְכֶם֙ אֶל־אֶ֣רֶץ הַכַּרְמֶ֔ל לֶאֱכֹ֥ל פִּרְיָ֖הּ וְטוּבָ֑הּ וַתָּבֹ֙אוּ֙ וַתְּטַמְּא֣וּ אֶת־אַרְצִ֔י וְנַחֲלָתִ֥י שַׂמְתֶּ֖ם לְתוֹעֵבָֽה׃


And I brought you into a plentiful country, to eat its fruit and its bounty; but when you entered, you defiled my land, and made my heritage an abomination.


###### Jeremiah 2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/2:8)


הַכֹּהֲנִ֗ים לֹ֤א אָֽמְרוּ֙ אַיֵּ֣ה יְהֹוָ֔ה וְתֹפְשֵׂ֤י הַתּוֹרָה֙ לֹ֣א יְדָע֔וּנִי וְהָרֹעִ֖ים פָּ֣שְׁעוּ בִ֑י וְהַנְּבִאִים֙ נִבְּא֣וּ בַבַּ֔עַל וְאַחֲרֵ֥י לֹֽא־יוֹעִ֖לוּ הָלָֽכוּ׃


The priests said not, Where is the Lord? and they that handle the Tora knew me not: the rulers also transgressed against me, and the prophets prophesied by the Ba῾al, and walked after things that do not profit.


###### Jeremiah 2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/2:9)

Wherefore I will yet plead with you, says the Lord, and with your children’s children will I plead.

For pass over the isles of Kittiyyim, and see; and send to Qedar, and consider diligently, and see if there has been such a thing.

Can a maid forget her ornaments, or a bride her attire? yet my people have forgotten me days without number.

How well thou dost direct thy path to seek love! Therefore thou hast even taught bad women thy ways.


###### Jeremiah 2:34

Also in thy skirts is found the lifeblood of the innocent poor: whom thou didst not find in the act of breaking in. Yet in spite of all these things

thou sayst, Because I am innocent, surely his anger has turned from me. Behold, I will enter into judgment with thee, because thou sayst, I have not sinned.

## Chapter 3

###### Jeremiah 3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:1)


לֵאמֹ֡ר הֵ֣ן יְשַׁלַּ֣ח אִ֣ישׁ אֶת־אִשְׁתּוֹ֩ וְהָלְכָ֨ה מֵאִתּ֜וֹ וְהָיְתָ֣ה לְאִישׁ־אַחֵ֗ר הֲיָשׁ֤וּב אֵלֶ֙יהָ֙ ע֔וֹד הֲל֛וֹא חָנ֥וֹף תֶּחֱנַ֖ף הָאָ֣רֶץ הַהִ֑יא וְאַ֗תְּ זָנִית֙ רֵעִ֣ים רַבִּ֔ים וְשׁ֥וֹב אֵלַ֖י נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


It was said, If a man put away his wife, and she go from him, and become another man’s, shall he return to her again? shall not that land be greatly polluted? but thou hast played the harlot with many lovers; and wilt thou yet return again to me? says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:2)


שְׂאִי־עֵינַ֨יִךְ עַל־שְׁפָיִ֜ם וּרְאִ֗י אֵיפֹה֙ לֹ֣א (שגלת) [שֻׁכַּ֔בְתְּ] עַל־דְּרָכִים֙ יָשַׁ֣בְתְּ לָהֶ֔ם כַּעֲרָבִ֖י בַּמִּדְבָּ֑ר וַתַּחֲנִ֣יפִי אֶ֔רֶץ בִּזְנוּתַ֖יִךְ וּבְרָעָתֵֽךְ׃


Lift up thy eyes to the high places, and see where thou hast not been lain with. On the highways hast thou sat for them, as the ῾Aravian in the wilderness; and thou hast polluted the land with thy harlotries and with thy wickedness.


###### Jeremiah 3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:3)


וַיִּמָּנְע֣וּ רְבִבִ֔ים וּמַלְק֖וֹשׁ ל֣וֹא הָיָ֑ה וּמֵ֨צַח אִשָּׁ֤ה זוֹנָה֙ הָ֣יָה לָ֔ךְ מֵאַ֖נְתְּ הִכָּלֵֽם׃


Therefore the showers have been withheld, and there has been no latter rain; and thou didst have a harlot’s forehead, thou didst refuse to be ashamed.


###### Jeremiah 3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:4)


הֲל֣וֹא מֵעַ֔תָּה (קראתי) [קָרָ֥את] לִ֖י אָבִ֑י אַלּ֥וּף נְעֻרַ֖י אָֽתָּה׃


Wilt thou not from this time cry to me, My father, Thou art the guide of my youth?


###### Jeremiah 3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:5)


הֲיִנְטֹ֣ר לְעוֹלָ֔ם אִם־יִשְׁמֹ֖ר לָנֶ֑צַח הִנֵּ֥ה דִבַּ֛רְתְּ וַתַּעֲשִׂ֥י הָרָע֖וֹת וַתּוּכָֽל׃{פ}


Will he bear a grudge for ever? will he keep it to the end? Behold, thou hast spoken and done evil things and hast had thy way.


###### Jeremiah 3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:6)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר יְהֹוָ֜ה אֵלַ֗י בִּימֵי֙ יֹאשִׁיָּ֣הוּ הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ הֲֽרָאִ֔יתָ אֲשֶׁ֥ר עָשְׂתָ֖ה מְשֻׁבָ֣ה יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל הֹלְכָ֨ה הִ֜יא עַל־כׇּל־הַ֣ר גָּבֹ֗הַּ וְאֶל־תַּ֛חַת כׇּל־עֵ֥ץ רַעֲנָ֖ן וַתִּזְנִי־שָֽׁם׃


The Lord said also to me in the days of Yoshiyyahu the king, Hast thou seen that which faithless Yisra᾽el has done? she is gone up upon every high mountain and under every green tree, and there has played the harlot.


###### Jeremiah 3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:7)


וָאֹמַ֗ר אַחֲרֵ֨י עֲשׂוֹתָ֧הּ אֶת־כׇּל־אֵ֛לֶּה אֵלַ֥י תָּשׁ֖וּב וְלֹא־שָׁ֑בָה (ותראה) [וַתֵּ֛רֶא] בָּגוֹדָ֥ה אֲחוֹתָ֖הּ יְהוּדָֽה׃


And I said, After she has done all these things, she will return to me. But she did not return. And her treacherous sister Yehuda saw it.


###### Jeremiah 3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:8)


וָאֵ֗רֶא כִּ֤י עַל־כׇּל־אֹדוֹת֙ אֲשֶׁ֤ר נִֽאֲפָה֙ מְשֻׁבָ֣ה יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל שִׁלַּחְתִּ֕יהָ וָאֶתֵּ֛ן אֶת־סֵ֥פֶר כְּרִיתֻתֶ֖יהָ אֵלֶ֑יהָ וְלֹ֨א יָרְאָ֜ה בֹּגֵדָ֤ה יְהוּדָה֙ אֲחוֹתָ֔הּ וַתֵּ֖לֶךְ וַתִּ֥זֶן גַּם־הִֽיא׃


And I saw when, forasmuch as faithless Yisra᾽el had committed adultery, I had put her away, and given her a bill of divorce; that her treacherous sister Yehuda was not afraid but went and played the harlot also.


###### Jeremiah 3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:9)


וְהָיָה֙ מִקֹּ֣ל זְנוּתָ֔הּ וַתֶּחֱנַ֖ף אֶת־הָאָ֑רֶץ וַתִּנְאַ֥ף אֶת־הָאֶ֖בֶן וְאֶת־הָעֵֽץ׃


And it came to pass through the lightness of her harlotry, that she defiled the land, and committed adultery with stones and with wood.


###### Jeremiah 3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:10)


וְגַם־בְּכׇל־זֹ֗את לֹא־שָׁ֨בָה אֵלַ֜י בָּגוֹדָ֧ה אֲחוֹתָ֛הּ יְהוּדָ֖ה בְּכׇל־לִבָּ֑הּ כִּ֥י אִם־בְּשֶׁ֖קֶר נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


And yet for all this her treacherous sister Yehuda has not turned to me with her whole heart, but in pretence, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 3:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:11)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר יְהֹוָה֙ אֵלַ֔י צִדְּקָ֥ה נַפְשָׁ֖הּ מְשֻׁבָ֣ה יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל מִבֹּגֵדָ֖ה יְהוּדָֽה׃


And the Lord said to me, Faithless Yisra᾽el has proved herself more just than treacherous Yehuda.


###### Jeremiah 3:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:12)


הָלֹ֡ךְ וְֽקָרָ֩אתָ֩ אֶת־הַדְּבָרִ֨ים הָאֵ֜לֶּה צָפ֗וֹנָה וְ֠אָמַרְתָּ֠ שׁ֣וּבָה מְשֻׁבָ֤ה יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה לוֹא־אַפִּ֥יל פָּנַ֖י בָּכֶ֑ם כִּֽי־חָסִ֤יד אֲנִי֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה לֹ֥א אֶטּ֖וֹר לְעוֹלָֽם׃


Go and proclaim these words toward the north, and say, Return thou faithless Yisra᾽el, says the Lord; and I will not frown upon you: for I am merciful, says the Lord, and I will not bear a grudge for ever.


###### Jeremiah 3:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:13)


אַ֚ךְ דְּעִ֣י עֲוֺנֵ֔ךְ כִּ֛י בַּיהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהַ֖יִךְ פָּשָׁ֑עַתְּ וַתְּפַזְּרִ֨י אֶת־דְּרָכַ֜יִךְ לַזָּרִ֗ים תַּ֚חַת כׇּל־עֵ֣ץ רַעֲנָ֔ן וּבְקוֹלִ֥י לֹֽא־שְׁמַעְתֶּ֖ם נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


Only acknowledge thy iniquity, that thou hast transgressed against the Lord thy God, and hast scattered thy ways to the strangers under every green tree, and you have not obeyed my voice, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 3:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:14)


שׁ֣וּבוּ בָנִ֤ים שֽׁוֹבָבִים֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה כִּ֥י אָנֹכִ֖י בָּעַ֣לְתִּי בָכֶ֑ם וְלָקַחְתִּ֨י אֶתְכֶ֜ם אֶחָ֣ד מֵעִ֗יר וּשְׁנַ֙יִם֙ מִמִּשְׁפָּחָ֔ה וְהֵבֵאתִ֥י אֶתְכֶ֖ם צִיּֽוֹן׃


Turn, O faithless children, says the Lord; for I have taken you to myself: and I will take you one of a city, and two of a family, and I will bring you to Żiyyon:


###### Jeremiah 3:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:15)


וְנָתַתִּ֥י לָכֶ֛ם רֹעִ֖ים כְּלִבִּ֑י וְרָע֥וּ אֶתְכֶ֖ם דֵּעָ֥ה וְהַשְׂכֵּֽיל׃


and I will give you shepherds according to my heart, who shall feed you with knowledge and understanding.


###### Jeremiah 3:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:16)


וְהָיָ֡ה כִּ֣י תִרְבּוּ֩ וּפְרִיתֶ֨ם בָּאָ֜רֶץ בַּיָּמִ֤ים הָהֵ֙מָּה֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה לֹא־יֹ֣אמְרוּ ע֗וֹד אֲרוֹן֙ בְּרִית־יְהֹוָ֔ה וְלֹ֥א יַעֲלֶ֖ה עַל־לֵ֑ב וְלֹ֤א יִזְכְּרוּ־בוֹ֙ וְלֹ֣א יִפְקֹ֔דוּ וְלֹ֥א יֵעָשֶׂ֖ה עֽוֹד׃


And it shall come to pass, when you multiply and increase in the land, in those days, says the Lord, they shall say no more, The ark of the covenant of the Lord: nor shall it come to mind: nor shall they remember it; nor shall they miss it; nor shall that be done any more.


###### Jeremiah 3:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:17)


בָּעֵ֣ת הַהִ֗יא יִקְרְא֤וּ לִירוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ כִּסֵּ֣א יְהֹוָ֔ה וְנִקְו֨וּ אֵלֶ֧יהָ כׇֽל־הַגּוֹיִ֛ם לְשֵׁ֥ם יְהֹוָ֖ה לִירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְלֹא־יֵלְכ֣וּ ע֔וֹד אַחֲרֵ֕י שְׁרִר֖וּת לִבָּ֥ם הָרָֽע׃{ס}


At that time they shall call Yerushalayim the throne of the Lord; and all the nations shall be gathered to it, to the name of the Lord, to Yerushalayim: nor shall they walk any more after the stubbornness of their evil heart.


###### Jeremiah 3:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:18)


בַּיָּמִ֣ים הָהֵ֔מָּה יֵלְכ֥וּ בֵית־יְהוּדָ֖ה עַל־בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וְיָבֹ֤אוּ יַחְדָּו֙ מֵאֶ֣רֶץ צָפ֔וֹן עַל־הָאָ֕רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֥ר הִנְחַ֖לְתִּי אֶת־אֲבוֹתֵיכֶֽם׃


In those days the house of Yehuda shall walk with the house of Yisra᾽el, and they shall come together out of the land of the north to the land that I have given for an inheritance to your fathers.


###### Jeremiah 3:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:19)


וְאָנֹכִ֣י אָמַ֗רְתִּי אֵ֚יךְ אֲשִׁיתֵ֣ךְ בַּבָּנִ֔ים וְאֶתֶּן־לָךְ֙ אֶ֣רֶץ חֶמְדָּ֔ה נַחֲלַ֥ת צְבִ֖י צִבְא֣וֹת גּוֹיִ֑ם וָאֹמַ֗ר אָבִי֙ (תקראו) [תִּקְרְאִי־]לִ֔י וּמֵאַחֲרַ֖י לֹ֥א (תשובו) [תָשֽׁוּבִי]׃


But I said, How shall I put thee among the sons, and give thee a pleasant land, the finest heritage of the hosts of nations? and I said, Thou shalt call me, My father; and thou shalt not turn away from me.


###### Jeremiah 3:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:20)


אָכֵ֛ן בָּגְדָ֥ה אִשָּׁ֖ה מֵרֵעָ֑הּ כֵּ֣ן בְּגַדְתֶּ֥ם בִּ֛י בֵּ֥ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


Surely as a wife treacherously departs from her husband, so have you dealt treacherously with me, O house of Yisra᾽el, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 3:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:21)


ק֚וֹל עַל־שְׁפָיִ֣ים נִשְׁמָ֔ע בְּכִ֥י תַחֲנוּנֵ֖י בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל כִּ֤י הֶעֱווּ֙ אֶת־דַּרְכָּ֔ם שָׁכְח֖וּ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיהֶֽם׃


A voice is heard upon the high hills, weeping and supplications of the children of Yisra᾽el: for they have perverted their way, and they have forgotten the Lord their God.


###### Jeremiah 3:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:22)


שׁ֚וּבוּ בָּנִ֣ים שׁוֹבָבִ֔ים אֶרְפָּ֖ה מְשׁוּבֹתֵיכֶ֑ם הִנְנוּ֙ אָתָ֣נוּ לָ֔ךְ כִּ֥י אַתָּ֖ה יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ׃


Return, faithless children, and I will heal your backslidings. Here we are, we are come to Thee; for Thou art the Lord our God.


###### Jeremiah 3:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:23)


אָכֵ֥ן לַשֶּׁ֛קֶר מִגְּבָע֖וֹת הָמ֣וֹן הָרִ֑ים אָכֵן֙ בַּיהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵ֔ינוּ תְּשׁוּעַ֖ת יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


Truly from the worship on the hills comes delusion, and from the uproar on the mountains: truly in the Lord our God is the salvation of Yisra᾽el.


###### Jeremiah 3:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:24)


וְהַבֹּ֗שֶׁת אָ֥כְלָ֛ה אֶת־יְגִ֥יעַ אֲבוֹתֵ֖ינוּ מִנְּעוּרֵ֑ינוּ אֶת־צֹאנָם֙ וְאֶת־בְּקָרָ֔ם אֶת־בְּנֵיהֶ֖ם וְאֶת־בְּנוֹתֵיהֶֽם׃


For the shameful idolatry has devoured the labour of our fathers from our youth; their flocks and their herds, their sons and their daughters.


###### Jeremiah 3:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/3:25)


נִשְׁכְּבָ֣ה בְּבׇשְׁתֵּ֗נוּ וּֽתְכַסֵּ֘נוּ֮ כְּלִמָּתֵ֒נוּ֒ כִּי֩ לַיהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהֵ֜ינוּ חָטָ֗אנוּ אֲנַ֙חְנוּ֙ וַאֲבוֹתֵ֔ינוּ מִנְּעוּרֵ֖ינוּ וְעַד־הַיּ֣וֹם הַזֶּ֑ה וְלֹ֣א שָׁמַ֔עְנוּ בְּק֖וֹל יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ׃{ס}


Let us lie down in our shame, and let our confusion cover us: for we have sinned against the Lord our God, we and our fathers, from our youth even to this day, and have not obeyed the voice of the Lord our God.



## Chapter 4

###### Jeremiah 4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:1)


אִם־תָּשׁ֨וּב יִשְׂרָאֵ֧ל׀נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֛ה אֵלַ֖י תָּשׁ֑וּב וְאִם־תָּסִ֧יר שִׁקּוּצֶ֛יךָ מִפָּנַ֖י וְלֹ֥א תָנֽוּד׃


If thou wilt return, O Yisra᾽el, says the Lord, return to me: and if thou wilt put away thy abominations out of my sight, without wavering,


###### Jeremiah 4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:2)


וְנִשְׁבַּ֙עְתָּ֙ חַי־יְהֹוָ֔ה בֶּאֱמֶ֖ת בְּמִשְׁפָּ֣ט וּבִצְדָקָ֑ה וְהִתְבָּ֥רְכוּ ב֛וֹ גּוֹיִ֖ם וּב֥וֹ יִתְהַלָּֽלוּ׃{ס}


and wilt swear, As the Lord lives, in truth, in judgment, and in righteousness; then the nations shall bless themselves in him, and in him shall they glory.


###### Jeremiah 4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:3)


כִּי־כֹ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה לְאִ֤ישׁ יְהוּדָה֙ וְלִיר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם נִ֥ירוּ לָכֶ֖ם נִ֑יר וְאַֽל־תִּזְרְע֖וּ אֶל־קֹצִֽים׃


For thus says the Lord to the men of Yehuda and Yerushalayim, Break up your fallow ground, and sow not among thorns.


###### Jeremiah 4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:4)


הִמֹּ֣לוּ לַיהֹוָ֗ה וְהָסִ֙רוּ֙ עׇרְל֣וֹת לְבַבְכֶ֔ם אִ֥ישׁ יְהוּדָ֖ה וְיֹשְׁבֵ֣י יְרוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם פֶּן־תֵּצֵ֨א כָאֵ֜שׁ חֲמָתִ֗י וּבָֽעֲרָה֙ וְאֵ֣ין מְכַבֶּ֔ה מִפְּנֵ֖י רֹ֥עַ מַעַלְלֵיכֶֽם׃


Circumcise yourselves to the Lord, and take away the foreskins of your heart, you men of Yehuda and inhabitants of Yerushalayim: lest my fury come forth like fire, and burn so that no one can quench it, because of the evil of your doings.


###### Jeremiah 4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:5)


הַגִּ֣ידוּ בִֽיהוּדָ֗ה וּבִירוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ הַשְׁמִ֔יעוּ וְאִמְר֕וּ (ותקעו) [תִּקְע֥וּ] שׁוֹפָ֖ר בָּאָ֑רֶץ קִרְא֤וּ מַלְאוּ֙ וְאִמְר֔וּ הֵאָסְפ֥וּ וְנָב֖וֹאָה אֶל־עָרֵ֥י הַמִּבְצָֽר׃


Declare in Yehuda, and publish in Yerushalayim; and say, Blow the shofar in the land: cry, gather together, and say, Assemble yourselves, and let us go into the fortified cities.


###### Jeremiah 4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:6)


שְׂאוּ־נֵ֣ס צִיּ֔וֹנָה הָעִ֖יזוּ אַֽל־תַּעֲמֹ֑דוּ כִּ֣י רָעָ֗ה אָנֹכִ֛י מֵבִ֥יא מִצָּפ֖וֹן וְשֶׁ֥בֶר גָּדֽוֹל׃


Set up the standard toward Żiyyon: seek cover, stay not: for I will bring evil from the north, and a great destruction.


###### Jeremiah 4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:7)


עָלָ֤ה אַרְיֵה֙ מִֽסֻּבְּכ֔וֹ וּמַשְׁחִ֣ית גּוֹיִ֔ם נָסַ֖ע יָצָ֣א מִמְּקֹמ֑וֹ לָשׂ֤וּם אַרְצֵךְ֙ לְשַׁמָּ֔ה עָרַ֥יִךְ תִּצֶּ֖ינָה מֵאֵ֥ין יוֹשֵֽׁב׃


The lion is come up from his thicket, and the destroyer of nations is on his way; he is gone out of his place to make thy land desolate; and thy cities shall be laid waste, without inhabitant.


###### Jeremiah 4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:8)


עַל־זֹ֛את חִגְר֥וּ שַׂקִּ֖ים סִפְד֣וּ וְהֵילִ֑ילוּ כִּ֥י לֹֽא־שָׁ֛ב חֲר֥וֹן אַף־יְהֹוָ֖ה מִמֶּֽנּוּ׃{פ}


For this gird on sackcloth, lament and howl: for the fierce anger of the Lord is not turned back from us.


###### Jeremiah 4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:9)


וְהָיָ֤ה בַיּוֹם־הַהוּא֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה יֹאבַ֥ד לֵב־הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ וְלֵ֣ב הַשָּׂרִ֑ים וְנָשַׁ֙מּוּ֙ הַכֹּ֣הֲנִ֔ים וְהַנְּבִאִ֖ים יִתְמָֽהוּ׃{ס}


And it shall come to pass on that day, says the Lord, that the heart of the king shall fail, and the heart of the princes; and the priests shall be astonished, and the prophets shall wonder.


###### Jeremiah 4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:10)


וָֽאֹמַ֞ר אֲהָ֣הּ**׀** אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה אָכֵן֩ הַשֵּׁ֨א הִשֵּׁ֜אתָ לָעָ֤ם הַזֶּה֙ וְלִירוּשָׁלַ֣͏ִם לֵאמֹ֔ר שָׁל֖וֹם יִהְיֶ֣ה לָכֶ֑ם וְנָגְעָ֥ה חֶ֖רֶב עַד־הַנָּֽפֶשׁ׃


Then said I, Ah, Lord God surely Thou hast greatly deceived this people and Yerushalayim, saying, You shall have peace; whereas the sword reaches to the soul.


###### Jeremiah 4:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:11)


בָּעֵ֣ת הַהִ֗יא יֵאָמֵ֤ר לָעָם־הַזֶּה֙ וְלִיר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם ר֣וּחַ צַ֤ח שְׁפָיִם֙ בַּמִּדְבָּ֔ר דֶּ֖רֶךְ בַּת־עַמִּ֑י ל֥וֹא לִזְר֖וֹת וְל֥וֹא לְהָבַֽר׃


At that time shall it be said of this people and of Yerushalayim, A dry wind of the high hills in the wilderness towards the daughter of my people, not a wind to winnow, nor to cleanse the corn,


###### Jeremiah 4:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:12)


ר֧וּחַ מָלֵ֛א מֵאֵ֖לֶּה יָ֣בוֹא לִ֑י עַתָּ֕ה גַּם־אֲנִ֛י אֲדַבֵּ֥ר מִשְׁפָּטִ֖ים אוֹתָֽם׃


but a wind stronger than those shall come for me: now also will I give sentence against them.


###### Jeremiah 4:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:13)


הִנֵּ֣ה**׀** כַּעֲנָנִ֣ים יַעֲלֶ֗ה וְכַסּוּפָה֙ מַרְכְּבוֹתָ֔יו קַלּ֥וּ מִנְּשָׁרִ֖ים סוּסָ֑יו א֥וֹי לָ֖נוּ כִּ֥י שֻׁדָּֽדְנוּ׃


Behold, he shall come up as clouds, and his chariots shall be as a storm wind: his horses are swifter than eagles. Woe to us! for we are ruined.


###### Jeremiah 4:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:14)


כַּבְּסִ֨י מֵרָעָ֤ה לִבֵּךְ֙ יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם לְמַ֖עַן תִּוָּשֵׁ֑עִי עַד־מָתַ֛י תָּלִ֥ין בְּקִרְבֵּ֖ךְ מַחְשְׁב֥וֹת אוֹנֵֽךְ׃


O Yerushalayim, wash thy heart from wickedness, that thou mayst be saved. How long shall thy thoughts of calamity lodge within thee?


###### Jeremiah 4:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:15)


כִּ֛י ק֥וֹל מַגִּ֖יד מִדָּ֑ן וּמַשְׁמִ֥יעַ אָ֖וֶן מֵהַ֥ר אֶפְרָֽיִם׃


For a voice declares from Dan, and publishes calamity from mount Efrayim.


###### Jeremiah 4:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:16)


הַזְכִּ֣ירוּ לַגּוֹיִ֗ם הִנֵּה֙ הַשְׁמִ֣יעוּ עַל־יְרוּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם נֹצְרִ֥ים בָּאִ֖ים מֵאֶ֣רֶץ הַמֶּרְחָ֑ק וַֽיִּתְּנ֛וּ עַל־עָרֵ֥י יְהוּדָ֖ה קוֹלָֽם׃


Make mention to the nations; behold, publish against Yerushalayim, that watchers come from a far country, and give out their voice against the cities of Yehuda.


###### Jeremiah 4:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:17)


כְּשֹׁמְרֵ֣י שָׂדַ֔י הָי֥וּ עָלֶ֖יהָ מִסָּבִ֑יב כִּי־אֹתִ֥י מָרָ֖תָה נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


As keepers of a field, are they against her round about; because she has been rebellious against me, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 4:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:18)


דַּרְכֵּךְ֙ וּמַ֣עֲלָלַ֔יִךְ עָשׂ֥וֹ אֵ֖לֶּה לָ֑ךְ זֹ֤את רָעָתֵךְ֙ כִּ֣י מָ֔ר כִּ֥י נָגַ֖ע עַד־לִבֵּֽךְ׃{פ}


Thy way and thy doings have brought these things upon thee; this is thy wickedness, and it is bitter; it reaches to thy heart.


###### Jeremiah 4:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:19)


מֵעַ֣י**׀** מֵעַ֨י׀[אוֹחִ֜ילָה] (אחולה) קִיר֥וֹת לִבִּ֛י הֹמֶה־לִּ֥י לִבִּ֖י לֹ֣א אַחֲרִ֑שׁ כִּ֣י ק֤וֹל שׁוֹפָר֙ (שמעתי) [שָׁמַ֣עַתְּ] נַפְשִׁ֔י תְּרוּעַ֖ת מִלְחָמָֽה׃


My bowels, my bowels! I am shaken in the chambers of my heart; my heart moans within me; I cannot hold my peace, because thou hast heard, O my soul, the sound of the shofar, the alarm of war.


###### Jeremiah 4:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:20)


שֶׁ֤בֶר עַל־שֶׁ֙בֶר֙ נִקְרָ֔א כִּ֥י שֻׁדְּדָ֖ה כׇּל־הָאָ֑רֶץ פִּתְאֹם֙ שֻׁדְּד֣וּ אֹֽהָלַ֔י רֶ֖גַע יְרִיעֹתָֽי׃


Destruction follows upon destruction; for the whole land is spoiled: suddenly are my tents spoiled, and my curtains in a moment.


###### Jeremiah 4:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:21)


עַד־מָתַ֖י אֶרְאֶה־נֵּ֑ס אֶשְׁמְעָ֖ה ק֥וֹל שׁוֹפָֽר׃{פ}


How long shall I see the standard, and hear the sound of the shofar?


###### Jeremiah 4:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:22)


כִּ֣י**׀** אֱוִ֣יל עַמִּ֗י אוֹתִי֙ לֹ֣א יָדָ֔עוּ בָּנִ֤ים סְכָלִים֙ הֵ֔מָּה וְלֹ֥א נְבוֹנִ֖ים הֵ֑מָּה חֲכָמִ֥ים הֵ֙מָּה֙ לְהָרַ֔ע וּלְהֵיטִ֖יב לֹ֥א יָדָֽעוּ׃


For my people is foolish, they do not know me; they are sottish children, and they have no understanding: they are wise to do evil, but to do good they have no knowledge.


###### Jeremiah 4:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:23)


רָאִ֙יתִי֙ אֶת־הָאָ֔רֶץ וְהִנֵּה־תֹ֖הוּ וָבֹ֑הוּ וְאֶל־הַשָּׁמַ֖יִם וְאֵ֥ין אוֹרָֽם׃


I beheld the earth, and, lo, it was waste and void; and the heavens, and they had no light.


###### Jeremiah 4:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:24)


רָאִ֙יתִי֙ הֶהָרִ֔ים וְהִנֵּ֖ה רֹעֲשִׁ֑ים וְכׇל־הַגְּבָע֖וֹת הִתְקַלְקָֽלוּ׃


I beheld the mountains, and, lo, they trembled, and all the hills moved to and fro.


###### Jeremiah 4:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:25)


רָאִ֕יתִי וְהִנֵּ֖ה אֵ֣ין הָאָדָ֑ם וְכׇל־ע֥וֹף הַשָּׁמַ֖יִם נָדָֽדוּ׃


I beheld, and, lo, there was no man, and all the birds of the sky were fled.


###### Jeremiah 4:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:26)


רָאִ֕יתִי וְהִנֵּ֥ה הַכַּרְמֶ֖ל הַמִּדְבָּ֑ר וְכׇל־עָרָ֗יו נִתְּצוּ֙ מִפְּנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה מִפְּנֵ֖י חֲר֥וֹן אַפּֽוֹ׃{ס}


I beheld, and, lo, the fruitful place was a wilderness, and all its cities were broken down, at the presence of the Lord, and by his fierce anger.


###### Jeremiah 4:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:27)


כִּי־כֹה֙ אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה שְׁמָמָ֥ה תִֽהְיֶ֖ה כׇּל־הָאָ֑רֶץ וְכָלָ֖ה לֹ֥א אֶעֱשֶֽׂה׃


For thus says the Lord, The whole land shall be desolate; yet will I not make a full end.


###### Jeremiah 4:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:28)


עַל־זֹאת֙ תֶּאֱבַ֣ל הָאָ֔רֶץ וְקָדְר֥וּ הַשָּׁמַ֖יִם מִמָּ֑עַל עַ֤ל כִּֽי־דִבַּ֙רְתִּי֙ זַמֹּ֔תִי וְלֹ֥א נִחַ֖מְתִּי וְלֹא־אָשׁ֥וּב מִמֶּֽנָּה׃


For this shall the earth mourn, and the heavens above be black: because I have spoken it, I have purposed it, and will not relent, neither will I turn back from it.


###### Jeremiah 4:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:29)


מִקּ֨וֹל פָּרָ֜שׁ וְרֹ֣מֵה קֶ֗שֶׁת בֹּרַ֙חַת֙ כׇּל־הָעִ֔יר בָּ֚אוּ בֶּעָבִ֔ים וּבַכֵּפִ֖ים עָל֑וּ כׇּל־הָעִ֣יר עֲזוּבָ֔ה וְאֵין־יוֹשֵׁ֥ב בָּהֵ֖ן אִֽישׁ׃


The whole city flees at the noise of the horsemen and bowmen; they go into thickets, and climb up upon the rocks; every city is forsaken, and not a man dwells therein.


###### Jeremiah 4:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:30)


(ואתי) [וְאַ֨תְּ] שָׁד֜וּד מַֽה־תַּעֲשִׂ֗י כִּֽי־תִלְבְּשִׁ֨י שָׁנִ֜י כִּֽי־תַעְדִּ֣י עֲדִֽי־זָהָ֗ב כִּֽי־תִקְרְעִ֤י בַפּוּךְ֙ עֵינַ֔יִךְ לַשָּׁ֖וְא תִּתְיַפִּ֑י מָאֲסוּ־בָ֥ךְ עֹגְבִ֖ים נַפְשֵׁ֥ךְ יְבַקֵּֽשׁוּ׃


And thou, O ruined one, what wilt thou do? Though thou clothest thyself with crimson, though thou deckest thyself with ornaments of gold, though thou enlarge thy eyes with paint, in vain shalt thou make thyself fair; thy lovers will despise thee, they will seek thy life.


###### Jeremiah 4:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/4:31)


כִּי֩ ק֨וֹל כְּחוֹלָ֜ה שָׁמַ֗עְתִּי צָרָה֙ כְּמַבְכִּירָ֔ה ק֧וֹל בַּת־צִיּ֛וֹן תִּתְיַפֵּ֖חַ תְּפָרֵ֣שׂ כַּפֶּ֑יהָ אֽוֹי־נָ֣א לִ֔י כִּֽי־עָיְפָ֥ה נַפְשִׁ֖י לְהֹרְגִֽים׃{פ}


For I have heard a voice as of a woman in travail, the anguish as of her that brings forth her first child, the voice of the daughter of Żiyyon, sobbing and spreading out her hands, saying, Woe is me, now! for my soul faints before the slayers.



## Chapter 5

###### Jeremiah 5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/5:1)


שׁוֹטְט֞וּ בְּחוּצ֣וֹת יְרוּשָׁלַ֗͏ִם וּרְאוּ־נָ֤א וּדְעוּ֙ וּבַקְשׁ֣וּ בִרְחוֹבוֹתֶ֔יהָ אִם־תִּמְצְא֣וּ אִ֔ישׁ אִם־יֵ֛שׁ עֹשֶׂ֥ה מִשְׁפָּ֖ט מְבַקֵּ֣שׁ אֱמוּנָ֑ה וְאֶסְלַ֖ח לָֽהּ׃


Run to and fro through the streets of Yerushalayim, and see now, and know, and seek in its broad places, if you can find a man, if there be any that does justice, that seeks the truth; and I will pardon it.


###### Jeremiah 5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/5:2)

And though they say, As the Lord lives; surely they swear falsely.


###### Jeremiah 5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/5:3)


יְהֹוָ֗ה עֵינֶ֘יךָ֮ הֲל֣וֹא לֶאֱמוּנָה֒ הִכִּ֤יתָה אֹתָם֙ וְֽלֹא־חָ֔לוּ כִּלִּיתָ֕ם מֵאֲנ֖וּ קַ֣חַת מוּסָ֑ר חִזְּק֤וּ פְנֵיהֶם֙ מִסֶּ֔לַע מֵאֲנ֖וּ לָשֽׁוּב׃


O Lord, are not Thy eyes upon the truth? Thou hast stricken them, but they have not smarted; Thou hast consumed them, but they have refused to receive correction: they have made their faces harder than a rock; they have refused to return.


###### Jeremiah 5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/5:4)


וַאֲנִ֣י אָמַ֔רְתִּי אַךְ־דַּלִּ֖ים הֵ֑ם נוֹאֲל֕וּ כִּ֣י לֹ֤א יָֽדְעוּ֙ דֶּ֣רֶךְ יְהֹוָ֔ה מִשְׁפַּ֖ט אֱלֹהֵיהֶֽם׃


Therefore I said, Surely these are poor; they are foolish: for they know not the way of the Lord, nor the judgment of their God.

אֵֽלְכָה־לִּ֤י אֶל־הַגְּדֹלִים֙ וַאֲדַבְּרָ֣ה אוֹתָ֔ם כִּ֣י הֵ֗מָּה יָֽדְעוּ֙ דֶּ֣רֶךְ יְהֹוָ֔ה מִשְׁפַּ֖ט אֱלֹהֵיהֶ֑ם אַ֣ךְ הֵ֤מָּה יַחְדָּו֙ שָׁ֣בְרוּ עֹ֔ל נִתְּק֖וּ מוֹסֵרֽוֹת׃


I will get me to the great men, and will speak to them; for they have known the way of the Lord, and the judgment of their God: but these all alike have broken the yoke, and burst the bonds.

עַל־כֵּן֩ הִכָּ֨ם אַרְיֵ֜ה מִיַּ֗עַר זְאֵ֤ב עֲרָבוֹת֙ יְשׇׁדְדֵ֔ם נָמֵ֤ר שֹׁקֵד֙ עַל־עָ֣רֵיהֶ֔ם כׇּל־הַיּוֹצֵ֥א מֵהֵ֖נָּה יִטָּרֵ֑ף כִּ֤י רַבּוּ֙ פִּשְׁעֵיהֶ֔ם עָצְמ֖וּ מְשֻׁבוֹתֵיהֶֽם׃


Therefore a lion out of the forest shall slay them, and a wolf from the deserts shall spoil them, a leopard shall watch over their cities: everyone that goes out from there shall be torn in pieces: because their transgressions are many, and their backslidings are increased.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/5:7)


אֵ֤י לָזֹאת֙ (אסלוח) [אֶֽסְלַֽח־]לָ֔ךְ בָּנַ֣יִךְ עֲזָב֔וּנִי וַיִּשָּׁבְע֖וּ בְּלֹ֣א אֱלֹהִ֑ים וָאַשְׂבִּ֤עַ אוֹתָם֙ וַיִּנְאָ֔פוּ וּבֵ֥ית זוֹנָ֖ה יִתְגּוֹדָֽדוּ׃

הַעַל־אֵ֥לֶּה לֽוֹא־אֶפְקֹ֖ד נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה וְאִם֙ בְּג֣וֹי אֲשֶׁר־כָּזֶ֔ה לֹ֥א תִתְנַקֵּ֖ם נַפְשִֽׁי׃{ס}

עֲל֤וּ בְשָׁרוֹתֶ֙יהָ֙ וְשַׁחֵ֔תוּ וְכָלָ֖ה אַֽל־תַּעֲשׂ֑וּ הָסִ֙ירוּ֙ נְטִ֣ישׁוֹתֶ֔יהָ כִּ֛י ל֥וֹא לַיהֹוָ֖ה הֵֽמָּה׃


Go up upon her vine rows, and destroy; but make not a full end: take away her branches; for they are not the Lord’s.

כִּי֩ בָג֨וֹד בָּגְד֜וּ בִּ֗י בֵּ֧ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֛ל וּבֵ֥ית יְהוּדָ֖ה נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃

כִּֽחֲשׁוּ֙ בַּיהֹוָ֔ה וַיֹּאמְר֖וּ לוֹא־ה֑וּא וְלֹֽא־תָב֤וֹא עָלֵ֙ינוּ֙ רָעָ֔ה וְחֶ֥רֶב וְרָעָ֖ב ל֥וֹא נִרְאֶֽה׃

הִנְנִ֣י מֵבִיא֩ עֲלֵיכֶ֨ם גּ֧וֹי מִמֶּרְחָ֛ק בֵּ֥ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה גּ֣וֹי**׀** אֵיתָ֣ן ה֗וּא גּ֤וֹי מֵֽעוֹלָם֙ ה֔וּא גּ֚וֹי לֹא־תֵדַ֣ע לְשֹׁנ֔וֹ וְלֹ֥א תִשְׁמַ֖ע מַה־יְדַבֵּֽר׃

וְאָכַ֨ל קְצִֽירְךָ֜ וְלַחְמֶ֗ךָ יֹֽאכְלוּ֙ בָּנֶ֣יךָ וּבְנוֹתֶ֔יךָ יֹאכַ֤ל צֹֽאנְךָ֙ וּבְקָרֶ֔ךָ יֹאכַ֥ל גַּפְנְךָ֖ וּתְאֵנָתֶ֑ךָ יְרֹשֵׁ֞שׁ עָרֵ֣י מִבְצָרֶ֗יךָ אֲשֶׁ֥ר אַתָּ֛ה בֹּטֵ֥חַ בָּהֵ֖נָּה בֶּחָֽרֶב׃


And they shall eat up thy harvest, and thy bread, which thy sons and thy daughters should eat: they shall eat up thy flocks and thy herds: they shall eat up thy vines and thy fig trees: they shall batter thy fortified cities, wherein thou dost trust, with the sword.

Nevertheless in those days, says the Lord, I will not make a full end with you.


###### Jeremiah 5:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/5:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/5:19)


וְהָיָה֙ כִּ֣י תֹאמְר֔וּ תַּ֣חַת מֶ֗ה עָשָׂ֨ה יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהֵ֛ינוּ לָ֖נוּ אֶת־כׇּל־אֵ֑לֶּה וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֲלֵיהֶ֗ם כַּאֲשֶׁ֨ר עֲזַבְתֶּ֤ם אוֹתִי֙ וַתַּעַבְד֞וּ אֱלֹהֵ֤י נֵכָר֙ בְּאַרְצְכֶ֔ם כֵּ֚ן תַּעַבְד֣וּ זָרִ֔ים בְּאֶ֖רֶץ לֹ֥א לָכֶֽם׃{פ}


And it shall come to pass, when you shall say, Why does the Lord our God do all these things to us? then shalt thou answer them, As you have forsaken me, and served strange gods in your land, so shall you serve strangers in a land that is not yours.

Declare this in the house of Ya῾aqov, and publish it in Yehuda, saying,

שִׁמְעוּ־נָ֣א זֹ֔את עַ֥ם סָכָ֖ל וְאֵ֣ין לֵ֑ב עֵינַ֤יִם לָהֶם֙ וְלֹ֣א יִרְא֔וּ אׇזְנַ֥יִם לָהֶ֖ם וְלֹ֥א יִשְׁמָֽעוּ׃


Hear now this, O foolish people, and without understanding; who have eyes, and see not; who have ears, and hear not:


###### Jeremiah 5:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/5:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/5:22)


הַאוֹתִ֨י לֹא־תִירָ֜אוּ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֗ה אִ֤ם מִפָּנַי֙ לֹ֣א תָחִ֔ילוּ אֲשֶׁר־שַׂ֤מְתִּי חוֹל֙ גְּב֣וּל לַיָּ֔ם חׇק־עוֹלָ֖ם וְלֹ֣א יַעַבְרֶ֑נְהוּ וַיִּֽתְגָּעֲשׁוּ֙ וְלֹ֣א יוּכָ֔לוּ וְהָמ֥וּ גַלָּ֖יו וְלֹ֥א יַעַבְרֻֽנְהוּ׃


Do you not fear me? says the Lord: will you not tremble at my presence, who have placed the sand for the bound of the sea by a perpetual decree, that it cannot pass it: and though its waves toss themselves, yet can they not prevail; though they roar, yet can they not pass over it?


###### Jeremiah 5:23

But this people has a revolting and a rebellious heart; they are revolted and gone.


###### Jeremiah 5:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/5:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/5:24)


וְלֽוֹא־אָמְר֣וּ בִלְבָבָ֗ם נִ֤ירָא נָא֙ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵ֔ינוּ הַנֹּתֵ֗ן גֶּ֛שֶׁם (וירה) [יוֹרֶ֥ה] וּמַלְק֖וֹשׁ בְּעִתּ֑וֹ שְׁבֻעֹ֛ת חֻקּ֥וֹת קָצִ֖יר יִשְׁמׇר־לָֽנוּ׃
