###### Jeremiah 5:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/5:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/5:28)


שָֽׁמְנ֣וּ עָֽשְׁת֗וּ גַּ֚ם עָבְר֣וּ דִבְרֵי־רָ֔ע דִּ֣ין לֹא־דָ֔נוּ דִּ֥ין יָת֖וֹם וְיַצְלִ֑יחוּ וּמִשְׁפַּ֥ט אֶבְיוֹנִ֖ים לֹ֥א שָׁפָֽטוּ׃


They are grown fat, and sleek: yea, they overpass in deeds of wickedness: they judge not the cause of the fatherless, that they may prosper; and the right of the needy do they not judge.


###### Jeremiah 5:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/5:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/5:29)


הַעַל־אֵ֥לֶּה לֹֽא־אֶפְקֹ֖ד נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה אִ֚ם בְּג֣וֹי אֲשֶׁר־כָּזֶ֔ה לֹ֥א תִתְנַקֵּ֖ם נַפְשִֽׁי׃{ס}


Shall I not punish for these things? says the Lord: shall not my soul be avenged on such a nation as this?


###### Jeremiah 5:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/5:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/5:30)


שַׁמָּה֙ וְשַׁ֣עֲרוּרָ֔ה נִהְיְתָ֖ה בָּאָֽרֶץ׃


An appalling and horrible thing is committed in the land;


###### Jeremiah 5:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/5:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/5:31)


הַנְּבִאִ֞ים נִבְּא֣וּ בַשֶּׁ֗קֶר וְהַכֹּֽהֲנִים֙ יִרְדּ֣וּ עַל־יְדֵיהֶ֔ם וְעַמִּ֖י אָ֣הֲבוּ כֵ֑ן וּמַֽה־תַּעֲשׂ֖וּ לְאַחֲרִיתָֽהּ׃


the prophets prophesy falsely, and the priests bear rule by their means; and my people love to have it so: and what will you do when the end of it comes?



## Chapter 6

###### Jeremiah 6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:1)


הָעִ֣זוּ**׀** בְּנֵ֣י בִנְיָמִ֗ן מִקֶּ֙רֶב֙ יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם וּבִתְק֙וֹעַ֙ תִּקְע֣וּ שׁוֹפָ֔ר וְעַל־בֵּ֥ית הַכֶּ֖רֶם שְׂא֣וּ מַשְׂאֵ֑ת כִּ֥י רָעָ֛ה נִשְׁקְפָ֥ה מִצָּפ֖וֹן וְשֶׁ֥בֶר גָּדֽוֹל׃


O children of Binyamin, flee for safety out of the midst of Yerushalayim, and blow the shofar in Teqoa, and set up a beacon in Bet-hakkerem: for evil appears out of the north, and great destruction.


###### Jeremiah 6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:2)


הַנָּוָה֙ וְהַמְּעֻנָּגָ֔ה דָּמִ֖יתִי בַּת־צִיּֽוֹן׃


Thou seemest comely and delicate, O daughter of Żiyyon.


###### Jeremiah 6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:3)


אֵלֶ֛יהָ יָבֹ֥אוּ רֹעִ֖ים וְעֶדְרֵיהֶ֑ם תָּקְע֨וּ עָלֶ֤יהָ אֹֽהָלִים֙ סָבִ֔יב רָע֖וּ אִ֥ישׁ אֶת־יָדֽוֹ׃


Yet shepherds with their flocks shall come unto her; they shall pitch their tents against her round about; they shall feed every one in his place.


###### Jeremiah 6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:4)


קַדְּשׁ֤וּ עָלֶ֙יהָ֙ מִלְחָמָ֔ה ק֖וּמוּ וְנַעֲלֶ֣ה בַֽצׇּהֳרָ֑יִם א֥וֹי לָ֙נוּ֙ כִּֽי־פָנָ֣ה הַיּ֔וֹם כִּ֥י יִנָּט֖וּ צִלְלֵי־עָֽרֶב׃


Prepare war against her; arise, and let us go up at noon. Woe to us! for the day declines, for the shadows of the evening are lengthened.


###### Jeremiah 6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:5)


ק֚וּמוּ וְנַעֲלֶ֣ה בַלָּ֔יְלָה וְנַשְׁחִ֖יתָה אַרְמְנוֹתֶֽיהָ׃{פ}


Arise, and let us go by night, and let us destroy her palaces.


###### Jeremiah 6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:6)


כִּ֣י כֹ֤ה אָמַר֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֔וֹת כִּרְת֣וּ עֵצָ֔הֿ וְשִׁפְכ֥וּ עַל־יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם סֹֽלְלָ֑ה הִ֚יא הָעִ֣יר הׇפְקַ֔ד כֻּלָּ֖הּ עֹ֥שֶׁק בְּקִרְבָּֽהּ׃


For thus says the Lord of hosts, Hew down trees, and cast up a mound against Yerushalayim: this is the city to be punished; there is oppression everywhere in the midst of her.


###### Jeremiah 6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:7)


כְּהָקִ֥יר (בור) [בַּ֙יִר֙] מֵימֶ֔יהָ כֵּ֖ן הֵקֵ֣רָה רָעָתָ֑הּ חָמָ֣ס וָ֠שֹׁ֠ד יִשָּׁ֨מַע בָּ֧הּ עַל־פָּנַ֛י תָּמִ֖יד חֳלִ֥י וּמַכָּֽה׃


As a well keeps its water fresh, so she keeps fresh her wickedness: violence and spoil, grief and wounds, is heard in her before me continually.


###### Jeremiah 6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:8)


הִוָּֽסְרִי֙ יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם פֶּן־תֵּקַ֥ע נַפְשִׁ֖י מִמֵּ֑ךְ פֶּן־אֲשִׂימֵ֣ךְ שְׁמָמָ֔ה אֶ֖רֶץ ל֥וֹא נוֹשָֽׁבָה׃{פ}


Be thou instructed, O Yerushalayim, lest my soul depart from thee; lest I make thee desolate, a land not inhabited.


###### Jeremiah 6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:9)


כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֔וֹת עוֹלֵ֛ל יְעוֹלְל֥וּ כַגֶּ֖פֶן שְׁאֵרִ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל הָשֵׁב֙ יָֽדְךָ֔ כְּבוֹצֵ֖ר עַל־סַלְסִלּֽוֹת׃


Thus says the Lord of hosts, They shall thoroughly glean the remnant of Yisra᾽el like a vine: pass thy hand again like a grapegatherer over the branches.


###### Jeremiah 6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:10)


עַל־מִ֨י אֲדַבְּרָ֤ה וְאָעִ֙ידָה֙ וְיִשְׁמָ֔עוּ הִנֵּה֙ עֲרֵלָ֣ה אׇזְנָ֔ם וְלֹ֥א יוּכְל֖וּ לְהַקְשִׁ֑יב הִנֵּ֣ה דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֗ה הָיָ֥ה לָהֶ֛ם לְחֶרְפָּ֖ה לֹ֥א יַחְפְּצוּ־בֽוֹ׃


To whom shall I speak, and give warning, that they may hear? behold, their ear is uncircumcised, and they cannot hearken: behold, the word of the Lord is unto them a reproach; they have no delight in it.


###### Jeremiah 6:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:11)


וְאֵת֩ חֲמַ֨ת יְהֹוָ֤ה׀מָלֵ֙אתִי֙ נִלְאֵ֣יתִי הָכִ֔יל שְׁפֹ֤ךְ עַל־עוֹלָל֙ בַּח֔וּץ וְעַ֛ל ס֥וֹד בַּחוּרִ֖ים יַחְדָּ֑ו כִּֽי־גַם־אִ֤ישׁ עִם־אִשָּׁה֙ יִלָּכֵ֔דוּ זָקֵ֖ן עִם־מְלֵ֥א יָמִֽים׃


Therefore I am full of the fury of the Lord; I am weary of holding it in: I will pour it out upon the infants in the street, and upon the assembly of young men together: for even the husband with the wife shall be taken, the aged with him that is full of days.


###### Jeremiah 6:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:12)


וְנָסַ֤בּוּ בָֽתֵּיהֶם֙ לַאֲחֵרִ֔ים שָׂד֥וֹת וְנָשִׁ֖ים יַחְדָּ֑ו כִּֽי־אַטֶּ֧ה אֶת־יָדִ֛י עַל־יֹשְׁבֵ֥י הָאָ֖רֶץ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


And their houses shall be turned over to others, with their fields and wives together: for I will stretch out my hand upon the inhabitants of the land, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 6:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:13)


כִּ֤י מִקְּטַנָּם֙ וְעַד־גְּדוֹלָ֔ם כֻּלּ֖וֹ בּוֹצֵ֣עַ בָּ֑צַע וּמִנָּבִיא֙ וְעַד־כֹּהֵ֔ן כֻּלּ֖וֹ עֹ֥שֶׂה שָּֽׁקֶר׃


For from the least of them even to the greatest of them everyone is greedy for gain; and from the prophet even to the priest everyone deals falsely.


###### Jeremiah 6:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:14)


וַֽיְרַפְּא֞וּ אֶת־שֶׁ֤בֶר עַמִּי֙ עַל־נְקַלָּ֔ה לֵאמֹ֖ר שָׁל֣וֹם׀שָׁל֑וֹם וְאֵ֖ין שָׁלֽוֹם׃


They have healed the hurt of the daughter of my people superficially, saying, Peace, peace; when there is no peace.


###### Jeremiah 6:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:15)


הֹבִ֕ישׁוּ כִּ֥י תוֹעֵבָ֖ה עָשׂ֑וּ גַּם־בּ֣וֹשׁ לֹֽא־יֵב֗וֹשׁוּ גַּם־הַכְלִים֙ לֹ֣א יָדָ֔עוּ לָכֵ֞ן יִפְּל֧וּ בַנֹּפְלִ֛ים בְּעֵת־פְּקַדְתִּ֥ים יִכָּשְׁל֖וּ אָמַ֥ר יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


They should have been ashamed when they had committed abomination; but they were not at all ashamed, neither could they blush: therefore they shall fall among the fallen: at the time that I punish them they shall stumble, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 6:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:16)


כֹּ֣ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֡ה עִמְדוּ֩ עַל־דְּרָכִ֨ים וּרְא֜וּ וְשַׁאֲל֣וּ**׀** לִנְתִב֣וֹת עוֹלָ֗ם אֵי־זֶ֨ה דֶ֤רֶךְ הַטּוֹב֙ וּלְכוּ־בָ֔הּ וּמִצְא֥וּ מַרְגּ֖וֹעַ לְנַפְשְׁכֶ֑ם וַיֹּאמְר֖וּ לֹ֥א נֵלֵֽךְ׃


Thus says the Lord, Stand on the highways, and see, and ask for the old paths, where the good way is, and walk upon it, and you shall find rest for your souls. But they said, We will not walk therein.


###### Jeremiah 6:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:17)


וַהֲקִמֹתִ֤י עֲלֵיכֶם֙ צֹפִ֔ים הַקְשִׁ֖יבוּ לְק֣וֹל שׁוֹפָ֑ר וַיֹּאמְר֖וּ לֹ֥א נַקְשִֽׁיב׃


And I set watchmen over you, saying, Hearken to the sound of the shofar. But they said, We will not hearken.


###### Jeremiah 6:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:18)


לָכֵ֖ן שִׁמְע֣וּ הַגּוֹיִ֑ם וּדְעִ֥י עֵדָ֖ה אֶת־אֲשֶׁר־בָּֽם׃


Therefore hear, you nations, and know, O congregation, what is among them.


###### Jeremiah 6:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:19)


שִׁמְעִ֣י הָאָ֔רֶץ הִנֵּ֨ה אָנֹכִ֜י מֵבִ֥יא רָעָ֛ה אֶל־הָעָ֥ם הַזֶּ֖ה פְּרִ֣י מַחְשְׁבוֹתָ֑ם כִּ֤י עַל־דְּבָרַי֙ לֹ֣א הִקְשִׁ֔יבוּ וְתוֹרָתִ֖י וַיִּמְאֲסוּ־בָֽהּ׃


Hear, O earth: behold, I will bring evil upon this people, the fruit of their thoughts, because they have not hearkened to my words, nor to my Tora, but have rejected it.


###### Jeremiah 6:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:20)


לָמָּה־זֶּ֨ה לִ֤י לְבוֹנָה֙ מִשְּׁבָ֣א תָב֔וֹא וְקָנֶ֥ה הַטּ֖וֹב מֵאֶ֣רֶץ מֶרְחָ֑ק עֹלֽוֹתֵיכֶם֙ לֹ֣א לְרָצ֔וֹן וְזִבְחֵיכֶ֖ם לֹא־עָ֥רְבוּ לִֽי׃


To what purpose comes incense to me from Sheva, and the sweet cane from a far country? your burnt offerings are not acceptable, nor your sacrifices sweet to me.


###### Jeremiah 6:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:21)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה הִנְנִ֥י נֹתֵ֛ן אֶל־הָעָ֥ם הַזֶּ֖ה מִכְשֹׁלִ֑ים וְכָ֣שְׁלוּ בָ֠ם אָב֨וֹת וּבָנִ֥ים יַחְדָּ֛ו שָׁכֵ֥ן וְרֵע֖וֹ (יאבדו) [וְאָבָֽדוּ]׃{פ}


Therefore thus says the Lord, Behold, I will lay stumblingblocks before this people, and the fathers and the sons together shall fall upon them; the neighbour and his friend shall perish.


###### Jeremiah 6:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:22)


כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה הִנֵּ֛ה עַ֥ם בָּ֖א מֵאֶ֣רֶץ צָפ֑וֹן וְג֣וֹי גָּד֔וֹל יֵע֖וֹר מִיַּרְכְּתֵי־אָֽרֶץ׃


Thus says the Lord, Behold, a people comes from the north country, and a great nation shall stir from the sides of the earth.


###### Jeremiah 6:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:23)


קֶ֣שֶׁת וְכִיד֞וֹן יַחֲזִ֗יקוּ אַכְזָרִ֥י הוּא֙ וְלֹ֣א יְרַחֵ֔מוּ קוֹלָם֙ כַּיָּ֣ם יֶהֱמֶ֔ה וְעַל־סוּסִ֖ים יִרְכָּ֑בוּ עָר֗וּךְ כְּאִישׁ֙ לַמִּלְחָמָ֔ה עָלַ֖יִךְ בַּת־צִיּֽוֹן׃


They shall lay hold on bow and spear; they are cruel, and have no mercy; their voice roars like the sea; and they ride upon horses, set in array as men for war against thee, O daughter of Żiyyon.


###### Jeremiah 6:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:24)


שָׁמַ֥עְנוּ אֶת־שׇׁמְע֖וֹ רָפ֣וּ יָדֵ֑ינוּ צָרָה֙ הֶחֱזִקַ֔תְנוּ חִ֖יל כַּיּֽוֹלֵדָֽה׃


We have heard the report of it: our hands grow feeble: anguish has taken hold of us, and pain, as of a woman in travail.


###### Jeremiah 6:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:25)


אַל־[תֵּֽצְאוּ֙] (תצאי) הַשָּׂדֶ֔ה וּבַדֶּ֖רֶךְ אַל־[תֵּלֵ֑כוּ] (תלכי) כִּ֚י חֶ֣רֶב לְאֹיֵ֔ב מָג֖וֹר מִסָּבִֽיב׃


Go not forth into the field, nor walk by the way; for the sword of the enemy is there, and terror on every side.


###### Jeremiah 6:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:26)


בַּת־עַמִּ֤י חִגְרִי־שָׂק֙ וְהִתְפַּלְּשִׁ֣י בָאֵ֔פֶר אֵ֤בֶל יָחִיד֙ עֲשִׂ֣י לָ֔ךְ מִסְפַּ֖ד תַּמְרוּרִ֑ים כִּ֣י פִתְאֹ֔ם יָבֹ֥א הַשֹּׁדֵ֖ד עָלֵֽינוּ׃


O daughter of my people, gird on sackcloth, and roll in ashes: make thee mourning, as for an only son, most bitter lamentation: for the spoiler shall suddenly come upon us.


###### Jeremiah 6:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:27)


בָּח֛וֹן נְתַתִּ֥יךָ בְעַמִּ֖י מִבְצָ֑ר וְתֵדַ֕ע וּבָחַנְתָּ֖ אֶת־דַּרְכָּֽם׃


I have set thee for a tower and a fortress among my people, that thou mayst know and test their way.


###### Jeremiah 6:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:28)


כֻּלָּם֙ סָרֵ֣י סוֹרְרִ֔ים הֹלְכֵ֥י רָכִ֖יל נְחֹ֣שֶׁת וּבַרְזֶ֑ל כֻּלָּ֥ם מַשְׁחִיתִ֖ים הֵֽמָּה׃


They are all grievous revolters, going about with slanders: they are brass and iron; they all deal corruptly.


###### Jeremiah 6:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:29)


נָחַ֣ר מַפֻּ֔חַ (מאשתם) [מֵאֵ֖שׁ תַּ֣ם] עֹפָ֑רֶת לַשָּׁוְא֙ צָרַ֣ף צָר֔וֹף וְרָעִ֖ים לֹ֥א נִתָּֽקוּ׃


The bellows blow fiercely, the lead is consumed by the fire; but the founder melts in vain, for the wicked are not separated.


###### Jeremiah 6:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/6:30)


כֶּ֣סֶף נִמְאָ֔ס קָרְא֖וּ לָהֶ֑ם כִּֽי־מָאַ֥ס יְהֹוָ֖ה בָּהֶֽם׃{פ}


Base silver shall they be called, for the Lord has rejected them.



## Chapter 7

###### Jeremiah 7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:1)


הַדָּבָר֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הָיָ֣ה אֶֽל־יִרְמְיָ֔הוּ מֵאֵ֥ת יְהֹוָ֖ה לֵאמֹֽר׃


The word that came to Yirmeyahu from the Lord, saying,


###### Jeremiah 7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:2)


עֲמֹ֗ד בְּשַׁ֙עַר֙ בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֔ה וְקָרָ֣אתָ שָּׁ֔ם אֶת־הַדָּבָ֖ר הַזֶּ֑ה וְאָמַרְתָּ֞ שִׁמְע֣וּ דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֗ה כׇּל־יְהוּדָה֙ הַבָּאִים֙ בַּשְּׁעָרִ֣ים הָאֵ֔לֶּה לְהִֽשְׁתַּחֲוֺ֖ת לַיהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


Stand in the gate of the Lord’s house, and proclaim there this word, and say, Hear the word of the Lord, all Yehuda, that enter in at these gates to worship the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:3)


כֹּֽה־אָמַ֞ר יְהֹוָ֤ה צְבָאוֹת֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל הֵיטִ֥יבוּ דַרְכֵיכֶ֖ם וּמַעַלְלֵיכֶ֑ם וַאֲשַׁכְּנָ֣ה אֶתְכֶ֔ם בַּמָּק֖וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃


Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Yisra᾽el, Amend your ways and your doings, and I will cause you to dwell in this place.


###### Jeremiah 7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:4)


אַל־תִּבְטְח֣וּ לָכֶ֔ם אֶל־דִּבְרֵ֥י הַשֶּׁ֖קֶר לֵאמֹ֑ר הֵיכַ֤ל יְהֹוָה֙ הֵיכַ֣ל יְהֹוָ֔ה הֵיכַ֥ל יְהֹוָ֖ה הֵֽמָּה׃


Trust not in lying words, saying, The temple of the Lord, The temple of the Lord, The temple of the Lord, are these.


###### Jeremiah 7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:5)


כִּ֤י אִם־הֵיטֵיב֙ תֵּיטִ֔יבוּ אֶת־דַּרְכֵיכֶ֖ם וְאֶת־מַעַלְלֵיכֶ֑ם אִם־עָשׂ֤וֹ תַֽעֲשׂוּ֙ מִשְׁפָּ֔ט בֵּ֥ין אִ֖ישׁ וּבֵ֥ין רֵעֵֽהוּ׃


For if you thoroughly amend your ways and your doings; if you thoroughly execute justice between a man and his neighbour;


###### Jeremiah 7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:6)


גֵּ֣ר יָת֤וֹם וְאַלְמָנָה֙ לֹ֣א תַעֲשֹׁ֔קוּ וְדָ֣ם נָקִ֔י אַֽל־תִּשְׁפְּכ֖וּ בַּמָּק֣וֹם הַזֶּ֑ה וְאַחֲרֵ֨י אֱלֹהִ֧ים אֲחֵרִ֛ים לֹ֥א תֵלְכ֖וּ לְרַ֥ע לָכֶֽם׃


if you oppress not the stranger, the fatherless, and the widow, and shed not innocent blood in this place, neither walk after other gods to your hurt:


###### Jeremiah 7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:7)


וְשִׁכַּנְתִּ֤י אֶתְכֶם֙ בַּמָּק֣וֹם הַזֶּ֔ה בָּאָ֕רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֥ר נָתַ֖תִּי לַאֲבוֹתֵיכֶ֑ם לְמִן־עוֹלָ֖ם וְעַד־עוֹלָֽם׃


then will I cause you to dwell in this place, in the land that I gave to your fathers, for ever and ever.


###### Jeremiah 7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:8)


הִנֵּ֤ה אַתֶּם֙ בֹּטְחִ֣ים לָכֶ֔ם עַל־דִּבְרֵ֖י הַשָּׁ֑קֶר לְבִלְתִּ֖י הוֹעִֽיל׃


Behold, you trust in lying words, that cannot profit.


###### Jeremiah 7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:9)


הֲגָנֹ֤ב׀רָצֹ֙חַ֙ וְֽנָאֹ֔ף וְהִשָּׁבֵ֥עַ לַשֶּׁ֖קֶר וְקַטֵּ֣ר לַבָּ֑עַל וְהָלֹ֗ךְ אַחֲרֵ֛י אֱלֹהִ֥ים אֲחֵרִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹֽא־יְדַעְתֶּֽם׃


Will you steal, murder, and commit adultery, and swear falsely, and burn incense to the Ba῾al, and walk after other gods whom you know not;


###### Jeremiah 7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:10)


וּבָאתֶ֞ם וַעֲמַדְתֶּ֣ם לְפָנַ֗י בַּבַּ֤יִת הַזֶּה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר נִקְרָֽא־שְׁמִ֣י עָלָ֔יו וַאֲמַרְתֶּ֖ם נִצַּ֑לְנוּ לְמַ֣עַן עֲשׂ֔וֹת אֵ֥ת כׇּל־הַתּוֹעֵב֖וֹת הָאֵֽלֶּה׃


and come and stand before me in this house, which is called by my name, and say, We are delivered; that you may do all these abominations?


###### Jeremiah 7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:11)


הַֽמְעָרַ֣ת פָּרִצִ֗ים הָיָ֨ה הַבַּ֧יִת הַזֶּ֛ה אֲשֶׁר־נִקְרָֽא־שְׁמִ֥י עָלָ֖יו בְּעֵינֵיכֶ֑ם גַּ֧ם אָנֹכִ֛י הִנֵּ֥ה רָאִ֖יתִי נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


Is this house, which is called by my name, become a den of robbers in your eyes? Behold, I myself have seen it, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 7:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:12)


כִּ֣י לְכוּ־נָ֗א אֶל־מְקוֹמִי֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בְּשִׁיל֔וֹ אֲשֶׁ֨ר שִׁכַּ֧נְתִּֽי שְׁמִ֛י שָׁ֖ם בָּרִֽאשׁוֹנָ֑ה וּרְאוּ֙ אֵ֣ת אֲשֶׁר־עָשִׂ֣יתִי ל֔וֹ מִפְּנֵ֕י רָעַ֖ת עַמִּ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


But go now to my place which was in Shilo, where I set my name at the first, and see what I did to it for the wickedness of my people Yisra᾽el.


###### Jeremiah 7:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:13)


וְעַתָּ֗ה יַ֧עַן עֲשׂוֹתְכֶ֛ם אֶת־כׇּל־הַמַּעֲשִׂ֥ים הָאֵ֖לֶּה נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה וָאֲדַבֵּ֨ר אֲלֵיכֶ֜ם הַשְׁכֵּ֤ם וְדַבֵּר֙ וְלֹ֣א שְׁמַעְתֶּ֔ם וָאֶקְרָ֥א אֶתְכֶ֖ם וְלֹ֥א עֲנִיתֶֽם׃


And now, because you have done all these deeds, says the Lord, and though I spoke to you, from morning till night, but you did not listen; and I called you, but you did not answer;


###### Jeremiah 7:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:14)


וְעָשִׂ֜יתִי לַבַּ֣יִת**׀** אֲשֶׁ֧ר נִֽקְרָא־שְׁמִ֣י עָלָ֗יו אֲשֶׁ֤ר אַתֶּם֙ בֹּטְחִ֣ים בּ֔וֹ וְלַ֨מָּק֔וֹם אֲשֶׁר־נָתַ֥תִּי לָכֶ֖ם וְלַאֲבוֹתֵיכֶ֑ם כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר עָשִׂ֖יתִי לְשִׁלֽוֹ׃


therefore will I do to this house, which is called by my name, and in which you trust, and to the place which I gave to you and to your fathers, as I have done to Shilo.


###### Jeremiah 7:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:15)


וְהִשְׁלַכְתִּ֥י אֶתְכֶ֖ם מֵעַ֣ל פָּנָ֑י כַּאֲשֶׁ֤ר הִשְׁלַ֙כְתִּי֙ אֶת־כׇּל־אֲחֵיכֶ֔ם אֵ֖ת כׇּל־זֶ֥רַע אֶפְרָֽיִם׃{פ}


And I will cast you out of my sight, as I have cast out all your brethren, the whole seed of Efrayim.


###### Jeremiah 7:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:16)


וְאַתָּ֞ה אַל־תִּתְפַּלֵּ֣ל**׀** בְּעַד־הָעָ֣ם הַזֶּ֗ה וְאַל־תִּשָּׂ֧א בַעֲדָ֛ם רִנָּ֥ה וּתְפִלָּ֖ה וְאַל־תִּפְגַּע־בִּ֑י כִּֽי־אֵינֶ֥נִּי שֹׁמֵ֖עַ אֹתָֽךְ׃


Therefore pray not thou for this people, neither lift up cry nor prayer for them, neither make intercession to me: for I will not hear thee.


###### Jeremiah 7:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:17)


הַאֵינְךָ֣ רֹאֶ֔ה מָ֛ה הֵ֥מָּה עֹשִׂ֖ים בְּעָרֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֑ה וּבְחֻצ֖וֹת יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


Seest thou not what they do in the cities of Yehuda and in the streets of Yerushalayim?


###### Jeremiah 7:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:18)


הַבָּנִ֞ים מְלַקְּטִ֣ים עֵצִ֗ים וְהָאָבוֹת֙ מְבַעֲרִ֣ים אֶת־הָאֵ֔שׁ וְהַנָּשִׁ֖ים לָשׁ֣וֹת בָּצֵ֑ק לַעֲשׂ֨וֹת כַּוָּנִ֜ים לִמְלֶ֣כֶת הַשָּׁמַ֗יִם וְהַסֵּ֤ךְ נְסָכִים֙ לֵאלֹהִ֣ים אֲחֵרִ֔ים לְמַ֖עַן הַכְעִסֵֽנִי׃


The children gather wood, and the fathers kindle the fire, and the women knead dough, to make cakes to the queen of heaven, and to pour out drink offerings to other gods, that they may provoke me to anger.


###### Jeremiah 7:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:19)


הַאֹתִ֛י הֵ֥ם מַכְעִסִ֖ים נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה הֲל֣וֹא אֹתָ֔ם לְמַ֖עַן בֹּ֥שֶׁת פְּנֵיהֶֽם׃


Do they provoke me to anger? says the Lord: do they not provoke themselves to their own disgrace?


###### Jeremiah 7:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:20)


לָכֵ֞ן כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר**׀** אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה הִנֵּ֨ה אַפִּ֤י וַחֲמָתִי֙ נִתֶּ֙כֶת֙ אֶל־הַמָּק֣וֹם הַזֶּ֔ה עַל־הָאָדָם֙ וְעַל־הַבְּהֵמָ֔ה וְעַל־עֵ֥ץ הַשָּׂדֶ֖ה וְעַל־פְּרִ֣י הָאֲדָמָ֑ה וּבָעֲרָ֖ה וְלֹ֥א תִכְבֶּֽה׃{פ}


Therefore thus says the Lord God; Behold, my anger and my fury shall be poured out upon this place, upon man, and upon beast, and upon the trees of the field, and upon the fruit of the ground; and it shall burn, and shall not be quenched.


###### Jeremiah 7:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:21)


כֹּ֥ה אָמַ֛ר יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל עֹלוֹתֵיכֶ֛ם סְפ֥וּ עַל־זִבְחֵיכֶ֖ם וְאִכְל֥וּ בָשָֽׂר׃


Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Yisra᾽el; Add your burnt offerings to your sacrifices, and eat meat.


###### Jeremiah 7:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:22)


כִּ֠י לֹֽא־דִבַּ֤רְתִּי אֶת־אֲבֽוֹתֵיכֶם֙ וְלֹ֣א צִוִּיתִ֔ים בְּי֛וֹם (הוציא) [הוֹצִיאִ֥י] אוֹתָ֖ם מֵאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרָ֑יִם עַל־דִּבְרֵ֥י עוֹלָ֖ה וָזָֽבַח׃


For I did not speak to your fathers, nor command them in the day that I brought them out of the land of Miżrayim, concerning burnt offerings or sacrifices:


###### Jeremiah 7:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:23)


כִּ֣י אִֽם־אֶת־הַדָּבָ֣ר הַ֠זֶּ֠ה צִוִּ֨יתִי אוֹתָ֤ם לֵאמֹר֙ שִׁמְע֣וּ בְקוֹלִ֔י וְהָיִ֤יתִי לָכֶם֙ לֵֽאלֹהִ֔ים וְאַתֶּ֖ם תִּֽהְיוּ־לִ֣י לְעָ֑ם וַהֲלַכְתֶּ֗ם בְּכׇל־הַדֶּ֙רֶךְ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר אֲצַוֶּ֣ה אֶתְכֶ֔ם לְמַ֖עַן יִיטַ֥ב לָכֶֽם׃


but this thing I commanded them, saying, Obey my voice, and I will be your God, and you shall be my people: and walk in all the ways that I have commanded you, that it may be well with you.


###### Jeremiah 7:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:24)


וְלֹ֤א שָֽׁמְעוּ֙ וְלֹא־הִטּ֣וּ אֶת־אׇזְנָ֔ם וַיֵּֽלְכוּ֙ בְּמֹ֣עֵצ֔וֹת בִּשְׁרִר֖וּת לִבָּ֣ם הָרָ֑ע וַיִּהְי֥וּ לְאָח֖וֹר וְלֹ֥א לְפָנִֽים׃


But they did not hearken, nor incline their ear, but walked in the counsels and in the imagination of their evil heart, and went backward, and not forward.


###### Jeremiah 7:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:25)


לְמִן־הַיּ֗וֹם אֲשֶׁ֨ר יָצְא֤וּ אֲבֽוֹתֵיכֶם֙ מֵאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרַ֔יִם עַ֖ד הַיּ֣וֹם הַזֶּ֑ה וָאֶשְׁלַ֤ח אֲלֵיכֶם֙ אֶת־כׇּל־עֲבָדַ֣י הַנְּבִיאִ֔ים י֖וֹם הַשְׁכֵּ֥ם וְשָׁלֹֽחַ׃


From the day that your fathers came forth out of the land of Miżrayim to this day I have sent to you all my servants the prophets, sending them from morning till night.


###### Jeremiah 7:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:26)


וְל֤וֹא שָֽׁמְעוּ֙ אֵלַ֔י וְלֹ֥א הִטּ֖וּ אֶת־אׇזְנָ֑ם וַיַּקְשׁוּ֙ אֶת־עׇרְפָּ֔ם הֵרֵ֖עוּ מֵאֲבוֹתָֽם׃


Yet they did not hearken to me, nor incline their ear, but stiffened their neck: they did worse than their fathers.


###### Jeremiah 7:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:27)


וְדִבַּרְתָּ֤ אֲלֵיהֶם֙ אֶת־כׇּל־הַדְּבָרִ֣ים הָאֵ֔לֶּה וְלֹ֥א יִשְׁמְע֖וּ אֵלֶ֑יךָ וְקָרָ֥אתָ אֲלֵיהֶ֖ם וְלֹ֥א יַעֲנֽוּכָה׃


Therefore thou shalt speak all these words to them; but they will not hearken to thee: thou shalt also call to them; but they will not answer thee.


###### Jeremiah 7:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:28)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֲלֵיהֶ֗ם זֶ֤ה הַגּוֹי֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר לֽוֹא־שָׁמְע֗וּ בְּקוֹל֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהָ֔יו וְלֹ֥א לָקְח֖וּ מוּסָ֑ר אָֽבְדָה֙ הָאֱמוּנָ֔ה וְנִכְרְתָ֖ה מִפִּיהֶֽם׃{ס}


But thou shalt say to them, This is a nation that does not obey the voice of the Lord their God, nor does it receive correction: truthfulness is perished, and is cut off from their mouth.


###### Jeremiah 7:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:29)


גׇּזִּ֤י נִזְרֵךְ֙ וְֽהַשְׁלִ֔יכִי וּשְׂאִ֥י עַל־שְׁפָיִ֖ם קִינָ֑ה כִּ֚י מָאַ֣ס יְהֹוָ֔ה וַיִּטֹּ֖שׁ אֶת־דּ֥וֹר עֶבְרָתֽוֹ׃


Cut off thy hair, and cast it away, and take up a lamentation on the high hills; for the Lord has rejected and forsaken the generation of his wrath.


###### Jeremiah 7:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:30)


כִּי־עָשׂ֨וּ בְנֵי־יְהוּדָ֥ה הָרַ֛ע בְּעֵינַ֖י נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה שָׂ֣מוּ שִׁקּוּצֵיהֶ֗ם בַּבַּ֛יִת אֲשֶׁר־נִקְרָֽא־שְׁמִ֥י עָלָ֖יו לְטַמְּאֽוֹ׃


For the children of Yehuda have done evil in my sight, says the Lord: they have set their abominations in the house which is called by my name, to pollute it.


###### Jeremiah 7:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:31)


וּבָנ֞וּ בָּמ֣וֹת הַתֹּ֗פֶת אֲשֶׁר֙ בְּגֵ֣יא בֶן־הִנֹּ֔ם לִשְׂרֹ֛ף אֶת־בְּנֵיהֶ֥ם וְאֶת־בְּנֹתֵיהֶ֖ם בָּאֵ֑שׁ אֲשֶׁר֙ לֹ֣א צִוִּ֔יתִי וְלֹ֥א עָלְתָ֖ה עַל־לִבִּֽי׃{פ}


And they have built the high places of Tofet, which is in the valley of Ben-hinnom, to burn their sons and their daughters in the fire, which I did not command them, nor did it come into my heart.


###### Jeremiah 7:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:32)


לָכֵ֞ן הִנֵּֽה־יָמִ֤ים בָּאִים֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה וְלֹא־יֵאָמֵ֨ר ע֤וֹד הַתֹּ֙פֶת֙ וְגֵ֣יא בֶן־הִנֹּ֔ם כִּ֖י אִם־גֵּ֣יא הַהֲרֵגָ֑ה וְקָבְר֥וּ בְתֹ֖פֶת מֵאֵ֥ין מָקֽוֹם׃


Therefore, behold, days are coming, says the Lord, it shall no more be called Tofet, nor the valley of Ben-hinnom, but the Valley of Slaughter: for they shall bury in Tofet, because there is no room.


###### Jeremiah 7:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:33)


וְֽהָ֨יְתָ֜ה נִבְלַ֨ת הָעָ֤ם הַזֶּה֙ לְמַֽאֲכָ֔ל לְע֥וֹף הַשָּׁמַ֖יִם וּלְבֶהֱמַ֣ת הָאָ֑רֶץ וְאֵ֖ין מַחֲרִֽיד׃


And the carcasses of this people shall be food for the birds of the heaven, and for the beasts of the earth; and none shall frighten them away.


###### Jeremiah 7:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/7:34)


וְהִשְׁבַּתִּ֣י**׀** מֵעָרֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֗ה וּמֵֽחֻצוֹת֙ יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם ק֤וֹל שָׂשׂוֹן֙ וְק֣וֹל שִׂמְחָ֔ה ק֥וֹל חָתָ֖ן וְק֣וֹל כַּלָּ֑ה כִּ֥י לְחׇרְבָּ֖ה תִּהְיֶ֥ה הָאָֽרֶץ׃


Then will I cause to cease from the cities of Yehuda, and from the streets of Yerushalayim, the voice of mirth, and the voice of gladness, the voice of the bridegroom, and the voice of the bride: for the land shall be desolate.



## Chapter 8

###### Jeremiah 8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:1)


בָּעֵ֣ת הַהִ֣יא נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֡ה (ויציאו) [יוֹצִ֣יאוּ] אֶת־עַצְמ֣וֹת מַלְכֵי־יְהוּדָ֣ה וְאֶת־עַצְמוֹת־שָׂרָיו֩ וְאֶת־עַצְמ֨וֹת הַכֹּהֲנִ֜ים וְאֵ֣ת**׀** עַצְמ֣וֹת הַנְּבִיאִ֗ים וְאֵ֛ת עַצְמ֥וֹת יוֹשְׁבֵֽי־יְרוּשָׁלָ֖͏ִם מִקִּבְרֵיהֶֽם׃


At that time, says the Lord, they shall bring out the bones of the kings of Yehuda, and the bones of his princes, and the bones of the priests, and the bones of the prophets, and the bones of the inhabitants of Yerushalayim, out of their graves:


###### Jeremiah 8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:2)


וּשְׁטָחוּם֩ לַשֶּׁ֨מֶשׁ וְלַיָּרֵ֜חַ וּלְכֹ֣ל**׀** צְבָ֣א הַשָּׁמַ֗יִם אֲשֶׁ֨ר אֲהֵב֜וּם וַאֲשֶׁ֤ר עֲבָדוּם֙ וַֽאֲשֶׁר֙ הָלְכ֣וּ אַחֲרֵיהֶ֔ם וַאֲשֶׁ֣ר דְּרָשׁ֔וּם וַאֲשֶׁ֥ר הִֽשְׁתַּחֲו֖וּ לָהֶ֑ם לֹ֤א יֵאָֽסְפוּ֙ וְלֹ֣א יִקָּבֵ֔רוּ לְדֹ֛מֶן עַל־פְּנֵ֥י הָאֲדָמָ֖ה יִהְיֽוּ׃


and they shall spread them before the sun, and the moon, and all the host of heaven, whom they have loved, and whom they have served, and after whom they have walked, and whom they have sought, and whom they have worshipped: they shall not be gathered, nor be buried; they shall be for dung upon the face of the earth.


###### Jeremiah 8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:3)


וְנִבְחַ֥ר מָ֙וֶת֙ מֵחַיִּ֔ים לְכֹ֗ל הַשְּׁאֵרִית֙ הַנִּשְׁאָרִ֔ים מִן־הַמִּשְׁפָּחָ֥ה הָרָעָ֖ה הַזֹּ֑את בְּכׇל־הַמְּקֹמ֤וֹת הַנִּשְׁאָרִים֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הִדַּחְתִּ֣ים שָׁ֔ם נְאֻ֖ם יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָאֽוֹת׃{ס}


And death shall be chosen rather than life by all the residue of those who remain of this evil family, who remain in all the places into which I have driven them, says the Lord of hosts.


###### Jeremiah 8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:4)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֲלֵיהֶ֗ם כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה הֲיִפְּל֖וּ וְלֹ֣א יָק֑וּמוּ אִם־יָשׁ֖וּב וְלֹ֥א יָשֽׁוּב׃


Moreover thou shalt say to them, Thus says the Lord; Shall men fall and not rise up again? shall a man turn away, and not turn back again?


###### Jeremiah 8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:5)


מַדּ֨וּעַ שׁוֹבְבָ֜ה הָעָ֥ם הַזֶּ֛ה יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם מְשֻׁבָ֣ה נִצַּ֑חַת הֶחֱזִ֙יקוּ֙ בַּתַּרְמִ֔ת מֵאֲנ֖וּ לָשֽׁוּב׃


Why then is this people of Yerushalayim turned away in a perpetual backsliding? they hold fast to deceit, they refuse to turn back again.


###### Jeremiah 8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:6)


הִקְשַׁ֤בְתִּי וָֽאֶשְׁמָע֙ לוֹא־כֵ֣ן יְדַבֵּ֔רוּ אֵ֣ין אִ֗ישׁ נִחָם֙ עַל־רָ֣עָת֔וֹ לֵאמֹ֖ר מֶ֣ה עָשִׂ֑יתִי כֻּלֹּ֗ה שָׁ֚ב (במרצותם) [בִּמְר֣וּצָתָ֔ם] כְּס֥וּס שׁוֹטֵ֖ף בַּמִּלְחָמָֽה׃


I hearkened and heard, but they spoke not aright: no man repents him of his wickedness, saying, What have I done? everyone turns to his own course, as the horse rushes into the battle.


###### Jeremiah 8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:7)


גַּם־חֲסִידָ֣ה בַשָּׁמַ֗יִם יָֽדְעָה֙ מוֹעֲדֶ֔יהָ וְתֹ֤ר (וסוס) [וְסִיס֙] וְעָג֔וּר שָׁמְר֖וּ אֶת־עֵ֣ת בֹּאָ֑נָה וְעַמִּ֕י לֹ֣א יָדְע֔וּ אֵ֖ת מִשְׁפַּ֥ט יְהֹוָֽה׃


Yea, the stork in the heaven knows her appointed times; and the turtle and the swift and the crane observe the time of their coming; but my people do not know the judgment of the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:8)


אֵיכָ֤ה תֹֽאמְרוּ֙ חֲכָמִ֣ים אֲנַ֔חְנוּ וְתוֹרַ֥ת יְהֹוָ֖ה אִתָּ֑נוּ אָכֵן֙ הִנֵּ֣ה לַשֶּׁ֣קֶר עָשָׂ֔ה עֵ֖ט שֶׁ֥קֶר סֹפְרִֽים׃


How can you say, We are wise, and the Tora of the Lord is with us? Surely, the pen wrought in vain, in vain the scribes.


###### Jeremiah 8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:9)


הֹבִ֣שׁוּ חֲכָמִ֔ים חַ֖תּוּ וַיִּלָּכֵ֑דוּ הִנֵּ֤ה בִדְבַר־יְהֹוָה֙ מָאָ֔סוּ וְחׇכְמַ֥ת מֶ֖ה לָהֶֽם׃


The wise men are ashamed, they are dismayed and taken: lo, they have rejected the word of the Lord; and what wisdom is in them?


###### Jeremiah 8:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:10)


לָכֵן֩ אֶתֵּ֨ן אֶת־נְשֵׁיהֶ֜ם לַאֲחֵרִ֗ים שְׂדֽוֹתֵיהֶם֙ לְי֣וֹרְשִׁ֔ים כִּ֤י מִקָּטֹן֙ וְעַד־גָּד֔וֹל כֻּלֹּ֖ה בֹּצֵ֣עַ בָּ֑צַע מִנָּבִיא֙ וְעַד־כֹּהֵ֔ן כֻּלֹּ֖ה עֹ֥שֶׂה שָּֽׁקֶר׃


Therefore will I give their wives to others, and their fields to those who shall take possession of them: for everyone from the least to the greatest is greedy for gain, from the prophet to the priest everyone deals falsely.


###### Jeremiah 8:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:11)


וַיְרַפּ֞וּ אֶת־שֶׁ֤בֶר בַּת־עַמִּי֙ עַל־נְקַלָּ֔ה לֵאמֹ֖ר שָׁל֣וֹם׀שָׁל֑וֹם וְאֵ֖ין שָׁלֽוֹם׃


For they have healed the hurt of the daughter of my people superficially, saying, Peace, peace; when there is no peace.


###### Jeremiah 8:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:12)


הֹבִ֕שׁוּ כִּ֥י תוֹעֵבָ֖ה עָשׂ֑וּ גַּם־בּ֣וֹשׁ לֹֽא־יֵבֹ֗שׁוּ וְהִכָּלֵם֙ לֹ֣א יָדָ֔עוּ לָכֵ֞ן יִפְּל֣וּ בַנֹּפְלִ֗ים בְּעֵ֧ת פְּקֻדָּתָ֛ם יִכָּשְׁל֖וּ אָמַ֥ר יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


They should have been ashamed when they had committed abomination; but they were not at all ashamed, neither could they blush; therefore shall they fall among the fallen: in the time of their punishment they shall stumble, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 8:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:13)


אָסֹ֥ף אֲסִיפֵ֖ם נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה אֵין֩ עֲנָבִ֨ים בַּגֶּ֜פֶן וְאֵ֧ין תְּאֵנִ֣ים בַּתְּאֵנָ֗ה וְהֶעָלֶה֙ נָבֵ֔ל וָאֶתֵּ֥ן לָהֶ֖ם יַעַבְרֽוּם׃


I will surely consume them, says the Lord: there shall be no grapes on the vine, nor figs on the fig tree, and the leaf shall fade; the things that I have given them shall pass away from them.


###### Jeremiah 8:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:14)


עַל־מָה֙ אֲנַ֣חְנוּ יֹֽשְׁבִ֔ים הֵאָסְפ֗וּ וְנָב֛וֹא אֶל־עָרֵ֥י הַמִּבְצָ֖ר וְנִדְּמָה־שָּׁ֑ם כִּי֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהֵ֤ינוּ הֲדִמָּ֙נוּ֙ וַיַּשְׁקֵ֣נוּ מֵי־רֹ֔אשׁ כִּ֥י חָטָ֖אנוּ לַיהֹוָֽה׃


Why do we sit still? assemble yourselves, and let us enter into the fortified cities, and let us be cut off there: for the Lord our God has cut us off, and given us water of gall to drink, because we have sinned against the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 8:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:15)


קַוֵּ֥ה לְשָׁל֖וֹם וְאֵ֣ין ט֑וֹב לְעֵ֥ת מַרְפֵּ֖ה וְהִנֵּ֥ה בְעָתָֽה׃


We looked for peace, but no good came; and for a time of health, and behold terror!


###### Jeremiah 8:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:16)


מִדָּ֤ן נִשְׁמַע֙ נַחְרַ֣ת סוּסָ֔יו מִקּוֹל֙ מִצְהֲל֣וֹת אַבִּירָ֔יו רָעֲשָׁ֖ה כׇּל־הָאָ֑רֶץ וַיָּב֗וֹאוּ וַיֹּֽאכְלוּ֙ אֶ֣רֶץ וּמְלוֹאָ֔הּ עִ֖יר וְיֹ֥שְׁבֵי בָֽהּ׃{פ}


The snorting of his horses is heard from Dan: the whole land trembles at the sound of the neighing of his strong ones; for they are come, and have devoured the land, and all that is in it; the city, and those who dwell therein.


###### Jeremiah 8:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:17)


כִּי֩ הִנְנִ֨י מְשַׁלֵּ֜חַ בָּכֶ֗ם נְחָשִׁים֙ צִפְעֹנִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר אֵין־לָהֶ֖ם לָ֑חַשׁ וְנִשְּׁכ֥וּ אֶתְכֶ֖ם נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


For, behold, I will send venomous serpents among you, which will not be charmed, and they shall bite you, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 8:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:18)


מַבְלִ֥יגִיתִ֖י עֲלֵ֣י יָג֑וֹן עָלַ֖י לִבִּ֥י דַוָּֽי׃


When I would comfort myself against sorrow, my heart is faint in me.


###### Jeremiah 8:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:19)


הִנֵּה־ק֞וֹל שַֽׁוְעַ֣ת בַּת־עַמִּ֗י מֵאֶ֙רֶץ֙ מַרְחַקִּ֔ים הַֽיהֹוָה֙ אֵ֣ין בְּצִיּ֔וֹן אִם־מַלְכָּ֖הּ אֵ֣ין בָּ֑הּ מַדּ֗וּעַ הִכְעִס֛וּנִי בִּפְסִלֵיהֶ֖ם בְּהַבְלֵ֥י נֵכָֽר׃


Behold the voice of the cry of the daughter of my people coming from a far country: Is not the Lord in Żiyyon? is not her king in her? Why have they provoked me to anger with their carved idols, and with foreign vanities?


###### Jeremiah 8:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:20)


עָבַ֥ר קָצִ֖יר כָּ֣לָה קָ֑יִץ וַאֲנַ֖חְנוּ ל֥וֹא נוֹשָֽׁעְנוּ׃


The harvest is past, the summer is ended, and we are not saved.


###### Jeremiah 8:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:21)


עַל־שֶׁ֥בֶר בַּת־עַמִּ֖י הׇשְׁבָּ֑רְתִּי קָדַ֕רְתִּי שַׁמָּ֖ה הֶחֱזִקָֽתְנִי׃


For the hurt of the daughter of my people, I am broken; I am thrown into gloom: astonishment has taken hold of me.


###### Jeremiah 8:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:22)


הַצֳּרִי֙ אֵ֣ין בְּגִלְעָ֔ד אִם־רֹפֵ֖א אֵ֣ין שָׁ֑ם כִּ֗י מַדּ֙וּעַ֙ לֹ֣א עָֽלְתָ֔ה אֲרֻכַ֖ת בַּת־עַמִּֽי׃{ס}


Is there no balm in Gil῾ad; is there no physician there? why then is not the health of the daughter of my people recovered?


###### Jeremiah 8:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/8:23)


מִֽי־יִתֵּ֤ן רֹאשִׁי֙ מַ֔יִם וְעֵינִ֖י מְק֣וֹר דִּמְעָ֑ה וְאֶבְכֶּה֙ יוֹמָ֣ם וָלַ֔יְלָה אֵ֖ת חַֽלְלֵ֥י בַת־עַמִּֽי׃{ס}


Oh, that my head were waters, and my eyes a fountain of tears, that I might weep day and night for the slain of the daughter of my people!



## Chapter 9

###### Jeremiah 9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:1)


מִֽי־יִתְּנֵ֣נִי בַמִּדְבָּ֗ר מְלוֹן֙ אֹֽרְחִ֔ים וְאֶֽעֶזְבָה֙ אֶת־עַמִּ֔י וְאֵלְכָ֖ה מֵאִתָּ֑ם כִּ֤י כֻלָּם֙ מְנָ֣אֲפִ֔ים עֲצֶ֖רֶת בֹּגְדִֽים׃


Oh, that I were in the wilderness, in a lodging place of wayfaring men; that I might leave my people, and go from them! for they are all adulterers, an assembly of treacherous men.


###### Jeremiah 9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:2)


וַֽיַּדְרְכ֤וּ אֶת־לְשׁוֹנָם֙ קַשְׁתָּ֣ם שֶׁ֔קֶר וְלֹ֥א לֶאֱמוּנָ֖ה גָּבְר֣וּ בָאָ֑רֶץ כִּי֩ מֵרָעָ֨ה אֶל־רָעָ֧ה׀יָצָ֛אוּ וְאֹתִ֥י לֹא־יָדָ֖עוּ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


And they bend their tongues, their bow of falsehood: but they are not valiant for the truth upon the earth; for they proceed from evil to evil, and they know me not, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:3)


אִ֤ישׁ מֵרֵעֵ֙הוּ֙ הִשָּׁמֵ֔רוּ וְעַל־כׇּל־אָ֖ח אַל־תִּבְטָ֑חוּ כִּ֤י כׇל־אָח֙ עָק֣וֹב יַעְקֹ֔ב וְכׇל־רֵ֖עַ רָכִ֥יל יַהֲלֹֽךְ׃


Take heed everyone of his neighbour, and trust not in any brother: for every brother acts deceitfully, and every neighbour goes about with slanders.


###### Jeremiah 9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:4)


וְאִ֤ישׁ בְּרֵעֵ֙הוּ֙ יְהָתֵ֔לּוּ וֶאֱמֶ֖ת לֹ֣א יְדַבֵּ֑רוּ לִמְּד֧וּ לְשׁוֹנָ֛ם דַּבֶּר־שֶׁ֖קֶר הַעֲוֵ֥ה נִלְאֽוּ׃


And they deceive everyone his neighbour, and do not speak the truth: they have taught their tongue to speak lies, and weary themselves to commit iniquity.


###### Jeremiah 9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:5)


שִׁבְתְּךָ֖ בְּת֣וֹךְ מִרְמָ֑ה בְּמִרְמָ֛ה מֵאֲנ֥וּ דַעַת־אוֹתִ֖י נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


Thy habitation is in the midst of deceit; through deceit they refuse to know me, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:6)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֔וֹת הִנְנִ֥י צוֹרְפָ֖ם וּבְחַנְתִּ֑ים כִּי־אֵ֣יךְ אֶֽעֱשֶׂ֔ה מִפְּנֵ֖י בַּת־עַמִּֽי׃


Therefore thus says the Lord of hosts, Behold, I will smelt them, and try them; for how else shall I do for the daughter of my people?


###### Jeremiah 9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:7)


חֵ֥ץ (שוחט) [שָׁח֛וּט] לְשׁוֹנָ֖ם מִרְמָ֣ה דִבֵּ֑ר בְּפִ֗יו שָׁל֤וֹם אֶת־רֵעֵ֙הוּ֙ יְדַבֵּ֔ר וּבְקִרְבּ֖וֹ יָשִׂ֥ים אׇרְבּֽוֹ׃


Their tongue is a sharpened arrow; it speaks deceit: one speaks peaceably to his neighbour with his mouth, but in heart he lies in wait for him.


###### Jeremiah 9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:8)


הַעַל־אֵ֥לֶּה לֹא־אֶפְקׇד־בָּ֖ם נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה אִ֚ם בְּג֣וֹי אֲשֶׁר־כָּזֶ֔ה לֹ֥א תִתְנַקֵּ֖ם נַפְשִֽׁי׃{ס}


Shall I not punish them for these things? says the Lord: shall not my soul be avenged on such a nation as this?


###### Jeremiah 9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:9)


עַל־הֶ֨הָרִ֜ים אֶשָּׂ֧א בְכִ֣י וָנֶ֗הִי וְעַל־נְא֤וֹת מִדְבָּר֙ קִינָ֔ה כִּ֤י נִצְּתוּ֙ מִבְּלִי־אִ֣ישׁ עֹבֵ֔ר וְלֹ֥א שָׁמְע֖וּ ק֣וֹל מִקְנֶ֑ה מֵע֤וֹף הַשָּׁמַ֙יִם֙ וְעַד־בְּהֵמָ֔ה נָדְד֖וּ הָלָֽכוּ׃


For the mountains will I take up a weeping and wailing, and for the pastures of the wilderness a lamentation, because they are burned up, so that none can pass through them; neither can men hear the sound of the cattle; both the birds of the heavens and the beasts are fled; they are gone.


###### Jeremiah 9:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:10)


וְנָתַתִּ֧י אֶת־יְרוּשָׁלַ֛͏ִם לְגַלִּ֖ים מְע֣וֹן תַּנִּ֑ים וְאֶת־עָרֵ֧י יְהוּדָ֛ה אֶתֵּ֥ן שְׁמָמָ֖ה מִבְּלִ֥י יוֹשֵֽׁב׃{ס}


And I will make Yerushalayim heaps, a den of jackals; and I will make the cities of Yehuda desolate, without inhabitant.


###### Jeremiah 9:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:11)


מִֽי־הָאִ֤ישׁ הֶחָכָם֙ וְיָבֵ֣ן אֶת־זֹ֔את וַאֲשֶׁ֨ר דִּבֶּ֧ר פִּֽי־יְהֹוָ֛ה אֵלָ֖יו וְיַגִּדָ֑הּ עַל־מָה֙ אָבְדָ֣ה הָאָ֔רֶץ נִצְּתָ֥ה כַמִּדְבָּ֖ר מִבְּלִ֥י עֹבֵֽר׃{ס}


Who is the wise man, that may understand this? and who is he to whom the mouth of the Lord has spoken, that he may declare it? Why does the land perish, and is burned up like a wilderness, that none passes through?


###### Jeremiah 9:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:12)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר יְהֹוָ֔ה עַל־עׇזְבָם֙ אֶת־תּ֣וֹרָתִ֔י אֲשֶׁ֥ר נָתַ֖תִּי לִפְנֵיהֶ֑ם וְלֹא־שָׁמְע֥וּ בְקוֹלִ֖י וְלֹא־הָ֥לְכוּ בָֽהּ׃


And the Lord says, Because they have forsaken my Tora which I set before them, and have not obeyed my voice, nor walked therein;


###### Jeremiah 9:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:13)


וַיֵּ֣לְכ֔וּ אַחֲרֵ֖י שְׁרִר֣וּת לִבָּ֑ם וְאַֽחֲרֵי֙ הַבְּעָלִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר לִמְּד֖וּם אֲבוֹתָֽם׃{פ}


but have walked after the stubbornness of their own heart, and after the Be῾alim, as their fathers taught them:


###### Jeremiah 9:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:14)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּה־אָמַ֞ר יְהֹוָ֤ה צְבָאוֹת֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל הִנְנִ֧י מַאֲכִילָ֛ם אֶת־הָעָ֥ם הַזֶּ֖ה לַעֲנָ֑ה וְהִשְׁקִיתִ֖ים מֵי־רֹֽאשׁ׃


therefore thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Yisra᾽el; Behold, I will feed them, this people, with wormwood, and give them water of gall to drink.


###### Jeremiah 9:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:15)


וַהֲפִֽצוֹתִים֙ בַּגּוֹיִ֔ם אֲשֶׁר֙ לֹ֣א יָדְע֔וּ הֵ֖מָּה וַאֲבוֹתָ֑ם וְשִׁלַּחְתִּ֤י אַֽחֲרֵיהֶם֙ אֶת־הַחֶ֔רֶב עַ֥ד כַּלּוֹתִ֖י אוֹתָֽם׃{פ}


I will scatter them also among the nations, whom neither they nor their fathers have known: and I will send a sword after them, till I have consumed them.


###### Jeremiah 9:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:16)


כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֔וֹת הִתְבּ֥וֹנְנ֛וּ וְקִרְא֥וּ לַמְקוֹנְנ֖וֹת וּתְבוֹאֶ֑ינָה וְאֶל־הַחֲכָמ֥וֹת שִׁלְח֖וּ וְתָבֽוֹאנָה׃


Thus says the Lord of hosts, Consider, and call for the mourning women, that they may come; and send for the skilful women, that they may come:


###### Jeremiah 9:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:17)


וּתְמַהֵ֕רְנָה וְתִשֶּׂ֥נָה עָלֵ֖ינוּ נֶ֑הִי וְתֵרַ֤דְנָה עֵינֵ֙ינוּ֙ דִּמְעָ֔ה וְעַפְעַפֵּ֖ינוּ יִזְּלוּ־מָֽיִם׃


and let them make haste, and take up a wailing for us, that our eyes may run down with tears, and our eyelids gush out with waters.


###### Jeremiah 9:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:18)


כִּ֣י ק֥וֹל נְהִ֛י נִשְׁמַ֥ע מִצִּיּ֖וֹן אֵ֣יךְ שֻׁדָּ֑דְנוּ בֹּ֤שְׁנֽוּ מְאֹד֙ כִּֽי־עָזַ֣בְנוּ אָ֔רֶץ כִּ֥י הִשְׁלִ֖יכוּ מִשְׁכְּנוֹתֵֽינוּ׃{ס}


For a voice of wailing is heard out of Żiyyon, How are we ruined! we are greatly confounded, because we have forsaken the land, because our dwellings have cast us out.


###### Jeremiah 9:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:19)


כִּֽי־שְׁמַ֤עְנָה נָשִׁים֙ דְּבַר־יְהֹוָ֔ה וְתִקַּ֥ח אׇזְנְכֶ֖ם דְּבַר־פִּ֑יו וְלַמֵּ֤דְנָה בְנֽוֹתֵיכֶם֙ נֶ֔הִי וְאִשָּׁ֥ה רְעוּתָ֖הּ קִינָֽה׃


Yet hear the word of the Lord, O you women, and let your ear receive the word of his mouth, and teach your daughters wailing, and every one her neighbour lamentation.


###### Jeremiah 9:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:20)


כִּי־עָ֤לָֽה מָ֙וֶת֙ בְּחַלּוֹנֵ֔ינוּ בָּ֖א בְּאַרְמְנוֹתֵ֑ינוּ לְהַכְרִ֤ית עוֹלָל֙ מִח֔וּץ בַּחוּרִ֖ים מֵרְחֹבֽוֹת׃


For death is come up into our windows, and is entered into our palaces, to cut off the children from the streets and the young men from the broad places.


###### Jeremiah 9:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:21)


דַּבֵּ֗ר כֹּ֚ה נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה וְנָֽפְלָה֙ נִבְלַ֣ת הָאָדָ֔ם כְּדֹ֖מֶן עַל־פְּנֵ֣י הַשָּׂדֶ֑ה וּכְעָמִ֛יר מֵאַחֲרֵ֥י הַקֹּצֵ֖ר וְאֵ֥ין מְאַסֵּֽף׃{ס}


Speak, Thus says the Lord, And the carcasses of men shall fall as dung upon the open field, and as the handful after the harvester, and none shall gather them.


###### Jeremiah 9:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:22)


כֹּ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה אַל־יִתְהַלֵּ֤ל חָכָם֙ בְּחׇכְמָת֔וֹ וְאַל־יִתְהַלֵּ֥ל הַגִּבּ֖וֹר בִּגְבוּרָת֑וֹ אַל־יִתְהַלֵּ֥ל עָשִׁ֖יר בְּעׇשְׁרֽוֹ׃


Thus says the Lord, Let not the wise man glory in his wisdom, neither let the mighty man glory in his might, let not the rich man glory in his riches:


###### Jeremiah 9:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:23)


כִּ֣י אִם־בְּזֹ֞את יִתְהַלֵּ֣ל הַמִּתְהַלֵּ֗ל הַשְׂכֵּל֮ וְיָדֹ֣עַ אוֹתִי֒ כִּ֚י אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה עֹ֥שֶׂה חֶ֛סֶד מִשְׁפָּ֥ט וּצְדָקָ֖ה בָּאָ֑רֶץ כִּֽי־בְאֵ֥לֶּה חָפַ֖צְתִּי נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


but let him that glories glory in this, that he understands and knows me, that I am the Lord who exercise faithful love, justice, and righteousness, in the earth: for in these things I delight, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 9:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:24)


הִנֵּ֛ה יָמִ֥ים בָּאִ֖ים נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה וּפָ֣קַדְתִּ֔י עַל־כׇּל־מ֖וּל בְּעׇרְלָֽה׃


Behold, days are coming, says the Lord, when I will punish all the circumcised with the uncircumcised;


###### Jeremiah 9:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/9:25)


עַל־מִצְרַ֣יִם וְעַל־יְהוּדָ֗ה וְעַל־אֱד֞וֹם וְעַל־בְּנֵ֤י עַמּוֹן֙ וְעַל־מוֹאָ֔ב וְעַל֙ כׇּל־קְצוּצֵ֣י פֵאָ֔ה הַיֹּשְׁבִ֖ים בַּמִּדְבָּ֑ר כִּ֤י כׇל־הַגּוֹיִם֙ עֲרֵלִ֔ים וְכׇל־בֵּ֥ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל עַרְלֵי־לֵֽב׃{פ}


Miżrayim, and Yehuda, and Edom, and the children of ῾Ammon, and Mo᾽av, and all the desert dwellers that have the corners of their hair cut off; for all these nations are uncircumcised, and all the house of Yisra᾽el are uncircumcised in the heart.



## Chapter 10

###### Jeremiah 10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:1)


שִׁמְע֣וּ אֶת־הַדָּבָ֗ר אֲשֶׁ֨ר דִּבֶּ֧ר יְהֹוָ֛ה עֲלֵיכֶ֖ם בֵּ֥ית יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


Hear the word which the Lord speaks to you, O house of Yisra᾽el:


###### Jeremiah 10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:2)


כֹּ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה אֶל־דֶּ֤רֶךְ הַגּוֹיִם֙ אַל־תִּלְמָ֔דוּ וּמֵאֹת֥וֹת הַשָּׁמַ֖יִם אַל־תֵּחָ֑תּוּ כִּי־יֵחַ֥תּוּ הַגּוֹיִ֖ם מֵהֵֽמָּה׃


thus says the Lord, Learn not the way of the nations, and be not dismayed at the signs of heaven; for the nations are dismayed at them.


###### Jeremiah 10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:3)


כִּֽי־חֻקּ֥וֹת הָעַמִּ֖ים הֶ֣בֶל ה֑וּא כִּי־עֵץ֙ מִיַּ֣עַר כְּרָת֔וֹ מַעֲשֵׂ֥ה יְדֵי־חָרָ֖שׁ בַּֽמַּעֲצָֽד׃


For the customs of the people are vanity: a tree out of the forest is cut down, the work of the hands of the workman, with the ax.


###### Jeremiah 10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:4)


בְּכֶ֥סֶף וּבְזָהָ֖ב יְיַפֵּ֑הוּ בְּמַסְמְר֧וֹת וּבְמַקָּב֛וֹת יְחַזְּק֖וּם וְל֥וֹא יָפִֽיק׃


They deck it with silver and with gold; they fasten it with nails and with hammers, that it move not.


###### Jeremiah 10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:5)


כְּתֹ֨מֶר מִקְשָׁ֥ה הֵ֙מָּה֙ וְלֹ֣א יְדַבֵּ֔רוּ נָשׂ֥וֹא יִנָּשׂ֖וּא כִּ֣י לֹ֣א יִצְעָ֑דוּ אַל־תִּֽירְא֤וּ מֵהֶם֙ כִּי־לֹ֣א יָרֵ֔עוּ וְגַם־הֵיטֵ֖יב אֵ֥ין אוֹתָֽם׃{פ}


They are like a rigid post, and they cannot speak; they must needs be carried, because they cannot walk. Be not afraid of them; for they cannot do evil, neither is it in them to do good.


###### Jeremiah 10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:6)


מֵאֵ֥ין כָּמ֖וֹךָ יְהֹוָ֑ה גָּד֥וֹל אַתָּ֛ה וְגָד֥וֹל שִׁמְךָ֖ בִּגְבוּרָֽה׃


Forasmuch as there is none like Thee, O Lord; Thou art great, and Thy name is great in might.


###### Jeremiah 10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:7)


מִ֣י לֹ֤א יִרָֽאֲךָ֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ הַגּוֹיִ֔ם כִּ֥י לְךָ֖ יָאָ֑תָה כִּ֣י בְכׇל־חַכְמֵ֧י הַגּוֹיִ֛ם וּבְכׇל־מַלְכוּתָ֖ם מֵאֵ֥ין כָּמֽוֹךָ׃


Who would not fear Thee, O King of the nations? for to Thee it is fitting: for among all the wise men of the nations, and in all their kingdoms, there is none like Thee.


###### Jeremiah 10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:8)


וּבְאַחַ֖ת יִבְעֲר֣וּ וְיִכְסָ֑לוּ מוּסַ֥ר הֲבָלִ֖ים עֵ֥ץ הֽוּא׃


Stupid and senseless are they all: the teaching of their vain idols is but – wood!


###### Jeremiah 10:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:9)


כֶּ֣סֶף מְרֻקָּ֞ע מִתַּרְשִׁ֣ישׁ יוּבָ֗א וְזָהָב֙ מֵֽאוּפָ֔ז מַעֲשֵׂ֥ה חָרָ֖שׁ וִידֵ֣י צוֹרֵ֑ף תְּכֵ֤לֶת וְאַרְגָּמָן֙ לְבוּשָׁ֔ם מַעֲשֵׂ֥ה חֲכָמִ֖ים כֻּלָּֽם׃


Beaten silver is brought from Tarshish, and gold from Ufaz, the work of the crafts-man, and of the hands of the founder: blue and purple is their clothing: they are all the work of skilful men.


###### Jeremiah 10:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:10)


וַיהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהִים֙ אֱמֶ֔ת הֽוּא־אֱלֹהִ֥ים חַיִּ֖ים וּמֶ֣לֶךְ עוֹלָ֑ם מִקִּצְפּוֹ֙ תִּרְעַ֣שׁ הָאָ֔רֶץ וְלֹא־יָכִ֥לוּ גוֹיִ֖ם זַעְמֽוֹ׃{פ}


But the Lord is the true God, he is the living God, and an everlasting king: at his anger the earth shall tremble, and the nations shall not be able to abide his indignation.


###### Jeremiah 10:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:11)


כִּדְנָה֙ תֵּאמְר֣וּן לְה֔וֹם אֱלָ֣הַיָּ֔א דִּֽי־שְׁמַיָּ֥א וְאַרְקָ֖א לָ֣א עֲבַ֑דוּ יֵאבַ֧דוּ מֵאַרְעָ֛א וּמִן־תְּח֥וֹת שְׁמַיָּ֖א אֵֽלֶּה׃{ס}


Thus shall you say to them, The gods that have not made the heavens and the earth, they shall perish from the earth, and from under these heavens.


###### Jeremiah 10:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:12)


עֹשֵׂ֥ה אֶ֙רֶץ֙ בְּכֹח֔וֹ מֵכִ֥ין תֵּבֵ֖ל בְּחׇכְמָת֑וֹ וּבִתְבוּנָת֖וֹ נָטָ֥ה שָׁמָֽיִם׃


He has made the earth by his power, he has established the world by his wisdom, and has stretched out the heavens by his understanding.


###### Jeremiah 10:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:13)


לְק֨וֹל תִּתּ֜וֹ הֲמ֥וֹן מַ֙יִם֙ בַּשָּׁמַ֔יִם וַיַּעֲלֶ֥ה נְשִׂאִ֖ים מִקְצֵ֣ה (ארץ) [הָאָ֑רֶץ] בְּרָקִ֤ים לַמָּטָר֙ עָשָׂ֔ה וַיּ֥וֹצֵא ר֖וּחַ מֵאֹצְרֹתָֽיו׃


When his voice resounds with the great mass of water in the heavens, and he raises vapours from the ends of the earth; when he makes lightning flashes among the rain, and brings forth the wind out of his storehouses:
