[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:14)


נִבְעַ֤ר כׇּל־אָדָם֙ מִדַּ֔עַת הֹבִ֥ישׁ כׇּל־צוֹרֵ֖ף מִפָּ֑סֶל כִּ֛י שֶׁ֥קֶר נִסְכּ֖וֹ וְלֹא־ר֥וּחַ בָּֽם׃


every man becomes stupid, without knowledge: every founder is put to shame by his carved idol: for his molten image is false, and there is no breath in them.


###### Jeremiah 10:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:15)


הֶ֣בֶל הֵ֔מָּה מַעֲשֵׂ֖ה תַּעְתֻּעִ֑ים בְּעֵ֥ת פְּקֻדָּתָ֖ם יֹאבֵֽדוּ׃


They are vanity, the work of delusion: in the time of their punishment they shall perish.


###### Jeremiah 10:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:16)


לֹֽא־כְאֵ֜לֶּה חֵ֣לֶק יַעֲקֹ֗ב כִּֽי־יוֹצֵ֤ר הַכֹּל֙ ה֔וּא וְיִ֨שְׂרָאֵ֔ל שֵׁ֖בֶט נַחֲלָת֑וֹ יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת שְׁמֽוֹ׃{ס}


The portion of Ya῾aqov is not like them: for he is the former of all things; and Yisra᾽el is the tribe of his inheritance: The Lord of hosts is his name.


###### Jeremiah 10:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:17)


אִסְפִּ֥י מֵאֶ֖רֶץ כִּנְעָתֵ֑ךְ (ישבתי) [יֹשֶׁ֖בֶת] בַּמָּצֽוֹר׃{ס}


Gather up thy wares out of the land, O inhabitant of the besieged city.


###### Jeremiah 10:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:18)


כִּי־כֹה֙ אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה הִנְנִ֥י קוֹלֵ֛עַ אֶת־יוֹשְׁבֵ֥י הָאָ֖רֶץ בַּפַּ֣עַם הַזֹּ֑את וַהֲצֵרֹ֥תִי לָהֶ֖ם לְמַ֥עַן יִמְצָֽאוּ׃{ס}


For thus says the Lord, Behold, I will sling out the inhabitants of the land at this time, and will distress them, that they may feel it.


###### Jeremiah 10:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:19)


א֥וֹי לִי֙ עַל־שִׁבְרִ֔י נַחְלָ֖ה מַכָּתִ֑י וַאֲנִ֣י אָמַ֔רְתִּי אַ֛ךְ זֶ֥ה חֳלִ֖י וְאֶשָּׂאֶֽנּוּ׃


Woe is me for my hurt! my wound is grievous: but I said, Truly this is the affliction, and I must bear it.


###### Jeremiah 10:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:20)


אׇהֳלִ֣י שֻׁדָּ֔ד וְכׇל־מֵיתָרַ֖י נִתָּ֑קוּ בָּנַ֤י יְצָאֻ֙נִי֙ וְאֵינָ֔ם אֵין־נֹטֶ֥ה עוֹד֙ אׇֽהֳלִ֔י וּמֵקִ֖ים יְרִיעוֹתָֽי׃


My tent is spoiled, and all my cords are broken: my children are gone out of me, and they are not: there is none to stretch forth my tent any more, and to set up my curtains.


###### Jeremiah 10:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:21)


כִּ֤י נִבְעֲרוּ֙ הָרֹעִ֔ים וְאֶת־יְהֹוָ֖ה לֹ֣א דָרָ֑שׁוּ עַל־כֵּן֙ לֹ֣א הִשְׂכִּ֔ילוּ וְכׇל־מַרְעִיתָ֖ם נָפֽוֹצָה׃{פ}


For the shepherds are become brutish, and have not sought the Lord: therefore they have not prospered, and all their flocks are scattered.


###### Jeremiah 10:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:22)


ק֤וֹל שְׁמוּעָה֙ הִנֵּ֣ה בָאָ֔ה וְרַ֥עַשׁ גָּד֖וֹל מֵאֶ֣רֶץ צָפ֑וֹן לָשׂ֞וּם אֶת־עָרֵ֧י יְהוּדָ֛ה שְׁמָמָ֖ה מְע֥וֹן תַּנִּֽים׃{ס}


Behold, the noise of the report is come, and a great commotion out of the north country, to make the cities of Yehuda desolate, and a den of jackals.


###### Jeremiah 10:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:23)


יָדַ֣עְתִּי יְהֹוָ֔ה כִּ֛י לֹ֥א לָאָדָ֖ם דַּרְכּ֑וֹ לֹֽא־לְאִ֣ישׁ הֹלֵ֔ךְ וְהָכִ֖ין אֶֽת־צַעֲדֽוֹ׃


O Lord, I know that a man’s way is not his own; it is not in man to direct his steps as he walks.


###### Jeremiah 10:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:24)


יַסְּרֵ֥נִי יְהֹוָ֖ה אַךְ־בְּמִשְׁפָּ֑ט אַל־בְּאַפְּךָ֖ פֶּן־תַּמְעִטֵֽנִי׃


O Lord, correct me, but in due measure, not in Thy anger, lest Thou bring me to nothing.


###### Jeremiah 10:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/10:25)


שְׁפֹ֣ךְ חֲמָתְךָ֗ עַל־הַגּוֹיִם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹֽא־יְדָע֔וּךָ וְעַל֙ מִשְׁפָּח֔וֹת אֲשֶׁ֥ר בְּשִׁמְךָ֖ לֹ֣א קָרָ֑אוּ כִּֽי־אָכְל֣וּ אֶֽת־יַעֲקֹ֗ב וַאֲכָלֻ֙הוּ֙ וַיְכַלֻּ֔הוּ וְאֶת־נָוֵ֖הוּ הֵשַֽׁמּוּ׃{פ}


Pour out Thy fury upon the nations that know Thee not, and upon the families that call not on Thy name: for they have devoured Ya῾aqov, devoured him, and consumed him, and have made his habitation desolate.



## Chapter 11

###### Jeremiah 11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:1)


הַדָּבָר֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הָיָ֣ה אֶֽל־יִרְמְיָ֔הוּ מֵאֵ֥ת יְהֹוָ֖ה לֵאמֹֽר׃


The word that came to Yirmeyahu from the Lord, saying,


###### Jeremiah 11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:2)


שִׁמְע֕וּ אֶת־דִּבְרֵ֖י הַבְּרִ֣ית הַזֹּ֑את וְדִבַּרְתֶּם֙ אֶל־אִ֣ישׁ יְהוּדָ֔ה וְעַל־יֹשְׁבֵ֖י יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


Hear the words of this covenant, and speak to the men of Yehuda, and to the inhabitants of Yerushalayim;


###### Jeremiah 11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:3)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֲלֵיהֶ֔ם כֹּֽה־אָמַ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל אָר֣וּר הָאִ֔ישׁ אֲשֶׁר֙ לֹ֣א יִשְׁמַ֔ע אֶת־דִּבְרֵ֖י הַבְּרִ֥ית הַזֹּֽאת׃


and say to them, Thus says the Lord God of Yisra᾽el; Cursed be the man that obeys not the words of this covenant,


###### Jeremiah 11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:4)


אֲשֶׁ֣ר צִוִּ֣יתִי אֶת־אֲבֽוֹתֵיכֶ֡ם בְּי֣וֹם הוֹצִיאִֽי־אוֹתָ֣ם מֵאֶֽרֶץ־מִצְרַ֩יִם֩ מִכּ֨וּר הַבַּרְזֶ֜ל לֵאמֹ֗ר שִׁמְע֤וּ בְקוֹלִי֙ וַעֲשִׂיתֶ֣ם אוֹתָ֔ם כְּכֹ֥ל אֲשֶׁר־אֲצַוֶּ֖ה אֶתְכֶ֑ם וִהְיִ֤יתֶם לִי֙ לְעָ֔ם וְאָ֣נֹכִ֔י אֶהְיֶ֥ה לָכֶ֖ם לֵאלֹהִֽים׃


which I commanded your fathers on the day that I brought them out of the land of Miżrayim, from the iron furnace, saying, Obey my voice, and do them, according to all which I command you: so shall you be my people, and I will be your God:


###### Jeremiah 11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:5)


לְמַ֩עַן֩ הָקִ֨ים אֶת־הַשְּׁבוּעָ֜ה אֲשֶׁר־נִשְׁבַּ֣עְתִּי לַאֲבֽוֹתֵיכֶ֗ם לָתֵ֤ת לָהֶם֙ אֶ֣רֶץ זָבַ֥ת חָלָ֛ב וּדְבַ֖שׁ כַּיּ֣וֹם הַזֶּ֑ה וָאַ֥עַן וָאֹמַ֖ר אָמֵ֥ן׀יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


that I may perform the oath which I have sworn to your fathers, to give them a land flowing with milk and honey, as it is this day. Then answered I, and said, Amen, O Lord.


###### Jeremiah 11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:6)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר יְהֹוָה֙ אֵלַ֔י קְרָ֨א אֶת־כׇּל־הַדְּבָרִ֤ים הָאֵ֙לֶּה֙ בְּעָרֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֔ה וּבְחֻצ֥וֹת יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם לֵאמֹ֑ר שִׁמְע֗וּ אֶת־דִּבְרֵי֙ הַבְּרִ֣ית הַזֹּ֔את וַעֲשִׂיתֶ֖ם אוֹתָֽם׃


Then the Lord said to me, Proclaim all these words in the cities of Yehuda, and in the streets of Yerushalayim, saying, Hear the words of this covenant, and do them.


###### Jeremiah 11:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:7)


כִּי֩ הָעֵ֨ד הַעִדֹ֜תִי בַּאֲבֽוֹתֵיכֶ֗ם בְּיוֹם֩ הַעֲלוֹתִ֨י אוֹתָ֜ם מֵאֶ֤רֶץ מִצְרַ֙יִם֙ וְעַד־הַיּ֣וֹם הַזֶּ֔ה הַשְׁכֵּ֥ם וְהָעֵ֖ד לֵאמֹ֑ר שִׁמְע֖וּ בְּקוֹלִֽי׃


For I earnestly forewarned your fathers on the day that I brought them up out of the land of Miżrayim, to this day, forewarning them from morning till night, saying, Obey my voice.


###### Jeremiah 11:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:8)


וְלֹ֤א שָֽׁמְעוּ֙ וְלֹא־הִטּ֣וּ אֶת־אׇזְנָ֔ם וַיֵּ֣לְכ֔וּ אִ֕ישׁ בִּשְׁרִיר֖וּת לִבָּ֣ם הָרָ֑ע וָאָבִ֨יא עֲלֵיהֶ֜ם אֶֽת־כׇּל־דִּבְרֵ֧י הַבְּרִית־הַזֹּ֛את אֲשֶׁר־צִוִּ֥יתִי לַעֲשׂ֖וֹת וְלֹ֥א עָשֽׂוּ׃{ס}


Yet they obeyed not, nor inclined their ear, but walked every one in the stubbornness of their evil heart: therefore I brought upon them all the words of this covenant, which I commanded them to do; but they did them not.


###### Jeremiah 11:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:9)


וַיֹּ֥אמֶר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלָ֑י נִֽמְצָא־קֶ֙שֶׁר֙ בְּאִ֣ישׁ יְהוּדָ֔ה וּבְיֹשְׁבֵ֖י יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


And the Lord said to me, A conspiracy is found among the men of Yehuda, and among the inhabitants of Yerushalayim.


###### Jeremiah 11:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:10)


שָׁ֩בוּ֩ עַל־עֲוֺנֹ֨ת אֲבוֹתָ֜ם הָרִאשֹׁנִ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֤ר מֵֽאֲנוּ֙ לִשְׁמ֣וֹעַ אֶת־דְּבָרַ֔י וְהֵ֣מָּה הָלְכ֗וּ אַחֲרֵ֛י אֱלֹהִ֥ים אֲחֵרִ֖ים לְעׇבְדָ֑ם הֵפֵ֤רוּ בֵֽית־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ וּבֵ֣ית יְהוּדָ֔ה אֶת־בְּרִיתִ֕י אֲשֶׁ֥ר כָּרַ֖תִּי אֶת־אֲבוֹתָֽם׃{ס}


They have turned back to the iniquities of their forefathers, who refused to hear my words; and they have gone after other gods to serve them: the house of Yisra᾽el and the house of Yehuda have broken my covenant which I made with their fathers.


###### Jeremiah 11:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:11)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה הִנְנִ֨י מֵבִ֤יא אֲלֵיהֶם֙ רָעָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־יוּכְל֖וּ לָצֵ֣את מִמֶּ֑נָּה וְזָעֲק֣וּ אֵלַ֔י וְלֹ֥א אֶשְׁמַ֖ע אֲלֵיהֶֽם׃


Therefore thus says the Lord, Behold, I will bring evil upon them, which they shall not be able to escape; and though they shall cry to me, I will not hearken to them.


###### Jeremiah 11:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:12)


וְֽהָלְכ֞וּ עָרֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֗ה וְיֹֽשְׁבֵי֙ יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם וְזָֽעֲקוּ֙ אֶל־הָ֣אֱלֹהִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֛ר הֵ֥ם מְקַטְּרִ֖ים לָהֶ֑ם וְהוֹשֵׁ֛עַ לֹא־יוֹשִׁ֥יעוּ לָהֶ֖ם בְּעֵ֥ת רָעָתָֽם׃


Then shall the cities of Yehuda and inhabitants of Yerushalayim go, and cry to the gods to whom they offer incense: but they shall not save them at all in the time of their trouble.


###### Jeremiah 11:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:13)


כִּ֚י מִסְפַּ֣ר עָרֶ֔יךָ הָי֥וּ אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ יְהוּדָ֑ה וּמִסְפַּ֞ר חֻצ֣וֹת יְרוּשָׁלַ֗͏ִם שַׂמְתֶּ֤ם מִזְבְּחוֹת֙ לַבֹּ֔שֶׁת מִזְבְּח֖וֹת לְקַטֵּ֥ר לַבָּֽעַל׃{ס}


For according to the number of thy cities are thy gods, O Yehuda; and according to the number of the streets of Yerushalayim have you set up altars to the shameful thing, altars to burn incense to the Ba῾al.


###### Jeremiah 11:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:14)


וְאַתָּ֗ה אַל־תִּתְפַּלֵּל֙ בְּעַד־הָעָ֣ם הַזֶּ֔ה וְאַל־תִּשָּׂ֥א בַעֲדָ֖ם רִנָּ֣ה וּתְפִלָּ֑ה כִּ֣י**׀** אֵינֶ֣נִּי שֹׁמֵ֗עַ בְּעֵ֛ת קׇרְאָ֥ם אֵלַ֖י בְּעַ֥ד רָעָתָֽם׃{ס}


Therefore pray not thou for this people, nor lift up a cry or prayer for them: for I will not hear them at the time that they cry to me for their trouble.


###### Jeremiah 11:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:15)


מֶ֣ה לִידִידִ֞י בְּבֵיתִ֗י עֲשׂוֹתָ֤הּ הַֽמְזִמָּ֙תָה֙ הָרַבִּ֔ים וּבְשַׂר־קֹ֖דֶשׁ יַעַבְר֣וּ מֵעָלָ֑יִךְ כִּ֥י רָעָתֵ֖כִי אָ֥ז תַּעֲלֹֽזִי׃


What has my beloved to do in my house, seeing she has acted vilely with many, and the holy flesh is passed from thee? when thou doest evil, then thou dost rejoice.


###### Jeremiah 11:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:16)


זַ֤יִת רַֽעֲנָן֙ יְפֵ֣ה פְרִי־תֹ֔אַר קָרָ֥א יְהֹוָ֖ה שְׁמֵ֑ךְ לְק֣וֹל**׀** הֲמוּלָּ֣ה גְדֹלָ֗ה הִצִּ֥ית אֵשׁ֙ עָלֶ֔יהָ וְרָע֖וּ דָּלִיּוֹתָֽיו׃


The Lord called thy name, A green olive tree, fair with goodly fruit: with the noise of a great tumult he has kindled fire upon it, and the branches of it are broken.


###### Jeremiah 11:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:17)


וַיהֹוָ֤ה צְבָאוֹת֙ הַנּוֹטֵ֣עַ אוֹתָ֔ךְ דִּבֶּ֥ר עָלַ֖יִךְ רָעָ֑ה בִּ֠גְלַ֠ל רָעַ֨ת בֵּֽית־יִשְׂרָאֵ֜ל וּבֵ֣ית יְהוּדָ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר עָשׂ֥וּ לָהֶ֛ם לְהַכְעִסֵ֖נִי לְקַטֵּ֥ר לַבָּֽעַל׃{פ}


For the Lord of hosts, that planted thee, has pronounced evil against thee, because of the evil which the house of Yisra᾽el and the house of Yehuda, have done to provoke me to anger in offering incense to the Ba῾al.


###### Jeremiah 11:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:18)


וַיהֹוָ֥ה הוֹדִיעַ֖נִי וָאֵדָ֑עָה אָ֖ז הִרְאִיתַ֥נִי מַעַלְלֵיהֶֽם׃


And the Lord has given me knowledge of it, and I know it: then Thou didst show me their doings.


###### Jeremiah 11:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:19)


וַאֲנִ֕י כְּכֶ֥בֶשׂ אַלּ֖וּף יוּבַ֣ל לִטְב֑וֹחַ וְלֹֽא־יָדַ֜עְתִּי כִּֽי־עָלַ֣י**׀** חָשְׁב֣וּ מַחֲשָׁב֗וֹת נַשְׁחִ֨יתָה עֵ֤ץ בְּלַחְמוֹ֙ וְנִכְרְתֶ֙נּוּ֙ מֵאֶ֣רֶץ חַיִּ֔ים וּשְׁמ֖וֹ לֹא־יִזָּכֵ֥ר עֽוֹד׃


But I was like a gentle lamb brought to the slaughter; and I knew not that they had devised devices against me, saying, Let us destroy the tree with its fruit, and let us cut him off from the land of the living, that his name may be no more remembered.


###### Jeremiah 11:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:20)


וַיהֹוָ֤ה צְבָאוֹת֙ שֹׁפֵ֣ט צֶ֔דֶק בֹּחֵ֥ן כְּלָי֖וֹת וָלֵ֑ב אֶרְאֶ֤ה נִקְמָֽתְךָ֙ מֵהֶ֔ם כִּ֥י אֵלֶ֖יךָ גִּלִּ֥יתִי אֶת־רִיבִֽי׃{ס}


But, O Lord of hosts, that dost judge righteously, that dost try the reins and the heart, let me see Thy vengeance on them: for to Thee have I revealed my cause.


###### Jeremiah 11:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:21)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּֽה־אָמַ֤ר יְהֹוָה֙ עַל־אַנְשֵׁ֣י עֲנָת֔וֹת הַֽמְבַקְשִׁ֥ים אֶֽת־נַפְשְׁךָ֖ לֵאמֹ֑ר לֹ֤א תִנָּבֵא֙ בְּשֵׁ֣ם יְהֹוָ֔ה וְלֹ֥א תָמ֖וּת בְּיָדֵֽנוּ׃{פ}


Therefore thus says the Lord concerning the men of ῾Anatot, that seek thy life, saying, Prophesy not in the name of the Lord, that thou die not by our hand:


###### Jeremiah 11:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:22)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֔וֹת הִנְנִ֥י פֹקֵ֖ד עֲלֵיהֶ֑ם הַבַּחוּרִים֙ יָמֻ֣תוּ בַחֶ֔רֶב בְּנֵיהֶם֙ וּבְנ֣וֹתֵיהֶ֔ם יָמֻ֖תוּ בָּרָעָֽב׃


therefore thus says the Lord of hosts, Behold, I will punish them: the young men shall die by the sword; their sons and their daughters shall die by famine:


###### Jeremiah 11:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/11:23)


וּשְׁאֵרִ֕ית לֹ֥א תִהְיֶ֖ה לָהֶ֑ם כִּי־אָבִ֥יא רָעָ֛ה אֶל־אַנְשֵׁ֥י עֲנָת֖וֹת שְׁנַ֥ת פְּקֻדָּתָֽם׃{ס}


and there shall be no remnant of them: for I will bring evil upon the men of ῾Anatot, the year of their punishment.



## Chapter 12

###### Jeremiah 12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:1)


צַדִּ֤יק אַתָּה֙ יְהֹוָ֔ה כִּ֥י אָרִ֖יב אֵלֶ֑יךָ אַ֤ךְ מִשְׁפָּטִים֙ אֲדַבֵּ֣ר אֹתָ֔ךְ מַדּ֗וּעַ דֶּ֤רֶךְ רְשָׁעִים֙ צָלֵ֔חָה שָׁל֖וּ כׇּל־בֹּ֥גְדֵי בָֽגֶד׃


Right wouldst Thou be, O Lord, if I were to contend with Thee: yet I will reason these points of justice with Thee: Why does the way of the wicked prosper? why are all they happy that deal very treacherously?


###### Jeremiah 12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:2)


נְטַעְתָּם֙ גַּם־שֹׁרָ֔שׁוּ יֵלְכ֖וּ גַּם־עָ֣שׂוּ פֶ֑רִי קָר֤וֹב אַתָּה֙ בְּפִיהֶ֔ם וְרָח֖וֹק מִכִּלְיוֹתֵיהֶֽם׃


Thou hast planted them, indeed, they have taken root: they grow, indeed they bring forth fruit: Thou art near in their mouth, and far from their inward parts.


###### Jeremiah 12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:3)


וְאַתָּ֤ה יְהֹוָה֙ יְדַעְתָּ֔נִי תִּרְאֵ֕נִי וּבָחַנְתָּ֥ לִבִּ֖י אִתָּ֑ךְ הַתִּקֵם֙ כְּצֹ֣אן לְטִבְחָ֔ה וְהַקְדִּשֵׁ֖ם לְי֥וֹם הֲרֵגָֽה׃{פ}


But Thou, O Lord, knowst me: Thou hast seen me, and tried my heart towards Thee: pull them out like sheep for the slaughter, and prepare them for the day of slaughter.


###### Jeremiah 12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:4)


עַד־מָתַי֙ תֶּאֱבַ֣ל הָאָ֔רֶץ וְעֵ֥שֶׂב כׇּל־הַשָּׂדֶ֖ה יִיבָ֑שׁ מֵרָעַ֣ת יֹֽשְׁבֵי־בָ֗הּ סָפְתָ֤ה בְהֵמוֹת֙ וָע֔וֹף כִּ֣י אָֽמְר֔וּ לֹ֥א יִרְאֶ֖ה אֶת־אַחֲרִיתֵֽנוּ׃


How long shall the land mourn, and the herbs of the whole field wither? for the wickedness of them that dwell there, the beasts and the birds are consumed; because they said, He shall not see our last end.


###### Jeremiah 12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:5)


כִּ֣י אֶת־רַגְלִ֥ים׀רַ֙צְתָּה֙ וַיַּלְא֔וּךָ וְאֵ֥יךְ תְּתַחֲרֶ֖ה אֶת־הַסּוּסִ֑ים וּבְאֶ֤רֶץ שָׁלוֹם֙ אַתָּ֣ה בוֹטֵ֔חַ וְאֵ֥יךְ תַּעֲשֶׂ֖ה בִּגְא֥וֹן הַיַּרְדֵּֽן׃


If thou hast run with the footmen, and they have wearied thee, then how canst thou contend with horses? And dost thou feel secure in the land of peace? how wilt thou do in the wild country of the Yarden?


###### Jeremiah 12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:6)


כִּ֧י גַם־אַחֶ֣יךָ וּבֵית־אָבִ֗יךָ גַּם־הֵ֙מָּה֙ בָּ֣גְדוּ בָ֔ךְ גַּם־הֵ֛מָּה קָרְא֥וּ אַחֲרֶ֖יךָ מָלֵ֑א אַל־תַּאֲמֵ֣ן בָּ֔ם כִּֽי־יְדַבְּר֥וּ אֵלֶ֖יךָ טוֹבֽוֹת׃{ס}


For even thy brethren, and the house of thy father, even they deal treacherously with thee: indeed, they are in full cry after thee: believe them not, though they speak fair words to thee.


###### Jeremiah 12:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:7)


עָזַ֙בְתִּי֙ אֶת־בֵּיתִ֔י נָטַ֖שְׁתִּי אֶת־נַחֲלָתִ֑י נָתַ֛תִּי אֶת־יְדִד֥וּת נַפְשִׁ֖י בְּכַ֥ף אֹיְבֶֽיהָ׃


I have forsaken my house, I have left my heritage; I have given the dearly beloved of my soul into the hand of her enemies.


###### Jeremiah 12:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:8)


הָיְתָה־לִּ֥י נַחֲלָתִ֖י כְּאַרְיֵ֣ה בַיָּ֑עַר נָתְנָ֥ה עָלַ֛י בְּקוֹלָ֖הּ עַל־כֵּ֥ן שְׂנֵאתִֽיהָ׃


My heritage has become to me as a lion in the forest; it cries out against me: therefore have I hated it.


###### Jeremiah 12:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:9)


הַעַ֨יִט צָב֤וּעַ נַֽחֲלָתִי֙ לִ֔י הַעַ֖יִט סָבִ֣יב עָלֶ֑יהָ לְכ֗וּ אִסְפ֛וּ כׇּל־חַיַּ֥ת הַשָּׂדֶ֖ה הֵתָ֥יוּ לְאׇכְלָֽה׃


My inheritance is a long clawed eagle to me. But the eagle is round about her! Come, assemble together all the wild beasts of the field, bring them to devour.


###### Jeremiah 12:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:10)


רֹעִ֤ים רַבִּים֙ שִׁחֲת֣וּ כַרְמִ֔י בֹּסְס֖וּ אֶת־חֶלְקָתִ֑י נָ֥תְנ֛וּ אֶת־חֶלְקַ֥ת חֶמְדָּתִ֖י לְמִדְבַּ֥ר שְׁמָמָֽה׃


Many shepherds have destroyed my vineyard, they have trodden my portion under foot, they have made my pleasant portion a desolate wilderness.


###### Jeremiah 12:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:11)


שָׂמָהּ֙ לִשְׁמָמָ֔ה אָבְלָ֥ה עָלַ֖י שְׁמֵמָ֑ה נָשַׁ֙מָּה֙ כׇּל־הָאָ֔רֶץ כִּ֛י אֵ֥ין אִ֖ישׁ שָׂ֥ם עַל־לֵֽב׃


They have made it desolate, and being desolate it mourns to me; the whole land is made desolate, because no man lays it to heart.


###### Jeremiah 12:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:12)


עַֽל־כׇּל־שְׁפָיִ֣ם בַּמִּדְבָּ֗ר בָּ֚אוּ שֹֽׁדְדִ֔ים כִּ֣י חֶ֤רֶב לַֽיהֹוָה֙ אֹֽכְלָ֔ה מִקְצֵה־אֶ֖רֶץ וְעַד־קְצֵ֣ה הָאָ֑רֶץ אֵ֥ין שָׁל֖וֹם לְכׇל־בָּשָֽׂר׃{ס}


The spoilers are come upon all high hills through the wilderness: for the sword of the Lord devours from the one end of the land to the other end of the land: no flesh has peace.


###### Jeremiah 12:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:13)


זָרְע֤וּ חִטִּים֙ וְקֹצִ֣ים קָצָ֔רוּ נֶחְל֖וּ לֹ֣א יוֹעִ֑לוּ וּבֹ֙שׁוּ֙ מִתְּבוּאֹ֣תֵיכֶ֔ם מֵחֲר֖וֹן אַף־יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


They have sown wheat, but have reaped thorns: they have put themselves to pain, but shall not profit: be then ashamed of your harvests because of the fierce anger of the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 12:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:14)


כֹּ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה עַל־כׇּל־שְׁכֵנַי֙ הָֽרָעִ֔ים הַנֹּֽגְעִים֙ בַּֽנַּחֲלָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־הִנְחַ֥לְתִּי אֶת־עַמִּ֖י אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל הִנְנִ֤י נֹֽתְשָׁם֙ מֵעַ֣ל אַדְמָתָ֔ם וְאֶת־בֵּ֥ית יְהוּדָ֖ה אֶתּ֥וֹשׁ מִתּוֹכָֽם׃


Thus says the Lord against all my evil neighbours, that touch the inheritance which I have caused my people Yisra᾽el to inherit; Behold, I will pluck them out of their land, and pluck out the house of Yehuda from among them.


###### Jeremiah 12:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:15)


וְהָיָ֗ה אַֽחֲרֵי֙ נׇתְשִׁ֣י אוֹתָ֔ם אָשׁ֖וּב וְרִחַמְתִּ֑ים וַהֲשִׁבֹתִ֛ים אִ֥ישׁ לְנַחֲלָת֖וֹ וְאִ֥ישׁ לְאַרְצֽוֹ׃


And it shall come to pass, after I have plucked them out I will return, and have compassion on them, and will bring them back, every man to his heritage, and every man to his land.


###### Jeremiah 12:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:16)


וְהָיָ֡ה אִם־לָמֹ֣ד יִלְמְדוּ֩ אֶת־דַּֽרְכֵ֨י עַמִּ֜י לְהִשָּׁבֵ֤עַ בִּשְׁמִי֙ חַי־יְהֹוָ֔ה כַּאֲשֶׁ֤ר לִמְּדוּ֙ אֶת־עַמִּ֔י לְהִשָּׁבֵ֖עַ בַּבָּ֑עַל וְנִבְנ֖וּ בְּת֥וֹךְ עַמִּֽי׃


And it shall come to pass, if they will diligently learn the ways of my people, to swear by my name, As the Lord lives; just as they taught my people to swear by the Ba῾al; then shall they be built in the midst of my people.


###### Jeremiah 12:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/12:17)


וְאִ֖ם לֹ֣א יִשְׁמָ֑עוּ וְנָ֨תַשְׁתִּ֜י אֶת־הַגּ֥וֹי הַה֛וּא נָת֥וֹשׁ וְאַבֵּ֖ד נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


But if they will not obey, I will utterly pluck up and destroy that nation, says the Lord.



## Chapter 13

###### Jeremiah 13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:1)


כֹּה־אָמַ֨ר יְהֹוָ֜ה אֵלַ֗י הָל֞וֹךְ וְקָנִ֤יתָ לְּךָ֙ אֵז֣וֹר פִּשְׁתִּ֔ים וְשַׂמְתּ֖וֹ עַל־מׇתְנֶ֑יךָ וּבַמַּ֖יִם לֹ֥א תְבִאֵֽהוּ׃


Thus says the Lord to me, Go and get thee a linen girdle, and put it upon thy loins, and put it not in water.


###### Jeremiah 13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:2)


וָאֶקְנֶ֥ה אֶת־הָאֵז֖וֹר כִּדְבַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֑ה וָאָשִׂ֖ם עַל־מׇתְנָֽי׃{פ}


So I got a girdle according to the word of the Lord, and put in on my loins.


###### Jeremiah 13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:3)


וַיְהִ֧י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֛ה אֵלַ֖י שֵׁנִ֥ית לֵאמֹֽר׃


And the word of the Lord came to me the second time, saying,


###### Jeremiah 13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:4)


קַ֧ח אֶת־הָאֵז֛וֹר אֲשֶׁ֥ר קָנִ֖יתָ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עַל־מׇתְנֶ֑יךָ וְקוּם֙ לֵ֣ךְ פְּרָ֔תָה וְטׇמְנֵ֥הוּ שָׁ֖ם בִּנְקִ֥יק הַסָּֽלַע׃


Take the girdle that thou hast got, which is upon thy loins, and arise, go to Perat, and hide it there in a hole of the rock.


###### Jeremiah 13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:5)


וָאֵלֵ֕ךְ וָאֶטְמְנֵ֖הוּ בִּפְרָ֑ת כַּאֲשֶׁ֛ר צִוָּ֥ה יְהֹוָ֖ה אוֹתִֽי׃


So I went, and hid it in Perat, as the Lord commanded me.


###### Jeremiah 13:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:6)


וַיְהִ֕י מִקֵּ֖ץ יָמִ֣ים רַבִּ֑ים וַיֹּ֨אמֶר יְהֹוָ֜ה אֵלַ֗י ק֚וּם לֵ֣ךְ פְּרָ֔תָה וְקַ֤ח מִשָּׁם֙ אֶת־הָ֣אֵז֔וֹר אֲשֶׁ֥ר צִוִּיתִ֖יךָ לְטׇמְנוֹ־שָֽׁם׃


And it came to pass after many days, that the Lord said to me, Arise, go to Perat, and take from there the girdle, which I commanded thee to hide there.


###### Jeremiah 13:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:7)


וָאֵלֵ֣ךְ פְּרָ֔תָה וָאֶחְפֹּ֗ר וָֽאֶקַּח֙ אֶת־הָ֣אֵז֔וֹר מִן־הַמָּק֖וֹם אֲשֶׁר־טְמַנְתִּ֣יו שָׁ֑מָּה וְהִנֵּה֙ נִשְׁחַ֣ת הָאֵז֔וֹר לֹ֥א יִצְלַ֖ח לַכֹּֽל׃{פ}


Then I went to Perat, and dug, and took the girdle from the place where I had hid it: and, behold, the girdle was spoilt, it was profitable for nothing.


###### Jeremiah 13:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:8)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


Then the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Jeremiah 13:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:9)


כֹּ֖ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֑ה כָּ֠כָה אַשְׁחִ֞ית אֶת־גְּא֧וֹן יְהוּדָ֛ה וְאֶת־גְּא֥וֹן יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם הָרָֽב׃


Thus says the Lord, After this manner will I spoil the pride of Yehuda, and the great pride of Yerushalayim.


###### Jeremiah 13:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:10)


הָעָם֩ הַזֶּ֨ה הָרָ֜ע הַֽמֵּאֲנִ֣ים**׀** לִשְׁמ֣וֹעַ אֶת־דְּבָרַ֗י הַהֹֽלְכִים֙ בִּשְׁרִר֣וּת לִבָּ֔ם וַיֵּלְכ֗וּ אַֽחֲרֵי֙ אֱלֹהִ֣ים אֲחֵרִ֔ים לְעׇבְדָ֖ם וּלְהִשְׁתַּחֲוֺ֣ת לָהֶ֑ם וִיהִי֙ כָּאֵז֣וֹר הַזֶּ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹֽא־יִצְלַ֖ח לַכֹּֽל׃{ס}


This evil people, which refuse to hear my words, which walk in the stubbornness of their heart, and go after other gods, to serve them, and to worship them, shall even be as this girdle, which is good for nothing.


###### Jeremiah 13:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:11)


כִּ֡י כַּאֲשֶׁר֩ יִדְבַּ֨ק הָאֵז֜וֹר אֶל־מׇתְנֵי־אִ֗ישׁ כֵּ֣ן הִדְבַּ֣קְתִּי אֵ֠לַ֠י אֶת־כׇּל־בֵּ֨ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֜ל וְאֶת־כׇּל־בֵּ֤ית יְהוּדָה֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה לִֽהְי֥וֹת לִי֙ לְעָ֔ם וּלְשֵׁ֥ם וְלִתְהִלָּ֖ה וּלְתִפְאָ֑רֶת וְלֹ֖א שָׁמֵֽעוּ׃


For as the girdle cleaves to the loins of a man, so have I caused the whole house of Yisra᾽el and the whole house of Yehuda, to cleave to me, says the Lord; that they might be to me for a people, and for a name, and for a praise, and for a glory: but they would not hear.


###### Jeremiah 13:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:12)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֨ אֲלֵיהֶ֜ם אֶת־הַדָּבָ֣ר הַזֶּ֗ה{ס}כֹּֽה־אָמַ֤ר יְהֹוָה֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל כׇּל־נֵ֖בֶל יִמָּ֣לֵא יָ֑יִן וְאָמְר֣וּ אֵלֶ֔יךָ הֲיָדֹ֙עַ֙ לֹ֣א נֵדַ֔ע כִּ֥י כׇל־נֵ֖בֶל יִמָּ֥לֵא יָֽיִן׃


Therefore thou shalt speak to them this word; Thus says the Lord God of Yisra᾽el, Every bottle shall be filled with wine; and when they shall say to thee, Do we not certainly know that every bottle shall be filled with wine?


###### Jeremiah 13:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:13)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֨ אֲלֵיהֶ֜ם כֹּֽה־אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה הִנְנִ֣י מְמַלֵּ֣א אֶת־כׇּל־יֹשְׁבֵ֣י הָאָ֪רֶץ הַזֹּ֟את וְאֶת־הַמְּלָכִ֣ים הַיֹּשְׁבִים֩ לְדָוִ֨ד עַל־כִּסְא֜וֹ וְאֶת־הַכֹּהֲנִ֣ים וְאֶת־הַנְּבִאִ֗ים וְאֵ֛ת כׇּל־יֹשְׁבֵ֥י יְרוּשָׁלָ֖͏ִם שִׁכָּרֽוֹן׃


then shalt thou say to them, Thus says the Lord, Behold, I will fill all the inhabitants of this land, even the kings of the house of David that sit upon his throne, and the priests, and the prophets, and all the inhabitants of Yerushalayim, with drunkenness.


###### Jeremiah 13:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:14)


וְנִפַּצְתִּים֩ אִ֨ישׁ אֶל־אָחִ֜יו וְהָאָב֧וֹת וְהַבָּנִ֛ים יַחְדָּ֖ו נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה לֹֽא־אֶחְמ֧וֹל וְלֹא־אָח֛וּס וְלֹ֥א אֲרַחֵ֖ם מֵהַשְׁחִיתָֽם׃


And I will dash them one against another, even the fathers and the sons together, says the Lord: I will not pity, nor spare, nor have mercy, but destroy them.


###### Jeremiah 13:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:15)


שִׁמְע֥וּ וְהַאֲזִ֖ינוּ אַל־תִּגְבָּ֑הוּ כִּ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה דִּבֵּֽר׃


Hear, and give ear; be not proud: for the Lord has spoken.


###### Jeremiah 13:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:16)


תְּנוּ֩ לַיהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֤ם כָּבוֹד֙ בְּטֶ֣רֶם יַחְשִׁ֔ךְ וּבְטֶ֛רֶם יִֽתְנַגְּפ֥וּ רַגְלֵיכֶ֖ם עַל־הָ֣רֵי נָ֑שֶׁף וְקִוִּיתֶ֤ם לְאוֹר֙ וְשָׂמָ֣הּ לְצַלְמָ֔וֶת (ישית) [וְשִׁ֖ית] לַעֲרָפֶֽל׃


Give glory to the Lord your God, before it grows dark, and before your feet stumble upon the mountains of twilight, and, while you look for light, he turn it into the shadow of death, and make it gross darkness.


###### Jeremiah 13:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:17)


וְאִם֙ לֹ֣א תִשְׁמָע֔וּהָ בְּמִסְתָּרִ֥ים תִּבְכֶּֽה־נַפְשִׁ֖י מִפְּנֵ֣י גֵוָ֑ה וְדָמֹ֨עַ תִּדְמַ֜ע וְתֵרַ֤ד עֵינִי֙ דִּמְעָ֔ה כִּ֥י נִשְׁבָּ֖ה עֵ֥דֶר יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


But if you will not hear it, my soul shall weep in secret for your pride; and my eye shall weep sore, and run down with tears, because the Lord’s flock is carried away captive.


###### Jeremiah 13:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:18)


אֱמֹ֥ר לַמֶּ֛לֶךְ וְלַגְּבִירָ֖ה הַשְׁפִּ֣ילוּ שֵׁ֑בוּ כִּ֤י יָרַד֙ מַרְאֲשׁ֣וֹתֵיכֶ֔ם עֲטֶ֖רֶת תִּֽפְאַרְתְּכֶֽם׃


Say to the king and to the queen mother, Humble yourselves, sit down: for your glorious crown has fallen from your head.


###### Jeremiah 13:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:19)


עָרֵ֥י הַנֶּ֛גֶב סֻגְּר֖וּ וְאֵ֣ין פֹּתֵ֑חַ הׇגְלָ֧ת יְהוּדָ֛ה כֻּלָּ֖הּ הׇגְלָ֥ת שְׁלוֹמִֽים׃{ס}


The cities of the Negev shall be shut up, and none shall open them: Yehuda shall be carried away captive entirely, it shall be wholly carried away captive.


###### Jeremiah 13:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:20)


(שאי) [שְׂא֤וּ] עֵֽינֵיכֶם֙ (וראי) [וּרְא֔וּ] הַבָּאִ֖ים מִצָּפ֑וֹן אַיֵּ֗ה הָעֵ֙דֶר֙ נִתַּן־לָ֔ךְ צֹ֖אן תִּפְאַרְתֵּֽךְ׃


Lift up your eyes, and behold them that come from the north: where is the flock that was given thee, thy beautiful flock?


###### Jeremiah 13:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:21)


מַה־תֹּֽאמְרִי֙ כִּֽי־יִפְקֹ֣ד עָלַ֔יִךְ וְ֠אַ֠תְּ לִמַּ֨דְתְּ אֹתָ֥ם עָלַ֛יִךְ אַלֻּפִ֖ים לְרֹ֑אשׁ הֲל֤וֹא חֲבָלִים֙ יֹֽאחֱז֔וּךְ כְּמ֖וֹ אֵ֥שֶׁת לֵדָֽה׃


What wilt thou say when he shall punish thee? for thou hast taught them to be thy captains, as chief over thee: shall not pangs take hold of thee, as a woman in travail?


###### Jeremiah 13:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:22)


וְכִ֤י תֹֽאמְרִי֙ בִּלְבָבֵ֔ךְ מַדּ֖וּעַ קְרָאֻ֣נִי אֵ֑לֶּה בְּרֹ֧ב עֲוֺנֵ֛ךְ נִגְל֥וּ שׁוּלַ֖יִךְ נֶחְמְס֥וּ עֲקֵבָֽיִךְ׃


And if thou say in thy heart, Why come these things upon me? For the greatness of thy iniquity are thy skirts uncovered, and thy heels made bare.


###### Jeremiah 13:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:23)


הֲיַהֲפֹ֤ךְ כּוּשִׁי֙ עוֹר֔וֹ וְנָמֵ֖ר חֲבַרְבֻּרֹתָ֑יו גַּם־אַתֶּם֙ תּוּכְל֣וּ לְהֵיטִ֔יב לִמֻּדֵ֖י הָרֵֽעַ׃


Can the Kushite change his skin, or the leopard his spots? then may you also do good, that are accustomed to do evil.


###### Jeremiah 13:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:24)


וַאֲפִיצֵ֖ם כְּקַשׁ־עוֹבֵ֑ר לְר֖וּחַ מִדְבָּֽר׃


Therefore will I scatter them as the stubble that passes away by the wind of the wilderness.


###### Jeremiah 13:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:25)


זֶ֣ה גוֹרָלֵ֧ךְ מְנָת־מִדַּ֛יִךְ מֵאִתִּ֖י נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה אֲשֶׁר֙ שָׁכַ֣חַתְּ אוֹתִ֔י וַֽתִּבְטְחִ֖י בַּשָּֽׁקֶר׃


This is thy lot, the portion I have measured out to thee, says the Lord; because thou hast forgotten me, and trusted in falsehood.


###### Jeremiah 13:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:26)


וְגַם־אֲנִ֛י חָשַׂ֥פְתִּי שׁוּלַ֖יִךְ עַל־פָּנָ֑יִךְ וְנִרְאָ֖ה קְלוֹנֵֽךְ׃


Therefore will I strip thy skirts about thy face, that thy shame may be seen.


###### Jeremiah 13:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/13:27)


נִאֻפַ֤יִךְ וּמִצְהֲלוֹתַ֙יִךְ֙ זִמַּ֣ת זְנוּתֵ֔ךְ עַל־גְּבָעוֹת֙ בַּשָּׂדֶ֔ה רָאִ֖יתִי שִׁקּוּצָ֑יִךְ א֥וֹי לָךְ֙ יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם לֹ֣א תִטְהֲרִ֔י אַחֲרֵ֥י מָתַ֖י עֹֽד׃{ס}


I have seen thy adulteries, and thy neighings, thy disgusting harlotry, and thy abominable idols on the hills in the field. Woe to thee, O Yerushalayim! wilt thou not be made clean? How much longer will it be?



## Chapter 14

###### Jeremiah 14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:1)


אֲשֶׁ֨ר הָיָ֤ה דְבַר־יְהֹוָה֙ אֶֽל־יִרְמְיָ֔הוּ עַל־דִּבְרֵ֖י הַבַּצָּרֽוֹת׃


The word of the Lord that came to Yirmeyahu concerning the droughts.


###### Jeremiah 14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:2)


אָבְלָ֣ה יְהוּדָ֔ה וּשְׁעָרֶ֥יהָ אֻמְלְל֖וּ קָדְר֣וּ לָאָ֑רֶץ וְצִוְחַ֥ת יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם עָלָֽתָה׃


Yehuda mourns, and its gates languish in gloom to the ground; and the cry of Yerushalayim is gone up.


###### Jeremiah 14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:3)


וְאַדִּ֣רֵיהֶ֔ם שָׁלְח֥וּ (צעוריהם) [צְעִֽירֵיהֶ֖ם] לַמָּ֑יִם בָּ֣אוּ עַל־גֵּבִ֞ים לֹא־מָ֣צְאוּ מַ֗יִם שָׁ֤בוּ כְלֵיהֶם֙ רֵיקָ֔ם בֹּ֥שׁוּ וְהׇכְלְמ֖וּ וְחָפ֥וּ רֹאשָֽׁם׃


And their nobles have sent their little ones for water: they come to the pits, and find no water; they return with their vessels empty; they are ashamed and confounded, and cover their heads.


###### Jeremiah 14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:4)


בַּעֲב֤וּר הָֽאֲדָמָה֙ חַ֔תָּה כִּ֛י לֹא־הָיָ֥ה גֶ֖שֶׁם בָּאָ֑רֶץ בֹּ֥שׁוּ אִכָּרִ֖ים חָפ֥וּ רֹאשָֽׁם׃


Because of the ground which is cracked, for there has been no rain on the earth, the ploughmen are ashamed, they cover their heads.


###### Jeremiah 14:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:5)


כִּ֤י גַם־אַיֶּ֙לֶת֙ בַּשָּׂדֶ֔ה יָלְדָ֖ה וְעָז֑וֹב כִּ֥י לֹֽא־הָיָ֖ה דֶּֽשֶׁא׃


For the hind also calves in the field, and forsakes its young, because there is no grass.


###### Jeremiah 14:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:6)


וּפְרָאִים֙ עָמְד֣וּ עַל־שְׁפָיִ֔ם שָׁאֲפ֥וּ ר֖וּחַ כַּתַּנִּ֑ים כָּל֥וּ עֵינֵיהֶ֖ם כִּי־אֵ֥ין עֵֽשֶׂב׃


And the wild asses stand in the high places, they snuff up the wind like jackals; their eyes fail, because there is no herbage.


###### Jeremiah 14:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:7)


אִם־עֲוֺנֵ֙ינוּ֙ עָ֣נוּ בָ֔נוּ יְהֹוָ֕ה עֲשֵׂ֖ה לְמַ֣עַן שְׁמֶ֑ךָ כִּי־רַבּ֥וּ מְשׁוּבֹתֵ֖ינוּ לְךָ֥ חָטָֽאנוּ׃


O Lord, though our iniquities testify against us, do Thou it for Thy name’s sake: for our backslidings are many; we have sinned against Thee.


###### Jeremiah 14:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:8)


מִקְוֵה֙ יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל מוֹשִׁיע֖וֹ בְּעֵ֣ת צָרָ֑ה לָ֤מָּה תִֽהְיֶה֙ כְּגֵ֣ר בָּאָ֔רֶץ וּכְאֹרֵ֖חַ נָטָ֥ה לָלֽוּן׃


O the hope of Yisra᾽el, its saviour in time of trouble, why shouldst Thou be as a stranger in the land, and as a wayfaring man that turns aside to tarry for a night?


###### Jeremiah 14:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:9)


לָ֤מָּה תִֽהְיֶה֙ כְּאִ֣ישׁ נִדְהָ֔ם כְּגִבּ֖וֹר לֹא־יוּכַ֣ל לְהוֹשִׁ֑יעַ וְאַתָּ֧ה בְקִרְבֵּ֣נוּ יְהֹוָ֗ה וְשִׁמְךָ֛ עָלֵ֥ינוּ נִקְרָ֖א אַל־תַּנִּחֵֽנוּ׃{ס}


Why shouldst Thou be as a man surprised, as a mighty man that cannot save? yet Thou, O Lord, art in the midst of us, and we are called by Thy name; leave us not.


###### Jeremiah 14:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:10)


כֹּה־אָמַ֨ר יְהֹוָ֜ה לָעָ֣ם הַזֶּ֗ה כֵּ֤ן אָֽהֲבוּ֙ לָנ֔וּעַ רַגְלֵיהֶ֖ם לֹ֣א חָשָׂ֑כוּ וַֽיהֹוָה֙ לֹ֣א רָצָ֔ם עַתָּה֙ יִזְכֹּ֣ר עֲוֺנָ֔ם וְיִפְקֹ֖ד חַטֹּאתָֽם׃{פ}


Thus says the Lord to this people, Thus have they loved to wander, they have not restrained their feet, therefore the Lord does not accept them; he will now remember their iniquity, and punish their sins.


###### Jeremiah 14:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:11)


וַיֹּ֥אמֶר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלָ֑י אַל־תִּתְפַּלֵּ֛ל בְּעַד־הָעָ֥ם הַזֶּ֖ה לְטוֹבָֽה׃


Then said the Lord to me, Pray not for this people for their good.


###### Jeremiah 14:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:12)


כִּ֣י יָצֻ֗מוּ אֵינֶ֤נִּי שֹׁמֵ֙עַ֙ אֶל־רִנָּתָ֔ם וְכִ֧י יַעֲל֛וּ עֹלָ֥ה וּמִנְחָ֖ה אֵינֶ֣נִּי רֹצָ֑ם כִּ֗י בַּחֶ֙רֶב֙ וּבָרָעָ֣ב וּבַדֶּ֔בֶר אָנֹכִ֖י מְכַלֶּ֥ה אוֹתָֽם׃{ס}


When they fast, I will not hear their cry; and when they offer burnt offering and a meal offering, I will not accept them: but I will consume them by the sword, and by the famine, and by the pestilence.


###### Jeremiah 14:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:13)


וָאֹמַ֞ר אֲהָ֣הּ**׀** אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה הִנֵּ֨ה הַנְּבִאִ֜ים אֹמְרִ֤ים לָהֶם֙ לֹא־תִרְא֣וּ חֶ֔רֶב וְרָעָ֖ב לֹֽא־יִהְיֶ֣ה לָכֶ֑ם כִּֽי־שְׁל֤וֹם אֱמֶת֙ אֶתֵּ֣ן לָכֶ֔ם בַּמָּק֖וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃{ס}


Then said I, Ah, Lord God! behold, the prophets say to them, You shall not see the sword, neither shall you have famine; but I will give you assured peace in this place.


###### Jeremiah 14:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:14)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר יְהֹוָ֜ה אֵלַ֗י שֶׁ֚קֶר הַנְּבִאִים֙ נִבְּאִ֣ים בִּשְׁמִ֔י לֹ֤א שְׁלַחְתִּים֙ וְלֹ֣א צִוִּיתִ֔ים וְלֹ֥א דִבַּ֖רְתִּי אֲלֵיהֶ֑ם חֲז֨וֹן שֶׁ֜קֶר וְקֶ֤סֶם (ואלול ותרמות) [וֶאֱלִיל֙ וְתַרְמִ֣ית] לִבָּ֔ם הֵ֖מָּה מִֽתְנַבְּאִ֥ים לָכֶֽם׃{ס}


Then the Lord said to me, The prophets prophesy lies in my name: I sent them not, neither have I commanded them, neither spoke I to them: they prophesy to you a false vision and divination, and a thing of nought, and the deceit of their own heart.


###### Jeremiah 14:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:15)


לָכֵ֞ן כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה עַֽל־הַנְּבִאִ֞ים הַנִּבְּאִ֣ים בִּשְׁמִי֮ וַאֲנִ֣י לֹֽא־שְׁלַחְתִּים֒ וְהֵ֙מָּה֙ אֹֽמְרִ֔ים חֶ֣רֶב וְרָעָ֔ב לֹ֥א יִהְיֶ֖ה בָּאָ֣רֶץ הַזֹּ֑את בַּחֶ֤רֶב וּבָֽרָעָב֙ יִתַּ֔מּוּ הַנְּבִאִ֖ים הָהֵֽמָּה׃


Therefore thus says the Lord concerning the prophets that prophesy in my name, and I sent them not, yet they say, Sword and famine shall not be in this land; By sword and famine shall those prophets be consumed.


###### Jeremiah 14:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:16)


וְהָעָ֣ם אֲשֶׁר־הֵ֣מָּה נִבְּאִ֣ים לָהֶ֡ם יִהְי֣וּ מֻשְׁלָכִים֩ בְּחֻצ֨וֹת יְרוּשָׁלַ֜͏ִם מִפְּנֵ֣י**׀** הָרָעָ֣ב וְהַחֶ֗רֶב וְאֵ֤ין מְקַבֵּר֙ לָהֵ֔מָּה הֵ֣מָּה נְשֵׁיהֶ֔ם וּבְנֵיהֶ֖ם וּבְנֹתֵיהֶ֑ם וְשָׁפַכְתִּ֥י עֲלֵיהֶ֖ם אֶת־רָעָתָֽם׃


And the people to whom they prophesy shall be cast out in the streets of Yerushalayim because of the famine and the sword; and they shall have none to bury them, neither them, their wives, nor their sons, nor their daughters: for I will pour their wickedness upon them.


###### Jeremiah 14:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:17)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֤ אֲלֵיהֶם֙ אֶת־הַדָּבָ֣ר הַזֶּ֔ה תֵּרַ֨דְנָה עֵינַ֥י דִּמְעָ֛ה לַ֥יְלָה וְיוֹמָ֖ם וְאַל־תִּדְמֶ֑ינָה כִּי֩ שֶׁ֨בֶר גָּד֜וֹל נִשְׁבְּרָ֗ה בְּתוּלַת֙ בַּת־עַמִּ֔י מַכָּ֖ה נַחְלָ֥ה מְאֹֽד׃


Therefore thou shalt say this word to them; Let my eyes run down with tears night and day, and let them not cease: for the virgin daughter of my people is broken with a great wound, with a very grievous blow.


###### Jeremiah 14:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:18)


אִם־יָצָ֣אתִי הַשָּׂדֶ֗ה וְהִנֵּה֙ חַלְלֵי־חֶ֔רֶב וְאִם֙ בָּ֣אתִי הָעִ֔יר וְהִנֵּ֖ה תַּחֲלוּאֵ֣י רָעָ֑ב כִּֽי־גַם־נָבִ֧יא גַם־כֹּהֵ֛ן סָחֲר֥וּ אֶל־אֶ֖רֶץ וְלֹ֥א יָדָֽעוּ׃{ס}


If I go forth into the field, then behold the slain with the sword! and if I enter into the city, then behold those that are sick with famine! for both prophet and priest go about into a land that they know not.


###### Jeremiah 14:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:19)


הֲמָאֹ֨ס מָאַ֜סְתָּ אֶת־יְהוּדָ֗ה אִם־בְּצִיּוֹן֙ גָּעֲלָ֣ה נַפְשֶׁ֔ךָ מַדּ֙וּעַ֙ הִכִּיתָ֔נוּ וְאֵ֥ין לָ֖נוּ מַרְפֵּ֑א קַוֵּ֤ה לְשָׁלוֹם֙ וְאֵ֣ין ט֔וֹב וּלְעֵ֥ת מַרְפֵּ֖א וְהִנֵּ֥ה בְעָתָֽה׃


Hast Thou utterly rejected Yehuda? has Thy soul despised Żiyyon? why hast Thou smitten us, and there is no healing for us? we looked for peace, and there is no good; and for the time of healing, and behold terror!


###### Jeremiah 14:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:20)


יָדַ֧עְנוּ יְהֹוָ֛ה רִשְׁעֵ֖נוּ עֲוֺ֣ן אֲבוֹתֵ֑ינוּ כִּ֥י חָטָ֖אנוּ לָֽךְ׃


We acknowledge, O Lord, our wickedness, the iniquity of our fathers: for we have sinned against Thee.


###### Jeremiah 14:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:21)


אַל־תִּנְאַץ֙ לְמַ֣עַן שִׁמְךָ֔ אַל־תְּנַבֵּ֖ל כִּסֵּ֣א כְבוֹדֶ֑ךָ זְכֹ֕ר אַל־תָּפֵ֥ר בְּרִיתְךָ֖ אִתָּֽנוּ׃


Do not abhor us, for Thy name’s sake, do not dishonour the throne of Thy glory: remember, break not Thy covenant with us.


###### Jeremiah 14:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/14:22)


הֲיֵ֨שׁ בְּהַבְלֵ֤י הַגּוֹיִם֙ מַגְשִׁמִ֔ים וְאִם־הַשָּׁמַ֖יִם יִתְּנ֣וּ רְבִבִ֑ים הֲלֹ֨א אַתָּה־ה֜וּא יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהֵ֙ינוּ֙ וּנְקַוֶּה־לָּ֔ךְ כִּֽי־אַתָּ֥ה עָשִׂ֖יתָ אֶת־כׇּל־אֵֽלֶּה׃{פ}


Are there any among the worthless gods of the nations that can cause rain? or can the heavens give showers? art not Thou he, O Lord our God? therefore we set our hope on Thee: for Thou hast made all these things.



## Chapter 15

###### Jeremiah 15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:1)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר יְהֹוָה֙ אֵלַ֔י אִם־יַעֲמֹ֨ד מֹשֶׁ֤ה וּשְׁמוּאֵל֙ לְפָנַ֔י אֵ֥ין נַפְשִׁ֖י אֶל־הָעָ֣ם הַזֶּ֑ה שַׁלַּ֥ח מֵעַל־פָּנַ֖י וְיֵצֵֽאוּ׃


Then said the Lord to me, Though Moshe and Shemu᾽el were to stand before me, my mind could not incline towards this people: send them out of my sight, and let them depart.


###### Jeremiah 15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:2)


וְהָיָ֛ה כִּי־יֹאמְר֥וּ אֵלֶ֖יךָ אָ֣נָה נֵצֵ֑א וְאָמַרְתָּ֨ אֲלֵיהֶ֜ם כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר לַמָּ֤וֶת לַמָּ֙וֶת֙ וַאֲשֶׁ֤ר לַחֶ֙רֶב֙ לַחֶ֔רֶב וַאֲשֶׁ֤ר לָרָעָב֙ לָרָעָ֔ב וַאֲשֶׁ֥ר לַשְּׁבִ֖י לַשֶּֽׁבִי׃


And it shall come to pass, if they say to thee, Whither shall we depart? then thou shalt tell them, Thus says the Lord; Such as are for death, to death; and such as are for the sword, to the sword; and such as are for the famine, to the famine; and such as are for the captivity, to the captivity.


###### Jeremiah 15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:3)


וּפָקַדְתִּ֨י עֲלֵיהֶ֜ם אַרְבַּ֤ע מִשְׁפָּחוֹת֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה אֶת־הַחֶ֣רֶב לַהֲרֹ֔ג וְאֶת־הַכְּלָבִ֖ים לִסְחֹ֑ב וְאֶת־ע֧וֹף הַשָּׁמַ֛יִם וְאֶת־בֶּהֱמַ֥ת הָאָ֖רֶץ לֶאֱכֹ֥ל וּלְהַשְׁחִֽית׃


And I will appoint over them four kinds of ravagers, says the Lord: the sword to slay, and the dogs to drag away, and the birds of the heaven, and the beasts of the earth, to devour and destroy.


###### Jeremiah 15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:4)


וּנְתַתִּ֣ים (לזועה) [לְזַעֲוָ֔ה] לְכֹ֖ל מַמְלְכ֣וֹת הָאָ֑רֶץ בִּ֠גְלַ֠ל מְנַשֶּׁ֤ה בֶן־יְחִזְקִיָּ֙הוּ֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֔ה עַ֥ל אֲשֶׁר־עָשָׂ֖ה בִּירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


And I will make them into a horror for all the kingdoms of the earth, on account of Menashshe the son of Yeĥizqiyyahu king of Yehuda, for that which he did in Yerushalayim.


###### Jeremiah 15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:5)


כִּ֠י מִֽי־יַחְמֹ֤ל עָלַ֙יִךְ֙ יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם וּמִ֖י יָנ֣וּד לָ֑ךְ וּמִ֣י יָס֔וּר לִשְׁאֹ֥ל לְשָׁלֹ֖ם לָֽךְ׃


For who shall have pity upon thee, O Yerushalayim, or who shall bemoan thee? or who shall go aside to ask how thou doest?


###### Jeremiah 15:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:6)


אַ֣תְּ נָטַ֥שְׁתְּ אֹתִ֛י נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֖ה אָח֣וֹר תֵּלֵ֑כִי וָאַ֨ט אֶת־יָדִ֤י עָלַ֙יִךְ֙ וָֽאַשְׁחִיתֵ֔ךְ נִלְאֵ֖יתִי הִנָּחֵֽם׃


Thou hast forsaken me, says the Lord, thou art gone backward: therefore will I stretch out my hand against thee, and destroy thee; I am weary of relenting.


###### Jeremiah 15:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:7)


וָאֶזְרֵ֥ם בְּמִזְרֶ֖ה בְּשַׁעֲרֵ֣י הָאָ֑רֶץ שִׁכַּ֤לְתִּי אִבַּ֙דְתִּי֙ אֶת־עַמִּ֔י מִדַּרְכֵיהֶ֖ם לוֹא־שָֽׁבוּ׃


And I will winnow them with a winnowing fork in the gates of the land; I will bereave them of children, I will destroy my people, since they return not from their ways.


###### Jeremiah 15:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:8)


עָֽצְמוּ־לִ֤י אַלְמְנוֹתָו֙ מֵח֣וֹל יַמִּ֔ים הֵבֵ֨אתִי לָהֶ֥ם עַל־אֵ֛ם בָּח֖וּר שֹׁדֵ֣ד בַּֽצׇּהֳרָ֑יִם הִפַּ֤לְתִּי עָלֶ֙יהָ֙ פִּתְאֹ֔ם עִ֖יר וּבֶהָלֽוֹת׃


Their widows are increased to me above the sand of the seas: I have brought upon them, upon the mothers of the young men, a spoiler at noonday: I have caused tumult and terrors to fall upon it suddenly.


###### Jeremiah 15:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:9)


אֻמְלְלָ֞ה יֹלֶ֣דֶת הַשִּׁבְעָ֗ה נָפְחָ֥ה נַפְשָׁ֛הּ (באה) [בָּ֥א] שִׁמְשָׁ֛הּ בְּעֹ֥ד יוֹמָ֖ם בּ֣וֹשָׁה וְחָפֵ֑רָה וּשְׁאֵרִיתָ֗ם לַחֶ֧רֶב אֶתֵּ֛ן לִפְנֵ֥י אֹיְבֵיהֶ֖ם נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


She that has born seven languishes: she has fainted away; her sun is gone down while it was yet day: she has been ashamed and confounded: and the residue of them will I deliver to the sword before their enemies, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 15:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:10)


אֽוֹי־לִ֣י אִמִּ֔י כִּ֣י יְלִדְתִּ֗נִי אִ֥ישׁ רִ֛יב וְאִ֥ישׁ מָד֖וֹן לְכׇל־הָאָ֑רֶץ לֹֽא־נָשִׁ֥יתִי וְלֹא־נָֽשׁוּ־בִ֖י כֻּלֹּ֥ה מְקַֽלְלַֽוְנִי׃{ס}


Woe is me, my mother, that thou hast born me a man of strife and a man of contention to the whole earth! I have neither lent, nor have men lent to me; yet every one of them curses me.


###### Jeremiah 15:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:11)


אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה אִם־לֹ֥א (שרותך) [שֵׁרִיתִ֖יךָ] לְט֑וֹב אִם־ל֣וֹא**׀** הִפְגַּ֣עְתִּֽי בְךָ֗ בְּעֵ֥ת רָעָ֛ה וּבְעֵ֥ת צָרָ֖ה אֶת־הָאֹיֵֽב׃


The Lord said, Have I not provided for thy welfare; have I not made the enemy entreat thy intercession, in the time of evil and in the time of affliction.


###### Jeremiah 15:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:12)


הֲיָרֹ֨עַ בַּרְזֶ֧ל׀בַּרְזֶ֛ל מִצָּפ֖וֹן וּנְחֹֽשֶׁת׃


Shall iron break iron from the north, and brass.


###### Jeremiah 15:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:13)


חֵילְךָ֧ וְאוֹצְרוֹתֶ֛יךָ לָבַ֥ז אֶתֵּ֖ן לֹ֣א בִמְחִ֑יר וּבְכׇל־חַטֹּאותֶ֖יךָ וּבְכׇל־גְּבוּלֶֽיךָ׃


Thy substance and thy treasures will I give to the spoil without price, and that for all thy sins, even in all thy borders.


###### Jeremiah 15:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:14)


וְהַֽעֲבַרְתִּי֙ אֶת־אֹ֣יְבֶ֔יךָ בְּאֶ֖רֶץ לֹ֣א יָדָ֑עְתָּ כִּי־אֵ֛שׁ קָדְחָ֥ה בְאַפִּ֖י עֲלֵיכֶ֥ם תּוּקָֽד׃{ס}


And I will cause thee to pass with thy enemies into a land which thou knowst not: for a fire is kindled in my anger, which shall burn upon you.


###### Jeremiah 15:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:15)


אַתָּ֧ה יָדַ֣עְתָּ יְהֹוָ֗ה זׇכְרֵ֤נִי וּפׇקְדֵ֙נִי֙ וְהִנָּ֤קֶם לִי֙ מֵרֹ֣דְפַ֔י אַל־לְאֶ֥רֶךְ אַפְּךָ֖ תִּקָּחֵ֑נִי דַּ֕ע שְׂאֵתִ֥י עָלֶ֖יךָ חֶרְפָּֽה׃


O Lord, Thou knowst: remember me, and be mindful of me, and revenge me of my persecutors; take me not away in Thy long suffering: know that for Thy sake I have suffered insult.


###### Jeremiah 15:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:16)


נִמְצְא֤וּ דְבָרֶ֙יךָ֙ וָאֹ֣כְלֵ֔ם וַיְהִ֤י (דבריך) [דְבָֽרְךָ֙] לִ֔י לְשָׂשׂ֖וֹן וּלְשִׂמְחַ֣ת לְבָבִ֑י כִּֽי־נִקְרָ֤א שִׁמְךָ֙ עָלַ֔י יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵ֥י צְבָאֽוֹת׃{ס}


Thy words were found, and I did eat them; and Thy word was to me the joy and rejoicing of my heart: for I am called by Thy name, O Lord God of hosts.


###### Jeremiah 15:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:17)


לֹא־יָשַׁ֥בְתִּי בְסוֹד־מְשַׂחֲקִ֖ים וָאֶעְלֹ֑ז מִפְּנֵ֤י יָֽדְךָ֙ בָּדָ֣ד יָשַׁ֔בְתִּי כִּי־זַ֖עַם מִלֵּאתָֽנִי׃


I sat not in the assembly of the merrymakers, nor rejoiced; I sat alone because of Thy hand: for Thou hast filled me with indignation.


###### Jeremiah 15:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:18)


לָ֣מָּה הָיָ֤ה כְאֵבִי֙ נֶ֔צַח וּמַכָּתִ֖י אֲנוּשָׁ֑ה מֵֽאֲנָה֙ הֵרָפֵ֔א הָי֨וֹ תִהְיֶ֥ה לִי֙ כְּמ֣וֹ אַכְזָ֔ב מַ֖יִם לֹ֥א נֶאֱמָֽנוּ׃{ס}


Why is my pain perpetual, and my wound incurable, refusing to be healed? wilt Thou be to me like a deceitful stream, like waters that fail?


###### Jeremiah 15:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:19)


לָכֵ֞ן כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה אִם־תָּשׁ֤וּב וַאֲשִֽׁיבְךָ֙ לְפָנַ֣י תַּעֲמֹ֔ד וְאִם־תּוֹצִ֥יא יָקָ֛ר מִזּוֹלֵ֖ל כְּפִ֣י תִֽהְיֶ֑ה יָשֻׁ֤בוּ הֵ֙מָּה֙ אֵלֶ֔יךָ וְאַתָּ֖ה לֹא־תָשׁ֥וּב אֲלֵיהֶֽם׃


Therefore thus says the Lord, If thou return, then will I bring thee back, and thou shalt stand before me: and if thou bring out the precious from the vile, thou shalt be as my mouth: let them return to thee; but return not thou to them.


###### Jeremiah 15:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:20)


וּנְתַתִּ֜יךָ לָעָ֣ם הַזֶּ֗ה לְחוֹמַ֤ת נְחֹ֙שֶׁת֙ בְּצוּרָ֔ה וְנִלְחֲמ֥וּ אֵלֶ֖יךָ וְלֹא־י֣וּכְלוּ לָ֑ךְ כִּֽי־אִתְּךָ֥ אֲנִ֛י לְהוֹשִׁיעֲךָ֥ וּלְהַצִּילֶ֖ךָ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


And I will make thee to this people a fortified wall of brass: and they shall fight against thee, but they shall not prevail against thee: for I am with thee to save thee and to deliver thee, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 15:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/15:21)


וְהִצַּלְתִּ֖יךָ מִיַּ֣ד רָעִ֑ים וּפְדִתִ֖יךָ מִכַּ֥ף עָרִצִֽים׃{ס}


And I will deliver thee out of the hand of the wicked, and I will redeem thee out of the hand of the terrible.



## Chapter 16

###### Jeremiah 16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:1)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


The word of the Lord came also to me, saying,


###### Jeremiah 16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:2)


לֹא־תִקַּ֥ח לְךָ֖ אִשָּׁ֑ה וְלֹֽא־יִהְי֤וּ לְךָ֙ בָּנִ֣ים וּבָנ֔וֹת בַּמָּק֖וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃{ס}


Thou shalt not take thee a wife, neither shalt thou have sons or daughters in this place.


###### Jeremiah 16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:3)


כִּי־כֹ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה עַל־הַבָּנִים֙ וְעַל־הַבָּנ֔וֹת הַיִּלּוֹדִ֖ים בַּמָּק֣וֹם הַזֶּ֑ה וְעַֽל־אִמֹּתָ֞ם הַיֹּלְד֣וֹת אוֹתָ֗ם וְעַל־אֲבוֹתָ֛ם הַמּוֹלִדִ֥ים אוֹתָ֖ם בָּאָ֥רֶץ הַזֹּֽאת׃


For thus says the Lord concerning the sons and concerning the daughters that are born in this place, and concerning their mothers that bore them, and concerning their fathers that begot them in this land;


###### Jeremiah 16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:4)


מְמוֹתֵ֨י תַחֲלֻאִ֜ים יָמֻ֗תוּ לֹ֤א יִסָּֽפְדוּ֙ וְלֹ֣א יִקָּבֵ֔רוּ לְדֹ֛מֶן עַל־פְּנֵ֥י הָאֲדָמָ֖ה יִהְי֑וּ וּבַחֶ֤רֶב וּבָֽרָעָב֙ יִכְל֔וּ וְהָיְתָ֤ה נִבְלָתָם֙ לְמַאֲכָ֔ל לְע֥וֹף הַשָּׁמַ֖יִם וּלְבֶהֱמַ֥ת הָאָֽרֶץ׃{ס}


they shall die of grievous deaths; they shall not be lamented; neither shall they be buried; but they shall be as dung upon the face of the earth: and they shall be consumed by the sword, and by famine; and their carcasses shall be food for the birds of the sky, and for the beasts of the earth.


###### Jeremiah 16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:5)


כִּי־כֹ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה אַל־תָּבוֹא֙ בֵּ֣ית מַרְזֵ֔חַ וְאַל־תֵּלֵ֣ךְ לִסְפּ֔וֹד וְאַל־תָּנֹ֖ד לָהֶ֑ם כִּֽי־אָסַ֨פְתִּי אֶת־שְׁלוֹמִ֜י מֵאֵ֨ת הָעָ֤ם הַזֶּה֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה אֶת־הַחֶ֖סֶד וְאֶת־הָרַחֲמִֽים׃


For thus says the Lord, Enter not into the house of mourning, neither go to lament nor bemoan them: for I have taken away my peace from this people, says the Lord, both love and mercy.


###### Jeremiah 16:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:6)


וּמֵ֨תוּ גְדֹלִ֧ים וּקְטַנִּ֛ים בָּאָ֥רֶץ הַזֹּ֖את לֹ֣א יִקָּבֵ֑רוּ וְלֹא־יִסְפְּד֣וּ לָהֶ֔ם וְלֹ֣א יִתְגֹּדַ֔ד וְלֹ֥א יִקָּרֵ֖חַ לָהֶֽם׃


Both the great and the small shall die in this land: they shall not be buried, neither shall men lament for them, nor gash themselves, nor make themselves bald for them:


###### Jeremiah 16:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:7)


וְלֹא־יִפְרְס֥וּ לָהֶ֛ם עַל־אֵ֖בֶל לְנַחֲמ֣וֹ עַל־מֵ֑ת וְלֹֽא־יַשְׁק֤וּ אוֹתָם֙ כּ֣וֹס תַּנְחוּמִ֔ים עַל־אָבִ֖יו וְעַל־אִמּֽוֹ׃


neither shall men break bread for them during the mourning, to comfort him for the dead; neither shall men give them the cup of consolation to drink for his father or for his mother.


###### Jeremiah 16:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:8)


וּבֵית־מִשְׁתֶּ֥ה לֹֽא־תָב֖וֹא לָשֶׁ֣בֶת אוֹתָ֑ם לֶאֱכֹ֖ל וְלִשְׁתּֽוֹת׃{פ}


Thou shalt also not go into the house of feasting, to sit with them to eat and to drink.


###### Jeremiah 16:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:9)


כִּי֩ כֹ֨ה אָמַ֜ר יְהֹוָ֤ה צְבָאוֹת֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל הִנְנִ֨י מַשְׁבִּ֜ית מִן־הַמָּק֥וֹם הַזֶּ֛ה לְעֵינֵיכֶ֖ם וּבִימֵיכֶ֑ם ק֤וֹל שָׂשׂוֹן֙ וְק֣וֹל שִׂמְחָ֔ה ק֥וֹל חָתָ֖ן וְק֥וֹל כַּלָּֽה׃


For thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Yisra᾽el; Behold, I will cause to cease out of this place before your eyes, and in your days, the voice of mirth, and the voice of gladness, the voice of the bridegroom, and the voice of the bride.


###### Jeremiah 16:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:10)


וְהָיָ֗ה כִּ֤י תַגִּיד֙ לָעָ֣ם הַזֶּ֔ה אֵ֥ת כׇּל־הַדְּבָרִ֖ים הָאֵ֑לֶּה וְאָמְר֣וּ אֵלֶ֗יךָ עַל־מֶה֩ דִבֶּ֨ר יְהֹוָ֤ה עָלֵ֙ינוּ֙ אֵ֣ת כׇּל־הָרָעָ֤ה הַגְּדוֹלָה֙ הַזֹּ֔את וּמֶ֤ה עֲוֺנֵ֙נוּ֙ וּמֶ֣ה חַטָּאתֵ֔נוּ אֲשֶׁ֥ר חָטָ֖אנוּ לַיהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ׃


And it shall come to pass, when thou shalt tell this people all these words, and they shall say to thee, Why has the Lord pronounced all this great evil against us? or what is our iniquity? or what is our sin that we have committed against the Lord our God?


###### Jeremiah 16:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:11)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֲלֵיהֶ֗ם עַל֩ אֲשֶׁר־עָזְב֨וּ אֲבוֹתֵיכֶ֤ם אוֹתִי֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה וַיֵּלְכ֗וּ אַֽחֲרֵי֙ אֱלֹהִ֣ים אֲחֵרִ֔ים וַיַּעַבְד֖וּם וַיִּשְׁתַּחֲו֣וּ לָהֶ֑ם וְאֹתִ֣י עָזָ֔בוּ וְאֶת־תּוֹרָתִ֖י לֹ֥א שָׁמָֽרוּ׃


then shalt thou say to them, Because your fathers have forsaken me, says the Lord, and have walked after other gods, and have served them, and have worshipped them, and have forsaken me, and have not kept my Tora;


###### Jeremiah 16:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:12)


וְאַתֶּ֛ם הֲרֵעֹתֶ֥ם לַעֲשׂ֖וֹת מֵאֲבֽוֹתֵיכֶ֑ם וְהִנְּכֶ֣ם הֹלְכִ֗ים אִ֚ישׁ אַֽחֲרֵי֙ שְׁרִר֣וּת לִבּֽוֹ־הָרָ֔ע לְבִלְתִּ֖י שְׁמֹ֥עַ אֵלָֽי׃


and you have done worse than your fathers; for, behold, you walk every one after the stubbornness of his evil heart, that they may not hearken to me:


###### Jeremiah 16:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:13)


וְהֵטַלְתִּ֣י אֶתְכֶ֗ם מֵעַל֙ הָאָ֣רֶץ הַזֹּ֔את עַל־הָאָ֕רֶץ אֲשֶׁר֙ לֹ֣א יְדַעְתֶּ֔ם אַתֶּ֖ם וַאֲבוֹתֵיכֶ֑ם וַעֲבַדְתֶּם־שָׁ֞ם אֶת־אֱלֹהִ֤ים אֲחֵרִים֙ יוֹמָ֣ם וָלַ֔יְלָה אֲשֶׁ֛ר לֹא־אֶתֵּ֥ן לָכֶ֖ם חֲנִינָֽה׃{פ}


therefore will I cast you out of this land into a land that you know not, neither you nor your fathers; and there shall you serve other gods day and night; for I will show you no favour.


###### Jeremiah 16:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:14)


לָכֵ֛ן הִנֵּה־יָמִ֥ים בָּאִ֖ים נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה וְלֹא־יֵאָמֵ֥ר עוֹד֙ חַי־יְהֹוָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֧ר הֶעֱלָ֛ה אֶת־בְּנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל מֵאֶ֥רֶץ מִצְרָֽיִם׃


Therefore, behold, days are coming, says the Lord, when it shall no more be said, As the Lord lives, that brought up the children of Yisra᾽el out of the land of Miżrayim;


###### Jeremiah 16:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:15)


כִּ֣י אִם־חַי־יְהֹוָ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר הֶעֱלָ֜ה אֶת־בְּנֵ֤י יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ מֵאֶ֣רֶץ צָפ֔וֹן וּמִכֹּל֙ הָאֲרָצ֔וֹת אֲשֶׁ֥ר הִדִּיחָ֖ם שָׁ֑מָּה וַהֲשִֽׁבֹתִים֙ עַל־אַדְמָתָ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר נָתַ֖תִּי לַאֲבוֹתָֽם׃{פ}


but, As the Lord lives, that brought up the children of Yisra᾽el from the land of the north, and from all the lands into which he had driven them: and I will bring them back into their land that I gave to their fathers.


###### Jeremiah 16:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:16)


הִנְנִ֨י שֹׁלֵ֜חַ (לדוגים) [לְדַיָּגִ֥ים] רַבִּ֛ים נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֖ה וְדִיג֑וּם וְאַחֲרֵי־כֵ֗ן אֶשְׁלַח֙ לְרַבִּ֣ים צַיָּדִ֔ים וְצָד֞וּם מֵעַ֤ל כׇּל־הַר֙ וּמֵעַ֣ל כׇּל־גִּבְעָ֔ה וּמִנְּקִיקֵ֖י הַסְּלָעִֽים׃


Behold, I will send for many fishers, says the Lord, and they shall fish for them; and after will I send for many hunters, and they shall hunt them out from every mountain, and from every hill, and out of the clefts of the rocks.


###### Jeremiah 16:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:17)


כִּ֤י עֵינַי֙ עַל־כׇּל־דַּרְכֵיהֶ֔ם לֹ֥א נִסְתְּר֖וּ מִלְּפָנָ֑י וְלֹֽא־נִצְפַּ֥ן עֲוֺנָ֖ם מִנֶּ֥גֶד עֵינָֽי׃


For my eyes are upon all their ways: they are not hid from my face, neither is their iniquity hid from my eyes.


###### Jeremiah 16:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:18)


וְשִׁלַּמְתִּ֣י רִאשׁוֹנָ֗ה מִשְׁנֵ֤ה עֲוֺנָם֙ וְחַטָּאתָ֔ם עַ֖ל חַלְּלָ֣ם אֶת־אַרְצִ֑י בְּנִבְלַ֤ת שִׁקּֽוּצֵיהֶם֙ וְתוֹעֲב֣וֹתֵיהֶ֔ם מָלְא֖וּ אֶת־נַחֲלָתִֽי׃{פ}


And first I will recompense their iniquity and their sin double; because they have profaned my land, they have filled my inheritance with the carcasses of their detestable and abominable things.


###### Jeremiah 16:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:19)


יְהֹוָ֞ה עֻזִּ֧י וּמָעֻזִּ֛י וּמְנוּסִ֖י בְּי֣וֹם צָרָ֑ה אֵלֶ֗יךָ גּוֹיִ֤ם יָבֹ֙אוּ֙ מֵֽאַפְסֵי־אָ֔רֶץ וְיֹאמְר֗וּ אַךְ־שֶׁ֙קֶר֙ נָחֲל֣וּ אֲבוֹתֵ֔ינוּ הֶ֖בֶל וְאֵֽין־בָּ֥ם מוֹעִֽיל׃


O Lord, my strength, and my stronghold, and my refuge in the day of affliction, nations shall come to Thee from the ends of the earth, and shall say, Surely our fathers have inherited lies, vain idols, and things in which there is no profit.


###### Jeremiah 16:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:20)


הֲיַעֲשֶׂה־לּ֥וֹ אָדָ֖ם אֱלֹהִ֑ים וְהֵ֖מָּה לֹ֥א אֱלֹהִֽים׃


Shall a man make gods to himself, and they are no gods?


###### Jeremiah 16:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/16:21)


לָכֵן֙ הִנְנִ֣י מֽוֹדִיעָ֔ם בַּפַּ֣עַם הַזֹּ֔את אוֹדִיעֵ֥ם אֶת־יָדִ֖י וְאֶת־גְּבוּרָתִ֑י וְיָדְע֖וּ כִּֽי־שְׁמִ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


Therefore, behold, I will make known to them this once, I will cause them to know my hand and my might; and they shall know that my name is The Lord.



## Chapter 17

###### Jeremiah 17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/17:1)


חַטַּ֣את יְהוּדָ֗ה כְּתוּבָ֛ה בְּעֵ֥ט בַּרְזֶ֖ל בְּצִפֹּ֣רֶן שָׁמִ֑יר חֲרוּשָׁה֙ עַל־ל֣וּחַ לִבָּ֔ם וּלְקַרְנ֖וֹת מִזְבְּחוֹתֵיכֶֽם׃


The sin of Yehuda is written with a pen of iron, and with the point of a diamond: it is graven upon the tablet of their heart, and upon the horns of your altars.


###### Jeremiah 17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/17:2)


כִּזְכֹּ֤ר בְּנֵיהֶם֙ מִזְבְּחוֹתָ֔ם וַאֲשֵׁרֵיהֶ֖ם עַל־עֵ֣ץ רַעֲנָ֑ן עַ֖ל גְּבָע֥וֹת הַגְּבֹהֽוֹת׃


As they remember their children so they remember their altars and their asherim by the green trees upon the high hills.


###### Jeremiah 17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/17:3)

O mountain dweller in the fields, I will give thy substance and all thy treasures to the spoil; thy high places, because of sin, throughout all thy borders.

And thou, even thyself, shalt discontinue from thy heritage that I gave thee; and I will cause thee to serve thy enemies in the land which thou knowst not: for you have kindled a fire in my anger, which shall burn for ever.

For he shall be like the juniper tree in the desert, and shall not see when good comes; but shall inhabit the parched places in the wilderness, a salt land and not inhabited.

וְהָיָ֞ה כְּעֵ֣ץ**׀** שָׁת֣וּל עַל־מַ֗יִם וְעַל־יוּבַל֙ יְשַׁלַּ֣ח שׇׁרָשָׁ֔יו וְלֹ֤א (ירא) [יִרְאֶה֙] כִּי־יָ֣בֹא חֹ֔ם וְהָיָ֥ה עָלֵ֖הוּ רַעֲנָ֑ן וּבִשְׁנַ֤ת בַּצֹּ֙רֶת֙ לֹ֣א יִדְאָ֔ג וְלֹ֥א יָמִ֖ישׁ מֵעֲשׂ֥וֹת פֶּֽרִי׃


For he shall be like a tree planted by the waters, and that spreads out its roots by the river, and shall not see when the heat comes, but its leaf shall be green; and shall not be anxious in the year of drought, nor shall it cease from yielding fruit.

אֲנִ֧י יְהֹוָ֛ה חֹקֵ֥ר לֵ֖ב בֹּחֵ֣ן כְּלָי֑וֹת וְלָתֵ֤ת לְאִישׁ֙ כִּדְרָכָ֔ו כִּפְרִ֖י מַעֲלָלָֽיו׃{ס}

רְפָאֵ֤נִי יְהֹוָה֙ וְאֵ֣רָפֵ֔א הוֹשִׁיעֵ֖נִי וְאִוָּשֵׁ֑עָה כִּ֥י תְהִלָּתִ֖י אָֽתָּה׃


Heal me, O Lord, and I shall be healed; save me, and I shall be saved: for Thou art my praise.

Behold, they say to me, Where is the word of the Lord? let it come now.


###### Jeremiah 17:16

וַאֲנִ֞י לֹא־אַ֣צְתִּי**׀** מֵרֹעֶ֣ה אַחֲרֶ֗יךָ וְי֥וֹם אָנ֛וּשׁ לֹ֥א הִתְאַוֵּ֖יתִי אַתָּ֣ה יָדָ֑עְתָּ מוֹצָ֣א שְׂפָתַ֔י נֹ֥כַח פָּנֶ֖יךָ הָיָֽה׃


As for me, I did not hasten to be a shepherd to follow Thee: neither have I desired the woeful day; Thou knowst: that which came out of my lips was before Thee.


###### Jeremiah 17:17

Be not a terror to me: Thou art my hope in the day of evil.


###### Jeremiah 17:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/17:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/17:18)


יֵבֹ֤שׁוּ רֹֽדְפַי֙ וְאַל־אֵבֹ֣שָׁה אָ֔נִי יֵחַ֣תּוּ הֵ֔מָּה וְאַל־אֵחַ֖תָּה אָ֑נִי הָבִ֤יא עֲלֵיהֶם֙ י֣וֹם רָעָ֔ה וּמִשְׁנֶ֥ה שִׁבָּר֖וֹן שׇׁבְרֵֽם׃{ס}


Let them be confounded that persecute me, but let me not be confounded: let them be dismayed, but let me not be dismayed: bring upon them the day of evil, and destroy them with double destruction.

כֹּה־אָמַ֨ר יְהֹוָ֜ה אֵלַ֗י הָלֹ֤ךְ וְעָֽמַדְתָּ֙ בְּשַׁ֣עַר בְּנֵֽי־[הָעָ֔ם] (עם) אֲשֶׁ֨ר יָבֹ֤אוּ בוֹ֙ מַלְכֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֔ה וַאֲשֶׁ֖ר יֵ֣צְאוּ ב֑וֹ וּבְכֹ֖ל שַׁעֲרֵ֥י יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


Thus said the Lord to me; Go and stand in the gate of the children of the people, by which the kings of Yehuda come in, and by which they go out, and in all the gates of Yerushalayim:


###### Jeremiah 17:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/17:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/17:20)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֲ֠לֵיהֶ֠ם שִׁמְע֨וּ דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֜ה מַלְכֵ֤י יְהוּדָה֙ וְכׇל־יְהוּדָ֔ה וְכֹ֖ל יֹשְׁבֵ֣י יְרוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם הַבָּאִ֖ים בַּשְּׁעָרִ֥ים הָאֵֽלֶּה׃


and say to them, Hear the word of the Lord, you kings of Yehuda, and all Yehuda, and all the inhabitants of Yerushalayim, that enter in by these gates:


###### Jeremiah 17:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/17:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/17:21)


כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה הִשָּׁמְר֖וּ בְּנַפְשׁוֹתֵיכֶ֑ם וְאַל־תִּשְׂא֤וּ מַשָּׂא֙ בְּי֣וֹם הַשַּׁבָּ֔ת וַהֲבֵאתֶ֖ם בְּשַׁעֲרֵ֥י יְרֽוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


thus says the Lord; Take heed to yourselves, and bear no burden on the sabbath day, nor bring it in by the gates of Yerushalayim;


###### Jeremiah 17:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/17:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/17:22)


וְלֹא־תוֹצִ֨יאוּ מַשָּׂ֤א מִבָּֽתֵּיכֶם֙ בְּי֣וֹם הַשַּׁבָּ֔ת וְכׇל־מְלָאכָ֖ה לֹ֣א תַעֲשׂ֑וּ וְקִדַּשְׁתֶּם֙ אֶת־י֣וֹם הַשַּׁבָּ֔ת כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר צִוִּ֖יתִי אֶת־אֲבוֹתֵיכֶֽם׃


neither carry forth a burden out of your houses on the sabbath day, neither do any work, but hallow the sabbath day, as I commanded your fathers.


###### Jeremiah 17:23
