וְלֹ֣א שָׁמְע֔וּ וְלֹ֥א הִטּ֖וּ אֶת־אׇזְנָ֑ם וַיַּקְשׁוּ֙ אֶת־עׇרְפָּ֔ם לְבִלְתִּ֣י (שומע) [שְׁמ֔וֹעַ] וּלְבִלְתִּ֖י קַ֥חַת מוּסָֽר׃


But they obeyed not, neither inclined their ear, but made their neck stiff, that they might not hear, nor receive instruction.


###### Jeremiah 17:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/17:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/17:24)


וְ֠הָיָ֠ה אִם־שָׁמֹ֨עַ תִּשְׁמְע֤וּן אֵלַי֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה לְבִלְתִּ֣י**׀** הָבִ֣יא מַשָּׂ֗א בְּשַׁעֲרֵ֛י הָעִ֥יר הַזֹּ֖את בְּי֣וֹם הַשַּׁבָּ֑ת וּלְקַדֵּשׁ֙ אֶת־י֣וֹם הַשַּׁבָּ֔ת לְבִלְתִּ֥י עֲשֽׂוֹת־בֹּ֖ה כׇּל־מְלָאכָֽה׃


And it shall come to pass, if you diligently hearken to me, says the Lord, to bring in no burden through the gates of this city on the sabbath day, but hallow the sabbath day, to do no work on it;


###### Jeremiah 17:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/17:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/17:25)


וּבָ֣אוּ בְשַׁעֲרֵ֣י הָעִ֣יר הַזֹּ֡את מְלָכִ֣ים׀וְשָׂרִ֡ים יֹשְׁבִים֩ עַל־כִּסֵּ֨א דָוִ֜ד רֹכְבִ֣ים**׀** בָּרֶ֣כֶב וּבַסּוּסִ֗ים הֵ֚מָּה וְשָׂ֣רֵיהֶ֔ם אִ֥ישׁ יְהוּדָ֖ה וְיֹשְׁבֵ֣י יְרוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְיָשְׁבָ֥ה הָעִיר־הַזֹּ֖את לְעוֹלָֽם׃


then shall there enter into the gates of this city kings and princes who sit upon the throne of David, riding in chariots and on horses, they, and their princes, the men of Yehuda, and the inhabitants of Yerushalayim: and this city shall remain for ever.


###### Jeremiah 17:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/17:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/17:26)


וּבָ֣אוּ מֵעָרֵֽי־יְ֠הוּדָ֠ה וּמִסְּבִיב֨וֹת יְרוּשָׁלַ֜͏ִם וּמֵאֶ֣רֶץ בִּנְיָמִ֗ן וּמִן־הַשְּׁפֵלָ֤ה וּמִן־הָהָר֙ וּמִן־הַנֶּ֔גֶב מְבִאִ֛ים עוֹלָ֥ה וְזֶ֖בַח וּמִנְחָ֣ה וּלְבוֹנָ֑ה וּמְבִאֵ֥י תוֹדָ֖ה בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


And they shall come from the cities of Yehuda, and from the places about Yerushalayim, and from the land of Binyamin, and from the coastal plain, and from the mountains, and from the Negev, bringing burnt offerings, and sacrifices, and meal offerings, and incense, and bringing sacrifices of praise, to the house of the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 17:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/17:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/17:27)


וְאִם־לֹ֨א תִשְׁמְע֜וּ אֵלַ֗י לְקַדֵּשׁ֙ אֶת־י֣וֹם הַשַּׁבָּ֔ת וּלְבִלְתִּ֣י**׀** שְׂאֵ֣ת מַשָּׂ֗א וּבֹ֛א בְּשַׁעֲרֵ֥י יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם בְּי֣וֹם הַשַּׁבָּ֑ת וְהִצַּ֧תִּי אֵ֣שׁ בִּשְׁעָרֶ֗יהָ וְאָ֥כְלָ֛ה אַרְמְנ֥וֹת יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם וְלֹ֥א תִכְבֶּֽה׃{פ}


But if you will not hearken to me to hallow the sabbath day, and not to bear a burden, and if you enter in at the gates of Yerushalayim on the sabbath day; then will I kindle a fire in its gates, and it shall devour the palaces of Yerushalayim, and it shall not be quenched.



## Chapter 18

###### Jeremiah 18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:1)


הַדָּבָר֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הָיָ֣ה אֶֽל־יִרְמְיָ֔הוּ מֵאֵ֥ת יְהֹוָ֖ה לֵאמֹֽר׃


The word which came to Yirmeyahu from the Lord, saying,


###### Jeremiah 18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:2)


ק֥וּם וְיָרַדְתָּ֖ בֵּ֣ית הַיּוֹצֵ֑ר וְשָׁ֖מָּה אַשְׁמִיעֲךָ֥ אֶת־דְּבָרָֽי׃


Arise, and go down to the potter’s house, and there I will cause thee to hear my words.


###### Jeremiah 18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:3)


וָאֵרֵ֖ד בֵּ֣ית הַיּוֹצֵ֑ר (והנהו) [וְהִנֵּה־ה֛וּא] עֹשֶׂ֥ה מְלָאכָ֖ה עַל־הָאׇבְנָֽיִם׃


Then I went down to the potter’s house, and, behold, he was at work on the wheels.


###### Jeremiah 18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:4)


וְנִשְׁחַ֣ת הַכְּלִ֗י אֲשֶׁ֨ר ה֥וּא עֹשֶׂ֛ה בַּחֹ֖מֶר בְּיַ֣ד הַיּוֹצֵ֑ר וְשָׁ֗ב וַֽיַּעֲשֵׂ֙הוּ֙ כְּלִ֣י אַחֵ֔ר כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר יָשַׁ֛ר בְּעֵינֵ֥י הַיּוֹצֵ֖ר לַעֲשֽׂוֹת׃{ס}


And the vessel that he was making of clay was spoiled in the hand of the potter: so he made it again into another vessel, as it seemed good to the potter to make it.


###### Jeremiah 18:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:5)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֽוֹר׃


Then the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Jeremiah 18:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:6)


הֲכַיּוֹצֵ֨ר הַזֶּ֜ה לֹא־אוּכַ֨ל לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת לָכֶ֛ם בֵּ֥ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה הִנֵּ֤ה כַחֹ֙מֶר֙ בְּיַ֣ד הַיּוֹצֵ֔ר כֵּן־אַתֶּ֥ם בְּיָדִ֖י בֵּ֥ית יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃{ס}


O house of Yisra᾽el, cannot I do with you as this potter? says the Lord. Behold, as the clay is in the potter’s hand, so are you in my hand, O house of Yisra᾽el.


###### Jeremiah 18:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:7)


רֶ֣גַע אֲדַבֵּ֔ר עַל־גּ֖וֹי וְעַל־מַמְלָכָ֑ה לִנְת֥וֹשׁ וְלִנְת֖וֹץ וּֽלְהַאֲבִֽיד׃


At one time I speak concerning a nation, and concerning a kingdom, to pluck up, and to pull down, or to destroy;


###### Jeremiah 18:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:8)


וְשָׁב֙ הַגּ֣וֹי הַה֔וּא מֵרָ֣עָת֔וֹ אֲשֶׁ֥ר דִּבַּ֖רְתִּי עָלָ֑יו וְנִֽחַמְתִּי֙ עַל־הָ֣רָעָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר חָשַׁ֖בְתִּי לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת לֽוֹ׃{ס}


but if that nation, against whom I have pronounced, turn from their evil, I repent of the evil that I thought to do to them.


###### Jeremiah 18:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:9)


וְרֶ֣גַע אֲדַבֵּ֔ר עַל־גּ֖וֹי וְעַל־מַמְלָכָ֑ה לִבְנ֖וֹת וְלִנְטֽוֹעַ׃


And at another time, I speak concerning a nation, and concerning a kingdom, to build and to plant;


###### Jeremiah 18:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:10)


וְעָשָׂ֤ה (הרעה) [הָרַע֙] בְּעֵינַ֔י לְבִלְתִּ֖י שְׁמֹ֣עַ בְּקוֹלִ֑י וְנִֽחַמְתִּי֙ עַל־הַטּוֹבָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר אָמַ֖רְתִּי לְהֵיטִ֥יב אוֹתֽוֹ׃{ס}


but if it do evil in my sight, that it obey not my voice, then I repent of the good, with which I said I would benefit them.


###### Jeremiah 18:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:11)


וְעַתָּ֡ה אֱמׇר־נָ֣א אֶל־אִישׁ־יְהוּדָה֩ וְעַל־יוֹשְׁבֵ֨י יְרוּשָׁלַ֜͏ִם לֵאמֹ֗ר כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה הִנֵּ֨ה אָנֹכִ֜י יוֹצֵ֤ר עֲלֵיכֶם֙ רָעָ֔ה וְחֹשֵׁ֥ב עֲלֵיכֶ֖ם מַחֲשָׁבָ֑ה שׁ֣וּבוּ נָ֗א אִ֚ישׁ מִדַּרְכּ֣וֹ הָרָעָ֔ה וְהֵיטִ֥יבוּ דַרְכֵיכֶ֖ם וּמַעַלְלֵיכֶֽם׃


Now therefore speak to the men of Yehuda, and to the inhabitants of Yerushalayim, saying, Thus says the Lord; Behold, I frame evil against you, and devise a device against you: return now every one from his evil way, and amend your ways and your doings.


###### Jeremiah 18:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:12)


וְאָמְר֖וּ נוֹאָ֑שׁ כִּֽי־אַחֲרֵ֤י מַחְשְׁבוֹתֵ֙ינוּ֙ נֵלֵ֔ךְ וְאִ֛ישׁ שְׁרִר֥וּת לִבּֽוֹ־הָרָ֖ע נַעֲשֶֽׂה׃{פ}


And they said, There is no hope: but we will walk after our own devices, and we will every one do after the stubbornness of his evil heart.


###### Jeremiah 18:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:13)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה שַֽׁאֲלוּ־נָא֙ בַּגּוֹיִ֔ם מִ֥י שָׁמַ֖ע כָּאֵ֑לֶּה שַֽׁעֲרֻרִת֙ עָשְׂתָ֣ה מְאֹ֔ד בְּתוּלַ֖ת יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


Therefore thus says the Lord; Ask now among the heathen, who has heard such things: the virgin of Yisra᾽el has done a very horrible thing.


###### Jeremiah 18:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:14)


הֲיַעֲזֹ֥ב מִצּ֛וּר שָׂדַ֖י שֶׁ֣לֶג לְבָנ֑וֹן אִם־יִנָּֽתְשׁ֗וּ מַ֛יִם זָרִ֥ים קָרִ֖ים נוֹזְלִֽים׃


Does the snow of Levanon fail from the crags of the fields? or do the far off cold and flowing waters cease to run?


###### Jeremiah 18:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:15)


כִּֽי־שְׁכֵחֻ֥נִי עַמִּ֖י לַשָּׁ֣וְא יְקַטֵּ֑רוּ וַיַּכְשִׁל֤וּם בְּדַרְכֵיהֶם֙ שְׁבִילֵ֣י עוֹלָ֔ם לָלֶ֣כֶת נְתִיב֔וֹת דֶּ֖רֶךְ לֹ֥א סְלוּלָֽה׃


but my people have forgotten me; they burn incense to vain gods, and they stumble in their ways from the ancient paths, to walk in bypaths, in a way not levelled,


###### Jeremiah 18:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:16)


לָשׂ֥וּם אַרְצָ֛ם לְשַׁמָּ֖ה (שרוקת) [שְׁרִיק֣וֹת] עוֹלָ֑ם כֹּ֚ל עוֹבֵ֣ר עָלֶ֔יהָ יִשֹּׁ֖ם וְיָנִ֥יד בְּרֹאשֽׁוֹ׃


to make their land an astonishment, a perpetual hissing; everyone that passes by it shall be astonished, and shake his head.


###### Jeremiah 18:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:17)


כְּרוּחַ־קָדִ֥ים אֲפִיצֵ֖ם לִפְנֵ֣י אוֹיֵ֑ב עֹ֧רֶף וְלֹֽא־פָנִ֛ים אֶרְאֵ֖ם בְּי֥וֹם אֵידָֽם׃{ס}


I will scatter them as with an east wind before the enemy; I will show them my back, and not my face, in the day of their calamity.


###### Jeremiah 18:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:18)


וַיֹּאמְר֗וּ לְכ֨וּ וְנַחְשְׁבָ֥ה עַֽל־יִרְמְיָ֘הוּ֮ מַחֲשָׁבוֹת֒ כִּי֩ לֹא־תֹאבַ֨ד תּוֹרָ֜ה מִכֹּהֵ֗ן וְעֵצָה֙ מֵחָכָ֔ם וְדָבָ֖ר מִנָּבִ֑יא לְכוּ֙ וְנַכֵּ֣הוּ בַלָּשׁ֔וֹן וְאַל־נַקְשִׁ֖יבָה אֶל־כׇּל־דְּבָרָֽיו׃


Then said they, Come and let us contrive devices against Yirmeyahu; for Tora shall not perish from the priest, nor counsel from the wise, nor the word from the prophet. Come, and let us smite him with the tongue, and let us not give heed to any of his words.


###### Jeremiah 18:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:19)


הַקְשִׁ֥יבָה יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלָ֑י וּשְׁמַ֖ע לְק֥וֹל יְרִיבָֽי׃


Give heed to me, O Lord, and hearken to the voice of them that contend with me.


###### Jeremiah 18:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:20)


הַיְשֻׁלַּ֤ם תַּֽחַת־טוֹבָה֙ רָעָ֔ה כִּי־כָר֥וּ שׁוּחָ֖ה לְנַפְשִׁ֑י זְכֹ֣ר**׀** עׇמְדִ֣י לְפָנֶ֗יךָ לְדַבֵּ֤ר עֲלֵיהֶם֙ טוֹבָ֔ה לְהָשִׁ֥יב אֶת־חֲמָתְךָ֖ מֵהֶֽם׃


Shall evil be recompensed for good? for they have dug a pit for my soul. Remember that I stood before Thee to speak good for them, and to turn away Thy wrath from them.


###### Jeremiah 18:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:21)


לָכֵן֩ תֵּ֨ן אֶת־בְּנֵיהֶ֜ם לָרָעָ֗ב וְהַגִּרֵם֮ עַל־יְדֵי־חֶ֒רֶב֒ וְתִהְיֶ֨נָה נְשֵׁיהֶ֤ם שַׁכֻּלוֹת֙ וְאַלְמָנ֔וֹת וְאַ֨נְשֵׁיהֶ֔ם יִהְי֖וּ הֲרֻ֣גֵי מָ֑וֶת בַּח֣וּרֵיהֶ֔ם מֻכֵּי־חֶ֖רֶב בַּמִּלְחָמָֽה׃


Therefore deliver up their children to the famine, and pour out their blood by the sword; and let their wives be bereaved of their children, and be widows; and let their men be put to death; let their young men be slain by the sword in battle.


###### Jeremiah 18:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:22)


תִּשָּׁמַ֤ע זְעָקָה֙ מִבָּ֣תֵּיהֶ֔ם כִּֽי־תָבִ֧יא עֲלֵיהֶ֛ם גְּד֖וּד פִּתְאֹ֑ם כִּֽי־כָר֤וּ (שיחה) [שׁוּחָה֙] לְלׇכְדֵ֔נִי וּפַחִ֖ים טָמְנ֥וּ לְרַגְלָֽי׃


Let a cry be heard from their houses, when thou shalt bring a troop suddenly upon them: for they have dug a pit to take me, and hid snares for my feet.


###### Jeremiah 18:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/18:23)


וְאַתָּ֣ה יְ֠הֹוָ֠ה יָדַ֜עְתָּ אֶֽת־כׇּל־עֲצָתָ֤ם עָלַי֙ לַמָּ֔וֶת אַל־תְּכַפֵּר֙ עַל־עֲוֺנָ֔ם וְחַטָּאתָ֖ם מִלְּפָנֶ֣יךָ אַל־תֶּ֑מְחִי (והיו) [וְיִהְי֤וּ] מֻכְשָׁלִים֙ לְפָנֶ֔יךָ בְּעֵ֥ת אַפְּךָ֖ עֲשֵׂ֥ה בָהֶֽם׃{ס}


Yet, Lord, Thou knowst all their counsel against me to slay me: forgive not their iniquity, neither blot out their sin from Thy sight, but let them be overthrown before Thee; deal thus with them in the time of Thy anger.



## Chapter 19

###### Jeremiah 19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:1)


כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה הָלֹ֛ךְ וְקָנִ֥יתָ בַקְבֻּ֖ק יוֹצֵ֣ר חָ֑רֶשׂ וּמִזִּקְנֵ֣י הָעָ֔ם וּמִזִּקְנֵ֖י הַכֹּהֲנִֽים׃


Thus says the Lord, Go and get a potter’s earthen bottle, and take of the elders of the people, and of the elders of the priests;


###### Jeremiah 19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:2)


וְיָצָ֙אתָ֙ אֶל־גֵּ֣יא בֶן־הִנֹּ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֕ר פֶּ֖תַח שַׁ֣עַר (החרסות) [הַחַרְסִ֑ית] וְקָרָ֣אתָ שָּׁ֔ם אֶת־הַדְּבָרִ֖ים אֲשֶׁר־אֲדַבֵּ֥ר אֵלֶֽיךָ׃


and go out into the valley of Ben-hinnom, which is by the entry of the gate of Ḥarsit, and proclaim there the words that I shall tell thee,


###### Jeremiah 19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:3)


וְאָֽמַרְתָּ֙ שִׁמְע֣וּ דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֔ה מַלְכֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֔ה וְיֹשְׁבֵ֖י יְרוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם כֹּֽה־אָמַר֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה צְבָא֜וֹת אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל הִנְנִ֨י מֵבִ֤יא רָעָה֙ עַל־הַמָּק֣וֹם הַזֶּ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר כׇּל־שֹׁמְעָ֖הּ תִּצַּ֥לְנָה אׇזְנָֽיו׃


And say, Hear the word of the Lord, O kings of Yehuda, and inhabitants of Yerushalayim; Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Yisra᾽el; Behold, I will bring evil upon this place, which whoever hears it, his ears shall tingle.


###### Jeremiah 19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:4)


יַ֣עַן**׀** אֲשֶׁ֣ר עֲזָבֻ֗נִי וַֽיְנַכְּר֞וּ אֶת־הַמָּק֤וֹם הַזֶּה֙ וַיְקַטְּרוּ־בוֹ֙ לֵאלֹהִ֣ים אֲחֵרִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֧ר לֹא־יְדָע֛וּם הֵ֥מָּה וַאֲבוֹתֵיהֶ֖ם וּמַלְכֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֑ה וּמָ֥לְא֛וּ אֶת־הַמָּק֥וֹם הַזֶּ֖ה דַּ֥ם נְקִיִּֽם׃


Because they have forsaken me, and have estranged this place, and have burned incense in it to other gods, whom neither they nor their fathers have known, nor the kings of Yehuda, and have filled this place with the blood of innocents;


###### Jeremiah 19:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:5)


וּבָנ֞וּ אֶת־בָּמ֣וֹת הַבַּ֗עַל לִשְׂרֹ֧ף אֶת־בְּנֵיהֶ֛ם בָּאֵ֖שׁ עֹל֣וֹת לַבָּ֑עַל אֲשֶׁ֤ר לֹֽא־צִוִּ֙יתִי֙ וְלֹ֣א דִבַּ֔רְתִּי וְלֹ֥א עָלְתָ֖ה עַל־לִבִּֽי׃{פ}


they have built also the high places of the Ba῾al, to burn their sons with fire for burnt offerings to the Ba῾al, which I commanded not, nor spoke it, neither came it into my mind:


###### Jeremiah 19:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:6)


לָכֵ֞ן הִנֵּֽה־יָמִ֤ים בָּאִים֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה וְלֹא־יִקָּרֵא֩ לַמָּק֨וֹם הַזֶּ֥ה ע֛וֹד הַתֹּ֖פֶת וְגֵ֣יא בֶן־הִנֹּ֑ם כִּ֖י אִם־גֵּ֥יא הַהֲרֵגָֽה׃


therefore, behold, days are coming, says the Lord, when this place shall no more be called Tofet, nor The valley of Ben-hinnom, but The valley of Slaughter.


###### Jeremiah 19:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:7)


וּ֠בַקֹּתִ֠י אֶת־עֲצַ֨ת יְהוּדָ֤ה וִירוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ בַּמָּק֣וֹם הַזֶּ֔ה וְהִפַּלְתִּ֤ים בַּחֶ֙רֶב֙ לִפְנֵ֣י אֹֽיְבֵיהֶ֔ם וּבְיַ֖ד מְבַקְשֵׁ֣י נַפְשָׁ֑ם וְנָתַתִּ֤י אֶת־נִבְלָתָם֙ לְמַֽאֲכָ֔ל לְע֥וֹף הַשָּׁמַ֖יִם וּלְבֶהֱמַ֥ת הָאָֽרֶץ׃


And I will make void the counsel of Yehuda and Yerushalayim in this place; and I will cause them to fall by the sword before their enemies, and by the hands of them that seek their lives: and their carcasses will I give to be food for the birds of the sky, and for the beasts of the earth.


###### Jeremiah 19:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:8)


וְשַׂמְתִּי֙ אֶת־הָעִ֣יר הַזֹּ֔את לְשַׁמָּ֖ה וְלִשְׁרֵקָ֑ה כֹּ֚ל עֹבֵ֣ר עָלֶ֔יהָ יִשֹּׁ֥ם וְיִשְׁרֹ֖ק עַל־כׇּל־מַכֹּתֶֽהָ׃


And I will make this city desolate, and a hissing; every one that passes by it shall be astonished and hiss because of all its disasters.


###### Jeremiah 19:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:9)


וְהַאֲכַלְתִּ֞ים אֶת־בְּשַׂ֣ר בְּנֵיהֶ֗ם וְאֵת֙ בְּשַׂ֣ר בְּנֹתֵיהֶ֔ם וְאִ֥ישׁ בְּשַׂר־רֵעֵ֖הוּ יֹאכֵ֑לוּ בְּמָצוֹר֙ וּבְמָצ֔וֹק אֲשֶׁ֨ר יָצִ֧יקוּ לָהֶ֛ם אֹיְבֵיהֶ֖ם וּמְבַקְשֵׁ֥י נַפְשָֽׁם׃


And I will cause them to eat the flesh of their sons and the flesh of their daughters, and they shall eat every one the flesh of his friend, in the siege and in the distress, with which their enemies, and they that seek their lives, shall afflict them.


###### Jeremiah 19:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:10)


וְשָׁבַרְתָּ֖ הַבַּקְבֻּ֑ק לְעֵינֵי֙ הָאֲנָשִׁ֔ים הַהֹלְכִ֖ים אוֹתָֽךְ׃


Then shalt thou break the bottle in the sight of the men that go with thee,


###### Jeremiah 19:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:11)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֨ אֲלֵיהֶ֜ם כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר**׀** יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֗וֹת כָּ֣כָה אֶשְׁבֹּ֞ר אֶת־הָעָ֤ם הַזֶּה֙ וְאֶת־הָעִ֣יר הַזֹּ֔את כַּאֲשֶׁ֤ר יִשְׁבֹּר֙ אֶת־כְּלִ֣י הַיּוֹצֵ֔ר אֲשֶׁ֛ר לֹא־יוּכַ֥ל לְהֵרָפֵ֖ה ע֑וֹד וּבְתֹ֣פֶת יִקְבְּר֔וּ מֵאֵ֥ין מָק֖וֹם לִקְבּֽוֹר׃


And shalt say to them, Thus says the Lord of hosts; Even so will I break this people and this city, as one breaks a potter’s vessel, that cannot be made whole again: and they shall bury them in Tofet, till there be no place to bury.


###### Jeremiah 19:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:12)


כֵּֽן־אֶעֱשֶׂ֞ה לַמָּק֥וֹם הַזֶּ֛ה נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֖ה וּלְיֽוֹשְׁבָ֑יו וְלָתֵ֛ת אֶת־הָעִ֥יר הַזֹּ֖את כְּתֹֽפֶת׃


Thus will I do to this place, says the Lord, and to its inhabitants, and make this city as Tofet:


###### Jeremiah 19:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:13)


וְהָי֞וּ בָּתֵּ֣י יְרוּשָׁלַ֗͏ִם וּבָתֵּי֙ מַלְכֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֔ה כִּמְק֥וֹם הַתֹּ֖פֶת הַטְּמֵאִ֑ים לְכֹ֣ל הַבָּתִּ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֨ר קִטְּר֜וּ עַל־גַּגֹּֽתֵיהֶם֙ לְכֹל֙ צְבָ֣א הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם וְהַסֵּ֥ךְ נְסָכִ֖ים לֵאלֹהִ֥ים אֲחֵרִֽים׃{פ}


and the houses of Yerushalayim, and the houses of the kings of Yehuda, shall be defiled as the place of the Tofet, with all the houses upon the roofs of which they have burned incense to all the host of heaven, and have poured out drink offerings to other gods.


###### Jeremiah 19:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:14)


וַיָּבֹ֤א יִרְמְיָ֙הוּ֙ מֵֽהַתֹּ֔פֶת אֲשֶׁ֨ר שְׁלָח֧וֹ יְהֹוָ֛ה שָׁ֖ם לְהִנָּבֵ֑א וַֽיַּעֲמֹד֙ בַּחֲצַ֣ר בֵּית־יְהֹוָ֔ה וַיֹּ֖אמֶר אֶל־כׇּל־הָעָֽם׃{ס}


Then came Yirmeyahu from the Tofet, to which the Lord had sent him to prophesy; and he stood in the court of the Lord’s house; and said to all the people,


###### Jeremiah 19:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/19:15)


כֹּה־אָמַ֞ר יְהֹוָ֤ה צְבָאוֹת֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל הִנְנִ֨י מֵבִ֜י אֶל־הָעִ֤יר הַזֹּאת֙ וְעַל־כׇּל־עָרֶ֔יהָ אֵ֚ת כׇּל־הָ֣רָעָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר דִּבַּ֖רְתִּי עָלֶ֑יהָ כִּ֤י הִקְשׁוּ֙ אֶת־עׇרְפָּ֔ם לְבִלְתִּ֖י שְׁמ֥וֹעַ אֶת־דְּבָרָֽי׃


Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Yisra᾽el; Behold, I will bring upon this city and upon all her towns all the evil that I have pronounced against it, because they have made their necks stiff, that they might not hear my words.



## Chapter 20

###### Jeremiah 20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:1)


וַיִּשְׁמַ֤ע פַּשְׁחוּר֙ בֶּן־אִמֵּ֣ר הַכֹּהֵ֔ן וְהוּא־פָקִ֥יד נָגִ֖יד בְּבֵ֣ית יְהֹוָ֑ה אֶֽת־יִרְמְיָ֔הוּ נִבָּ֖א אֶת־הַדְּבָרִ֥ים הָאֵֽלֶּה׃


Now Pashĥur the son of Immer the priest, who was also chief governor in the house of the Lord, heard that Yirmeyahu prophesied these things.


###### Jeremiah 20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:2)


וַיַּכֶּ֣ה פַשְׁח֔וּר אֵ֖ת יִרְמְיָ֣הוּ הַנָּבִ֑יא וַיִּתֵּ֨ן אֹת֜וֹ עַל־הַמַּהְפֶּ֗כֶת אֲשֶׁ֨ר בְּשַׁ֤עַר בִּנְיָמִן֙ הָֽעֶלְי֔וֹן אֲשֶׁ֖ר בְּבֵ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


Then Pashĥur smote Yirmeyahu the prophet, and put him in the stocks that were in the high gate of Binyamin, which was by the house of the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:3)


וַֽיְהִי֙ מִֽמׇּחֳרָ֔ת וַיֹּצֵ֥א פַשְׁח֛וּר אֶֽת־יִרְמְיָ֖הוּ מִן־הַמַּהְפָּ֑כֶת וַיֹּ֨אמֶר אֵלָ֜יו יִרְמְיָ֗הוּ לֹ֤א פַשְׁחוּר֙ קָרָ֤א יְהֹוָה֙ שְׁמֶ֔ךָ כִּ֖י אִם־מָג֥וֹר מִסָּבִֽיב׃{ס}


And it came to pass on the morrow, that Pashĥur brought Yirmeyahu out of the stocks. Then Yirmeyahu said to him, The Lord has called thy name not Pashĥur, but Magor-missaviv (Terror all Around.)


###### Jeremiah 20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:4)


כִּ֣י כֹ֣ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֡ה הִנְנִי֩ נֹתֶנְךָ֨ לְמָג֜וֹר לְךָ֣ וּלְכׇל־אֹהֲבֶ֗יךָ וְנָ֥פְל֛וּ בְּחֶ֥רֶב אֹיְבֵיהֶ֖ם וְעֵינֶ֣יךָ רֹא֑וֹת וְאֶת־כׇּל־יְהוּדָ֗ה אֶתֵּן֙ בְּיַ֣ד מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֔ל וְהִגְלָ֥ם בָּבֶ֖לָה וְהִכָּ֥ם בֶּחָֽרֶב׃


For thus says the Lord, Behold, I will make thee a terror to thyself, and to all thy friends: and they shall fall by the sword of their enemies, and thy eyes shall behold it: and I will give all Yehuda into the hand of the king of Bavel, and he shall carry them captive into Bavel, and shall slay them with the sword.


###### Jeremiah 20:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:5)


וְנָתַתִּ֗י אֶת־כׇּל־חֹ֙סֶן֙ הָעִ֣יר הַזֹּ֔את וְאֶת־כׇּל־יְגִיעָ֖הּ וְאֶת־כׇּל־יְקָרָ֑הּ וְאֵ֨ת כׇּל־אוֹצְר֜וֹת מַלְכֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֗ה אֶתֵּן֙ בְּיַ֣ד אֹֽיְבֵיהֶ֔ם וּבְזָזוּם֙ וּלְקָח֔וּם וֶהֱבִיא֖וּם בָּבֶֽלָה׃


Moreover I will deliver all the wealth of this city, and all its gains, and all its precious things, and all the treasures of the kings of Yehuda will I give into the hand of their enemies, who shall spoil them, and take them, and carry them to Bavel.


###### Jeremiah 20:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:6)


וְאַתָּ֣ה פַשְׁח֗וּר וְכֹל֙ יֹשְׁבֵ֣י בֵיתֶ֔ךָ תֵּלְכ֖וּ בַּשֶּׁ֑בִי וּבָבֶ֣ל תָּב֗וֹא וְשָׁ֤ם תָּמוּת֙ וְשָׁ֣ם תִּקָּבֵ֔ר אַתָּה֙ וְכׇל־אֹֽהֲבֶ֔יךָ אֲשֶׁר־נִבֵּ֥אתָ לָהֶ֖ם בַּשָּֽׁקֶר׃{פ}


And thou, Pashĥur, and all that dwell in thy house shall go into captivity: and thou shalt come to Bavel, and there thou shalt die, and shalt be buried there, thou, and all thy friends, to whom thou hast prophesied lies.


###### Jeremiah 20:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:7)


פִּתִּיתַ֤נִי יְהֹוָה֙ וָאֶפָּ֔ת חֲזַקְתַּ֖נִי וַתּוּכָ֑ל הָיִ֤יתִי לִשְׂחוֹק֙ כׇּל־הַיּ֔וֹם כֻּלֹּ֖ה לֹעֵ֥ג לִֽי׃


O Lord, Thou didst persuade me, and I was persuaded: Thou art stronger than I, and hast prevailed: I am in derision daily, every one mocks me.


###### Jeremiah 20:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:8)


כִּֽי־מִדֵּ֤י אֲדַבֵּר֙ אֶזְעָ֔ק חָמָ֥ס וָשֹׁ֖ד אֶקְרָ֑א כִּֽי־הָיָ֨ה דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֥ה לִ֛י לְחֶרְפָּ֥ה וּלְקֶ֖לֶס כׇּל־הַיּֽוֹם׃


For whenever I speak, I cry out aloud, I shout of violence and ruin; therefore the word of the Lord is made a reproach unto me, and a derision all the day.


###### Jeremiah 20:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:9)


וְאָמַרְתִּ֣י לֹֽא־אֶזְכְּרֶ֗נּוּ וְלֹֽא־אֲדַבֵּ֥ר עוֹד֙ בִּשְׁמ֔וֹ וְהָיָ֤ה בְלִבִּי֙ כְּאֵ֣שׁ בֹּעֶ֔רֶת עָצֻ֖ר בְּעַצְמֹתָ֑י וְנִלְאֵ֥יתִי כַּֽלְכֵ֖ל וְלֹ֥א אוּכָֽל׃


Then I said, I will not make mention of him, nor speak any more in his name. But his word was in my heart like a burning fire shut up in my bones, and I am weary with containing myself, and I cannot.


###### Jeremiah 20:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:10)


כִּ֣י שָׁמַ֜עְתִּי דִּבַּ֣ת רַבִּים֮ מָג֣וֹר מִסָּבִיב֒ הַגִּ֙ידוּ֙ וְנַגִּידֶ֔נּוּ כֹּ֚ל אֱנ֣וֹשׁ שְׁלֹמִ֔י שֹׁמְרֵ֖י צַלְעִ֑י אוּלַ֤י יְפֻתֶּה֙ וְנ֣וּכְלָה ל֔וֹ וְנִקְחָ֥ה נִקְמָתֵ֖נוּ מִמֶּֽנּוּ׃


Though I heard the calumny of many, the terror all around. Denounce, and we will denounce him, say my familiar friends as they watch for my stumbling, Perhaps he will be persuaded, and we shall prevail against him, and we shall take our revenge on him.


###### Jeremiah 20:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:11)


וַיהֹוָ֤ה אוֹתִי֙ כְּגִבּ֣וֹר עָרִ֔יץ עַל־כֵּ֛ן רֹדְפַ֥י יִכָּשְׁל֖וּ וְלֹ֣א יֻכָ֑לוּ בֹּ֤שֽׁוּ מְאֹד֙ כִּי־לֹ֣א הִשְׂכִּ֔ילוּ כְּלִמַּ֥ת עוֹלָ֖ם לֹ֥א תִשָּׁכֵֽחַ׃


But the Lord is with me as a mighty terrible one: therefore my persecutors shall stumble, and they shall not prevail: they shall be greatly ashamed; for they shall not prosper: their everlasting confusion shall never be forgotten.


###### Jeremiah 20:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:12)


וַיהֹוָ֤ה צְבָאוֹת֙ בֹּחֵ֣ן צַדִּ֔יק רֹאֶ֥ה כְלָי֖וֹת וָלֵ֑ב אֶרְאֶ֤ה נִקְמָֽתְךָ֙ מֵהֶ֔ם כִּ֥י אֵלֶ֖יךָ גִּלִּ֥יתִי אֶת־רִיבִֽי׃{ס}


But, O Lord of hosts, that triest the righteous, and seest the inward parts and the heart, let me see Thy vengeance on them: for to Thee have I opened my cause.


###### Jeremiah 20:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:13)


שִׁ֚ירוּ לַֽיהֹוָ֔ה הַֽלְל֖וּ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֑ה כִּ֥י הִצִּ֛יל אֶת־נֶ֥פֶשׁ אֶבְי֖וֹן מִיַּ֥ד מְרֵעִֽים׃{ס}


Sing to the Lord, praise the Lord: for he has delivered the soul of the needy from the hand of evildoers.


###### Jeremiah 20:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:14)


אָר֣וּר הַיּ֔וֹם אֲשֶׁ֥ר יֻלַּ֖דְתִּי בּ֑וֹ י֛וֹם אֲשֶׁר־יְלָדַ֥תְנִי אִמִּ֖י אַל־יְהִ֥י בָרֽוּךְ׃


Cursed be the day on which I was born: let not the day on which my mother bore me be blessed.


###### Jeremiah 20:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:15)


אָר֣וּר הָאִ֗ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֨ר בִּשַּׂ֤ר אֶת־אָבִי֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר יֻֽלַּד־לְךָ֖ בֵּ֣ן זָכָ֑ר שַׂמֵּ֖חַ שִׂמְּחָֽהוּ׃


Cursed be the man who brought tidings to my father, saying, A man child is born to thee; making him very glad.


###### Jeremiah 20:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:16)


וְהָיָה֙ הָאִ֣ישׁ הַה֔וּא כֶּעָרִ֛ים אֲשֶׁר־הָפַ֥ךְ יְהֹוָ֖ה וְלֹ֣א נִחָ֑ם וְשָׁמַ֤ע זְעָקָה֙ בַּבֹּ֔קֶר וּתְרוּעָ֖ה בְּעֵ֥ת צׇהֳרָֽיִם׃


And let that man be as the cities which the Lord overthrew, and repented not: and let him hear a cry in the morning, and an alarm at noontide;


###### Jeremiah 20:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:17)


אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־מוֹתְתַ֖נִי מֵרָ֑חֶם וַתְּהִי־לִ֤י אִמִּי֙ קִבְרִ֔י וְרַחְמָ֖הֿ הֲרַ֥ת עוֹלָֽם׃


because he slew me not from the womb; so that my mother might have been my grave, and her womb always great.


###### Jeremiah 20:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/20:18)


לָ֤מָּה זֶּה֙ מֵרֶ֣חֶם יָצָ֔אתִי לִרְא֥וֹת עָמָ֖ל וְיָג֑וֹן וַיִּכְל֥וּ בְּבֹ֖שֶׁת יָמָֽי׃{פ}


Why did I come out of the womb to see labour and sorrow, that my days should be consumed with shame?



## Chapter 21

###### Jeremiah 21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:1)


הַדָּבָ֛ר אֲשֶׁר־הָיָ֥ה אֶֽל־יִרְמְיָ֖הוּ מֵאֵ֣ת יְהֹוָ֑ה בִּשְׁלֹ֨חַ אֵלָ֜יו הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ צִדְקִיָּ֗הוּ אֶת־פַּשְׁחוּר֙ בֶּן־מַלְכִּיָּ֔ה וְאֶת־צְפַנְיָ֧ה בֶן־מַעֲשֵׂיָ֛ה הַכֹּהֵ֖ן לֵאמֹֽר׃


The word which came to Yirmeyahu from the Lord, when king Żidqiyyahu sent to him Pashĥur the son of Malkiyya and Żefanya the son of Ma῾aseya the priest, saying,


###### Jeremiah 21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:2)


דְּרׇשׁ־נָ֤א בַעֲדֵ֙נוּ֙ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֔ה כִּ֛י נְבוּכַדְרֶאצַּ֥ר מֶֽלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֖ל נִלְחָ֣ם עָלֵ֑ינוּ אוּלַי֩ יַעֲשֶׂ֨ה יְהֹוָ֤ה אוֹתָ֙נוּ֙ כְּכׇל־נִפְלְאֹתָ֔יו וְיַעֲלֶ֖ה מֵעָלֵֽינוּ׃


Inquire, I pray thee, of the Lord for us; for Nevukhadreżżar, king of Bavel is making war against us; perhaps the Lord will deal with us according to all his wondrous works, so that he may go up from us.


###### Jeremiah 21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:3)


וַיֹּ֥אמֶר יִרְמְיָ֖הוּ אֲלֵיהֶ֑ם כֹּ֥ה תֹאמְרֻ֖ן אֶל־צִדְקִיָּֽהוּ׃{ס}


Then said Yirmeyahu to them, Thus shall you say to Żidqiyyahu;


###### Jeremiah 21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:4)


כֹּה־אָמַ֨ר יְהֹוָ֜ה אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל הִנְנִ֣י מֵסֵב֮ אֶת־כְּלֵ֣י הַמִּלְחָמָה֮ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בְּיֶדְכֶם֒ אֲשֶׁ֨ר אַתֶּ֜ם נִלְחָמִ֣ים בָּ֗ם אֶת־מֶ֤לֶךְ בָּבֶל֙ וְאֶת־הַכַּשְׂדִּ֔ים הַצָּרִ֣ים עֲלֵיכֶ֔ם מִח֖וּץ לַחוֹמָ֑ה וְאָסַפְתִּ֣י אוֹתָ֔ם אֶל־תּ֖וֹךְ הָעִ֥יר הַזֹּֽאת׃


Thus says the Lord God of Yisra᾽el; Behold, I will turn back the weapons of war that are in your hands, with which you fight against the king of Bavel, and against the Kasdim, who besiege you outside the walls, and I will assemble them into the midst of this city.


###### Jeremiah 21:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:5)


וְנִלְחַמְתִּ֤י אֲנִי֙ אִתְּכֶ֔ם בְּיָ֥ד נְטוּיָ֖ה וּבִזְר֣וֹעַ חֲזָקָ֑ה וּבְאַ֥ף וּבְחֵמָ֖ה וּבְקֶ֥צֶף גָּדֽוֹל׃


And I myself will fight against you with an outstretched hand and with a strong arm, even in anger, and in fury, and in great wrath.


###### Jeremiah 21:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:6)


וְהִכֵּיתִ֗י אֶת־יֽוֹשְׁבֵי֙ הָעִ֣יר הַזֹּ֔את וְאֶת־הָאָדָ֖ם וְאֶת־הַבְּהֵמָ֑ה בְּדֶ֥בֶר גָּד֖וֹל יָמֻֽתוּ׃


And I will smite the inhabitants of this city, both man and beast: they shall die of a great pestilence.


###### Jeremiah 21:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:7)


וְאַחֲרֵי־כֵ֣ן נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֡ה אֶתֵּ֣ן אֶת־צִדְקִיָּ֣הוּ מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֣ה וְאֶת־עֲבָדָ֣יו׀וְאֶת־הָעָ֡ם וְאֶת־הַנִּשְׁאָרִים֩ בָּעִ֨יר הַזֹּ֜את מִן־הַדֶּ֣בֶר**׀** מִן־הַחֶ֣רֶב וּמִן־הָרָעָ֗ב בְּיַד֙ נְבוּכַדְרֶאצַּ֣ר מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֔ל וּבְיַד֙ אֹֽיְבֵיהֶ֔ם וּבְיַ֖ד מְבַקְשֵׁ֣י נַפְשָׁ֑ם וְהִכָּ֣ם לְפִי־חֶ֔רֶב לֹא־יָח֣וּס עֲלֵיהֶ֔ם וְלֹ֥א יַחְמֹ֖ל וְלֹ֥א יְרַחֵֽם׃


And afterwards, says the Lord, I will deliver Żidqiyyahu king of Yehuda, and his servants, and the people, and such as are left over in this city from the pestilence, from the sword, and from the famine, into the hand of Nevukhadreżżar king of Bavel, and into the hand of their enemies, and into the hand of those that seek their life: and he shall smite them with the edge of the sword; he shall not spare them, neither have pity, nor have mercy.


###### Jeremiah 21:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:8)


וְאֶל־הָעָ֤ם הַזֶּה֙ תֹּאמַ֔ר כֹּ֖ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֑ה הִנְנִ֤י נֹתֵן֙ לִפְנֵיכֶ֔ם אֶת־דֶּ֥רֶךְ הַחַיִּ֖ים וְאֶת־דֶּ֥רֶךְ הַמָּֽוֶת׃


And to this people thou shalt say, Thus says the Lord; Behold, I set before you the way of life, and the way of death.


###### Jeremiah 21:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:9)


הַיֹּשֵׁב֙ בָּעִ֣יר הַזֹּ֔את יָמ֕וּת בַּחֶ֖רֶב וּבָרָעָ֣ב וּבַדָּ֑בֶר וְהַיּוֹצֵא֩ וְנָפַ֨ל עַל־הַכַּשְׂדִּ֜ים הַצָּרִ֤ים עֲלֵיכֶם֙ (יחיה) [וְחָיָ֔ה] וְהָיְתָה־לּ֥וֹ נַפְשׁ֖וֹ לְשָׁלָֽל׃


He that abides in this city shall die by the sword, and by the famine, and by the pestilence: but he that goes out, and falls away to the Kasdim that besiege you, he shall live, and his life shall be to him for booty.


###### Jeremiah 21:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:10)


כִּ֣י שַׂ֣מְתִּי פָ֠נַי בָּעִ֨יר הַזֹּ֧את לְרָעָ֛ה וְלֹ֥א לְטוֹבָ֖ה נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה בְּיַד־מֶ֤לֶךְ בָּבֶל֙ תִּנָּתֵ֔ן וּשְׂרָפָ֖הּ בָּאֵֽשׁ׃{ס}


For I have set my face against this city for evil, and not for good, says the Lord: it shall be given into the hand of the king of Bavel, and he shall burn it with fire.


###### Jeremiah 21:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:11)


וּלְבֵית֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֔ה שִׁמְע֖וּ דְּבַר־יְהֹוָֽה׃


And to the house of the king of Yehuda, say, Hear the word of the Lord;


###### Jeremiah 21:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:12)


בֵּ֣ית דָּוִ֗ד כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה דִּ֤ינוּ לַבֹּ֙קֶר֙ מִשְׁפָּ֔ט וְהַצִּ֥ילוּ גָז֖וּל מִיַּ֣ד עוֹשֵׁ֑ק פֶּן־תֵּצֵ֨א כָאֵ֜שׁ חֲמָתִ֗י וּבָֽעֲרָה֙ וְאֵ֣ין מְכַבֶּ֔ה מִפְּנֵ֖י רֹ֥עַ (מעלליהם) [מַעַלְלֵיכֶֽם]׃


O house of David, thus says the Lord; Execute judgment in the morning, and deliver him that is robbed out of the hand of the oppressor, lest my fury go forth like fire, and burn so that none can quench it, because of the evil of your doings.


###### Jeremiah 21:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:13)


הִנְנִ֨י אֵלַ֜יִךְ יֹשֶׁ֧בֶת הָעֵ֛מֶק צ֥וּר הַמִּישֹׁ֖ר נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה הָאֹֽמְרִים֙ מִֽי־יֵחַ֣ת עָלֵ֔ינוּ וּמִ֥י יָב֖וֹא בִּמְעוֹנוֹתֵֽינוּ׃


Behold, I am against thee, O inhabitant of the valley, O rock of the plain, says the Lord; who say, Who shall come down against us? or who shall enter into our habitations?


###### Jeremiah 21:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/21:14)


וּפָקַדְתִּ֧י עֲלֵיכֶ֛ם כִּפְרִ֥י מַעַלְלֵיכֶ֖ם נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה וְהִצַּ֤תִּי אֵשׁ֙ בְּיַעְרָ֔הּ וְאָכְלָ֖ה כׇּל־סְבִיבֶֽיהָ׃


But I will punish you according to the fruit of your doings, says the Lord: and I will kindle a fire in her forest, and it shall devour all that is round about her.



## Chapter 22

###### Jeremiah 22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:1)


כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה רֵ֖ד בֵּֽית־מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֑ה וְדִבַּרְתָּ֣ שָׁ֔ם אֶת־הַדָּבָ֖ר הַזֶּֽה׃


Thus says the Lord; Go down to the house of the king of Yehuda, and speak there this word,


###### Jeremiah 22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:2)


וְאָֽמַרְתָּ֙ שְׁמַ֣ע דְּבַר־יְהֹוָ֔ה מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֔ה הַיֹּשֵׁ֖ב עַל־כִּסֵּ֣א דָוִ֑ד אַתָּ֤ה וַעֲבָדֶ֙יךָ֙ וְעַמְּךָ֔ הַבָּאִ֖ים בַּשְּׁעָרִ֥ים הָאֵֽלֶּה׃


and say, Hear the word of the Lord, O king of Yehuda, that sittest upon the throne of David, thou, and thy servants, and thy people that enter in by these gates:


###### Jeremiah 22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:3)


כֹּ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה עֲשׂ֤וּ מִשְׁפָּט֙ וּצְדָקָ֔ה וְהַצִּ֥ילוּ גָז֖וּל מִיַּ֣ד עָשׁ֑וֹק וְגֵר֩ יָת֨וֹם וְאַלְמָנָ֤ה אַל־תֹּנוּ֙ אַל־תַּחְמֹ֔סוּ וְדָ֣ם נָקִ֔י אַֽל־תִּשְׁפְּכ֖וּ בַּמָּק֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃


thus says the Lord; Execute judgment and righteousness, and deliver the robbed out of the hand of the oppressor: and do no wrong, do no violence to the stranger, the fatherless, or the widow, neither shed innocent blood in this place.


###### Jeremiah 22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:4)


כִּ֤י אִם־עָשׂוֹ֙ תַּעֲשׂ֔וּ אֶת־הַדָּבָ֖ר הַזֶּ֑ה וּבָ֣אוּ בְשַׁעֲרֵ֣י הַבַּ֣יִת הַזֶּ֡ה מְלָכִים֩ יֹשְׁבִ֨ים לְדָוִ֜ד עַל־כִּסְא֗וֹ רֹֽכְבִים֙ בָּרֶ֣כֶב וּבַסּוּסִ֔ים ה֥וּא וַעֲבָדָ֖ו וְעַמּֽוֹ׃


For if you do this thing indeed, then shall there enter in by the gates of this house kings of the house of David sitting upon his throne, riding in chariots and on horses, he, and his servants, and his people.


###### Jeremiah 22:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:5)


וְאִם֙ לֹ֣א תִשְׁמְע֔וּ אֶת־הַדְּבָרִ֖ים הָאֵ֑לֶּה בִּ֤י נִשְׁבַּ֙עְתִּי֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה כִּֽי־לְחׇרְבָּ֥ה יִֽהְיֶ֖ה הַבַּ֥יִת הַזֶּֽה׃{פ}


But if you will not hear these words, I swear by myself, says the Lord, that this house shall become a desolation.


###### Jeremiah 22:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:6)


כִּי־כֹ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה עַל־בֵּית֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֔ה גִּלְעָ֥ד אַתָּ֛ה לִ֖י רֹ֣אשׁ הַלְּבָנ֑וֹן אִם־לֹ֤א אֲשִֽׁיתְךָ֙ מִדְבָּ֔ר עָרִ֖ים לֹ֥א (נושבה) [נוֹשָֽׁבוּ]׃


For thus says the Lord to the house of the king of Yehuda; Thou art as Gil῾ad to me, the summit of Levanon: yet surely I will make thee a wilderness, cities which are not inhabited.


###### Jeremiah 22:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:7)


וְקִדַּשְׁתִּ֥י עָלֶ֛יךָ מַשְׁחִתִ֖ים אִ֣ישׁ וְכֵלָ֑יו וְכָֽרְתוּ֙ מִבְחַ֣ר אֲרָזֶ֔יךָ וְהִפִּ֖ילוּ עַל־הָאֵֽשׁ׃


And I will prepare destroyers against thee, every one with his weapons: and they shall cut down thy choice cedars, and cast them into the fire.


###### Jeremiah 22:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:8)


וְעָֽבְרוּ֙ גּוֹיִ֣ם רַבִּ֔ים עַ֖ל הָעִ֣יר הַזֹּ֑את וְאָֽמְרוּ֙ אִ֣ישׁ אֶל־רֵעֵ֔הוּ עַל־מֶ֨ה עָשָׂ֤ה יְהֹוָה֙ כָּ֔כָה לָעִ֥יר הַגְּדוֹלָ֖ה הַזֹּֽאת׃


And many nations shall pass by this city, and they shall say every man to his neighbour, Why has the Lord done so to this great city?


###### Jeremiah 22:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:9)


וְאָ֣מְר֔וּ עַ֚ל אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָזְב֔וּ אֶת־בְּרִ֥ית יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵיהֶ֑ם וַיִּֽשְׁתַּחֲו֛וּ לֵאלֹהִ֥ים אֲחֵרִ֖ים וַיַּעַבְדֽוּם׃{ס}


Then they shall answer, Because they have forsaken the covenant of the Lord their God, and worshipped other gods, and served them.


###### Jeremiah 22:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:10)


אַל־תִּבְכּ֣וּ לְמֵ֔ת וְאַל־תָּנֻ֖דוּ ל֑וֹ בְּכ֤וּ בָכוֹ֙ לַהֹלֵ֔ךְ כִּ֣י לֹ֤א יָשׁוּב֙ ע֔וֹד וְרָאָ֖ה אֶת־אֶ֥רֶץ מוֹלַדְתּֽוֹ׃


Weep not for the dead, neither bemoan him: but weep sore for him that goes away: for he shall return no more, nor see his native land.


###### Jeremiah 22:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:11)


כִּ֣י כֹ֣ה אָֽמַר־יְ֠הֹוָ֠ה אֶל־שַׁלֻּ֨ם בֶּן־יֹאשִׁיָּ֜הוּ מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֗ה הַמֹּלֵךְ֙ תַּ֚חַת יֹאשִׁיָּ֣הוּ אָבִ֔יו אֲשֶׁ֥ר יָצָ֖א מִן־הַמָּק֣וֹם הַזֶּ֑ה לֹא־יָשׁ֥וּב שָׁ֖ם עֽוֹד׃


For thus says the Lord concerning Shallum the son of Yoshiyyahu king of Yehuda, who reigned instead of Yoshiyyahu his father, who went forth out of this place; He shall not return there any more:


###### Jeremiah 22:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:12)


כִּ֗י בִּמְק֛וֹם אֲשֶׁר־הִגְל֥וּ אֹת֖וֹ שָׁ֣ם יָמ֑וּת וְאֶת־הָאָ֥רֶץ הַזֹּ֖את לֹא־יִרְאֶ֥ה עֽוֹד׃{ס}


but he shall die in the place to which they have led him into exile, and shall see this land no more.


###### Jeremiah 22:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:13)


ה֣וֹי בֹּנֶ֤ה בֵיתוֹ֙ בְּֽלֹא־צֶ֔דֶק וַעֲלִיּוֹתָ֖יו בְּלֹ֣א מִשְׁפָּ֑ט בְּרֵעֵ֙הוּ֙ יַעֲבֹ֣ד חִנָּ֔ם וּפֹעֲל֖וֹ לֹ֥א יִתֶּן־לֽוֹ׃


Woe to him that builds his house by unrighteousness, and his chambers by injustice; that uses his neighbour’s service without wages, and gives him not for his hire;


###### Jeremiah 22:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:14)


הָאֹמֵ֗ר אֶבְנֶה־לִּי֙ בֵּ֣ית מִדּ֔וֹת וַעֲלִיּ֖וֹת מְרֻוָּחִ֑ים וְקָ֤רַֽע לוֹ֙ חַלּוֹנָ֔י וְסָפ֣וּן בָּאָ֔רֶז וּמָשׁ֖וֹחַ בַּשָּׁשַֽׁר׃


that says, I will build me a wide house with large upper chambers, and he cuts him out windows; and it is covered with rafters of cedar, and painted with vermilion.


###### Jeremiah 22:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:15)


הֲתִֽמְלֹ֔ךְ כִּ֥י אַתָּ֖ה מְתַחֲרֶ֣ה בָאָ֑רֶז אָבִ֜יךָ הֲל֧וֹא אָכַ֣ל וְשָׁתָ֗ה וְעָשָׂ֤ה מִשְׁפָּט֙ וּצְדָקָ֔ה אָ֖ז ט֥וֹב לֽוֹ׃


Shalt thou reign, because thou dost compete in cedar? did not thy father eat and drink, and do judgment and justice, and then it was well with him?


###### Jeremiah 22:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:16)


דָּ֛ן דִּין־עָנִ֥י וְאֶבְי֖וֹן אָ֣ז ט֑וֹב הֲלוֹא־הִ֛יא הַדַּ֥עַת אֹתִ֖י נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


He judged the cause of the poor and needy; then it was well with him: was not this to know me? says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 22:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:17)


כִּ֣י אֵ֤ין עֵינֶ֙יךָ֙ וְלִבְּךָ֔ כִּ֖י אִם־עַל־בִּצְעֶ֑ךָ וְעַ֤ל דַּֽם־הַנָּקִי֙ לִשְׁפּ֔וֹךְ וְעַל־הָעֹ֥שֶׁק וְעַל־הַמְּרוּצָ֖ה לַעֲשֽׂוֹת׃{ס}


But thou hast eyes and heart only for thy dishonest gain, and for shedding innocent blood, and for oppression, and for practising violence.


###### Jeremiah 22:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:18)


לָכֵ֞ן כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה אֶל־יְהוֹיָקִ֤ים בֶּן־יֹאשִׁיָּ֙הוּ֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֔ה לֹא־יִסְפְּד֣וּ ל֔וֹ ה֥וֹי אָחִ֖י וְה֣וֹי אָח֑וֹת לֹא־יִסְפְּד֣וּ ל֔וֹ ה֥וֹי אָד֖וֹן וְה֥וֹי הֹדֹֽה׃


Therefore thus says the Lord concerning Yehoyaqim the son of Yoshiyyahu king of Yehuda, They shall not lament for him, saying, Ah my brother! or, Ah sister! they shall not lament for him, saying, Ah lord! or, Ah his glory!


###### Jeremiah 22:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:19)


קְבוּרַ֥ת חֲמ֖וֹר יִקָּבֵ֑ר סָח֣וֹב וְהַשְׁלֵ֔ךְ מֵהָ֖לְאָה לְשַׁעֲרֵ֥י יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃{ס}


He shall be buried with the burial of an ass, drawn and cast forth beyond the gates of Yerushalayim.


###### Jeremiah 22:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:20)


עֲלִ֤י הַלְּבָנוֹן֙ וּֽצְעָ֔קִי וּבַבָּשָׁ֖ן תְּנִ֣י קוֹלֵ֑ךְ וְצַֽעֲקִי֙ מֵעֲבָרִ֔ים כִּ֥י נִשְׁבְּר֖וּ כׇּל־מְאַהֲבָֽיִךְ׃


Go up to Levanon, and cry; and lift up thy voice in Bashan, and cry from ῾Avarim: for all thy lovers are destroyed.


###### Jeremiah 22:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:21)


דִּבַּ֤רְתִּי אֵלַ֙יִךְ֙ בְּשַׁלְוֺתַ֔יִךְ אָמַ֖רְתְּ לֹ֣א אֶשְׁמָ֑ע זֶ֤ה דַרְכֵּךְ֙ מִנְּעוּרַ֔יִךְ כִּ֥י לֹא־שָׁמַ֖עַתְּ בְּקוֹלִֽי׃


I spoke to thee in thy prosperity; but thou didst say, I will not hear. This has been thy manner from thy youth, that thou didst not obey my voice.


###### Jeremiah 22:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:22)


כׇּל־רֹעַ֙יִךְ֙ תִּרְעֶה־ר֔וּחַ וּֽמְאַהֲבַ֖יִךְ בַּשְּׁבִ֣י יֵלֵ֑כוּ כִּ֣י אָ֤ז תֵּבֹ֙שִׁי֙ וְנִכְלַ֔מְתְּ מִכֹּ֖ל רָעָתֵֽךְ׃


The wind shall drive away all thy shepherds, and thy lovers shall go into captivity: surely then shalt thou be ashamed and confounded for all thy wickedness.


###### Jeremiah 22:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:23)


(ישבתי) [יֹשַׁבְתְּ֙] בַּלְּבָנ֔וֹן (מקננתי) [מְקֻנַּ֖נְתְּ] בָּאֲרָזִ֑ים מַה־נֵּחַנְתְּ֙ בְּבֹא־לָ֣ךְ חֲבָלִ֔ים חִ֖יל כַּיֹּלֵדָֽה׃


O inhabitant of Levanon, that makest thy nest in the cedars, how pitiable shalt thou be when pangs come upon thee, the pain as of a woman in travail!


###### Jeremiah 22:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:24)


חַי־אָ֘נִי֮ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָה֒ כִּ֣י אִם־יִהְיֶ֞ה כׇּנְיָ֤הוּ בֶן־יְהֽוֹיָקִים֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֔ה חוֹתָ֖ם עַל־יַ֣ד יְמִינִ֑י כִּ֥י מִשָּׁ֖ם אֶתְּקֶֽנְךָּ׃


As I live, says the Lord, though Konyahu the son of Yehoyaqim king of Yehuda were the signet upon my right hand, yet would I tear thee off;


###### Jeremiah 22:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:25)


וּנְתַתִּ֗יךָ בְּיַד֙ מְבַקְשֵׁ֣י נַפְשֶׁ֔ךָ וּבְיַ֛ד אֲשֶׁר־אַתָּ֥ה יָג֖וֹר מִפְּנֵיהֶ֑ם וּבְיַ֛ד נְבוּכַדְרֶאצַּ֥ר מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֖ל וּבְיַ֥ד הַכַּשְׂדִּֽים׃


and I will give thee into the hand of them that seek thy life, and into the hand of them whose face thou fearest, even into the hand of Nevukhadreżżar king of Bavel, and into the hand of the Kasdim.


###### Jeremiah 22:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:26)


וְהֵטַלְתִּ֣י אֹתְךָ֗ וְאֶֽת־אִמְּךָ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר יְלָדַ֔תְךָ עַ֚ל הָאָ֣רֶץ אַחֶ֔רֶת אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹֽא־יֻלַּדְתֶּ֖ם שָׁ֑ם וְשָׁ֖ם תָּמֽוּתוּ׃


And I will cast thee out, and thy mother that bore thee, into another country, where you were not born; and there shall you die.


###### Jeremiah 22:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:27)


וְעַל־הָאָ֗רֶץ אֲשֶׁר־הֵ֛ם מְנַשְּׂאִ֥ים אֶת־נַפְשָׁ֖ם לָשׁ֣וּב שָׁ֑ם שָׁ֖מָּה לֹ֥א יָשֽׁוּבוּ׃{פ}


But to the land to which they desire to return, to it shall they not return.


###### Jeremiah 22:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:28)


הַעֶ֨צֶב נִבְזֶ֜ה נָפ֗וּץ הָאִ֤ישׁ הַזֶּה֙ כׇּנְיָ֔הוּ אִ֨ם־כְּלִ֔י אֵ֥ין חֵ֖פֶץ בּ֑וֹ מַדּ֤וּעַ הֽוּטְלוּ֙ ה֣וּא וְזַרְע֔וֹ וְהֻ֨שְׁלְכ֔וּ עַל־הָאָ֖רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־יָדָֽעוּ׃


Is this man Konyahu a despised broken vessel, an object that no one cares for? why are they cast out, he and his seed, and banished to a land which they know not?


###### Jeremiah 22:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:29)


אֶ֥רֶץ אֶ֖רֶץ אָ֑רֶץ שִׁמְעִ֖י דְּבַר־יְהֹוָֽה׃


O land, land, land, hear the word of the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 22:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/22:30)


כֹּ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה כִּתְב֞וּ אֶת־הָאִ֤ישׁ הַזֶּה֙ עֲרִירִ֔י גֶּ֖בֶר לֹא־יִצְלַ֣ח בְּיָמָ֑יו כִּי֩ לֹ֨א יִצְלַ֜ח מִזַּרְע֗וֹ אִ֚ישׁ יֹשֵׁב֙ עַל־כִּסֵּ֣א דָוִ֔ד וּמֹשֵׁ֥ל ע֖וֹד בִּיהוּדָֽה׃{פ}


Thus says the Lord, Write this man childless, a man that shall not prosper in his days: for no man of his seed shall pros­per, sitting upon the throne of David, and ruling any more in Yehuda.



## Chapter 23

###### Jeremiah 23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:1)


ה֣וֹי רֹעִ֗ים מְאַבְּדִ֧ים וּמְפִצִ֛ים אֶת־צֹ֥אן מַרְעִיתִ֖י נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


Woe to the shepherds that destroy and scatter the sheep of my pasture! says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:2)


לָ֠כֵ֠ן כֹּה־אָמַ֨ר יְהֹוָ֜ה אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל עַֽל־הָרֹעִים֮ הָרֹעִ֣ים אֶת־עַמִּי֒ אַתֶּ֞ם הֲפִצֹתֶ֤ם אֶת־צֹאנִי֙ וַתַּדִּח֔וּם וְלֹ֥א פְקַדְתֶּ֖ם אֹתָ֑ם הִנְנִ֨י פֹקֵ֧ד עֲלֵיכֶ֛ם אֶת־רֹ֥עַ מַעַלְלֵיכֶ֖ם נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


Therefore, thus says the Lord God of Yisra᾽el against the shepherds that feed my people; You have scattered my flock, and driven them away, and have not taken care of them: behold, I will punish you for the evil of your doings, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:3)


וַאֲנִ֗י אֲקַבֵּץ֙ אֶת־שְׁאֵרִ֣ית צֹאנִ֔י מִכֹּל֙ הָאֲרָצ֔וֹת אֲשֶׁר־הִדַּ֥חְתִּי אֹתָ֖ם שָׁ֑ם וַהֲשִׁבֹתִ֥י אֶתְהֶ֛ן עַל־נְוֵהֶ֖ן וּפָר֥וּ וְרָבֽוּ׃


And I will gather the remnant of my flock out of all countries into which I have driven them, and will bring them back to their folds; and they shall be fruitful and increase.


###### Jeremiah 23:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:4)


וַהֲקִמֹתִ֧י עֲלֵיהֶ֛ם רֹעִ֖ים וְרָע֑וּם וְלֹא־יִֽירְא֨וּ ע֧וֹד וְלֹא־יֵחַ֛תּוּ וְלֹ֥א יִפָּקֵ֖דוּ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


And I will set up shepherds over them who shall feed them: and they shall fear no more, nor be dismayed, neither shall any be lacking, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 23:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:5)


הִנֵּ֨ה יָמִ֤ים בָּאִים֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה וַהֲקִמֹתִ֥י לְדָוִ֖ד צֶ֣מַח צַדִּ֑יק וּמָ֤לַךְ מֶ֙לֶךְ֙ וְהִשְׂכִּ֔יל וְעָשָׂ֛ה מִשְׁפָּ֥ט וּצְדָקָ֖ה בָּאָֽרֶץ׃


Behold, days are coming, says the Lord, when I will raise to David a righteous offshoot, and he shall reign as a king and prosper, and shall execute justice and righteousness in the earth.


###### Jeremiah 23:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:6)


בְּיָמָיו֙ תִּוָּשַׁ֣ע יְהוּדָ֔ה וְיִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל יִשְׁכֹּ֣ן לָבֶ֑טַח וְזֶה־שְּׁמ֥וֹ אֲֽשֶׁר־יִקְרְא֖וֹ יְהֹוָ֥ה׀צִדְקֵֽנוּ׃{פ}


In his days Yehuda shall be saved, and Yisra᾽el shall dwell safely: and this is his name whereby he shall be called, The Lord is our righteousness.


###### Jeremiah 23:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:7)


לָכֵ֛ן הִנֵּה־יָמִ֥ים בָּאִ֖ים נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה וְלֹא־יֹ֤אמְרוּ עוֹד֙ חַי־יְהֹוָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֧ר הֶעֱלָ֛ה אֶת־בְּנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל מֵאֶ֥רֶץ מִצְרָֽיִם׃


Therefore, behold, days are coming, says the Lord, when they shall no more say, As the Lord lives, who brought up the children of Yisra᾽el out of the land of Miżrayim;


###### Jeremiah 23:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:8)


כִּ֣י אִם־חַי־יְהֹוָ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֣ר הֶעֱלָה֩ וַאֲשֶׁ֨ר הֵבִ֜יא אֶת־זֶ֨רַע בֵּ֤ית יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ מֵאֶ֣רֶץ צָפ֔וֹנָה וּמִכֹּל֙ הָאֲרָצ֔וֹת אֲשֶׁ֥ר הִדַּחְתִּ֖ים שָׁ֑ם וְיָשְׁב֖וּ עַל־אַדְמָתָֽם׃{פ}


but, As the Lord lives, who brought up and who led the seed of the house of Yisra᾽el out of the north country, and from all countries into which I have driven them; and they shall dwell in their own land.


###### Jeremiah 23:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:9)


לַנְּבִאִ֞ים נִשְׁבַּ֧ר לִבִּ֣י בְקִרְבִּ֗י רָֽחֲפוּ֙ כׇּל־עַצְמוֹתַ֔י הָיִ֙יתִי֙ כְּאִ֣ישׁ שִׁכּ֔וֹר וּכְגֶ֖בֶר עֲבָ֣רוֹ יָ֑יִן מִפְּנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה וּמִפְּנֵ֖י דִּבְרֵ֥י קׇדְשֽׁוֹ׃


My heart within me is broken because of the prophets; all my bones shake; I am like a drunken man, and like a man whom wine has overcome, because of the Lord, and because of his holy words.


###### Jeremiah 23:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:10)


כִּ֤י מְנָֽאֲפִים֙ מָלְאָ֣ה הָאָ֔רֶץ כִּֽי־מִפְּנֵ֤י אָלָה֙ אָבְלָ֣ה הָאָ֔רֶץ יָבְשׁ֖וּ נְא֣וֹת מִדְבָּ֑ר וַתְּהִ֤י מְרֽוּצָתָם֙ רָעָ֔ה וּגְבוּרָתָ֖ם לֹא־כֵֽן׃


For the land is full of adulterers; for because of swearing, the land mourns; the pastures of the wilderness are dried up. For their course was evil, and their force unjust.


###### Jeremiah 23:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:11)


כִּֽי־גַם־נָבִ֥יא גַם־כֹּהֵ֖ן חָנֵ֑פוּ גַּם־בְּבֵיתִ֛י מָצָ֥אתִי רָעָתָ֖ם נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


For both prophet and priest are hypocrites; even in my house have I found their wickedness, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 23:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:12)


לָכֵן֩ יִהְיֶ֨ה דַרְכָּ֜ם לָהֶ֗ם כַּחֲלַקְלַקּוֹת֙ בָּאֲפֵלָ֔ה יִדַּ֖חוּ וְנָ֣פְלוּ בָ֑הּ כִּי־אָבִ֨יא עֲלֵיהֶ֥ם רָעָ֛ה שְׁנַ֥ת פְּקֻדָּתָ֖ם נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


Therefore their way shall be to them like slippery ways in the darkness: into which they shall be driven, and fall: for I will bring evil upon them, the year of their punishment, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 23:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:13)


וּבִנְבִיאֵ֥י שֹׁמְר֖וֹן רָאִ֣יתִי תִפְלָ֑ה הִנַּבְּא֣וּ בַבַּ֔עַל וַיַּתְע֥וּ אֶת־עַמִּ֖י אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


And I have seen folly in the prophets of Shomeron; they prophesied by the Ba῾al, and caused my people Yisra᾽el to err.


###### Jeremiah 23:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:14)


וּבִנְבִאֵ֨י יְרוּשָׁלַ֜͏ִם רָאִ֣יתִי שַׁעֲרוּרָ֗ה נָא֞וֹף וְהָלֹ֤ךְ בַּשֶּׁ֙קֶר֙ וְחִזְּקוּ֙ יְדֵ֣י מְרֵעִ֔ים לְבִ֨לְתִּי־שָׁ֔בוּ אִ֖ישׁ מֵרָעָת֑וֹ הָיוּ־לִ֤י כֻלָּם֙ כִּסְדֹ֔ם וְיֹשְׁבֶ֖יהָ כַּעֲמֹרָֽה׃{פ}


But in the prophets of Yerushalayim I have seen a horrible thing: they commit adultery, and walk in lies: they strengthen also the hands of evildoers, so that none returns from his wickedness: they are all of them become to me like Sedom, and its inhabitants like ῾Amora.


###### Jeremiah 23:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:15)


לָכֵ֞ן כֹּה־אָמַ֨ר יְהֹוָ֤ה צְבָאוֹת֙ עַל־הַנְּבִאִ֔ים הִנְנִ֨י מַאֲכִ֤יל אוֹתָם֙ לַעֲנָ֔ה וְהִשְׁקִתִ֖ים מֵי־רֹ֑אשׁ כִּ֗י מֵאֵת֙ נְבִיאֵ֣י יְרוּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם יָצְאָ֥ה חֲנֻפָּ֖ה לְכׇל־הָאָֽרֶץ׃{פ}


Therefore thus says the Lord of hosts concerning the prophets; Behold, I will feed them with wormwood, and make them drink the water of gall: for from the prophets of Yerushalayim has hypocrisy gone out to all the land.


###### Jeremiah 23:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:16)


כֹּה־אָמַ֞ר יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֗וֹת אַֽל־תִּשְׁמְע֞וּ עַל־דִּבְרֵ֤י הַנְּבִאִים֙ הַנִּבְּאִ֣ים לָכֶ֔ם מַהְבִּלִ֥ים הֵ֖מָּה אֶתְכֶ֑ם חֲז֤וֹן לִבָּם֙ יְדַבֵּ֔רוּ לֹ֖א מִפִּ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


Thus says the Lord of hosts, Hearken not to the words of the prophets that prophesy to you: they lead you into vanity; they speak a vision of their own heart, and not out of the mouth of the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 23:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:17)


אֹמְרִ֤ים אָמוֹר֙ לִֽמְנַאֲצַ֔י דִּבֶּ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה שָׁל֖וֹם יִהְיֶ֣ה לָכֶ֑ם וְ֠כֹ֠ל הֹלֵ֞ךְ בִּשְׁרִר֤וּת לִבּוֹ֙ אָֽמְר֔וּ לֹא־תָב֥וֹא עֲלֵיכֶ֖ם רָעָֽה׃


They say still to them that despise me, The Lord has said, You shall have peace; and they say to every one that walks after the stubbornness of his own heart, No evil shall come upon you.


###### Jeremiah 23:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:18)


כִּ֣י מִ֤י עָמַד֙ בְּס֣וֹד יְהֹוָ֔ה וְיֵ֖רֶא וְיִשְׁמַ֣ע אֶת־דְּבָר֑וֹ מִי־הִקְשִׁ֥יב (דברי) [דְּבָר֖וֹ] וַיִּשְׁמָֽע׃{ס}


For who has stood in the counsel of the Lord, that he might perceive and hear his word? who has marked his word, and heard it?


###### Jeremiah 23:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:19)


הִנֵּ֣ה**׀** סַעֲרַ֣ת יְהֹוָ֗ה חֵמָה֙ יָֽצְאָ֔ה וְסַ֖עַר מִתְחוֹלֵ֑ל עַ֛ל רֹ֥אשׁ רְשָׁעִ֖ים יָחֽוּל׃


Behold the storm wind of the Lord, fury has gone forth, a whirling tempest, it shall fall upon the head of the wicked.


###### Jeremiah 23:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:20)


לֹ֤א יָשׁוּב֙ אַף־יְהֹוָ֔ה עַד־עֲשֹׂת֥וֹ וְעַד־הֲקִימ֖וֹ מְזִמּ֣וֹת לִבּ֑וֹ בְּאַֽחֲרִית֙ הַיָּמִ֔ים תִּתְבּ֥וֹנְנוּ בָ֖הּ בִּינָֽה׃


The anger of the Lord shall not return, until he has executed, and till he has performed the thoughts of his heart: in the latter days you shall consider it perfectly.


###### Jeremiah 23:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:21)


לֹא־שָׁלַ֥חְתִּי אֶת־הַנְּבִאִ֖ים וְהֵ֣ם רָ֑צוּ לֹא־דִבַּ֥רְתִּי אֲלֵיהֶ֖ם וְהֵ֥ם נִבָּֽאוּ׃


I have not sent these prophets, yet they ran: I have not spoken to them, yet they prophesied.


###### Jeremiah 23:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:22)


וְאִם־עָמְד֖וּ בְּסוֹדִ֑י וְיַשְׁמִ֤עוּ דְבָרַי֙ אֶת־עַמִּ֔י וִֽישִׁבוּם֙ מִדַּרְכָּ֣ם הָרָ֔ע וּמֵרֹ֖עַ מַעַלְלֵיהֶֽם׃{ס}


But if they had stood in my counsel, then they should have caused my people to hear my words, and turned them from their evil way, and from the evil of their doings.


###### Jeremiah 23:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:23)


הַאֱלֹהֵ֧י מִקָּרֹ֛ב אָ֖נִי נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה וְלֹ֥א אֱלֹהֵ֖י מֵרָחֹֽק׃


Am I a God near at hand, says the Lord, and not a God afar off?


###### Jeremiah 23:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:24)


אִם־יִסָּתֵ֨ר אִ֧ישׁ בַּמִּסְתָּרִ֛ים וַאֲנִ֥י לֹֽא־אֶרְאֶ֖נּוּ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה הֲל֨וֹא אֶת־הַשָּׁמַ֧יִם וְאֶת־הָאָ֛רֶץ אֲנִ֥י מָלֵ֖א נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


Can any hide himself in secret places that I shall not see him? says the Lord. Do not I fill heaven and earth? says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 23:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:25)


שָׁמַ֗עְתִּי אֵ֤ת אֲשֶׁר־אָֽמְרוּ֙ הַנְּבִאִ֔ים הַֽנִּבְּאִ֥ים בִּשְׁמִ֛י שֶׁ֖קֶר לֵאמֹ֑ר חָלַ֖מְתִּי חָלָֽמְתִּי׃


I have heard what the prophets said, that prophesy lies in my name, saying, I have dreamed, I have dreamed.


###### Jeremiah 23:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:26)


עַד־מָתַ֗י הֲיֵ֛שׁ בְּלֵ֥ב הַנְּבִאִ֖ים נִבְּאֵ֣י הַשָּׁ֑קֶר וּנְבִיאֵ֖י תַּרְמִ֥ת לִבָּֽם׃


How long shall this be? Is it in the heart of the prophets that prophesy lies and the prophets of the deceit of their own heart;


###### Jeremiah 23:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:27)


הַחֹשְׁבִ֗ים לְהַשְׁכִּ֤יחַ אֶת־עַמִּי֙ שְׁמִ֔י בַּחֲל֣וֹמֹתָ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר יְסַפְּר֖וּ אִ֣ישׁ לְרֵעֵ֑הוּ כַּאֲשֶׁ֨ר שָׁכְח֧וּ אֲבוֹתָ֛ם אֶת־שְׁמִ֖י בַּבָּֽעַל׃


That think to cause my people to forget My name by their dreams which they tell every man to his neighbour, as their fathers forgot My name for the Ba῾al?


###### Jeremiah 23:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:28)


הַנָּבִ֞יא אֲשֶׁר־אִתּ֤וֹ חֲלוֹם֙ יְסַפֵּ֣ר חֲל֔וֹם וַאֲשֶׁ֤ר דְּבָרִי֙ אִתּ֔וֹ יְדַבֵּ֥ר דְּבָרִ֖י אֱמֶ֑ת מַה־לַתֶּ֥בֶן אֶת־הַבָּ֖ר נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


The prophet that has a dream, let him tell a dream; and he that has My word, let him speak My word faithfully. What is the chaff to the wheat? says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 23:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:29)


הֲל֨וֹא כֹ֧ה דְבָרִ֛י כָּאֵ֖שׁ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה וּכְפַטִּ֖ישׁ יְפֹ֥צֵֽץ סָֽלַע׃{ס}


Is not My word like a fire? says the Lord; and like a hammer that breaks the rock in pieces?


###### Jeremiah 23:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:30)


לָכֵ֛ן הִנְנִ֥י עַל־הַנְּבִאִ֖ים נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה מְגַנְּבֵ֣י דְבָרַ֔י אִ֖ישׁ מֵאֵ֥ת רֵעֵֽהוּ׃


Therefore, behold, I am against the prophets, says the Lord, that steal My words every one from his neighbour.


###### Jeremiah 23:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:31)


הִנְנִ֥י עַל־הַנְּבִיאִ֖ם נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה הַלֹּקְחִ֣ים לְשׁוֹנָ֔ם וַֽיִּנְאֲמ֖וּ נְאֻֽם׃


Behold, I am against the prophets, says the Lord, that use their tongues, and say, He says.


###### Jeremiah 23:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:32)


הִ֠נְנִ֠י עַֽל־נִבְּאֵ֞י חֲלֹמ֥וֹת שֶׁ֙קֶר֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה וַֽיְסַפְּרוּם֙ וַיַּתְע֣וּ אֶת־עַמִּ֔י בְּשִׁקְרֵיהֶ֖ם וּבְפַחֲזוּתָ֑ם וְאָנֹכִ֨י לֹֽא־שְׁלַחְתִּ֜ים וְלֹ֣א צִוִּיתִ֗ים וְהוֹעֵ֛יל לֹא־יוֹעִ֥ילוּ לָעָם־הַזֶּ֖ה נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


Behold, I am against them that prophesy lying dreams, says the Lord, and do tell them, and cause My people to err by their lies, and by their vain boasting; yet I sent them not, nor commanded them: therefore they shall not profit this people at all, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 23:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:33)


וְכִֽי־יִשְׁאָלְךָ֩ הָעָ֨ם הַזֶּ֜ה אֽוֹ־הַנָּבִ֤יא אֽוֹ־כֹהֵן֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר מַה־מַּשָּׂ֖א יְהֹוָ֑ה וְאָמַרְתָּ֤ אֲלֵיהֶם֙ אֶת־מַה־מַּשָּׂ֔א וְנָטַשְׁתִּ֥י אֶתְכֶ֖ם נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


And when this people, or the prophet, or a priest, shall ask thee, saying, What is the burden of the Lord? thou shalt then say to them, What burden? I will forsake you, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 23:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:34)


וְהַנָּבִ֤יא וְהַכֹּהֵן֙ וְהָעָ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר יֹאמַ֖ר מַשָּׂ֣א יְהֹוָ֑ה וּפָקַדְתִּ֛י עַל־הָאִ֥ישׁ הַה֖וּא וְעַל־בֵּיתֽוֹ׃


And as for the prophet, and the priest, and the people, that shall say, The burden of the Lord, I will even punish that man and his house.


###### Jeremiah 23:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:35)


כֹּ֥ה תֹאמְר֛וּ אִ֥ישׁ עַל־רֵעֵ֖הוּ וְאִ֣ישׁ אֶל־אָחִ֑יו מֶה־עָנָ֣ה יְהֹוָ֔ה וּמַה־דִּבֶּ֖ר יְהֹוָֽה׃


Thus shall you say every one to his neighbour, and every one to his brother, What has the Lord answered? and, What has the Lord spoken?


###### Jeremiah 23:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:36)


וּמַשָּׂ֥א יְהֹוָ֖ה לֹ֣א תִזְכְּרוּ־ע֑וֹד כִּ֣י הַמַּשָּׂ֗א יִֽהְיֶה֙ לְאִ֣ישׁ דְּבָר֔וֹ וַהֲפַכְתֶּ֗ם אֶת־דִּבְרֵי֙ אֱלֹהִ֣ים חַיִּ֔ים יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ׃


and the burden of the Lord shall you mention no more: for the burden shall belong to the man of his word; but you have perverted the words of the living God, of the Lord of hosts our God.


###### Jeremiah 23:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:37)


כֹּ֥ה תֹאמַ֖ר אֶל־הַנָּבִ֑יא מֶה־עָנָ֣ךְ יְהֹוָ֔ה וּמַה־דִּבֶּ֖ר יְהֹוָֽה׃


Thus shalt thou say to the prophet, What has the Lord answered thee? and, What has the Lord spoken?


###### Jeremiah 23:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:38)


וְאִם־מַשָּׂ֣א יְהֹוָה֮ תֹּאמֵ֒רוּ֒ לָכֵ֗ן כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה יַ֧עַן אֲמׇרְכֶ֛ם אֶת־הַדָּבָ֥ר הַזֶּ֖ה מַשָּׂ֣א יְהֹוָ֑ה וָאֶשְׁלַ֤ח אֲלֵיכֶם֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר לֹ֥א תֹאמְר֖וּ מַשָּׂ֥א יְהֹוָֽה׃


But if you say, The burden of the Lord; then thus says the Lord, Because you say this thing, The burden of the Lord, and I have sent to you, saying, You shall not say, The burden of the Lord;


###### Jeremiah 23:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:39)


לָכֵ֣ן הִנְנִ֔י וְנָשִׁ֥יתִי אֶתְכֶ֖ם נָשֹׁ֑א וְנָטַשְׁתִּ֣י אֶתְכֶ֗ם וְאֶת־הָעִיר֙ אֲשֶׁ֨ר נָתַ֧תִּי לָכֶ֛ם וְלַאֲבוֹתֵיכֶ֖ם מֵעַ֥ל פָּנָֽי׃


therefore, behold, I myself, will utterly forget you, and I will forsake you, and the city that I gave you and your fathers, and cast you out of my presence.


###### Jeremiah 23:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/23:40)


וְנָתַתִּ֥י עֲלֵיכֶ֖ם חֶרְפַּ֣ת עוֹלָ֑ם וּכְלִמּ֣וּת עוֹלָ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֖ר לֹ֥א תִשָּׁכֵֽחַ׃{פ}


And I will bring an everlasting reproach upon you, and a perpetual shame, which shall not be forgotten.



## Chapter 24

###### Jeremiah 24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/24:1)


הִרְאַ֘נִי֮ יְהֹוָה֒ וְהִנֵּ֗ה שְׁנֵי֙ דּוּדָאֵ֣י תְאֵנִ֔ים מוּעָדִ֕ים לִפְנֵ֖י הֵיכַ֣ל יְהֹוָ֑ה אַחֲרֵ֣י הַגְל֣וֹת נְבוּכַדְרֶאצַּ֣ר מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֡ל אֶת־יְכׇנְיָ֣הוּ בֶן־יְהוֹיָקִ֣ים מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָה֩ וְאֶת־שָׂרֵ֨י יְהוּדָ֜ה וְאֶת־הֶחָרָ֤שׁ וְאֶת־הַמַּסְגֵּר֙ מִיר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם וַיְבִאֵ֖ם בָּבֶֽל׃


The Lord showed me, and, behold, two baskets of figs were set before the temple of the Lord, after Nevukhadreżżar king of Bavel had carried away captive Yekhonyahu the son of Yehoyaqim the king of Yehuda, and the princes of Yehuda with the carpenters and smiths, from Yerushalayim, and had brought them to Bavel.


###### Jeremiah 24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/24:2)


הַדּ֣וּד אֶחָ֗ד תְּאֵנִים֙ טֹב֣וֹת מְאֹ֔ד כִּתְאֵנֵ֖י הַבַּכֻּר֑וֹת וְהַדּ֣וּד אֶחָ֗ד תְּאֵנִים֙ רָע֣וֹת מְאֹ֔ד אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־תֵאָכַ֖לְנָה מֵרֹֽעַ׃{פ}


One basket had very good figs, like the figs that are first ripe: and the other basket had very poor figs, which could not be eaten, they were so poor.


###### Jeremiah 24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/24:3)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר יְהֹוָ֜ה אֵלַ֗י מָה־אַתָּ֤ה רֹאֶה֙ יִרְמְיָ֔הוּ וָאֹמַ֖ר תְּאֵנִ֑ים הַתְּאֵנִ֤ים הַטֹּבוֹת֙ טֹב֣וֹת מְאֹ֔ד וְהָֽרָעוֹת֙ רָע֣וֹת מְאֹ֔ד אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־תֵאָכַ֖לְנָה מֵרֹֽעַ׃{פ}


Then said the Lord to me, What seest thou, Yirmeyahu? And I said, Figs; the good figs, very good; and the bad, very bad, that cannot be eaten, they are so poor.


###### Jeremiah 24:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/24:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/24:4)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


Again the word of the Lord came to me, saying,


###### Jeremiah 24:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/24:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/24:5)


כֹּֽה־אָמַ֤ר יְהֹוָה֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל כַּתְּאֵנִ֥ים הַטֹּב֖וֹת הָאֵ֑לֶּה כֵּֽן־אַכִּ֞יר אֶת־גָּל֣וּת יְהוּדָ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר שִׁלַּ֜חְתִּי מִן־הַמָּק֥וֹם הַזֶּ֛ה אֶ֥רֶץ כַּשְׂדִּ֖ים לְטוֹבָֽה׃


Thus says the Lord, the God of Yisra᾽el; Like these good figs, so will I favourably regard those that are carried away captive of Yehuda, whom I have sent out of this place into the land of the Kasdim.


###### Jeremiah 24:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/24:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/24:6)


וְשַׂמְתִּ֨י עֵינִ֤י עֲלֵיהֶם֙ לְטוֹבָ֔ה וַהֲשִׁבֹתִ֖ים עַל־הָאָ֣רֶץ הַזֹּ֑את וּבְנִיתִים֙ וְלֹ֣א אֶהֱרֹ֔ס וּנְטַעְתִּ֖ים וְלֹ֥א אֶתּֽוֹשׁ׃


For I will set my eyes upon them for good, and I will bring them again to this land: and I will build them, and not pull them down; and I will plant them, and not pluck them up.


###### Jeremiah 24:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/24:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/24:7)


וְנָתַתִּי֩ לָהֶ֨ם לֵ֜ב לָדַ֣עַת אֹתִ֗י כִּ֚י אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה וְהָיוּ־לִ֣י לְעָ֔ם וְאָ֣נֹכִ֔י אֶהְיֶ֥ה לָהֶ֖ם לֵאלֹהִ֑ים כִּֽי־יָשֻׁ֥בוּ אֵלַ֖י בְּכׇל־לִבָּֽם׃{ס}


And I will give them a heart to know me, that I am the Lord: and they shall be my people, and I will be their God: for they shall return to me with their whole heart.


###### Jeremiah 24:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/24:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/24:8)


וְכַתְּאֵנִים֙ הָרָע֔וֹת אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־תֵאָכַ֖לְנָה מֵרֹ֑עַ כִּי־כֹ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה כֵּ֣ן אֶ֠תֵּ֠ן אֶת־צִדְקִיָּ֨הוּ מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֤ה וְאֶת־שָׂרָיו֙ וְאֵ֣ת**׀** שְׁאֵרִ֣ית יְרוּשָׁלַ֗͏ִם הַנִּשְׁאָרִים֙ בָּאָ֣רֶץ הַזֹּ֔את וְהַיֹּשְׁבִ֖ים בְּאֶ֥רֶץ מִצְרָֽיִם׃


And as the bad figs, which cannot be eaten, they are so evil; surely thus says the Lord, So will I make Żidqiyyahu the king of Yehuda, and his princes, and the residue of Yerushalayim, that remain in this land, and them that dwell in the land of Miżrayim:


###### Jeremiah 24:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/24:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/24:9)


וּנְתַתִּים֙ (לזועה) [לְזַעֲוָ֣ה] לְרָעָ֔ה לְכֹ֖ל מַמְלְכ֣וֹת הָאָ֑רֶץ לְחֶרְפָּ֤ה וּלְמָשָׁל֙ לִשְׁנִינָ֣ה וְלִקְלָלָ֔ה בְּכׇל־הַמְּקֹמ֖וֹת אֲשֶֽׁר־אַדִּיחֵ֥ם שָֽׁם׃


I will make them a horror for evil to all the kingdoms of the earth, a reproach and a proverb, a taunt and curse, in all the places into which I shall drive them.


###### Jeremiah 24:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/24:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/24:10)


וְשִׁלַּ֣חְתִּי בָ֔ם אֶת־הַחֶ֖רֶב אֶת־הָרָעָ֣ב וְאֶת־הַדָּ֑בֶר עַד־תֻּמָּם֙ מֵעַ֣ל הָאֲדָמָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־נָתַ֥תִּי לָהֶ֖ם וְלַאֲבוֹתֵיהֶֽם׃{פ}


And I will send the sword, the famine, and the pestilence, among them, till they are consumed from off the land that I gave to them and to their fathers.



## Chapter 25

###### Jeremiah 25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/25:1)


הַדָּבָ֞ר אֲשֶׁר־הָיָ֤ה עַֽל־יִרְמְיָ֙הוּ֙ עַל־כׇּל־עַ֣ם יְהוּדָ֔ה בַּשָּׁנָה֙ הָֽרְבִעִ֔ית לִיהוֹיָקִ֥ים בֶּן־יֹאשִׁיָּ֖הוּ מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֑ה הִ֗יא הַשָּׁנָה֙ הָרִ֣אשֹׁנִ֔ית לִנְבוּכַדְרֶאצַּ֖ר מֶ֥לֶךְ בָּבֶֽל׃


The word that came to Yirmeyahu concerning all the people of Yehuda in the fourth year of Yehoyaqim the son of Yoshiyyahu king of Yehuda, that was the first year of Nevukhadreżżar, king of Bavel;


###### Jeremiah 25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/25:2)


אֲשֶׁ֨ר דִּבֶּ֜ר יִרְמְיָ֤הוּ הַנָּבִיא֙ עַל־כׇּל־עַ֣ם יְהוּדָ֔ה וְאֶ֛ל כׇּל־יֹשְׁבֵ֥י יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם לֵאמֹֽר׃


which Yirmeyahu the prophet spoke to all the people of Yehuda, and to all the inhabitants of Yerushalayim, saying,


###### Jeremiah 25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/25:3)


מִן־שְׁלֹ֣שׁ עֶשְׂרֵ֣ה שָׁנָ֡ה לְיֹאשִׁיָּ֣הוּ בֶן־אָמוֹן֩ מֶ֨לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֜ה וְעַ֣ד**׀** הַיּ֣וֹם הַזֶּ֗ה זֶ֚ה שָׁלֹ֤שׁ וְעֶשְׂרִים֙ שָׁנָ֔ה הָיָ֥ה דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלָ֑י וָאֲדַבֵּ֧ר אֲלֵיכֶ֛ם אַשְׁכֵּ֥ים וְדַבֵּ֖ר וְלֹ֥א שְׁמַעְתֶּֽם׃


From the thirteenth year of Yoshiyyahu, the son of Amon king of Yehuda, and until this day, these twenty three years, the word of the Lord has come to me, and I have spoken to you, from morning till night; but you have not hearkened.


###### Jeremiah 25:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/25:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/25:4)


וְשָׁלַח֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֲלֵיכֶ֜ם אֶֽת־כׇּל־עֲבָדָ֧יו הַנְּבִאִ֛ים הַשְׁכֵּ֥ם וְשָׁלֹ֖חַ וְלֹ֣א שְׁמַעְתֶּ֑ם וְלֹא־הִטִּיתֶ֥ם אֶֽת־אׇזְנְכֶ֖ם לִשְׁמֹֽעַ׃


And the Lord has sent to you all his servants the prophets, sending them from morning till night; but you have not hearkened, nor inclined your ear to hear.


###### Jeremiah 25:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/25:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/25:5)


לֵאמֹ֗ר שֽׁוּבוּ־נָ֞א אִ֣ישׁ מִדַּרְכּ֤וֹ הָרָעָה֙ וּמֵרֹ֣עַ מַעַלְלֵיכֶ֔ם וּשְׁבוּ֙ עַל־הָ֣אֲדָמָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר נָתַ֧ן יְהֹוָ֛ה לָכֶ֖ם וְלַאֲבֽוֹתֵיכֶ֑ם לְמִן־עוֹלָ֖ם וְעַד־עוֹלָֽם׃


They said, Turn back now everyone from his evil way, and from the evil of your doings, and dwell in the land that the Lord has given to you and to your fathers for ever and ever:


###### Jeremiah 25:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/25:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/25:6)


וְאַל־תֵּלְכ֗וּ אַֽחֲרֵי֙ אֱלֹהִ֣ים אֲחֵרִ֔ים לְעׇבְדָ֖ם וּלְהִשְׁתַּחֲוֺ֣ת לָהֶ֑ם וְלֹֽא־תַכְעִ֤יסוּ אוֹתִי֙ בְּמַעֲשֵׂ֣ה יְדֵיכֶ֔ם וְלֹ֥א אָרַ֖ע לָכֶֽם׃


and do not go after other gods to serve them, and to worship them, and do not provoke me to anger with the works of your hands; and I will do you no hurt.


###### Jeremiah 25:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/25:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/25:7)


וְלֹא־שְׁמַעְתֶּ֥ם אֵלַ֖י נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה לְמַ֧עַן (הכעסוני) [הַכְעִיסֵ֛נִי] בְּמַעֲשֵׂ֥ה יְדֵיכֶ֖ם לְרַ֥ע לָכֶֽם׃{פ}


Yet you have not hearkened to me, says the Lord; that you might provoke me to anger with the work of your hands to your own hurt.
