###### Jeremiah 25:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/25:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/25:34)


הֵילִ֨ילוּ הָרֹעִ֜ים וְזַעֲק֗וּ וְהִֽתְפַּלְּשׁוּ֙ אַדִּירֵ֣י הַצֹּ֔אן כִּי־מָלְא֥וּ יְמֵיכֶ֖ם לִטְב֑וֹחַ וּתְפוֹצ֣וֹתִיכֶ֔ם וּנְפַלְתֶּ֖ם כִּכְלִ֥י חֶמְדָּֽה׃


Howl, shepherds, and cry; and roll yourselves in dust, you chief of the flock: for the days are altogether come about for you to be slaughtered and dispersed, and you shall fall like a choice vessel.


###### Jeremiah 25:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/25:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/25:35)


וְאָבַ֥ד מָנ֖וֹס מִן־הָרֹעִ֑ים וּפְלֵיטָ֖ה מֵאַדִּירֵ֥י הַצֹּֽאן׃


And the shepherds shall have no way to flee, nor the chief of the flock to escape.


###### Jeremiah 25:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/25:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/25:36)


ק֚וֹל צַעֲקַ֣ת הָרֹעִ֔ים וִֽילְלַ֖ת אַדִּירֵ֣י הַצֹּ֑אן כִּֽי־שֹׁדֵ֥ד יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶת־מַרְעִיתָֽם׃


A voice of the cry of the shepherds, and a howling of the chief of the flock, shall be heard: for the Lord has ruined their pasture.


###### Jeremiah 25:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/25:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/25:37)


וְנָדַ֖מּוּ נְא֣וֹת הַשָּׁל֑וֹם מִפְּנֵ֖י חֲר֥וֹן אַף־יְהֹוָֽה׃


And the peaceable pastures are silent because of the fierce anger of the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 25:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/25:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/25:38)


עָזַ֥ב כַּכְּפִ֖יר סֻכּ֑וֹ כִּֽי־הָיְתָ֤ה אַרְצָם֙ לְשַׁמָּ֔ה מִפְּנֵי֙ חֲר֣וֹן הַיּוֹנָ֔ה וּמִפְּנֵ֖י חֲר֥וֹן אַפּֽוֹ׃{פ}


He has forsaken his covert, like the lion: for their land is desolate because of the fierceness of the oppressor, and because of his fierce anger.



## Chapter 26

###### Jeremiah 26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:1)


בְּרֵאשִׁ֗ית מַמְלְכ֛וּת יְהוֹיָקִ֥ים בֶּן־יֹאשִׁיָּ֖הוּ מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֑ה הָיָה֙ הַדָּבָ֣ר הַזֶּ֔ה מֵאֵ֥ת יְהֹוָ֖ה לֵאמֹֽר׃


In the beginning of the reign of Yehoyaqim son of Yoshiyyahu king of Yehuda came this word from the Lord, saying,


###### Jeremiah 26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:2)


כֹּ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה עֲמֹד֮ בַּחֲצַ֣ר בֵּית־יְהֹוָה֒ וְדִבַּרְתָּ֞ עַל־כׇּל־עָרֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֗ה הַבָּאִים֙ לְהִשְׁתַּחֲוֺ֣ת בֵּית־יְהֹוָ֔ה אֵ֚ת כׇּל־הַדְּבָרִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר צִוִּיתִ֖יךָ לְדַבֵּ֣ר אֲלֵיהֶ֑ם אַל־תִּגְרַ֖ע דָּבָֽר׃


Thus says the Lord; Stand in the court of the Lord’s house, and speak to all the cities of Yehuda, which come to worship in the Lord’s house, all the words that I command thee to speak to them; leave nothing out:


###### Jeremiah 26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:3)


אוּלַ֣י יִשְׁמְע֔וּ וְיָשֻׁ֕בוּ אִ֖ישׁ מִדַּרְכּ֣וֹ הָרָעָ֑ה וְנִחַמְתִּ֣י אֶל־הָרָעָ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר אָנֹכִ֤י חֹשֵׁב֙ לַעֲשׂ֣וֹת לָהֶ֔ם מִפְּנֵ֖י רֹ֥עַ מַעַלְלֵיהֶֽם׃


Perhaps they will hearken, and turn every man from his evil way, that I may relent of the evil, which I purpose to do to them because of the evil of their doings.


###### Jeremiah 26:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:4)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֲלֵיהֶ֔ם כֹּ֖ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֑ה אִם־לֹ֤א תִשְׁמְעוּ֙ אֵלַ֔י לָלֶ֙כֶת֙ בְּת֣וֹרָתִ֔י אֲשֶׁ֥ר נָתַ֖תִּי לִפְנֵיכֶֽם׃


And thou shalt say to them, Thus says the Lord; If you will not hearken to me, to walk in my Tora, which I have set before you,


###### Jeremiah 26:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:5)


לִשְׁמֹ֗עַ עַל־דִּבְרֵי֙ עֲבָדַ֣י הַנְּבִאִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר אָנֹכִ֖י שֹׁלֵ֣חַ אֲלֵיכֶ֑ם וְהַשְׁכֵּ֥ם וְשָׁלֹ֖חַ וְלֹ֥א שְׁמַעְתֶּֽם׃


to hearken to the words of my servants the prophets, whom I send to you, (sending from morning to night, but you have not hearkened;)


###### Jeremiah 26:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:6)


וְנָתַתִּ֛י אֶת־הַבַּ֥יִת הַזֶּ֖ה כְּשִׁלֹ֑ה וְאֶת־הָעִ֤יר (הזאתה) [הַזֹּאת֙] אֶתֵּ֣ן לִקְלָלָ֔ה לְכֹ֖ל גּוֹיֵ֥ הָאָֽרֶץ׃{פ}


then will I make this house like Shilo, and will make this city a curse to all the nations of the earth.


###### Jeremiah 26:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:7)


וַֽיִּשְׁמְע֛וּ הַכֹּהֲנִ֥ים וְהַנְּבִאִ֖ים וְכׇל־הָעָ֑ם אֶֽת־יִרְמְיָ֔הוּ מְדַבֵּ֛ר אֶת־הַדְּבָרִ֥ים הָאֵ֖לֶּה בְּבֵ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


So the priests and the prophets and all the people heard Yirmeyahu as he spoke these words in the house of the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 26:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:8)


וַיְהִ֣י**׀** כְּכַלּ֣וֹת יִרְמְיָ֗הוּ לְדַבֵּר֙ אֵ֣ת כׇּל־אֲשֶׁר־צִוָּ֣ה יְהֹוָ֔ה לְדַבֵּ֖ר אֶל־כׇּל־הָעָ֑ם וַיִּתְפְּשׂ֨וּ אֹת֜וֹ הַכֹּהֲנִ֧ים וְהַנְּבִיאִ֛ים וְכׇל־הָעָ֥ם לֵאמֹ֖ר מ֥וֹת תָּמֽוּת׃


Now it came to pass, when Yirmeyahu had made an end of speaking all that the Lord had commanded him to speak to all the people, that the priests and the prophets and all the people took hold of him, saying, Thou shalt surely die.


###### Jeremiah 26:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:9)


מַדּ֩וּעַ֩ נִבֵּ֨יתָ בְשֵׁם־יְהֹוָ֜ה לֵאמֹ֗ר כְּשִׁלוֹ֙ יִֽהְיֶה֙ הַבַּ֣יִת הַזֶּ֔ה וְהָעִ֥יר הַזֹּ֛את תֶּחֱרַ֖ב מֵאֵ֣ין יוֹשֵׁ֑ב וַיִּקָּהֵ֧ל כׇּל־הָעָ֛ם אֶֽל־יִרְמְיָ֖הוּ בְּבֵ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


Why hast thou prophesied in the name of the Lord, saying, This house shall be like Shilo, and this city shall be desolate without inhabitant? And all the people were gathered against Yirmeyahu in the house of the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 26:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:10)


וַֽיִּשְׁמְע֣וּ**׀** שָׂרֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֗ה אֵ֚ת הַדְּבָרִ֣ים הָאֵ֔לֶּה וַיַּעֲל֥וּ מִבֵּית־הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֑ה וַיֵּ֥שְׁב֛וּ בְּפֶ֥תַח שַֽׁעַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה הֶחָדָֽשׁ׃{ס}


When the princes of Yehuda heard these things, then they came up from the king’s house unto the house of the Lord, and sat down in the entry of the new gate of the Lord’s house.


###### Jeremiah 26:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:11)


וַיֹּ֨אמְר֜וּ הַכֹּהֲנִ֤ים וְהַנְּבִאִים֙ אֶל־הַשָּׂרִ֔ים וְאֶל־כׇּל־הָעָ֖ם לֵאמֹ֑ר מִשְׁפַּט־מָ֙וֶת֙ לָאִ֣ישׁ הַזֶּ֔ה כִּ֤י נִבָּא֙ אֶל־הָעִ֣יר הַזֹּ֔את כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר שְׁמַעְתֶּ֖ם בְּאׇזְנֵיכֶֽם׃


Then spoke the priests and the prophets to the princes and to all the people, saying, This man is worthy to die; for he has prophesied against this city, as you have heard with your ears.


###### Jeremiah 26:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:12)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר יִרְמְיָ֙הוּ֙ אֶל־כׇּל־הַשָּׂרִ֔ים וְאֶל־כׇּל־הָעָ֖ם לֵאמֹ֑ר יְהֹוָ֣ה שְׁלָחַ֗נִי לְהִנָּבֵ֞א אֶל־הַבַּ֤יִת הַזֶּה֙ וְאֶל־הָעִ֣יר הַזֹּ֔את אֵ֥ת כׇּל־הַדְּבָרִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר שְׁמַעְתֶּֽם׃


Then spoke Yirmeyahu to all the princes and to all the people, saying, The Lord sent me to prophesy against this house and against this city all the words that you have heard.


###### Jeremiah 26:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:13)


וְעַתָּ֗ה הֵיטִ֤יבוּ דַרְכֵיכֶם֙ וּמַ֣עַלְלֵיכֶ֔ם וְשִׁמְע֕וּ בְּק֖וֹל יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֑ם וְיִנָּחֵ֣ם יְהֹוָ֔ה אֶל־הָ֣רָעָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר דִּבֶּ֖ר עֲלֵיכֶֽם׃


Therefore now amend your ways and your doings, and obey the voice of the Lord your God; and the Lord will relent of the evil that he has pronounced against you.


###### Jeremiah 26:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:14)


וַאֲנִ֖י הִנְנִ֣י בְיֶדְכֶ֑ם עֲשׂוּ־לִ֛י כַּטּ֥וֹב וְכַיָּשָׁ֖ר בְּעֵינֵיכֶֽם׃


As for me, behold, I am in your hand: do with me as seems good and proper in your eyes.


###### Jeremiah 26:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:15)


אַ֣ךְ**׀** יָדֹ֣עַ תֵּדְע֗וּ כִּ֣י אִם־מְמִתִ֣ים אַתֶּם֮ אֹתִי֒ כִּי־דָ֣ם נָקִ֗י אַתֶּם֙ נֹתְנִ֣ים עֲלֵיכֶ֔ם וְאֶל־הָעִ֥יר הַזֹּ֖את וְאֶל־יֹשְׁבֶ֑יהָ כִּ֣י בֶאֱמֶ֗ת שְׁלָחַ֤נִי יְהֹוָה֙ עֲלֵיכֶ֔ם לְדַבֵּר֙ בְּאׇזְנֵיכֶ֔ם אֵ֥ת כׇּל־הַדְּבָרִ֖ים הָאֵֽלֶּה׃{ס}


But know for certain, that if you put me to death, you shall surely bring innocent blood upon yourselves, and upon this city, and upon its inhabitants: for of a truth the Lord has sent me to you to speak all these words in your ears.


###### Jeremiah 26:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:16)


וַיֹּאמְר֤וּ הַשָּׂרִים֙ וְכׇל־הָעָ֔ם אֶל־הַכֹּהֲנִ֖ים וְאֶל־הַנְּבִיאִ֑ים אֵין־לָאִ֤ישׁ הַזֶּה֙ מִשְׁפַּט־מָ֔וֶת כִּ֗י בְּשֵׁ֛ם יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵ֖ינוּ דִּבֶּ֥ר אֵלֵֽינוּ׃


Then said the princes and all the people to the priests and to the prophets; This man is not worthy to die: for he has spoken to us in the name of the Lord our God.


###### Jeremiah 26:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:17)


וַיָּקֻ֣מוּ אֲנָשִׁ֔ים מִזִּקְנֵ֖י הָאָ֑רֶץ וַיֹּ֣אמְר֔וּ אֶֽל־כׇּל־קְהַ֥ל הָעָ֖ם לֵאמֹֽר׃


Then rose up certain of the elders of the land, and spoke to all the assembly of the people, saying,


###### Jeremiah 26:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:18)


(מיכיה) [מִיכָה֙] הַמּ֣וֹרַשְׁתִּ֔י הָיָ֣ה נִבָּ֔א בִּימֵ֖י חִזְקִיָּ֣הוּ מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֑ה וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֶל־כׇּל־עַם֩ יְהוּדָ֨ה לֵאמֹ֜ר כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר**׀** יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֗וֹת צִיּ֞וֹן שָׂדֶ֤ה תֵחָרֵשׁ֙ וִירוּשָׁלַ֙יִם֙ עִיִּ֣ים תִּֽהְיֶ֔ה וְהַ֥ר הַבַּ֖יִת לְבָמ֥וֹת יָֽעַר׃


Mikha the Morashti prophesied in the days of Ḥizqiyyahu king of Yehuda, and spoke to all the people of Yehuda, saying, Thus says the Lord of hosts; Żiyyon shall be plowed like a field, and Yerushalayim shall become heaps, and the mountain of the house as the high places of a forest.


###### Jeremiah 26:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:19)


הֶהָמֵ֣ת הֱ֠מִתֻ֠הוּ חִזְקִיָּ֨הוּ מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֜ה וְכׇל־יְהוּדָ֗ה הֲלֹא֮ יָרֵ֣א אֶת־יְהֹוָה֒ וַיְחַל֙ אֶת־פְּנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה וַיִּנָּ֣חֶם יְהֹוָ֔ה אֶל־הָרָעָ֖ה אֲשֶׁר־דִּבֶּ֣ר עֲלֵיהֶ֑ם וַאֲנַ֗חְנוּ עֹשִׂ֛ים רָעָ֥ה גְדוֹלָ֖ה עַל־נַפְשׁוֹתֵֽינוּ׃


Did Ḥizqiyyahu king of Yehuda and all Yehuda put him to death? did he not rather fear the Lord, and beseech the Lord, and the Lord repented of the evil which he had pronounced against them? And shall we bring such a great evil against our souls?


###### Jeremiah 26:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:20)


וְגַם־אִ֗ישׁ הָיָ֤ה מִתְנַבֵּא֙ בְּשֵׁ֣ם יְהֹוָ֔ה אוּרִיָּ֙הוּ֙ בֶּֽן־שְׁמַעְיָ֔הוּ מִקִּרְיַ֖ת הַיְּעָרִ֑ים וַיִּנָּבֵ֞א עַל־הָעִ֤יר הַזֹּאת֙ וְעַל־הָאָ֣רֶץ הַזֹּ֔את כְּכֹ֖ל דִּבְרֵ֥י יִרְמְיָֽהוּ׃


And there was also a man that prophesied in the name of the Lord, Uriyyahu the son of Shema῾yahu of Qiryat-hayye῾arim, who prophesied against this city and against this land according to all the words of Yirmeyahu:


###### Jeremiah 26:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:21)


וַיִּשְׁמַ֣ע הַמֶּֽלֶךְ־יְ֠הוֹיָקִ֠ים וְכׇל־גִּבּוֹרָ֤יו וְכׇל־הַשָּׂרִים֙ אֶת־דְּבָרָ֔יו וַיְבַקֵּ֥שׁ הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ הֲמִית֑וֹ וַיִּשְׁמַ֤ע אוּרִיָּ֙הוּ֙ וַיִּרָ֔א וַיִּבְרַ֖ח וַיָּבֹ֥א מִצְרָֽיִם׃


and when Yehoyaqim the king, with all his mighty men, and all the princes, heard his words, the king sought to put him to death: but when Uriyyahu heard it, he was afraid, and fled, and went into Miżrayim;


###### Jeremiah 26:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:22)


וַיִּשְׁלַ֞ח הַמֶּ֧לֶךְ יְהוֹיָקִ֛ים אֲנָשִׁ֖ים מִצְרָ֑יִם אֵ֣ת אֶלְנָתָ֧ן בֶּן־עַכְבּ֛וֹר וַאֲנָשִׁ֥ים אִתּ֖וֹ אֶל־מִצְרָֽיִם׃


and Yehoyaqim the king sent men to Miżrayim, namely, Elnatan the son of ῾Akhbor, and others with him into Miżrayim.


###### Jeremiah 26:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:23)


וַיּוֹצִ֨יאוּ אֶת־אוּרִיָּ֜הוּ מִמִּצְרַ֗יִם וַיְבִאֻ֙הוּ֙ אֶל־הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ יְהוֹיָקִ֔ים וַיַּכֵּ֖הוּ בֶּחָ֑רֶב וַיַּשְׁלֵךְ֙ אֶת־נִבְלָת֔וֹ אֶל־קִבְרֵ֖י בְּנֵ֥י הָעָֽם׃


And they fetched Uriyyahu out of Miżrayim, and brought him to Yehoyaqim the king, who slew him with the sword, and cast his dead body upon the graves of the common people.


###### Jeremiah 26:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/26:24)


אַ֗ךְ יַ֚ד אֲחִיקָ֣ם בֶּן־שָׁפָ֔ן הָיְתָ֖ה אֶֽת־יִרְמְיָ֑הוּ לְבִלְתִּ֛י תֵּת־אֹת֥וֹ בְיַד־הָעָ֖ם לַהֲמִיתֽוֹ׃{פ}


Nevertheless the hand of Aĥiqam the son of Shafan was with Yirmeyahu, that they should not give him into the hand of the people to put him to death.



## Chapter 27

###### Jeremiah 27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:1)


בְּרֵאשִׁ֗ית מַמְלֶ֛כֶת יְהוֹיָקִ֥ם בֶּן־יֹאושִׁיָּ֖הוּ מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֑ה הָיָ֞ה הַדָּבָ֤ר הַזֶּה֙ אֶֽל־יִרְמְיָ֔ה מֵאֵ֥ת יְהֹוָ֖ה לֵאמֹֽר׃


In the beginning of the reign of Yehoyaqim the son of Yoshiyyahu king of Yehuda, came this word to Yirmeyahu from the Lord, saying,


###### Jeremiah 27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:2)


כֹּֽה־אָמַ֤ר יְהֹוָה֙ אֵלַ֔י עֲשֵׂ֣ה לְךָ֔ מוֹסֵר֖וֹת וּמֹט֑וֹת וּנְתַתָּ֖ם עַל־צַוָּארֶֽךָ׃


Thus says the Lord to me; Make bonds and bars for thyself, and put them upon thy neck,


###### Jeremiah 27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:3)


וְשִׁלַּחְתָּם֩ אֶל־מֶ֨לֶךְ אֱד֜וֹם וְאֶל־מֶ֣לֶךְ מוֹאָ֗ב וְאֶל־מֶ֙לֶךְ֙ בְּנֵ֣י עַמּ֔וֹן וְאֶל־מֶ֥לֶךְ צֹ֖ר וְאֶל־מֶ֣לֶךְ צִיד֑וֹן בְּיַ֤ד מַלְאָכִים֙ הַבָּאִ֣ים יְרוּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם אֶל־צִדְקִיָּ֖הוּ מֶ֥לֶךְ יְהוּדָֽה׃


and send them to the king of Edom, and to the king of Mo᾽av, and to the king of the children of ῾Ammon, and to the king of Żor, and to the king of Żidon, by the hand of the messengers which come to Yerushalayim to Żidqiyyahu king of Yehuda;


###### Jeremiah 27:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:4)


וְצִוִּיתָ֣ אֹתָ֔ם אֶל־אֲדֹנֵיהֶ֖ם לֵאמֹ֑ר כֹּה־אָמַ֞ר יְהֹוָ֤ה צְבָאוֹת֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל כֹּ֥ה תֹאמְר֖וּ אֶל־אֲדֹנֵיכֶֽם׃


and command them to say to their masters, Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Yisra᾽el; Thus shall you say to your masters;


###### Jeremiah 27:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:5)


אָנֹכִ֞י עָשִׂ֣יתִי אֶת־הָאָ֗רֶץ אֶת־הָאָדָ֤ם וְאֶת־הַבְּהֵמָה֙ אֲשֶׁר֙ עַל־פְּנֵ֣י הָאָ֔רֶץ בְּכֹחִי֙ הַגָּד֔וֹל וּבִזְרוֹעִ֖י הַנְּטוּיָ֑ה וּנְתַתִּ֕יהָ לַאֲשֶׁ֖ר יָשַׁ֥ר בְּעֵינָֽי׃


I have made the earth, the man and the beast that are upon the ground, by my great power and by my outstretched arm, and have given it to whom it seemed proper in my eyes.


###### Jeremiah 27:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:6)


וְעַתָּ֗ה אָֽנֹכִי֙ נָתַ֙תִּי֙ אֶת־כׇּל־הָאֲרָצ֣וֹת הָאֵ֔לֶּה בְּיַ֛ד נְבוּכַדְנֶאצַּ֥ר מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֖ל עַבְדִּ֑י וְגַם֙ אֶת־חַיַּ֣ת הַשָּׂדֶ֔ה נָתַ֥תִּי ל֖וֹ לְעׇבְדֽוֹ׃


And now have I given all these lands to the hand of Nevukhadneżżar the king of Bavel, my servant; and the beasts of the field have I given him also to serve him.


###### Jeremiah 27:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:7)


וְעָבְד֤וּ אֹתוֹ֙ כׇּל־הַגּוֹיִ֔ם וְאֶת־בְּנ֖וֹ וְאֶֽת־בֶּן־בְּנ֑וֹ עַ֣ד בֹּא־עֵ֤ת אַרְצוֹ֙ גַּם־ה֔וּא וְעָ֤בְדוּ בוֹ֙ גּוֹיִ֣ם רַבִּ֔ים וּמְלָכִ֖ים גְּדֹלִֽים׃


And all nations shall serve him, and his son, and his son’s son, until the time of his own land come also: and then many nations and great kings shall make him a slave.


###### Jeremiah 27:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:8)


וְהָיָ֨ה הַגּ֜וֹי וְהַמַּמְלָכָ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר לֹֽא־יַעַבְד֤וּ אֹתוֹ֙ אֶת־נְבוּכַדְנֶאצַּ֣ר מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֔ל וְאֵ֨ת אֲשֶׁ֤ר לֹֽא־יִתֵּן֙ אֶת־צַוָּאר֔וֹ בְּעֹ֖ל מֶ֣לֶךְ בָּבֶ֑ל בַּחֶ֩רֶב֩ וּבָרָעָ֨ב וּבַדֶּ֜בֶר אֶפְקֹ֨ד עַל־הַגּ֤וֹי הַהוּא֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה עַד־תֻּמִּ֥י אֹתָ֖ם בְּיָדֽוֹ׃


And it shall come to pass, that the nation and kingdom which will not serve this Nevukhadneżżar the king of Bavel, and that will not put their neck under the yoke of the king of Bavel, that nation will I punish, says the Lord, with the sword, and with the famine, and with the pestilence, until I have consumed them by his hand.


###### Jeremiah 27:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:9)


וְ֠אַתֶּ֠ם אַל־תִּשְׁמְע֨וּ אֶל־נְבִיאֵיכֶ֜ם וְאֶל־קֹסְמֵיכֶ֗ם וְאֶל֙ חֲלֹמֹ֣תֵיכֶ֔ם וְאֶל־עֹנְנֵיכֶ֖ם וְאֶל־כַּשָּׁפֵיכֶ֑ם אֲשֶׁר־הֵ֞ם אֹמְרִ֤ים אֲלֵיכֶם֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר לֹ֥א תַעַבְד֖וּ אֶת־מֶ֥לֶךְ בָּבֶֽל׃


Therefore hearken not to your prophets, nor to your diviners, nor to your dreamers, nor to your enchanters, nor to your sorcerers, who speak to you, saying, You shall not serve the king of Bavel:


###### Jeremiah 27:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:10)


כִּ֣י שֶׁ֔קֶר הֵ֖ם נִבְּאִ֣ים לָכֶ֑ם לְמַ֨עַן הַרְחִ֤יק אֶתְכֶם֙ מֵעַ֣ל אַדְמַתְכֶ֔ם וְהִדַּחְתִּ֥י אֶתְכֶ֖ם וַאֲבַדְתֶּֽם׃


for they prophesy a lie to you, to remove you far from your land; and that I should drive you out, and you should perish.


###### Jeremiah 27:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:11)


וְהַגּ֗וֹי אֲשֶׁ֨ר יָבִ֧יא אֶת־צַוָּאר֛וֹ בְּעֹ֥ל מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֖ל וַעֲבָד֑וֹ וְהִנַּחְתִּ֤יו עַל־אַדְמָתוֹ֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה וַעֲבָדָ֖הּ וְיָ֥שַׁב בָּֽהּ׃


But the nations that bring their neck under the yoke of the king of Bavel, and serve him, those will I let remain still in their own land, says the Lord; and they shall till it, and dwell in it.


###### Jeremiah 27:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:12)


וְאֶל־צִדְקִיָּ֤ה מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָה֙ דִּבַּ֔רְתִּי כְּכׇל־הַדְּבָרִ֥ים הָאֵ֖לֶּה לֵאמֹ֑ר הָבִ֨יאוּ אֶת־צַוְּארֵיכֶ֜ם בְּעֹ֣ל מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֗ל וְעִבְד֥וּ אֹת֛וֹ וְעַמּ֖וֹ וִֽחְיֽוּ׃


I spoke also to Żidqiyya king of Yehuda according to all these words, saying, Bring your necks under the yoke of the king of Bavel, and serve him and his people, and live.


###### Jeremiah 27:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:13)


לָ֤מָּה תָמ֙וּתוּ֙ אַתָּ֣ה וְעַמֶּ֔ךָ בַּחֶ֖רֶב בָּרָעָ֣ב וּבַדָּ֑בֶר כַּֽאֲשֶׁר֙ דִּבֶּ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה אֶל־הַגּ֕וֹי אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־יַעֲבֹ֖ד אֶת־מֶ֥לֶךְ בָּבֶֽל׃


Why will you die, thou and thy people, by the sword, by the famine, and by the pestilence, as the Lord has spoken against the nation that will not serve the king of Bavel?


###### Jeremiah 27:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:14)


וְאַֽל־תִּשְׁמְע֞וּ אֶל־דִּבְרֵ֣י הַנְּבִאִ֗ים הָאֹמְרִ֤ים אֲלֵיכֶם֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר לֹ֥א תַעַבְד֖וּ אֶת־מֶ֣לֶךְ בָּבֶ֑ל כִּ֣י שֶׁ֔קֶר הֵ֖ם נִבְּאִ֥ים לָכֶֽם׃


Therefore hearken not to the words of the prophets that speak to you, saying, You shall not serve the king of Bavel; for they prophesy a lie to you.


###### Jeremiah 27:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:15)


כִּ֣י לֹ֤א שְׁלַחְתִּים֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה וְהֵ֛ם נִבְּאִ֥ים בִּשְׁמִ֖י לַשָּׁ֑קֶר לְמַ֨עַן הַדִּיחִ֤י אֶתְכֶם֙ וַאֲבַדְתֶּ֔ם אַתֶּ֕ם וְהַנְּבִאִ֖ים הַֽנִּבְּאִ֥ים לָכֶֽם׃


For I have not sent them, says the Lord; but they prophesy a lie in my name; that I might drive you out, and that you might perish, you, and the prophets that prophesy to you.


###### Jeremiah 27:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:16)


וְאֶל־הַכֹּהֲנִים֩ וְאֶל־כׇּל־הָעָ֨ם הַזֶּ֜ה דִּבַּ֣רְתִּי לֵאמֹ֗ר כֹּה֮ אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָה֒ אַֽל־תִּשְׁמְע֞וּ אֶל־דִּבְרֵ֣י נְבִיאֵיכֶ֗ם הַֽנִּבְּאִ֤ים לָכֶם֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר הִנֵּ֨ה כְלֵ֧י בֵית־יְהֹוָ֛ה מוּשָׁבִ֥ים מִבָּבֶ֖לָה עַתָּ֣ה מְהֵרָ֑ה כִּ֣י שֶׁ֔קֶר הֵ֖מָּה נִבְּאִ֥ים לָכֶֽם׃


Also I spoke to the priests and to all this people, saying, Thus says the Lord; Hearken not to the words of your prophets that prophesy to you, saying, Behold, the vessels of the Lord’s house shall now shortly be brought back from Bavel: for they prophesy a lie to you.


###### Jeremiah 27:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:17)


אַל־תִּשְׁמְע֣וּ אֲלֵיהֶ֔ם עִבְד֥וּ אֶת־מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֖ל וִֽחְי֑וּ לָ֧מָּה תִֽהְיֶ֛ה הָעִ֥יר הַזֹּ֖את חׇרְבָּֽה׃


Hearken not to them; serve the king of Bavel, and live: why should this city be laid waste?


###### Jeremiah 27:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:18)


וְאִם־נְבִאִ֣ים הֵ֔ם וְאִם־יֵ֥שׁ דְּבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אִתָּ֑ם יִפְגְּעוּ־נָא֙ בַּיהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֔וֹת לְבִלְתִּי־בֹ֜אוּ הַכֵּלִ֣ים**׀** הַנּוֹתָרִ֣ים בְּבֵית־יְהֹוָ֗ה וּבֵ֨ית מֶ֧לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֛ה וּבִירוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם בָּבֶֽלָה׃


But if they are prophets, and if the word of the Lord be with them, let them now make intercession to the Lord of hosts, that the vessels which are left in the house of the Lord, and in the house of the king of Yehuda, and at Yerushalayim, go not to Bavel.


###### Jeremiah 27:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:19)


כִּ֣י כֹ֤ה אָמַר֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֔וֹת אֶל־הָֽעַמֻּדִ֔ים וְעַל־הַיָּ֖ם וְעַל־הַמְּכֹנ֑וֹת וְעַל֙ יֶ֣תֶר הַכֵּלִ֔ים הַנּוֹתָרִ֖ים בָּעִ֥יר הַזֹּֽאת׃


For thus says the Lord of hosts concerning the pillars, and concerning the sea, and concerning the bases, and concerning the residue of the vessels that remain in this city.


###### Jeremiah 27:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:20)


אֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹֽא־לְקָחָ֗ם נְבֽוּכַדְנֶאצַּר֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ בָּבֶ֔ל בַּ֠גְלוֹת֠וֹ אֶת־[יְכׇנְיָ֨ה] (יכוניה) בֶן־יְהוֹיָקִ֧ים מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֛ה מִירוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם בָּבֶ֑לָה וְאֵ֛ת כׇּל־חֹרֵ֥י יְהוּדָ֖ה וִירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


Which Nevukhadneżżar king of Bavel did not take, when he carried away Yekhonya the son of Yehoyaqim king of Yehuda captive from Yerushalayim to Bavel, and all the nobles of Yehuda and Yerushalayim:


###### Jeremiah 27:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:21)


כִּ֣י כֹ֥ה אָמַ֛ר יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל עַל־הַכֵּלִ֗ים הַנּֽוֹתָרִים֙ בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֔ה וּבֵ֥ית מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֖ה וִירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


for thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Yisra᾽el, concerning the vessels that remain in the house of the Lord, and in the house of the king of Yehuda and of Yerushalayim;


###### Jeremiah 27:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/27:22)


בָּבֶ֥לָה יוּבָ֖אוּ וְשָׁ֣מָּה יִֽהְי֑וּ עַ֠ד י֣וֹם פׇּקְדִ֤י אֹתָם֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה וְהַֽעֲלִיתִים֙ וַהֲשִׁ֣יבֹתִ֔ים אֶל־הַמָּק֖וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃{פ}


they shall be carried to Bavel, and there shall they be until the day that I take heed of them, says the Lord; then will I bring them up, and restore them to this place.



## Chapter 28

###### Jeremiah 28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:1)


וַיְהִ֣י**׀** בַּשָּׁנָ֣ה הַהִ֗יא בְּרֵאשִׁית֙ מַמְלֶ֙כֶת֙ צִדְקִיָּ֣ה מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֔ה (בשנת) [בַּשָּׁנָה֙] הָרְבִעִ֔ית בַּחֹ֖דֶשׁ הַחֲמִישִׁ֑י אָמַ֣ר אֵלַ֡י חֲנַנְיָה֩ בֶן־עַזּ֨וּר הַנָּבִ֜יא אֲשֶׁ֤ר מִגִּבְעוֹן֙ בְּבֵ֣ית יְהֹוָ֔ה לְעֵינֵ֧י הַכֹּהֲנִ֛ים וְכׇל־הָעָ֖ם לֵאמֹֽר׃


And it came to pass the same year, in the beginning of the reign of Żidqiyya king of Yehuda, in the fourth year and in the fifth month, that Ḥananya the son of Azur the prophet, who was of Giv῾on, spoke to me in the house of the Lord, in the presence of the priests and of all the people, saying,


###### Jeremiah 28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:2)


כֹּה־אָמַ֞ר יְהֹוָ֧ה צְבָא֛וֹת אֱלֹהֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל לֵאמֹ֑ר שָׁבַ֕רְתִּי אֶת־עֹ֖ל מֶ֥לֶךְ בָּבֶֽל׃


Thus speaks the Lord of hosts, the God of Yisra᾽el, saying, I have broken the yoke of the king of Bavel.


###### Jeremiah 28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:3)


בְּע֣וֹד**׀** שְׁנָתַ֣יִם יָמִ֗ים אֲנִ֤י מֵשִׁיב֙ אֶל־הַמָּק֣וֹם הַזֶּ֔ה אֶֽת־כׇּל־כְּלֵ֖י בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֑ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר לָקַ֜ח נְבוּכַדְנֶאצַּ֤ר מֶֽלֶךְ־בָּבֶל֙ מִן־הַמָּק֣וֹם הַזֶּ֔ה וַיְבִיאֵ֖ם בָּבֶֽל׃


Within two full years will I bring again to this place all the vessels of the Lord’s house, that Nevukhadneżżar king of Bavel took away from this place, and carried them to Bavel:


###### Jeremiah 28:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:4)


וְאֶת־יְכׇנְיָ֣ה בֶן־יְהוֹיָקִ֣ים מֶלֶךְ־יְ֠הוּדָ֠ה וְאֶת־כׇּל־גָּל֨וּת יְהוּדָ֜ה הַבָּאִ֣ים בָּבֶ֗לָה אֲנִ֥י מֵשִׁ֛יב אֶל־הַמָּק֥וֹם הַזֶּ֖ה נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה כִּ֣י אֶשְׁבֹּ֔ר אֶת־עֹ֖ל מֶ֥לֶךְ בָּבֶֽל׃


And I will bring again to this place Yekhonya the son of Yehoyaqim, king of Yehuda, with all the captives of Yehuda, that went into Bavel, says the Lord: for I will break the yoke of the king of Bavel.


###### Jeremiah 28:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:5)


וַיֹּ֙אמֶר֙ יִרְמְיָ֣ה הַנָּבִ֔יא אֶל־חֲנַנְיָ֖ה הַנָּבִ֑יא לְעֵינֵ֤י הַכֹּֽהֲנִים֙ וּלְעֵינֵ֣י כׇל־הָעָ֔ם הָעֹמְדִ֖ים בְּבֵ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


Then the prophet Yirmeya said to the prophet Ḥananya in the presence of the priests, and in the presence of all the people that stood in the house of the Lord,


###### Jeremiah 28:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:6)


וַיֹּ֙אמֶר֙ יִרְמְיָ֣ה הַנָּבִ֔יא אָמֵ֕ן כֵּ֖ן יַעֲשֶׂ֣ה יְהֹוָ֑ה יָקֵ֤ם יְהֹוָה֙ אֶת־דְּבָרֶ֔יךָ אֲשֶׁ֣ר נִבֵּ֗אתָ לְהָשִׁ֞יב כְּלֵ֤י בֵית־יְהֹוָה֙ וְכׇל־הַגּוֹלָ֔ה מִבָּבֶ֖ל אֶל־הַמָּק֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃


and the prophet Yirmeya said, Amen: the Lord do so: the Lord perform thy words which thou hast prophesied, to bring back the vessels of the Lord’s house, and all that were carried away captive, from Bavel to this place.


###### Jeremiah 28:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:7)


אַךְ־שְׁמַֽע־נָא֙ הַדָּבָ֣ר הַזֶּ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר אָנֹכִ֖י דֹּבֵ֣ר בְּאׇזְנֶ֑יךָ וּבְאׇזְנֵ֖י כׇּל־הָעָֽם׃


Nevertheless hear thou now this word that I speak in thy ears, and in the ears of all the people;


###### Jeremiah 28:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:8)


הַנְּבִיאִ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֨ר הָי֧וּ לְפָנַ֛י וּלְפָנֶ֖יךָ מִן־הָעוֹלָ֑ם וַיִּנָּ֨בְא֜וּ אֶל־אֲרָצ֤וֹת רַבּוֹת֙ וְעַל־מַמְלָכ֣וֹת גְּדֹל֔וֹת לְמִלְחָמָ֖ה וּלְרָעָ֥ה וּלְדָֽבֶר׃


the prophets that have been before me and before thee of old prophesied both against many countries, and against great kingdoms, of war, and of evil, and of pestilence.


###### Jeremiah 28:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:9)


הַנָּבִ֕יא אֲשֶׁ֥ר יִנָּבֵ֖א לְשָׁל֑וֹם בְּבֹא֙ דְּבַ֣ר הַנָּבִ֔יא יִוָּדַע֙ הַנָּבִ֔יא אֲשֶׁר־שְׁלָח֥וֹ יְהֹוָ֖ה בֶּאֱמֶֽת׃


As for the prophet who prophesies for peace, when the word of that prophet shall come to pass, then shall it be known that the Lord has truly sent the prophet.


###### Jeremiah 28:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:10)


וַיִּקַּ֞ח חֲנַנְיָ֤ה הַנָּבִיא֙ אֶת־הַמּוֹטָ֔ה מֵעַ֕ל צַוַּ֖אר יִרְמְיָ֣ה הַנָּבִ֑יא וַֽיִּשְׁבְּרֵֽהוּ׃


Then Ḥananya the prophet took the bar from off the prophet Yirmeya’s neck, and broke it.


###### Jeremiah 28:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:11)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר חֲנַנְיָה֩ לְעֵינֵ֨י כׇל־הָעָ֜ם לֵאמֹ֗ר כֹּה֮ אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָה֒ כָּ֣כָה אֶשְׁבֹּ֞ר אֶת־עֹ֣ל**׀** נְבֻכַדְנֶאצַּ֣ר מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֗ל בְּעוֹד֙ שְׁנָתַ֣יִם יָמִ֔ים מֵעַ֕ל צַוַּ֖אר כׇּל־הַגּוֹיִ֑ם וַיֵּ֛לֶךְ יִרְמְיָ֥ה הַנָּבִ֖יא לְדַרְכּֽוֹ׃{פ}


And Ḥananya spoke in the presence of all the people, saying, Thus said the Lord; Even so will I break the yoke of Nevukhadneżżar king of Bavel from the neck of all the nations within the space of two years. And the prophet Yirmeya went his way.


###### Jeremiah 28:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:12)


וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶֽל־יִרְמְיָ֑ה אַ֠חֲרֵ֠י שְׁב֞וֹר חֲנַנְיָ֤ה הַנָּבִיא֙ אֶת־הַמּוֹטָ֔ה מֵעַ֗ל צַוַּ֛אר יִרְמְיָ֥ה הַנָּבִ֖יא לֵאמֹֽר׃


Then the word of the Lord came to Yirmeyahu, after Ḥananya the prophet had broken the yoke from off the neck of the prophet Yirmeya, saying,


###### Jeremiah 28:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:13)


הָלוֹךְ֩ וְאָמַרְתָּ֨ אֶל־חֲנַנְיָ֜ה לֵאמֹ֗ר כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה מוֹטֹ֥ת עֵ֖ץ שָׁבָ֑רְתָּ וְעָשִׂ֥יתָ תַחְתֵּיהֶ֖ן מֹט֥וֹת בַּרְזֶֽל׃


Go and tell Ḥananya, saying, Thus says the Lord; Thou hast broken the bars of wood; but thou shalt make instead of them bars of iron.


###### Jeremiah 28:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:14)


כִּ֣י כֹֽה־אָמַר֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה צְבָא֜וֹת אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל עֹ֣ל בַּרְזֶ֡ל נָתַ֜תִּי עַל־צַוַּ֣אר**׀** כׇּל־הַגּוֹיִ֣ם הָאֵ֗לֶּה לַעֲבֹ֛ד אֶת־נְבֻכַדְנֶאצַּ֥ר מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֖ל וַעֲבָדֻ֑הוּ וְגַ֛ם אֶת־חַיַּ֥ת הַשָּׂדֶ֖ה נָתַ֥תִּי לֽוֹ׃


For thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Yisra᾽el; I have put a yoke of iron upon the neck of all these nations, that they may serve Nevukhadneżżar king of Bavel; and they shall serve him: and I have given him the beasts of the field also.


###### Jeremiah 28:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:15)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר יִרְמְיָ֧ה הַנָּבִ֛יא אֶל־חֲנַנְיָ֥ה הַנָּבִ֖יא שְׁמַֽע־נָ֣א חֲנַנְיָ֑ה לֹא־שְׁלָחֲךָ֣ יְהֹוָ֔ה וְאַתָּ֗ה הִבְטַ֛חְתָּ אֶת־הָעָ֥ם הַזֶּ֖ה עַל־שָֽׁקֶר׃


Then said the prophet Yirmeya to Ḥananya the prophet, Hear now, Ḥananya; The Lord has not sent thee; but thou makest this people to trust in a lie.


###### Jeremiah 28:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:16)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה הִנְנִי֙ מְשַֽׁלֵּחֲךָ֔ מֵעַ֖ל פְּנֵ֣י הָאֲדָמָ֑ה הַשָּׁנָה֙ אַתָּ֣ה מֵ֔ת כִּי־סָרָ֥ה דִבַּ֖רְתָּ אֶל־יְהֹוָֽה׃


Therefore thus says the Lord; Behold, I will cast thee from off the face of the earth: this year thou shalt die, because thou hast uttered rebellion against the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 28:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/28:17)


וַיָּ֛מׇת חֲנַנְיָ֥ה הַנָּבִ֖יא בַּשָּׁנָ֣ה הַהִ֑יא בַּחֹ֖דֶשׁ הַשְּׁבִיעִֽי׃{פ}


So Ḥananya the prophet died the same year in the seventh month.



## Chapter 29

###### Jeremiah 29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/29:1)


וְאֵ֙לֶּה֙ דִּבְרֵ֣י הַסֵּ֔פֶר אֲשֶׁ֥ר שָׁלַ֛ח יִרְמְיָ֥ה הַנָּבִ֖יא מִירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם אֶל־יֶ֜תֶר זִקְנֵ֣י הַגּוֹלָ֗ה וְאֶל־הַכֹּהֲנִ֤ים וְאֶל־הַנְּבִיאִים֙ וְאֶל־כׇּל־הָעָ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֨ר הֶגְלָ֧ה נְבוּכַדְנֶאצַּ֛ר מִירוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם בָּבֶֽלָה׃


Now these are the words of the letter that Yirmeya the prophet sent from Yerushalayim to the residue of the elders who were carried away captives, and to the priests, and to the prophets, and to all the people whom Nevukhadneżżar had carried away captive from Yerushalayim to Bavel:


###### Jeremiah 29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/29:2)


אַחֲרֵ֣י צֵ֣את יְכׇנְיָֽה־הַ֠מֶּ֠לֶךְ וְהַגְּבִירָ֨ה וְהַסָּרִיסִ֜ים שָׂרֵ֨י יְהוּדָ֧ה וִירוּשָׁלַ֛͏ִם וְהֶחָרָ֥שׁ וְהַמַּסְגֵּ֖ר מִירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


(after Yekhonya the king, and the queen mother, and the officers, the princes of Yehuda and Yerushalayim, and the craftsmen and the smiths, had departed from Yerushalayim;)


###### Jeremiah 29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/29:3)


בְּיַד֙ אֶלְעָשָׂ֣ה בֶן־שָׁפָ֔ן וּגְמַרְיָ֖ה בֶּן־חִלְקִיָּ֑ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר שָׁלַ֜ח צִדְקִיָּ֣ה מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֗ה אֶל־נְבוּכַדְנֶאצַּ֛ר מֶ֥לֶךְ בָּבֶ֖ל בָּבֶ֥לָה לֵאמֹֽר׃


by the hand of El῾asa the son of Shefan, and Gemarya the son of Ḥilqiyya, (whom Żidqiyya king of Yehuda had sent to Nevukhad­neżżar king of Bavel) to Bavel, saying,


###### Jeremiah 29:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/29:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/29:4)


כֹּ֥ה אָמַ֛ר יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל לְכׇ֨ל־הַגּוֹלָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־הִגְלֵ֥יתִי מִירוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם בָּבֶֽלָה׃


Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Yisra᾽el, to all that are carried away captives, whom I have caused to be carried away from Yerushalayim to Bavel;


###### Jeremiah 29:5

Then came the word of the Lord to Yirmeyahu, saying,


###### Jeremiah 29:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/29:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/29:31)


שְׁלַ֤ח עַל־כׇּל־הַגּוֹלָה֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה אֶל־שְׁמַעְיָ֖ה הַנֶּחֱלָמִ֑י יַ֡עַן אֲשֶׁר֩ נִבָּ֨א לָכֶ֜ם שְׁמַעְיָ֗ה וַֽאֲנִי֙ לֹ֣א שְׁלַחְתִּ֔יו וַיַּבְטַ֥ח אֶתְכֶ֖ם עַל־שָֽׁקֶר׃


Send to all them of the captivity, saying, Thus says the Lord concerning Shema῾ya the Neĥelamite; Because Shema῾ya has prophesied to you, and I sent him not, and he caused you to trust in a lie:


###### Jeremiah 29:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/29:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/29:32)


לָכֵ֞ן כֹּֽה־אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה הִנְנִ֨י פֹקֵ֜ד עַל־שְׁמַעְיָ֣ה הַנֶּחֱלָמִי֮ וְעַל־זַרְעוֹ֒ לֹא־יִהְיֶ֨ה ל֜וֹ אִ֣ישׁ**׀** יוֹשֵׁ֣ב**׀** בְּתוֹךְ־הָעָ֣ם הַזֶּ֗ה וְלֹֽא־יִרְאֶ֥ה בַטּ֛וֹב אֲשֶׁר־אֲנִ֥י עֹשֶֽׂה־לְעַמִּ֖י נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה כִּֽי־סָרָ֥ה דִבֶּ֖ר עַל־יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


therefore thus says the Lord; Behold, I will punish Shema῾ya the Neĥelamite, and his posterity: he shall not have a man to dwell among this people; neither shall he behold the good that I will do for my people, says the Lord; because he has uttered rebellion against the Lord.



## Chapter 30

###### Jeremiah 30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:1)


הַדָּבָר֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הָיָ֣ה אֶֽל־יִרְמְיָ֔הוּ מֵאֵ֥ת יְהֹוָ֖ה לֵאמֹֽר׃


The word that came to Yirmeyahu from the Lord, saying,


###### Jeremiah 30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:2)


כֹּֽה־אָמַ֧ר יְהֹוָ֛ה אֱלֹהֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל לֵאמֹ֑ר כְּתׇב־לְךָ֗ אֵ֧ת כׇּל־הַדְּבָרִ֛ים אֲשֶׁר־דִּבַּ֥רְתִּי אֵלֶ֖יךָ אֶל־סֵֽפֶר׃


Thus speaks the Lord God of Yisra᾽el, saying, Write all the words that I have spoken to thee in a book.


###### Jeremiah 30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:3)


כִּ֠י הִנֵּ֨ה יָמִ֤ים בָּאִים֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה וְ֠שַׁבְתִּ֠י אֶת־שְׁב֨וּת עַמִּ֧י יִשְׂרָאֵ֛ל וִֽיהוּדָ֖ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֑ה וַהֲשִׁבֹתִ֗ים אֶל־הָאָ֛רֶץ אֲשֶׁר־נָתַ֥תִּי לַאֲבוֹתָ֖ם וִֽירֵשֽׁוּהָ׃{פ}


For, lo, days are coming, says the Lord, when I will bring back the captivity of my people Yisra᾽el and Yehuda, says the Lord: and I will cause them to return to the land that I gave to their fathers, and they shall possess it.


###### Jeremiah 30:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:4)


וְאֵ֣לֶּה הַדְּבָרִ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֨ר דִּבֶּ֧ר יְהֹוָ֛ה אֶל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל וְאֶל־יְהוּדָֽה׃


And these are words that the Lord spoke concerning Yisra᾽el and concerning Yehuda.


###### Jeremiah 30:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:5)


כִּי־כֹה֙ אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה ק֥וֹל חֲרָדָ֖ה שָׁמָ֑עְנוּ פַּ֖חַד וְאֵ֥ין שָׁלֽוֹם׃


For thus says the Lord; We have heard a voice of trembling, of fear, and not of peace.


###### Jeremiah 30:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:6)


שַׁאֲלוּ־נָ֣א וּרְא֔וּ אִם־יֹלֵ֖ד זָכָ֑ר מַדּ֩וּעַ֩ רָאִ֨יתִי כׇל־גֶּ֜בֶר יָדָ֤יו עַל־חֲלָצָיו֙ כַּיּ֣וֹלֵדָ֔ה וְנֶהֶפְכ֥וּ כׇל־פָּנִ֖ים לְיֵרָקֽוֹן׃


Ask now, and see whether a man travails with child? why then do I see every man with his hands on his loins, as a woman in travail, and all faces are turned to paleness?


###### Jeremiah 30:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:7)


ה֗וֹי כִּ֥י גָד֛וֹל הַיּ֥וֹם הַה֖וּא מֵאַ֣יִן כָּמֹ֑הוּ וְעֵֽת־צָרָ֥ה הִיא֙ לְיַֽעֲקֹ֔ב וּמִמֶּ֖נָּה יִוָּשֵֽׁעַ׃


Alas! for that day is great, so that none is like it: and it is a time of trouble to Ya῾aqov; but he shall be saved out of it.


###### Jeremiah 30:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:8)


וְהָיָה֩ בַיּ֨וֹם הַה֜וּא נְאֻ֣ם**׀** יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֗וֹת אֶשְׁבֹּ֤ר עֻלּוֹ֙ מֵעַ֣ל צַוָּארֶ֔ךָ וּמוֹסְרוֹתֶ֖יךָ אֲנַתֵּ֑ק וְלֹא־יַעַבְדוּ־ב֥וֹ ע֖וֹד זָרִֽים׃


For it shall come to pass on that day, says the Lord of hosts, that I will break his yoke from off thy neck, and will burst thy hands, and strangers shall no more enslave him:


###### Jeremiah 30:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:9)


וְעָ֣בְד֔וּ אֵ֖ת יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹֽהֵיהֶ֑ם וְאֵת֙ דָּוִ֣ד מַלְכָּ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר אָקִ֖ים לָהֶֽם׃{ס}


but they shall serve the Lord their God, and David their king, whom I will raise up for them.


###### Jeremiah 30:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:10)


וְאַתָּ֡ה אַל־תִּירָא֩ עַבְדִּ֨י יַעֲקֹ֤ב נְאֻם־יְהֹוָה֙ וְאַל־תֵּחַ֣ת יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל כִּ֠י הִנְנִ֤י מוֹשִֽׁיעֲךָ֙ מֵֽרָח֔וֹק וְאֶֽת־זַרְעֲךָ֖ מֵאֶ֣רֶץ שִׁבְיָ֑ם וְשָׁ֧ב יַעֲקֹ֛ב וְשָׁקַ֥ט וְשַׁאֲנַ֖ן וְאֵ֥ין מַחֲרִֽיד׃


Therefore fear thou not, O my servant Ya῾aqov, says the Lord; neither be dismayed, O Yisra᾽el: for, lo, I will save thee from afar, and thy seed from the land of their captivity; and Ya῾aqov shall return, and shall be quiet and at ease, and none shall make him afraid.


###### Jeremiah 30:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:11)


כִּֽי־אִתְּךָ֥ אֲנִ֛י נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֖ה לְהֽוֹשִׁיעֶ֑ךָ כִּי֩ אֶעֱשֶׂ֨ה כָלָ֜ה בְּכׇֽל־הַגּוֹיִ֣ם**׀** אֲשֶׁ֧ר הֲפִצוֹתִ֣יךָ שָּׁ֗ם אַ֤ךְ אֹֽתְךָ֙ לֹֽא־אֶעֱשֶׂ֣ה כָלָ֔ה וְיִסַּרְתִּ֙יךָ֙ לַמִּשְׁפָּ֔ט וְנַקֵּ֖ה לֹ֥א אֲנַקֶּֽךָּ׃{ס}


For I am with thee, says the Lord, to save thee: for I will make a full end of all the nations where I have scattered thee, yet will I not make a full end of thee: but I will correct thee in due measure, and will not leave thee altogether unpunished.


###### Jeremiah 30:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:12)


כִּ֣י כֹ֥ה אָמַ֛ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אָנ֣וּשׁ לְשִׁבְרֵ֑ךְ נַחְלָ֖ה מַכָּתֵֽךְ׃


For thus says the Lord, Thy bruise is incurable, and thy wound is grievous.


###### Jeremiah 30:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:13)


אֵֽין־דָּ֥ן דִּינֵ֖ךְ לְמָז֑וֹר רְפֻא֥וֹת תְּעָלָ֖ה אֵ֥ין לָֽךְ׃


There is none to take up thy case, to bind up the wound: thou hast no healing medicines.


###### Jeremiah 30:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:14)


כׇּל־מְאַהֲבַ֣יִךְ שְׁכֵח֔וּךְ אוֹתָ֖ךְ לֹ֣א יִדְרֹ֑שׁוּ כִּי֩ מַכַּ֨ת אוֹיֵ֤ב הִכִּיתִיךְ֙ מוּסַ֣ר אַכְזָרִ֔י עַ֚ל רֹ֣ב עֲוֺנֵ֔ךְ עָצְמ֖וּ חַטֹּאתָֽיִךְ׃


All thy lovers have forgotten thee; they seek thee not; for I have wounded thee with the wound of an enemy, with the chastisement of a cruel one, for the multitude of thy iniquity; thy sins were increased.


###### Jeremiah 30:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:15)


מַה־תִּזְעַק֙ עַל־שִׁבְרֵ֔ךְ אָנ֖וּשׁ מַכְאֹבֵ֑ךְ עַ֣ל**׀** רֹ֣ב עֲוֺנֵ֗ךְ עָֽצְמוּ֙ חַטֹּאתַ֔יִךְ עָשִׂ֥יתִי אֵ֖לֶּה לָֽךְ׃


Why criest thou for thy hurt, that thy sorrow is incurable? for the multitude of thy iniquity, because thy sins were increased, I have done these things to thee.


###### Jeremiah 30:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:16)


לָכֵ֞ן כׇּל־אֹכְלַ֙יִךְ֙ יֵאָכֵ֔לוּ וְכׇל־צָרַ֥יִךְ כֻּלָּ֖ם בַּשְּׁבִ֣י יֵלֵ֑כוּ וְהָי֤וּ שֹׁאסַ֙יִךְ֙ לִמְשִׁסָּ֔ה וְכׇל־בֹּזְזַ֖יִךְ אֶתֵּ֥ן לָבַֽז׃


Therefore all they that devour thee shall be devoured; and all thy adversaries, every one of them, shall go into captivity; and they that spoil thee shall be a spoil, and all that plunder thee will I give for a prey.


###### Jeremiah 30:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:17)


כִּי֩ אַעֲלֶ֨ה אֲרֻכָ֥ה לָ֛ךְ וּמִמַּכּוֹתַ֥יִךְ אֶרְפָּאֵ֖ךְ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה כִּ֤י נִדָּחָה֙ קָ֣רְאוּ לָ֔ךְ צִיּ֣וֹן הִ֔יא דֹּרֵ֖שׁ אֵ֥ין לָֽהּ׃{ס}


For I will restore health to thee, and I will heal thee of thy wounds, says the Lord; because they called thee an Outcast, saying, This is Żiyyon, for whom no one cares.


###### Jeremiah 30:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:18)


כֹּ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה הִנְנִי־שָׁב֙ שְׁבוּת֙ אׇהֳלֵ֣י יַֽעֲק֔וֹב וּמִשְׁכְּנֹתָ֖יו אֲרַחֵ֑ם וְנִבְנְתָ֥ה עִיר֙ עַל־תִּלָּ֔הּ וְאַרְמ֖וֹן עַל־מִשְׁפָּט֥וֹ יֵשֵֽׁב׃


Thus says the Lord; Behold, I will bring back the captivity of Ya῾aqov’s tents, and have mercy on his dwellingplaces; and the city shall be built upon her own tel, and the palace shall stand on its proper place.


###### Jeremiah 30:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:19)


וְיָצָ֥א מֵהֶ֛ם תּוֹדָ֖ה וְק֣וֹל מְשַׂחֲקִ֑ים וְהִרְבִּתִים֙ וְלֹ֣א יִמְעָ֔טוּ וְהִכְבַּדְתִּ֖ים וְלֹ֥א יִצְעָֽרוּ׃


And out of them shall proceed thanksgiving and the voice of them that make merry: and I will multiply them, and they shall not be diminished; I will also glorify them, and they shall not be small.


###### Jeremiah 30:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:20)


וְהָי֤וּ בָנָיו֙ כְּקֶ֔דֶם וַעֲדָת֖וֹ לְפָנַ֣י תִּכּ֑וֹן וּפָ֣קַדְתִּ֔י עַ֖ל כׇּל־לֹחֲצָֽיו׃


Their children also shall be as before, and their congregation shall be established before me, and I will punish all that oppress them.


###### Jeremiah 30:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:21)


וְהָיָ֨ה אַדִּיר֜וֹ מִמֶּ֗נּוּ וּמֹֽשְׁלוֹ֙ מִקִּרְבּ֣וֹ יֵצֵ֔א וְהִקְרַבְתִּ֖יו וְנִגַּ֣שׁ אֵלָ֑י כִּי֩ מִ֨י הוּא־זֶ֜ה עָרַ֧ב אֶת־לִבּ֛וֹ לָגֶ֥שֶׁת אֵלַ֖י נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


And their prince shall be of themselves, and their governor shall proceed from the midst of them; and I will cause him to draw near, and he shall approach me: for who is this that engaged his heart to approach me? says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 30:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:22)


וִהְיִ֥יתֶם לִ֖י לְעָ֑ם וְאָ֣נֹכִ֔י אֶהְיֶ֥ה לָכֶ֖ם לֵאלֹהִֽים׃{ס}


And you shall be my people, and I will be your God.


###### Jeremiah 30:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:23)


הִנֵּ֣ה**׀** סַעֲרַ֣ת יְהֹוָ֗ה חֵמָה֙ יָֽצְאָ֔ה סַ֖עַר מִתְגּוֹרֵ֑ר עַ֛ל רֹ֥אשׁ רְשָׁעִ֖ים יָחֽוּל׃


Behold, the storm wind of the Lord goes out with fury, a sweeping storm: it shall fall with pain upon the head of the wicked.


###### Jeremiah 30:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/30:24)


לֹ֣א יָשׁ֗וּב חֲרוֹן֙ אַף־יְהֹוָ֔ה עַד־עֲשֹׂת֥וֹ וְעַד־הֲקִימ֖וֹ מְזִמּ֣וֹת לִבּ֑וֹ בְּאַחֲרִ֥ית הַיָּמִ֖ים תִּתְבּ֥וֹנְנוּ בָֽהּ׃


The fierce anger of the Lord shall not turn back, until he has done it, and until he has performed the intents of his heart: in the latter days you shall understand this.



## Chapter 31

###### Jeremiah 31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/31:1)


בָּעֵ֤ת הַהִיא֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה אֶֽהְיֶה֙ לֵֽאלֹהִ֔ים לְכֹ֖ל מִשְׁפְּח֣וֹת יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וְהֵ֖מָּה יִֽהְיוּ־לִ֥י לְעָֽם׃{ס}


At that time, says the Lord, will I be the God of all the families of Yisra᾽el, and they shall be my people.


###### Jeremiah 31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/31:2)


כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה מָצָ֥א חֵן֙ בַּמִּדְבָּ֔ר עַ֖ם שְׂרִ֣ידֵי חָ֑רֶב הָל֥וֹךְ לְהַרְגִּיע֖וֹ יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


Thus says the Lord, The people who were left of the sword found grace in the wilderness; when Yisra᾽el sought for rest.


###### Jeremiah 31:3

But every one shall die for his own iniquity: every man that eats sour grapes, his teeth shall be set on edge.


###### Jeremiah 31:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/31:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/31:31)


הִנֵּ֛ה יָמִ֥ים בָּאִ֖ים נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה וְכָרַתִּ֗י אֶת־בֵּ֧ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֛ל וְאֶת־בֵּ֥ית יְהוּדָ֖ה בְּרִ֥ית חֲדָשָֽׁה׃


Behold, days are coming, says the Lord, when I will make a new covenant with the house of Yisra᾽el, and with the house of Yehuda:


###### Jeremiah 31:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/31:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/31:32)


לֹ֣א כַבְּרִ֗ית אֲשֶׁ֤ר כָּרַ֙תִּי֙ אֶת־אֲבוֹתָ֔ם בְּיוֹם֙ הֶחֱזִיקִ֣י בְיָדָ֔ם לְהוֹצִיאָ֖ם מֵאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרָ֑יִם אֲשֶׁר־הֵ֜מָּה הֵפֵ֣רוּ אֶת־בְּרִיתִ֗י וְאָנֹכִ֛י בָּעַ֥לְתִּי בָ֖ם נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


not according to the covenant that I made with their fathers in the day that I took them by the hand to bring them out of the land of Miżrayim; which covenant of mine they broke, although I was their master, says the Lord;


###### Jeremiah 31:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/31:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/31:33)


כִּ֣י זֹ֣את הַבְּרִ֡ית אֲשֶׁ֣ר אֶכְרֹת֩ אֶת־בֵּ֨ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֜ל אַחֲרֵ֨י הַיָּמִ֤ים הָהֵם֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה נָתַ֤תִּי אֶת־תּֽוֹרָתִי֙ בְּקִרְבָּ֔ם וְעַל־לִבָּ֖ם אֶכְתְּבֶ֑נָּה וְהָיִ֤יתִי לָהֶם֙ לֵֽאלֹהִ֔ים וְהֵ֖מָּה יִֽהְיוּ־לִ֥י לְעָֽם׃


but this shall be the covenant that I will make with the house of Yisra᾽el after those days, says the Lord; I will put my Tora in their inward parts, and write it in their hearts; and will be their God, and they shall be my people,


###### Jeremiah 31:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/31:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/31:34)


וְלֹ֧א יְלַמְּד֣וּ ע֗וֹד אִ֣ישׁ אֶת־רֵעֵ֜הוּ וְאִ֤ישׁ אֶת־אָחִיו֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר דְּע֖וּ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֑ה כִּֽי־כוּלָּם֩ יֵדְע֨וּ אוֹתִ֜י לְמִקְּטַנָּ֤ם וְעַד־גְּדוֹלָם֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה כִּ֤י אֶסְלַח֙ לַֽעֲוֺנָ֔ם וּלְחַטָּאתָ֖ם לֹ֥א אֶזְכָּר־עֽוֹד׃{ס}


and they shall teach no more every man his neighbour, and every man his brother, saying, Know the Lord: for they shall all know me, from the least of them to the greatest of them, says the Lord: for I will forgive their iniquity, and I will remember their sin no more.


###### Jeremiah 31:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/31:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/31:35)


כֹּ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה נֹתֵ֥ן שֶׁ֙מֶשׁ֙ לְא֣וֹר יוֹמָ֔ם חֻקֹּ֛ת יָרֵ֥חַ וְכוֹכָבִ֖ים לְא֣וֹר לָ֑יְלָה רֹגַ֤ע הַיָּם֙ וַיֶּהֱמ֣וּ גַלָּ֔יו יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת שְׁמֽוֹ׃


Thus says the Lord, who gives the sun for a light by day, and the ordinances of the moon and of the stars for a light by night, who stirs up the sea that its waves roar; the Lord of hosts is his name:


###### Jeremiah 31:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/31:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/31:36)


אִם־יָמֻ֜שׁוּ הַחֻקִּ֥ים הָאֵ֛לֶּה מִלְּפָנַ֖י נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה גַּם֩ זֶ֨רַע יִשְׂרָאֵ֜ל יִשְׁבְּת֗וּ מִהְי֥וֹת גּ֛וֹי לְפָנַ֖י כׇּל־הַיָּמִֽים׃{ס}


If those ordinances depart from before me, says the Lord, then the seed of Yisra᾽el also shall cease from being a nation before me for ever.


###### Jeremiah 31:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/31:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/31:37)


כֹּ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה אִם־יִמַּ֤דּוּ שָׁמַ֙יִם֙ מִלְמַ֔עְלָה וְיֵחָקְר֥וּ מוֹסְדֵי־אֶ֖רֶץ לְמָ֑טָּה גַּם־אֲנִ֞י אֶמְאַ֨ס בְּכׇל־זֶ֧רַע יִשְׂרָאֵ֛ל עַֽל־כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֥ר עָשׂ֖וּ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


Thus says the Lord; If heaven above can be measured, and the foundations of the earth searched out beneath, then I will also cast off all the seed of Yisra᾽el for all that they have done, says the Lord God.


###### Jeremiah 31:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/31:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/31:38)


הִנֵּ֛ה יָמִ֥ים [בָּאִ֖ים] נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה וְנִבְנְתָ֤ה הָעִיר֙ לַיהֹוָ֔ה מִמִּגְדַּ֥ל חֲנַנְאֵ֖ל שַׁ֥עַר הַפִּנָּֽה׃


Behold, days are coming, says the Lord, when the city shall be built to the Lord from the tower of Ḥanan᾽el to the corner gate.


###### Jeremiah 31:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/31:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/31:39)


וְיָצָ֨א ע֜וֹד (קוה) [קָ֤ו] הַמִּדָּה֙ נֶגְדּ֔וֹ עַ֖ל גִּבְעַ֣ת גָּרֵ֑ב וְנָסַ֖ב גֹּעָֽתָה׃


And the measuring line shall go out further straight to the hill Garev, and shall turn round to go to Go῾a.


###### Jeremiah 31:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/31:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/31:40)


וְכׇל־הָעֵ֣מֶק הַפְּגָרִ֣ים׀וְהַדֶּ֡שֶׁן וְכׇֽל־[הַשְּׁדֵמוֹת֩] (השרמות) עַד־נַ֨חַל קִדְר֜וֹן עַד־פִּנַּ֨ת שַׁ֤עַר הַסּוּסִים֙ מִזְרָ֔חָה קֹ֖דֶשׁ לַיהֹוָ֑ה לֹא־יִנָּתֵ֧שׁ וְֽלֹא־יֵהָרֵ֛ס ע֖וֹד לְעוֹלָֽם׃{פ}


And the whole valley of the dead bodies, and of the ashes, and all the fields as far as the wadi of Qidron, to the corner of the horse gate towards the east, shall be holy to the Lord; it shall not be plucked up, nor thrown down any more for ever.



## Chapter 32

###### Jeremiah 32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:1)


הַדָּבָ֞ר אֲשֶׁר־הָיָ֤ה אֶֽל־יִרְמְיָ֙הוּ֙ מֵאֵ֣ת יְהֹוָ֔ה (בשנת) [בַּשָּׁנָה֙] הָעֲשִׂרִ֔ית לְצִדְקִיָּ֖הוּ מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֑ה הִ֧יא הַשָּׁנָ֛ה שְׁמֹנֶֽה־עֶשְׂרֵ֥ה שָׁנָ֖ה לִנְבֽוּכַדְרֶאצַּֽר׃


The word that came to Yirmeyahu from the Lord in the tenth year of Żidqiyyahu king of Yehuda, which was the eighteenth year of Nevukhadreżżar.


###### Jeremiah 32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:2)


וְאָ֗ז חֵ֚יל מֶ֣לֶךְ בָּבֶ֔ל צָרִ֖ים עַל־יְרוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְיִרְמְיָ֣הוּ הַנָּבִ֗יא הָיָ֤ה כָלוּא֙ בַּחֲצַ֣ר הַמַּטָּרָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֖ר בֵּֽית־מֶ֥לֶךְ יְהוּדָֽה׃


At that time, the king of Bavel’s army was besieging Yerushalayim: and Yirmeyahu the prophet was shut up in the court of the guard, which was in the house of the king of Yehuda.


###### Jeremiah 32:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:3)


אֲשֶׁ֣ר כְּלָא֔וֹ צִדְקִיָּ֥הוּ מֶֽלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֖ה לֵאמֹ֑ר מַדּ֩וּעַ֩ אַתָּ֨ה נִבָּ֜א לֵאמֹ֗ר כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה הִנְנִ֨י נֹתֵ֜ן אֶת־הָעִ֥יר הַזֹּ֛את בְּיַ֥ד מֶֽלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֖ל וּלְכָדָֽהּ׃


For Żidqiyyahu king of Yehuda had shut him up, saying, why dost thou prophesy, and say, Thus says the Lord, Behold, I will give this city into the hand of the king of Bavel, and he shall take it;


###### Jeremiah 32:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:4)


וְצִדְקִיָּ֙הוּ֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֔ה לֹ֥א יִמָּלֵ֖ט מִיַּ֣ד הַכַּשְׂדִּ֑ים כִּ֣י הִנָּתֹ֤ן יִנָּתֵן֙ בְּיַ֣ד מֶֽלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֔ל וְדִבֶּר־פִּ֣יו עִם־פִּ֔יו וְעֵינָ֖יו אֶת־עֵינָ֥ו תִּרְאֶֽינָה׃


and Żidqiyyahu king of Yehuda shall not escape out of the hand of the Kasdim, but shall surely be delivered into the hand of the king of Bavel, and shall speak with him mouth to mouth, and his eyes shall behold his eyes:


###### Jeremiah 32:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:5)


וּבָבֶ֞ל יוֹלִ֤ךְ אֶת־צִדְקִיָּ֙הוּ֙ וְשָׁ֣ם יִֽהְיֶ֔ה עַד־פׇּקְדִ֥י אֹת֖וֹ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה כִּ֧י תִֽלָּחֲמ֛וּ אֶת־הַכַּשְׂדִּ֖ים לֹ֥א תַצְלִֽיחוּ׃{פ}


and he shall lead Żidqiyyahu to Bavel, and there shall he be until I visit him, says the Lord: though you fight with the Kasdim, you shall not prosper?


###### Jeremiah 32:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:6)


וַיֹּ֖אמֶר יִרְמְיָ֑הוּ הָיָ֥ה דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


And Yirmeyahu said, The word of the Lord has come to me, saying,


###### Jeremiah 32:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:7)


הִנֵּ֣ה חֲנַמְאֵ֗ל בֶּן־שַׁלֻּם֙ דֹּֽדְךָ֔ בָּ֥א אֵלֶ֖יךָ לֵאמֹ֑ר קְנֵ֣ה לְךָ֗ אֶת־שָׂדִי֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בַּעֲנָת֔וֹת כִּ֥י לְךָ֛ מִשְׁפַּ֥ט הַגְּאֻלָּ֖ה לִקְנֽוֹת׃


Behold, Ḥanam᾽el the son of Shallum thy uncle shall come to thee, saying, Buy my field that is in ῾Anatot: for the right of redemption is thine to buy it.


###### Jeremiah 32:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:8)


וַיָּבֹ֣א אֵ֠לַ֠י חֲנַמְאֵ֨ל בֶּן־דֹּדִ֜י כִּדְבַ֣ר יְהֹוָה֮ אֶל־חֲצַ֣ר הַמַּטָּרָה֒ וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלַ֡י קְנֵ֣ה נָ֠א אֶת־שָׂדִ֨י אֲשֶׁר־בַּעֲנָת֜וֹת אֲשֶׁ֣ר**׀** בְּאֶ֣רֶץ בִּנְיָמִ֗ין כִּֽי־לְךָ֞ מִשְׁפַּ֧ט הַיְרֻשָּׁ֛ה וּלְךָ֥ הַגְּאֻלָּ֖ה קְנֵה־לָ֑ךְ וָאֵדַ֕ע כִּ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה הֽוּא׃


So Ḥanam᾽el my uncle’s son came to me in the court of the guard according to the word of the Lord, and said to me, Buy my field, I pray thee, that is in ῾Anatot, which is in the country of Binyamin: for the right of inheritance is thine, and the redemption is thine; buy it for thyself. Then I knew that this was the word of the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 32:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:9)


וָֽאֶקְנֶה֙ אֶת־הַשָּׂדֶ֔ה מֵאֵ֛ת חֲנַמְאֵ֥ל בֶּן־דֹּדִ֖י אֲשֶׁ֣ר בַּעֲנָת֑וֹת וָֽאֶשְׁקְלָה־לּוֹ֙ אֶת־הַכֶּ֔סֶף שִׁבְעָ֥ה שְׁקָלִ֖ים וַעֲשָׂרָ֥ה הַכָּֽסֶף׃


And I bought the field from Ḥanam᾽el my uncle’s son, that was in ῾Anatot, and weighed him the money, seventeen shekels of silver.


###### Jeremiah 32:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:10)


וָאֶכְתֹּ֤ב בַּסֵּ֙פֶר֙ וָֽאֶחְתֹּ֔ם וָאָעֵ֖ד עֵדִ֑ים וָאֶשְׁקֹ֥ל הַכֶּ֖סֶף בְּמֹאזְנָֽיִם׃


And I subscribed the deed, and sealed it, and took witnesses, and weighed the money in the balances.


###### Jeremiah 32:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:11)


וָאֶקַּ֖ח אֶת־סֵ֣פֶר הַמִּקְנָ֑ה אֶת־הֶֽחָת֛וּם הַמִּצְוָ֥ה וְהַחֻקִּ֖ים וְאֶת־הַגָּלֽוּי׃


So I took the deed of the purchase, both that which was sealed, containing the terms and conditions, and which was open:


###### Jeremiah 32:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:12)


וָאֶתֵּ֞ן אֶת־הַסֵּ֣פֶר הַמִּקְנָ֗ה אֶל־בָּר֣וּךְ בֶּן־נֵרִיָּה֮ בֶּן־מַחְסֵיָה֒ לְעֵינֵי֙ חֲנַמְאֵ֣ל דֹּדִ֔י וּלְעֵינֵי֙ הָֽעֵדִ֔ים הַכֹּתְבִ֖ים בְּסֵ֣פֶר הַמִּקְנָ֑ה לְעֵינֵי֙ כׇּל־הַיְּהוּדִ֔ים הַיֹּשְׁבִ֖ים בַּחֲצַ֥ר הַמַּטָּרָֽה׃


and I gave the deed of the purchase to Barukh the son of Neriyya, the son of Meĥaseya, in the sight of Ḥanam᾽el my uncle’s son, and in the presence of the witnesses that subscribed the deed of the purchase, before all the men of Yehuda that sat in the court of the guard.


###### Jeremiah 32:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:13)


וָאֲצַוֶּה֙ אֶת־בָּר֔וּךְ לְעֵינֵיהֶ֖ם לֵאמֹֽר׃


And I charged Barukh before them, saying,


###### Jeremiah 32:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:14)


כֹּה־אָמַר֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה צְבָא֜וֹת אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל לָק֣וֹחַ אֶת־הַסְּפָרִ֣ים הָאֵ֡לֶּה אֵ֣ת סֵ֩פֶר֩ הַמִּקְנָ֨ה הַזֶּ֜ה וְאֵ֣ת הֶחָת֗וּם וְאֵ֨ת סֵ֤פֶר הַגָּלוּי֙ הַזֶּ֔ה וּנְתַתָּ֖ם בִּכְלִי־חָ֑רֶשׂ לְמַ֥עַן יַעַמְד֖וּ יָמִ֥ים רַבִּֽים׃{ס}

וְנָתַתִּ֨י לָהֶ֜ם לֵ֤ב אֶחָד֙ וְדֶ֣רֶךְ אֶחָ֔ד לְיִרְאָ֥ה אוֹתִ֖י כׇּל־הַיָּמִ֑ים לְט֣וֹב לָהֶ֔ם וְלִבְנֵיהֶ֖ם אַחֲרֵיהֶֽם׃


and I will give them one heart, and one way, that they may fear me for ever, for their good, and that of their children after them:


###### Jeremiah 32:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:40)


וְכָרַתִּ֤י לָהֶם֙ בְּרִ֣ית עוֹלָ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֤ר לֹֽא־אָשׁוּב֙ מֵאַ֣חֲרֵיהֶ֔ם לְהֵיטִיבִ֖י אוֹתָ֑ם וְאֶת־יִרְאָתִי֙ אֶתֵּ֣ן בִּלְבָבָ֔ם לְבִלְתִּ֖י ס֥וּר מֵעָלָֽי׃


and I will make an everlasting covenant with them, that I will not turn away from doing good to them; but I will put My fear in their hearts, that they shall not turn aside from me.


###### Jeremiah 32:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:41)


וְשַׂשְׂתִּ֥י עֲלֵיהֶ֖ם לְהֵטִ֣יב אוֹתָ֑ם וּנְטַעְתִּ֞ים בָּאָ֤רֶץ הַזֹּאת֙ בֶּאֱמֶ֔ת בְּכׇל־לִבִּ֖י וּבְכׇל־נַפְשִֽׁי׃{ס}


And I will rejoice over them to do them good, and I will truly plant them in this land, with My whole heart and with My whole soul.


###### Jeremiah 32:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:42)


כִּי־כֹה֙ אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה כַּאֲשֶׁ֤ר הֵבֵ֙אתִי֙ אֶל־הָעָ֣ם הַזֶּ֔ה אֵ֛ת כׇּל־הָרָעָ֥ה הַגְּדוֹלָ֖ה הַזֹּ֑את כֵּ֣ן אָנֹכִ֞י מֵבִ֤יא עֲלֵיהֶם֙ אֶת־כׇּל־הַטּוֹבָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר אָנֹכִ֖י דֹּבֵ֥ר עֲלֵיהֶֽם׃


For thus says the Lord; Just as I have brought all this great evil upon this people, so will I bring upon them all the good that I have promised them.


###### Jeremiah 32:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:43)


וְנִקְנָ֥ה הַשָּׂדֶ֖ה בָּאָ֣רֶץ הַזֹּ֑את אֲשֶׁ֣ר**׀** אַתֶּ֣ם אֹמְרִ֗ים שְׁמָמָ֥ה הִיא֙ מֵאֵ֤ין אָדָם֙ וּבְהֵמָ֔ה נִתְּנָ֖ה בְּיַ֥ד הַכַּשְׂדִּֽים׃


And fields shall be brought in this land of which you say, It is desolate without man or beast; it is given into the hand of the Kasdim.


###### Jeremiah 32:44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/32:44)


שָׂד֞וֹת בַּכֶּ֣סֶף יִקְנ֗וּ וְכָת֨וֹב בַּסֵּ֥פֶר׀וְחָתוֹם֮ וְהָעֵ֣ד עֵדִים֒ בְּאֶ֨רֶץ בִּנְיָמִ֜ן וּבִסְבִיבֵ֣י יְרוּשָׁלַ֗͏ִם וּבְעָרֵ֤י יְהוּדָה֙ וּבְעָרֵ֣י הָהָ֔ר וּבְעָרֵ֥י הַשְּׁפֵלָ֖ה וּבְעָרֵ֣י הַנֶּ֑גֶב כִּֽי־אָשִׁ֥יב אֶת־שְׁבוּתָ֖ם נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


Men shall buy fields for money, and subscribe deeds, and seal them, and take witnesses, in the land of Binyamin, and in the places about Yerushalayim, and in the cities of Yehuda, and in the cities of the mountains, and in the cities of the coastal plain, and in the cities of the Negev: for I will cause their captivity to return, says the Lord.



## Chapter 33

###### Jeremiah 33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:1)


וַיְהִ֧י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֛ה אֶֽל־יִרְמְיָ֖הוּ שֵׁנִ֑ית וְהוּא֙ עוֹדֶ֣נּוּ עָצ֔וּר בַּחֲצַ֥ר הַמַּטָּרָ֖ה לֵאמֹֽר׃


Moreover the word of the Lord came to Yirmeyahu the second time, while he was still confined in the court of the guard, saying,


###### Jeremiah 33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:2)


כֹּה־אָמַ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה עֹשָׂ֑הּ יְהֹוָ֗ה יוֹצֵ֥ר אוֹתָ֛הּ לַהֲכִינָ֖הּ יְהֹוָ֥ה שְׁמֽוֹ׃


Thus says the Lord the maker of it, the Lord that formed it, to establish it; the Lord is his name;


###### Jeremiah 33:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:3)


קְרָ֥א אֵלַ֖י וְאֶעֱנֶ֑ךָּ וְאַגִּ֧ידָה לְּךָ֛ גְּדֹל֥וֹת וּבְצֻר֖וֹת לֹ֥א יְדַעְתָּֽם׃{פ}


Call to me, and I will answer thee, and show thee great and mighty things, which thou knowst not.


###### Jeremiah 33:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:4)


כִּי֩ כֹ֨ה אָמַ֤ר יְהֹוָה֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל עַל־בָּתֵּי֙ הָעִ֣יר הַזֹּ֔את וְעַל־בָּתֵּ֖י מַלְכֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֑ה הַנְּתֻצִ֕ים אֶל־הַסֹּלְל֖וֹת וְאֶל־הֶחָֽרֶב׃


For thus says the Lord, the God of Yisra᾽el, concerning the houses of this city, and concerning the houses of the kings of Yehuda, which are thrown down to withstand the siege works and the sword;


###### Jeremiah 33:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:5)


בָּאִ֗ים לְהִלָּחֵם֙ אֶת־הַכַּשְׂדִּ֔ים וּלְמַלְאָם֙ אֶת־פִּגְרֵ֣י הָאָדָ֔ם אֲשֶׁר־הִכֵּ֥יתִי בְאַפִּ֖י וּבַחֲמָתִ֑י וַאֲשֶׁ֨ר הִסְתַּ֤רְתִּי פָנַי֙ מֵהָעִ֣יר הַזֹּ֔את עַ֖ל כׇּל־רָעָתָֽם׃


they come to fight with the Kasdim, and thereby to fill them with the dead bodies of those men, whom I shall stay in my anger and in my fury, those on account of whose wickedness I have hid my face from this city.


###### Jeremiah 33:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:6)


הִנְנִ֧י מַעֲלֶה־לָּ֛הּ אֲרֻכָ֥ה וּמַרְפֵּ֖א וּרְפָאתִ֑ים וְגִלֵּיתִ֣י לָהֶ֔ם עֲתֶ֥רֶת שָׁל֖וֹם וֶאֱמֶֽת׃


Behold, I will bring it healing and cure, and I will cure them, and will reveal to them the abundance of peace and truth.


###### Jeremiah 33:7

וַהֲשִֽׁבֹתִי֙ אֶת־שְׁב֣וּת יְהוּדָ֔ה וְאֵ֖ת שְׁב֣וּת יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וּבְנִתִ֖ים כְּבָרִֽאשֹׁנָֽה׃


And I will cause the captivity of Yehuda and the captivity of Yisra᾽el to return, and will build them, as at the first.


###### Jeremiah 33:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:8)


וְטִ֣הַרְתִּ֔ים מִכׇּל־עֲוֺנָ֖ם אֲשֶׁ֣ר חָֽטְאוּ־לִ֑י וְסָלַחְתִּ֗י (לכול) [לְכׇל־]עֲוֺנֽוֹתֵיהֶם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר חָֽטְאוּ־לִ֔י וַאֲשֶׁ֖ר פָּ֥שְׁעוּ בִֽי׃


And I will cleanse them from all their iniquity, in which they have sinned against me; and I will pardon all their iniquities, in which they have sinned against me, and in which they have transgressed against me.


###### Jeremiah 33:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:9)


וְהָ֣יְתָה לִּ֗י לְשֵׁ֤ם שָׂשׂוֹן֙ לִתְהִלָּ֣ה וּלְתִפְאֶ֔רֶת לְכֹ֖ל גּוֹיֵ֣י הָאָ֑רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֨ר יִשְׁמְע֜וּ אֶת־כׇּל־הַטּוֹבָ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֤ר אָנֹכִי֙ עֹשֶׂ֣ה אוֹתָ֔ם וּפָחֲד֣וּ וְרָגְז֗וּ עַ֤ל כׇּל־הַטּוֹבָה֙ וְעַ֣ל כׇּל־הַשָּׁל֔וֹם אֲשֶׁ֥ר אָנֹכִ֖י עֹ֥שֶׂה לָּֽהּ׃{ס}


And it shall be to me a name of joy, a praise and an honour before all the nations of the earth, who shall hear all the good that I do to them: and they shall fear and tremble for all the goodness and for all the prosperity that I procure to it:


###### Jeremiah 33:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:10)


כֹּ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה עוֹד֮ יִשָּׁמַ֣ע בַּמָּקוֹם־הַזֶּה֒ אֲשֶׁר֙ אַתֶּ֣ם אֹֽמְרִ֔ים חָרֵ֣ב ה֔וּא מֵאֵ֥ין אָדָ֖ם וּמֵאֵ֣ין בְּהֵמָ֑ה בְּעָרֵ֤י יְהוּדָה֙ וּבְחֻצ֣וֹת יְרוּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם הַֽנְשַׁמּ֗וֹת מֵאֵ֥ין אָדָ֛ם וּמֵאֵ֥ין יוֹשֵׁ֖ב וּמֵאֵ֥ין בְּהֵמָֽה׃


Thus says the Lord; Again there shall be heard in this place, (which you say shall be desolate without man and without beast, namely, in the cities of Yehuda, and in the desolate streets of Yerushalayim, that are without man, and without inhabitant, and without beast,)


###### Jeremiah 33:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:11)


ק֣וֹל שָׂשׂ֞וֹן וְק֣וֹל שִׂמְחָ֗ה ק֣וֹל חָתָן֮ וְק֣וֹל כַּלָּה֒ ק֣וֹל אֹמְרִ֡ים הוֹדוּ֩ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֨ה צְבָא֜וֹת כִּי־ט֤וֹב יְהֹוָה֙ כִּֽי־לְעוֹלָ֣ם חַסְדּ֔וֹ מְבִאִ֥ים תּוֹדָ֖ה בֵּ֣ית יְהֹוָ֑ה כִּֽי־אָשִׁ֧יב אֶת־שְׁבוּת־הָאָ֛רֶץ כְּבָרִאשֹׁנָ֖ה אָמַ֥ר יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


the voice of joy, and the voice of gladness, the voice of the bridegroom, and the voice of the bride, the voice of them that shall say, Praise the Lord of hosts: for the Lord is good; for his loyal love endures for ever: when they shall bring the sacrifice of thanksgiving into the house of the Lord. For I will cause the captivity of the land to return, as at the first, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 33:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:12)


כֹּה־אָמַר֮ יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָאוֹת֒ ע֞וֹד יִהְיֶ֣ה**׀** בַּמָּק֣וֹם הַזֶּ֗ה הֶחָרֵ֛ב מֵאֵין־אָדָ֥ם וְעַד־בְּהֵמָ֖ה וּבְכׇל־עָרָ֑יו נְוֵ֣ה רֹעִ֔ים מַרְבִּצִ֖ים צֹֽאן׃


Thus says the Lord of hosts; Again in this place, which is desolate without man and without beast, and in all its cities, shall be a habitation of shepherds who make their flocks lie down.


###### Jeremiah 33:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:13)


בְּעָרֵ֨י הָהָ֜ר בְּעָרֵ֤י הַשְּׁפֵלָה֙ וּבְעָרֵ֣י הַנֶּ֔גֶב וּבְאֶ֧רֶץ בִּנְיָמִ֛ן וּבִסְבִיבֵ֥י יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם וּבְעָרֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֑ה עֹ֣ד תַּעֲבֹ֧רְנָה הַצֹּ֛אן עַל־יְדֵ֥י מוֹנֶ֖ה אָמַ֥ר יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


In the cities of the mountains, in the cities of the lowland, and in the cities of the Negev, and in the land of Binyamin, and in the places about Yerushalayim, and in the cities of Yehuda, shall the flocks pass again under the hands of him that counts them, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 33:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/33:14)


הִנֵּ֛ה יָמִ֥ים בָּאִ֖ים נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה וַהֲקִֽמֹתִי֙ אֶת־הַדָּבָ֣ר הַטּ֔וֹב אֲשֶׁ֥ר דִּבַּ֛רְתִּי אֶל־בֵּ֥ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל וְעַל־בֵּ֥ית יְהוּדָֽה׃


Behold, days are coming, says the Lord, when I will perform that good thing which I have promised to the house of Yisra᾽el and to the house of Yehuda.


###### Jeremiah 33:15
