So Yirmeyahu remained in the court of the guard until the day that Yerushalayim was taken: And he was there when Yerushalayim was taken.



## Chapter 39

###### Jeremiah 39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:1)


בַּשָּׁנָ֣ה הַ֠תְּשִׁעִ֠ית לְצִדְקִיָּ֨הוּ מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֜ה בַּחֹ֣דֶשׁ הָעֲשִׂרִ֗י בָּ֠א נְבוּכַדְרֶאצַּ֨ר מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֤ל וְכׇל־חֵילוֹ֙ אֶל־יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם וַיָּצֻ֖רוּ עָלֶֽיהָ׃


In the ninth year of Żidqiyyahu king of Yehuda, in the tenth month, Nevukhadreżżar king of Bavel and all his army came against Yerushalayim, and they besieged it.


###### Jeremiah 39:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:2)


בְּעַשְׁתֵּֽי־עֶשְׂרֵ֤ה שָׁנָה֙ לְצִדְקִיָּ֔הוּ בַּחֹ֥דֶשׁ הָרְבִיעִ֖י בְּתִשְׁעָ֣ה לַחֹ֑דֶשׁ הׇבְקְעָ֖ה הָעִֽיר׃


In the eleventh year of Żidqiyyahu, in the fourth month, the ninth day of the month, a breach was made in the city.


###### Jeremiah 39:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:3)


וַיָּבֹ֗אוּ כֹּ֚ל שָׂרֵ֣י מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֔ל וַיֵּשְׁב֖וּ בְּשַׁ֣עַר הַתָּ֑וֶךְ נֵרְגַ֣ל שַׂ֠רְאֶ֠צֶר סַֽמְגַּר־נְב֞וּ שַׂר־סְכִ֣ים רַב־סָרִ֗יס נֵרְגַ֤ל שַׂרְאֶ֙צֶר֙ רַב־מָ֔ג וְכׇ֨ל־שְׁאֵרִ֔ית שָׂרֵ֖י מֶ֥לֶךְ בָּבֶֽל׃


And all the princes of the king of Bavel, came in, and sat in the middle gate, namely Nergal-sar᾽eżer, Samgar-nevo, Sarsekhim Rav-saris, Nergal-sar᾽eżer Rav-mag, with all the remainder of the princes of the king of Bavel.


###### Jeremiah 39:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:4)


וַיְהִ֡י כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר רָ֠אָ֠ם צִדְקִיָּ֨הוּ מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֜ה וְכֹ֣ל**׀** אַנְשֵׁ֣י הַמִּלְחָמָ֗ה וַֽ֠יִּבְרְח֠וּ וַיֵּצְא֨וּ לַ֤יְלָה מִן־הָעִיר֙ דֶּ֚רֶךְ גַּ֣ן הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ בְּשַׁ֖עַר בֵּ֣ין הַחֹמֹתָ֑יִם וַיֵּצֵ֖א דֶּ֥רֶךְ הָעֲרָבָֽה׃


And it came to pass, that when Żidqiyyahu the king of Yehuda, and all the men of war saw them, then they fled, and went out of the city by night, by the way of the king’s garden, by the gate between the two walls: and he went out the way of the Arava.


###### Jeremiah 39:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:5)


וַיִּרְדְּפ֨וּ חֵיל־כַּשְׂדִּ֜ים אַחֲרֵיהֶ֗ם וַיַּשִּׂ֣גוּ אֶת־צִדְקִיָּ֘הוּ֮ בְּעַֽרְב֣וֹת יְרֵחוֹ֒ וַיִּקְח֣וּ אוֹת֗וֹ וַֽ֠יַּעֲלֻ֠הוּ אֶל־נְבוּכַדְרֶאצַּ֧ר מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֛ל רִבְלָ֖תָה בְּאֶ֣רֶץ חֲמָ֑ת וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר אִתּ֖וֹ מִשְׁפָּטִֽים׃


But the army of the Kasdim pursued after them, and overtook Żidqiyyahu in the plains of Yereĥo: and when they had taken him, they brought him up to Nevukhadreżżar king of Bavel to Rivla in the land of Ḥamat, where he gave judgment upon him.


###### Jeremiah 39:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:6)


וַיִּשְׁחַט֩ מֶ֨לֶךְ בָּבֶ֜ל אֶת־בְּנֵ֧י צִדְקִיָּ֛הוּ בְּרִבְלָ֖ה לְעֵינָ֑יו וְאֵת֙ כׇּל־חֹרֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֔ה שָׁחַ֖ט מֶ֥לֶךְ בָּבֶֽל׃


Then the king of Bavel slew the sons of Żidqiyyahu in Rivla before his eyes: also the king of Bavel slew all the nobles of Yehuda.


###### Jeremiah 39:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:7)


וְאֶת־עֵינֵ֥י צִדְקִיָּ֖הוּ עִוֵּ֑ר וַיַּאַסְרֵ֙הוּ֙ בַּֽנְחֻשְׁתַּ֔יִם לָבִ֥יא אֹת֖וֹ בָּבֶֽלָה׃


Moreover he put out Żidqiyyahu’s eyes, and bound him with fetters, to carry him to Bavel.


###### Jeremiah 39:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:8)


וְאֶת־בֵּ֤ית הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ וְאֶת־בֵּ֣ית הָעָ֔ם שָׂרְפ֥וּ הַכַּשְׂדִּ֖ים בָּאֵ֑שׁ וְאֶת־חֹמ֥וֹת יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם נָתָֽצוּ׃


And the Kasdim burned the king’s house, and the houses of the people, with fire, and broke down the walls of Yerushalayim.


###### Jeremiah 39:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:9)


וְאֵת֩ יֶ֨תֶר הָעָ֜ם הַנִּשְׁאָרִ֣ים בָּעִ֗יר וְאֶת־הַנֹּֽפְלִים֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר נָפְל֣וּ עָלָ֔יו וְאֵ֛ת יֶ֥תֶר הָעָ֖ם הַנִּשְׁאָרִ֑ים הֶגְלָ֛ה נְבוּזַרְאֲדָ֥ן רַב־טַבָּחִ֖ים בָּבֶֽל׃


Then Nevuzar᾽adan the captain of the guard carried away captive into Bavel the remnant of the people that remained in the city and the deserters who had deserted to him, with the rest of the people that remained.


###### Jeremiah 39:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:10)


וּמִן־הָעָ֣ם הַדַּלִּ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֤ר אֵין־לָהֶם֙ מְא֔וּמָה הִשְׁאִ֛יר נְבוּזַרְאֲדָ֥ן רַב־טַבָּחִ֖ים בְּאֶ֣רֶץ יְהוּדָ֑ה וַיִּתֵּ֥ן לָהֶ֛ם כְּרָמִ֥ים וִיגֵבִ֖ים בַּיּ֥וֹם הַהֽוּא׃


But Nevuzar᾽adan the captain of the guard left of the poor of the people, who had nothing, in the land of Yehuda, and gave them vineyards and fields at the same time.


###### Jeremiah 39:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:11)


וַיְצַ֛ו נְבוּכַדְרֶאצַּ֥ר מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֖ל עַֽל־יִרְמְיָ֑הוּ בְּיַ֛ד נְבוּזַרְאֲדָ֥ן רַב־טַבָּחִ֖ים לֵאמֹֽר׃


Now Nevukhadreżżar king of Bavel gave charge concerning Yirmeyahu to Nevuzar᾽adan the captain of the guard, saying,


###### Jeremiah 39:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:12)


קָחֶ֗נּוּ וְעֵינֶ֙יךָ֙ שִׂ֣ים עָלָ֔יו וְאַל־תַּ֥עַשׂ ל֖וֹ מְא֣וּמָה רָּ֑ע כִּ֗י (אם) כַּֽאֲשֶׁר֙ יְדַבֵּ֣ר אֵלֶ֔יךָ כֵּ֖ן עֲשֵׂ֥ה עִמּֽוֹ׃


Take him, and look well to him, and do him no harm; but do to him as he shall say to thee.


###### Jeremiah 39:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:13)


וַיִּשְׁלַ֞ח נְבוּזַרְאֲדָ֣ן רַב־טַבָּחִ֗ים וּנְבֽוּשַׁזְבָּן֙ רַב־סָרִ֔יס וְנֵרְגַ֥ל שַׂרְאֶ֖צֶר רַב־מָ֑ג וְכֹ֖ל רַבֵּ֥י מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶֽל׃


Then sent Nevuzar᾽adan the captain of the guard, and Nevushazban, the Rav-saris, and Nergal-sar᾽eżer the Rav-mag and all the princes of the king of Bavel:


###### Jeremiah 39:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:14)


וַיִּשְׁלְחוּ֩ וַיִּקְח֨וּ אֶֽת־יִרְמְיָ֜הוּ מֵחֲצַ֣ר הַמַּטָּרָ֗ה וַיִּתְּנ֤וּ אֹתוֹ֙ אֶל־גְּדַלְיָ֙הוּ֙ בֶּן־אֲחִיקָ֣ם בֶּן־שָׁפָ֔ן לְהוֹצִאֵ֖הוּ אֶל־הַבָּ֑יִת וַיֵּ֖שֶׁב בְּת֥וֹךְ הָעָֽם׃{ס}


and they sent, and took Yirmeyahu out of the court of the guard, and committed him to Gedalyahu the son of Aĥiqam the son of Shafan, that he should carry him home: so he dwelt among the people.


###### Jeremiah 39:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:15)


וְאֶֽל־יִרְמְיָ֖הוּ הָיָ֣ה דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֑ה בִּֽהְיֹת֣וֹ עָצ֔וּר בַּחֲצַ֥ר הַמַּטָּרָ֖ה לֵאמֹֽר׃


Now the word of the Lord came to Yirmeyahu, while he was shut up in the court of the guard, saying,


###### Jeremiah 39:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:16)


הָל֣וֹךְ וְאָמַרְתָּ֡ לְעֶבֶד־מֶ֨לֶךְ הַכּוּשִׁ֜י לֵאמֹ֗ר כֹּֽה־אָמַ֞ר יְהֹוָ֤ה צְבָאוֹת֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל הִנְנִי֩ מֵבִ֨י אֶת־דְּבָרַ֜י אֶל־הָעִ֥יר הַזֹּ֛את לְרָעָ֖ה וְלֹ֣א לְטוֹבָ֑ה וְהָי֥וּ לְפָנֶ֖יךָ בַּיּ֥וֹם הַהֽוּא׃


Go and speak to ῾Eved-melekh the Kushi, saying, Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Yisra᾽el, Behold, I will bring my words upon this city for evil, and not for good; and they shall be accomplished in that day before thee.


###### Jeremiah 39:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:17)


וְהִצַּלְתִּ֥יךָ בַיּוֹם־הַה֖וּא נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה וְלֹ֤א תִנָּתֵן֙ בְּיַ֣ד הָאֲנָשִׁ֔ים אֲשֶׁר־אַתָּ֥ה יָג֖וֹר מִפְּנֵיהֶֽם׃


But I will deliver thee on that day, says the Lord: and thou shalt not be given into the hand of the men of whom thou art afraid;


###### Jeremiah 39:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/39:18)


כִּ֤י מַלֵּט֙ אֲמַלֶּטְךָ֔ וּבַחֶ֖רֶב לֹ֣א תִפֹּ֑ל וְהָיְתָ֨ה לְךָ֤ נַפְשְׁךָ֙ לְשָׁלָ֔ל כִּֽי־בָטַ֥חְתָּ בִּ֖י נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


for I will surely deliver thee, and thou shalt not fall by the sword, but thy life shall be for a prey to thee: because thou hast put thy trust in me, says the Lord.



## Chapter 40

###### Jeremiah 40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:1)


הַדָּבָ֞ר אֲשֶׁר־הָיָ֤ה אֶֽל־יִרְמְיָ֙הוּ֙ מֵאֵ֣ת יְהֹוָ֔ה אַחַ֣ר**׀** שַׁלַּ֣ח אֹת֗וֹ נְבוּזַרְאֲדָ֛ן רַב־טַבָּחִ֖ים מִן־הָרָמָ֑ה בְּקַחְתּ֣וֹ אֹת֗וֹ וְהֽוּא־אָס֤וּר בָּֽאזִקִּים֙ בְּת֨וֹךְ כׇּל־גָּל֤וּת יְרוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ וִֽיהוּדָ֔ה הַמֻּגְלִ֖ים בָּבֶֽלָה׃


The word that came to Yirmeyahu from the Lord, after Nevuzar᾽adan the captain of the guard had let him go from Rama, when he had taken him bound as he was in chains among all the exiles of Yerushalayim and Yehuda who were carried away as exiles to Bavel.


###### Jeremiah 40:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:2)


וַיִּקַּ֥ח רַב־טַבָּחִ֖ים לְיִרְמְיָ֑הוּ וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֵלָ֔יו יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֗יךָ דִּבֶּר֙ אֶת־הָרָעָ֣ה הַזֹּ֔את אֶל־הַמָּק֖וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃


And the captain of the guard took Yirmeyahu, and said to him, The Lord thy God has pronounced this evil upon this place.


###### Jeremiah 40:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:3)


וַיָּבֵ֥א וַיַּ֛עַשׂ יְהֹוָ֖ה כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר דִּבֵּ֑ר כִּֽי־חֲטָאתֶ֤ם לַֽיהֹוָה֙ וְלֹא־שְׁמַעְתֶּ֣ם בְּקוֹל֔וֹ וְהָיָ֥ה לָכֶ֖ם (דבר) [הַדָּבָ֥ר] הַזֶּֽה׃


Now the Lord has brought it, and done according as he has said: because you have sinned against the Lord, and have not obeyed his voice, therefore this thing is come upon you.


###### Jeremiah 40:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:4)


וְעַתָּ֞ה הִנֵּ֧ה פִתַּחְתִּ֣יךָ הַיּ֗וֹם מִֽן־הָאזִקִּים֮ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עַל־יָדֶ֒ךָ֒ אִם־ט֨וֹב בְּעֵינֶ֜יךָ לָב֧וֹא אִתִּ֣י בָבֶ֗ל בֹּ֚א וְאָשִׂ֤ים אֶת־עֵינִי֙ עָלֶ֔יךָ וְאִם־רַ֧ע בְּעֵינֶ֛יךָ לָבֽוֹא־אִתִּ֥י בָבֶ֖ל חֲדָ֑ל רְאֵה֙ כׇּל־הָאָ֣רֶץ לְפָנֶ֔יךָ אֶל־ט֨וֹב וְאֶל־הַיָּשָׁ֧ר בְּעֵינֶ֛יךָ לָלֶ֥כֶת שָׁ֖מָּה לֵֽךְ׃


And now, behold, I release thee this day from the chains which were upon thy hand. If it seem good to thee to come with me into Bavel, come; and I will look after thee: but if it seem ill to thee to come with me into Bavel, do not come: behold, all the land is before thee: wherever it seems good and convenient for thee to go, go there.


###### Jeremiah 40:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:5)


וְעוֹדֶ֣נּוּ לֹֽא־יָשׁ֗וּב וְשֻׁ֡בָה אֶל־גְּדַלְיָ֣ה בֶן־אֲחִיקָ֣ם בֶּן־שָׁפָ֡ן אֲשֶׁר֩ הִפְקִ֨יד מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֜ל בְּעָרֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֗ה וְשֵׁ֤ב אִתּוֹ֙ בְּת֣וֹךְ הָעָ֔ם א֠וֹ אֶל־כׇּל־הַיָּשָׁ֧ר בְּעֵינֶ֛יךָ לָלֶ֖כֶת לֵ֑ךְ וַיִּתֶּן־ל֧וֹ רַב־טַבָּחִ֛ים אֲרֻחָ֥ה וּמַשְׂאֵ֖ת וַֽיְשַׁלְּחֵֽהוּ׃


(Then since he did not yet turn about;) Go back then, said he, Gedalya the son of Aĥiqam the son of Shafan, whom the king of Bavel has made governor over the cities of Yehuda, and dwell with him among the people: or go wherever it seems convenient to thee to go. So the captain of the guard gave him an allowance of food and a present, and let him go.


###### Jeremiah 40:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:6)


וַיָּבֹ֧א יִרְמְיָ֛הוּ אֶל־גְּדַלְיָ֥ה בֶן־אֲחִיקָ֖ם הַמִּצְפָּ֑תָה וַיֵּ֤שֶׁב אִתּוֹ֙ בְּת֣וֹךְ הָעָ֔ם הַנִּשְׁאָרִ֖ים בָּאָֽרֶץ׃{פ}


Then Yirmeyahu went to Gedalya the son of Aĥiqam to Miżpa; and dwelt with him among the people that were left in the land.


###### Jeremiah 40:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:7)


וַיִּשְׁמְעוּ֩ כׇל־שָׂרֵ֨י הַחֲיָלִ֜ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר בַּשָּׂדֶ֗ה הֵ֚מָּה וְאַנְשֵׁיהֶ֔ם כִּֽי־הִפְקִ֧יד מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֛ל אֶת־גְּדַלְיָ֥הוּ בֶן־אֲחִיקָ֖ם בָּאָ֑רֶץ וְכִ֣י**׀** הִפְקִ֣יד אִתּ֗וֹ אֲנָשִׁ֤ים וְנָשִׁים֙ וָטָ֔ף וּמִדַּלַּ֣ת הָאָ֔רֶץ מֵאֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־הׇגְל֖וּ בָּבֶֽלָה׃


Now when all the captains of the forces which were in the fields, they and their men, heard that the king of Bavel had made Gedalya the son of Aĥiqam governor in the land, and had committed to him men, and women, and children, and of the poor of the land, of them that were not carried away captive to Bavel;


###### Jeremiah 40:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:8)


וַיָּבֹ֥אוּ אֶל־גְּדַלְיָ֖ה הַמִּצְפָּ֑תָה וְיִשְׁמָעֵ֣אל בֶּן־נְתַנְיָ֡הוּ וְיוֹחָנָ֣ן וְיוֹנָתָ֣ן בְּנֵֽי־קָ֠רֵ֠חַ וּשְׂרָיָ֨ה בֶן־תַּנְחֻ֜מֶת וּבְנֵ֣י**׀** (עופי) [עֵיפַ֣י] הַנְּטֹפָתִ֗י וִֽיזַנְיָ֙הוּ֙ בֶּן־הַמַּ֣עֲכָתִ֔י הֵ֖מָּה וְאַנְשֵׁיהֶֽם׃


then they came to Gedalya to Miżpa, namely, Yishma᾽el the son of Netanyahu, and Yoĥanan and Yonatan the sons of Qareaĥ, and Seraya the son of Tanĥumet, and the sons of ῾Efay the Netofatite, and Yezanyahu the son of the Ma῾akhatite, they and their men.


###### Jeremiah 40:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:9)


וַיִּשָּׁבַ֨ע לָהֶ֜ם גְּדַלְיָ֨הוּ בֶן־אֲחִיקָ֤ם בֶּן־שָׁפָן֙ וּלְאַנְשֵׁיהֶ֣ם לֵאמֹ֔ר אַל־תִּֽירְא֖וּ מֵעֲב֣וֹד הַכַּשְׂדִּ֑ים שְׁב֣וּ בָאָ֗רֶץ וְעִבְד֛וּ אֶת־מֶ֥לֶךְ בָּבֶ֖ל וְיִיטַ֥ב לָכֶֽם׃


And Gedalyahu the son of Aĥiqam the son of Shafan swore to them and to their men, saying, Fear not to serve the Kasdim: dwell in the land and serve the king of Bavel, and it shall be well with you.


###### Jeremiah 40:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:10)


וַאֲנִ֗י הִנְנִ֤י יֹשֵׁב֙ בַּמִּצְפָּ֔ה לַֽעֲמֹד֙ לִפְנֵ֣י הַכַּשְׂדִּ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר יָבֹ֖אוּ אֵלֵ֑ינוּ וְאַתֶּ֡ם אִסְפוּ֩ יַ֨יִן וְקַ֜יִץ וְשֶׁ֗מֶן וְשִׂ֙מוּ֙ בִּכְלֵיכֶ֔ם וּשְׁב֖וּ בְּעָרֵיכֶ֥ם אֲשֶׁר־תְּפַשְׂתֶּֽם׃


As for me, behold, I will dwell at Miżpa to stand before the Kasdim who will come to us: but as for you, gather wine, and summer fruits, and oil, and put them in your vessels, and dwell in your cities that you have taken.


###### Jeremiah 40:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:11)


וְגַ֣ם כׇּֽל־הַיְּהוּדִ֡ים אֲשֶׁר־בְּמוֹאָ֣ב**׀** וּבִבְנֵֽי־עַמּ֨וֹן וּבֶאֱד֜וֹם וַאֲשֶׁ֤ר בְּכׇל־הָֽאֲרָצוֹת֙ שָֽׁמְע֔וּ כִּֽי־נָתַ֧ן מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֛ל שְׁאֵרִ֖ית לִיהוּדָ֑ה וְכִי֙ הִפְקִ֣יד עֲלֵיהֶ֔ם אֶת־גְּדַלְיָ֖הוּ בֶּן־אֲחִיקָ֥ם בֶּן־שָׁפָֽן׃


Likewise when all the men of Yehuda that were in Mo᾽av, and among the children of ῾Ammon, and in Edom, and that were in all the countries, heard that the king of Bavel had left a remnant of Yehuda, and that he had set over them Gedalyahu the son of Aĥiqam the son of Shafan;


###### Jeremiah 40:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:12)


וַיָּשֻׁ֣בוּ כׇל־הַיְּהוּדִ֗ים מִכׇּל־הַמְּקֹמוֹת֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר נִדְּחוּ־שָׁ֔ם וַיָּבֹ֧אוּ אֶרֶץ־יְהוּדָ֛ה אֶל־גְּדַלְיָ֖הוּ הַמִּצְפָּ֑תָה וַיַּאַסְפ֛וּ יַ֥יִן וָקַ֖יִץ הַרְבֵּ֥ה מְאֹֽד׃{ס}


then all the men of Yehuda returned out of all places unto which they were driven, and came to the land of Yehuda, to Gedalyahu, to Miżpa, and gathered a great abundance of wine and summer fruits.


###### Jeremiah 40:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:13)


וְיֽוֹחָנָן֙ בֶּן־קָרֵ֔חַ וְכׇל־שָׂרֵ֥י הַחֲיָלִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר בַּשָּׂדֶ֑ה בָּ֥אוּ אֶל־גְּדַלְיָ֖הוּ הַמִּצְפָּֽתָה׃


Moreover Yoĥanan the son of Qareaĥ, and all the captains of the forces that were in the countryside, came to Gedalyahu to Miżpa,


###### Jeremiah 40:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:14)


וַיֹּאמְר֣וּ אֵלָ֗יו הֲיָדֹ֤עַ תֵּדַע֙ כִּ֞י בַּעֲלִ֣יס**׀** מֶ֣לֶךְ בְּנֵֽי־עַמּ֗וֹן שָׁלַח֙ אֶת־יִשְׁמָעֵ֣אל בֶּן־נְתַנְיָ֔ה לְהַכֹּתְךָ֖ נָ֑פֶשׁ וְלֹא־הֶאֱמִ֣ין לָהֶ֔ם גְּדַלְיָ֖הוּ בֶּן־אֲחִיקָֽם׃


and said to him, Dost thou certainly know that Ba῾alis the king of the children of ῾Ammon has sent Yishma᾽el the son of Netanya to slay thee? But Gedalyahu the son of Aĥiqam did not believe them.


###### Jeremiah 40:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:15)


וְיוֹחָנָ֣ן בֶּן־קָרֵ֡חַ אָמַ֣ר אֶל־גְּדַלְיָהוּ֩ בַסֵּ֨תֶר בַּמִּצְפָּ֜ה לֵאמֹ֗ר אֵ֤לְכָה נָּא֙ וְאַכֶּה֙ אֶת־יִשְׁמָעֵ֣אל בֶּן־נְתַנְיָ֔ה וְאִ֖ישׁ לֹ֣א יֵדָ֑ע לָ֧מָּה יַכֶּ֣כָּה נֶּ֗פֶשׁ וְנָפֹ֙צוּ֙ כׇּל־יְהוּדָה֙ הַנִּקְבָּצִ֣ים אֵלֶ֔יךָ וְאָבְדָ֖ה שְׁאֵרִ֥ית יְהוּדָֽה׃


Then Yoĥanan the son of Qareaĥ spoke to Gedalyahu in Miżpa secretly, saying, Let me go, I pray thee, and I will slay the son of Netanya, and no man shall know it: why should he slay thee, that all Yehuda that are gathered to thee should be scattered, and the remnant of Yehuda should perish?


###### Jeremiah 40:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/40:16)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר גְּדַלְיָ֤הוּ בֶן־אֲחִיקָם֙ אֶל־יוֹחָנָ֣ן בֶּן־קָרֵ֔חַ אַֽל־[תַּעֲשֵׂ֖ה] (תעש) אֶת־הַדָּבָ֣ר הַזֶּ֑ה כִּי־שֶׁ֛קֶר אַתָּ֥ה דֹבֵ֖ר אֶל־יִשְׁמָעֵֽאל׃{פ}


But Gedalyahu the son of Aĥiqam said to Yoĥanan the son of Qareaĥ, Thou shalt not do this thing: for thou speakest falsely of Yishma᾽el.



## Chapter 41

###### Jeremiah 41:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:1)


וַיְהִ֣י**׀** בַּחֹ֣דֶשׁ הַשְּׁבִיעִ֗י בָּ֣א יִשְׁמָעֵ֣אל בֶּן־נְתַנְיָ֣ה בֶן־אֱלִישָׁמָ֣ע מִזֶּ֣רַע הַ֠מְּלוּכָ֠ה וְרַבֵּ֨י הַמֶּ֜לֶךְ וַעֲשָׂרָ֨ה אֲנָשִׁ֥ים אִתּ֛וֹ אֶל־גְּדַלְיָ֥הוּ בֶן־אֲחִיקָ֖ם הַמִּצְפָּ֑תָה וַיֹּ֨אכְלוּ שָׁ֥ם לֶ֛חֶם יַחְדָּ֖ו בַּמִּצְפָּֽה׃


Now it came to pass in the seventh month, that Yishma᾽el the son of Netanya the son of Elishama, of the seed royal, and some of the chief officers of the king, and ten men with him, came to Gedalyahu the son of Aĥiqam to Miżpa; and there they did eat bread together in Miżpa.


###### Jeremiah 41:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:2)


וַיָּ֩קׇם֩ יִשְׁמָעֵ֨אל בֶּן־נְתַנְיָ֜ה וַעֲשֶׂ֥רֶת הָאֲנָשִׁ֣ים**׀** אֲשֶׁר־הָי֣וּ אִתּ֗וֹ וַ֠יַּכּ֠וּ אֶת־גְּדַלְיָ֨הוּ בֶן־אֲחִיקָ֧ם בֶּן־שָׁפָ֛ן בַּחֶ֖רֶב וַיָּ֣מֶת אֹת֑וֹ אֲשֶׁר־הִפְקִ֥יד מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֖ל בָּאָֽרֶץ׃


Then Yishma᾽el the son of Netanya and the ten men that were with him arose, and struck Gedalyahu the son of Aĥiqam the son of Shafan with the sword, and slew him, whom the king of Bavel had made governor over the land.


###### Jeremiah 41:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:3)


וְאֵ֣ת כׇּל־הַיְּהוּדִ֗ים אֲשֶׁר־הָי֨וּ אִתּ֤וֹ אֶת־גְּדַלְיָ֙הוּ֙ בַּמִּצְפָּ֔ה וְאֶת־הַכַּשְׂדִּ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר נִמְצְאוּ־שָׁ֑ם אֵ֚ת אַנְשֵׁ֣י הַמִּלְחָמָ֔ה הִכָּ֖ה יִשְׁמָעֵֽאל׃


And Yishma᾽el slew all the men of Yehuda that were with him, with Gedalyahu, at Miżpa, and the Kasdim that were found there, and the men of war.


###### Jeremiah 41:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:4)


וַיְהִ֛י בַּיּ֥וֹם הַשֵּׁנִ֖י לְהָמִ֣ית אֶת־גְּדַלְיָ֑הוּ וְאִ֖ישׁ לֹ֥א יָדָֽע׃


And it came to pass on the second day after the killing of Gedalyahu, (and no man knew it,)


###### Jeremiah 41:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:5)


וַיָּבֹ֣אוּ אֲ֠נָשִׁ֠ים מִשְּׁכֶ֞ם מִשִּׁל֤וֹ וּמִשֹּֽׁמְרוֹן֙ שְׁמֹנִ֣ים אִ֔ישׁ מְגֻלְּחֵ֥י זָקָ֛ן וּקְרֻעֵ֥י בְגָדִ֖ים וּמִתְגֹּֽדְדִ֑ים וּמִנְחָ֤ה וּלְבוֹנָה֙ בְּיָדָ֔ם לְהָבִ֖יא בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


that there came certain men from Shekhem, from Shilo, and from Shomeron, eighty men, (their beards shaven, and their clothes rent, and having cut themselves,) with offerings and incense in their hand, to bring them to the house of the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 41:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:6)


וַ֠יֵּצֵ֠א יִשְׁמָעֵ֨אל בֶּן־נְתַנְיָ֤ה לִקְרָאתָם֙ מִן־הַמִּצְפָּ֔ה הֹלֵ֥ךְ הָלֹ֖ךְ וּבֹכֶ֑ה וַֽיְהִי֙ כִּפְגֹ֣שׁ אֹתָ֔ם וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֲלֵיהֶ֔ם בֹּ֖אוּ אֶל־גְּדַלְיָ֥הוּ בֶן־אֲחִיקָֽם׃


And Yishma᾽el the son of Netanya went out of Miżpa to meet them, weeping as he went: and it came to pass, as he met them, that he said to them, Come to Gedalyahu the son of Aĥiqam.


###### Jeremiah 41:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:7)


וַיְהִ֕י כְּבוֹאָ֖ם אֶל־תּ֣וֹךְ הָעִ֑יר וַיִּשְׁחָטֵ֞ם יִשְׁמָעֵ֤אל בֶּן־נְתַנְיָה֙ אֶל־תּ֣וֹךְ הַבּ֔וֹר ה֖וּא וְהָאֲנָשִׁ֥ים אֲשֶׁר־אִתּֽוֹ׃


And when they came into the midst of the city, Yishma᾽el the son of Netanya slew them, and cast them into the midst of the pit, he, and the men that were with him.


###### Jeremiah 41:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:8)


וַעֲשָׂרָ֨ה אֲנָשִׁ֜ים נִמְצְאוּ־בָ֗ם וַיֹּאמְר֤וּ אֶל־יִשְׁמָעֵאל֙ אַל־תְּמִתֵ֔נוּ כִּֽי־יֶשׁ־לָ֤נוּ מַטְמֹנִים֙ בַּשָּׂדֶ֔ה חִטִּ֥ים וּשְׂעֹרִ֖ים וְשֶׁ֣מֶן וּדְבָ֑שׁ וַיֶּחְדַּ֕ל וְלֹ֥א הֱמִיתָ֖ם בְּת֥וֹךְ אֲחֵיהֶֽם׃


But ten men were found among them that said to Yishma᾽el, Slay us not: for we have in the countryside hidden stores, of wheat, and of barley, and of oil, and of honey. So he desisted, and did not slay them among their brethren.


###### Jeremiah 41:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:9)


וְהַבּ֗וֹר אֲשֶׁר֩ הִשְׁלִ֨יךְ שָׁ֤ם יִשְׁמָעֵאל֙ אֵ֣ת**׀** כׇּל־פִּגְרֵ֣י הָאֲנָשִׁ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֤ר הִכָּה֙ בְּיַד־גְּדַלְיָ֔הוּ ה֗וּא אֲשֶׁ֤ר עָשָׂה֙ הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ אָסָ֔א מִפְּנֵ֖י בַּעְשָׁ֣א מֶלֶךְ־יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל אֹת֗וֹ מִלֵּ֛א יִשְׁמָעֵ֥אל בֶּן־נְתַנְיָ֖הוּ חֲלָלִֽים׃


Now the pit wherein into which Yishma᾽el had cast all the dead bodies of the men, whom he had slain because of Gedalyahu, was that which Asa the king had made for fear of Ba῾sha king of Yisra᾽el: and Yishma᾽el the son of Netanyahu filled it with the slain.


###### Jeremiah 41:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:10)


וַיִּ֣שְׁבְּ׀יִ֠שְׁמָעֵ֠אל אֶת־כׇּל־שְׁאֵרִ֨ית הָעָ֜ם אֲשֶׁ֣ר בַּמִּצְפָּ֗ה אֶת־בְּנ֤וֹת הַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ וְאֶת־כׇּל־הָעָם֙ הַנִּשְׁאָרִ֣ים בַּמִּצְפָּ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֣ר הִפְקִ֗יד נְבֽוּזַרְאֲדָן֙ רַב־טַבָּחִ֔ים אֶת־גְּדַלְיָ֖הוּ בֶּן־אֲחִיקָ֑ם וַיִּשְׁבֵּם֙ יִשְׁמָעֵ֣אל בֶּן־נְתַנְיָ֔ה וַיֵּ֕לֶךְ לַעֲבֹ֖ר אֶל־בְּנֵ֥י עַמּֽוֹן׃{ס}


Then Yishma᾽el carried away captive all the residue of the people that were in Miżpa, the king’s daughters, and all the people that remained in Miżpa, whom Nevuzar᾽adan the captain of the guard had committed to Gedalyahu the son of Aĥiqam: and Yishma᾽el the son of Netanya carried them away captive, and departed to go over to the children of ῾Ammon.


###### Jeremiah 41:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:11)


וַיִּשְׁמַע֙ יוֹחָנָ֣ן בֶּן־קָרֵ֔חַ וְכׇל־שָׂרֵ֥י הַחֲיָלִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר אִתּ֑וֹ אֵ֤ת כׇּל־הָרָעָה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשָׂ֔ה יִשְׁמָעֵ֖אל בֶּן־נְתַנְיָֽה׃


But when Yoĥanan the son of Qareaĥ, and all the captains of the forces that were with him, heard of all the evil that Yishma᾽el the son of Netanya had done,


###### Jeremiah 41:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:12)


וַיִּקְחוּ֙ אֶת־כׇּל־הָ֣אֲנָשִׁ֔ים וַיֵּ֣לְכ֔וּ לְהִלָּחֵ֖ם עִם־יִשְׁמָעֵ֣אל בֶּן־נְתַנְיָ֑ה וַיִּמְצְא֣וּ אֹת֔וֹ אֶל־מַ֥יִם רַבִּ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר בְּגִבְעֽוֹן׃


then they took all the men, and went to fight with Yishma᾽el the son of Netanya, and found him by the great pool of waters that are in Giv῾on.


###### Jeremiah 41:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:13)


וַיְהִ֗י כִּרְא֤וֹת כׇּל־הָעָם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר אֶת־יִשְׁמָעֵ֔אל אֶת־יֽוֹחָנָן֙ בֶּן־קָרֵ֔חַ וְאֵ֛ת כׇּל־שָׂרֵ֥י הַחֲיָלִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר אִתּ֑וֹ וַיִּשְׂמָֽחוּ׃


Now it came to pass, that when all the people who were with Yishma᾽el saw Yoĥanan the son of Qareaĥ, and all the captains of the forces that were with him, then they were glad.


###### Jeremiah 41:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:14)


וַיָּסֹ֙בּוּ֙ כׇּל־הָעָ֔ם אֲשֶׁר־שָׁבָ֥ה יִשְׁמָעֵ֖אל מִן־הַמִּצְפָּ֑ה וַיָּשֻׁ֙בוּ֙ וַיֵּ֣לְכ֔וּ אֶל־יוֹחָנָ֖ן בֶּן־קָרֵֽחַ׃


So all the people that Yishma᾽el had carried away captive from Miżpa turned about and came back, and went to Yoĥanan the son of Qareaĥ.


###### Jeremiah 41:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:15)


וְיִשְׁמָעֵ֣אל בֶּן־נְתַנְיָ֗ה נִמְלַט֙ בִּשְׁמֹנָ֣ה אֲנָשִׁ֔ים מִפְּנֵ֖י יוֹחָנָ֑ן וַיֵּ֖לֶךְ אֶל־בְּנֵ֥י עַמּֽוֹן׃{ס}


But Yishma᾽el the son of Netanya escaped from Yoĥanan with eight men, and went to the children of ‘Ammon.


###### Jeremiah 41:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:16)


וַיִּקַּח֩ יוֹחָנָ֨ן בֶּן־קָרֵ֜חַ וְכׇל־שָׂרֵ֧י הַחֲיָלִ֣ים אֲשֶׁר־אִתּ֗וֹ אֵ֣ת כׇּל־שְׁאֵרִ֤ית הָעָם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הֵ֠שִׁ֠יב מֵאֵ֨ת יִשְׁמָעֵ֤אל בֶּן־נְתַנְיָה֙ מִן־הַמִּצְפָּ֔ה אַחַ֣ר הִכָּ֔ה אֶת־גְּדַלְיָ֖ה בֶּן־אֲחִיקָ֑ם גְּבָרִ֞ים אַנְשֵׁ֣י הַמִּלְחָמָ֗ה וְנָשִׁ֤ים וְטַף֙ וְסָ֣רִסִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר הֵשִׁ֖יב מִגִּבְעֽוֹן׃


Then Yoĥanan the son of Qareaĥ, and all the captains of the forces that were with him, took all the remnant of the people from Miżpa, whom he had recovered from Yishma᾽el the son of Netanya, (after the latter had slain Gedalya the son of Aĥiqam,) mighty men of war, and the women, and the children, and the eunuchs whom he then brought from Giv῾on:


###### Jeremiah 41:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:17)


וַיֵּלְכ֗וּ וַיֵּֽשְׁבוּ֙ בְּגֵר֣וּת (כמוהם) [כִּמְהָ֔ם] אֲשֶׁר־אֵ֖צֶל בֵּ֣ית לָ֑חֶם לָלֶ֖כֶת לָב֥וֹא מִצְרָֽיִם׃


and they departed, and dwelt in Gerut-kimaham, which is by Bet-leĥem, to go to enter into Miżrayim,


###### Jeremiah 41:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/41:18)


מִפְּנֵי֙ הַכַּשְׂדִּ֔ים כִּ֥י יָֽרְא֖וּ מִפְּנֵיהֶ֑ם כִּֽי־הִכָּ֞ה יִשְׁמָעֵ֣אל בֶּן־נְתַנְיָ֗ה אֶת־גְּדַלְיָ֙הוּ֙ בֶּן־אֲחִיקָ֔ם אֲשֶׁר־הִפְקִ֥יד מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֖ל בָּאָֽרֶץ׃{פ}


because of the Kasdim: for they were afraid of them, because of Yishma᾽el the son of Netanya had slain Gedalyahu the son of Aĥiqam, whom the king of Bavel had made governor over the land.



## Chapter 42

###### Jeremiah 42:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:1)


וַֽיִּגְּשׁוּ֙ כׇּל־שָׂרֵ֣י הַחֲיָלִ֔ים וְיֽוֹחָנָן֙ בֶּן־קָרֵ֔חַ וִיזַנְיָ֖ה בֶּן־הוֹשַֽׁעְיָ֑ה וְכׇל־הָעָ֖ם מִקָּטֹ֥ן וְעַד־גָּדֽוֹל׃


Then all the captains of the forces, and Yoĥanan the son of Qareaĥ, and Yezanya the son of Hosha῾ya, and all the people from the least even to the greatest, came near,


###### Jeremiah 42:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:2)


וַיֹּאמְר֞וּ אֶל־יִרְמְיָ֣הוּ הַנָּבִ֗יא תִּפׇּל־נָ֤א תְחִנָּתֵ֙נוּ֙ לְפָנֶ֔יךָ וְהִתְפַּלֵּ֤ל בַּעֲדֵ֙נוּ֙ אֶל־יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ בְּעַ֖ד כׇּל־הַשְּׁאֵרִ֣ית הַזֹּ֑את כִּֽי־נִשְׁאַ֤רְנֽוּ מְעַט֙ מֵֽהַרְבֵּ֔ה כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר עֵינֶ֖יךָ רֹא֥וֹת אֹתָֽנוּ׃


and said to Yirmeyahu the prophet, Let now our supplication be accepted before thee, and pray for us to the Lord thy God, for all this remnant; (for we are left but a few of many, as thy eyes see us: )


###### Jeremiah 42:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:3)


וְיַגֶּד־לָ֙נוּ֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ אֶת־הַדֶּ֖רֶךְ אֲשֶׁ֣ר נֵֽלֶךְ־בָּ֑הּ וְאֶת־הַדָּבָ֖ר אֲשֶׁ֥ר נַעֲשֶֽׂה׃


that the Lord thy God may show us the way in which we may walk, and the thing we may do.


###### Jeremiah 42:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:4)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר אֲלֵיהֶ֜ם יִרְמְיָ֤הוּ הַנָּבִיא֙ שָׁמַ֔עְתִּי הִנְנִ֧י מִתְפַּלֵּ֛ל אֶל־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֖ם כְּדִבְרֵיכֶ֑ם וְֽהָיָ֡ה כׇּֽל־הַדָּבָר֩ אֲשֶׁר־יַעֲנֶ֨ה יְהֹוָ֤ה אֶתְכֶם֙ אַגִּ֣יד לָכֶ֔ם לֹא־אֶמְנַ֥ע מִכֶּ֖ם דָּבָֽר׃


Then Yirmeyahu the prophet said to them, I have heard you; behold, I will pray to the Lord your God according to your words; and it shall come to pass, that whatever thing the Lord shall answer you, I will declare it to you; I will keep nothing back from you.


###### Jeremiah 42:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:5)


וְהֵ֙מָּה֙ אָמְר֣וּ אֶֽל־יִרְמְיָ֔הוּ יְהִ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ בָּ֔נוּ לְעֵ֖ד אֱמֶ֣ת וְנֶאֱמָ֑ן אִם־לֹ֡א כְּֽכׇל־הַ֠דָּבָ֠ר אֲשֶׁ֨ר יִֽשְׁלָחֲךָ֜ יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהֶ֛יךָ אֵלֵ֖ינוּ כֵּ֥ן נַעֲשֶֽׂה׃


Then they said to Yirmeyahu, The Lord be a true and faithful witness between us, if we do not act according to all the things for which the Lord thy God shall send thee to us.


###### Jeremiah 42:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:6)


אִם־ט֣וֹב וְאִם־רָ֔ע בְּק֣וֹל**׀** יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵ֗ינוּ אֲשֶׁ֨ר (אנו) [אֲנַ֜חְנוּ] שֹׁלְחִ֥ים אֹתְךָ֛ אֵלָ֖יו נִשְׁמָ֑ע לְמַ֙עַן֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר יִֽיטַב־לָ֔נוּ כִּ֣י נִשְׁמַ֔ע בְּק֖וֹל יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ׃{פ}


Whether it be good, or whether it be evil, we will obey the voice of the Lord our God, to whom we send thee; that it may be well with us, when we obey the voice of the Lord our God.


###### Jeremiah 42:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:7)


וַיְהִ֕י מִקֵּ֖ץ עֲשֶׂ֣רֶת יָמִ֑ים וַיְהִ֥י דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶֽל־יִרְמְיָֽהוּ׃


And it came to pass after ten days, that the word of the Lord came to Yirmeyahu.


###### Jeremiah 42:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:8)


וַיִּקְרָ֗א אֶל־יֽוֹחָנָן֙ בֶּן־קָרֵ֔חַ וְאֶ֛ל כׇּל־שָׂרֵ֥י הַחֲיָלִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר אִתּ֑וֹ וּ֨לְכׇל־הָעָ֔ם לְמִקָּטֹ֖ן וְעַד־גָּדֽוֹל׃


Then he called Yoĥanan the son of Qareaĥ, and all the captains of the forces who were with him, and all the people from the least even to the greatest,


###### Jeremiah 42:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:9)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֲלֵיהֶ֔ם כֹּה־אָמַ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל אֲשֶׁ֨ר שְׁלַחְתֶּ֤ם אֹתִי֙ אֵלָ֔יו לְהַפִּ֥יל תְּחִנַּתְכֶ֖ם לְפָנָֽיו׃


and he said to them, Thus says the Lord, the God of Yisra᾽el, to whom you sent me to present your supplication before him;


###### Jeremiah 42:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:10)


אִם־שׁ֤וֹב תֵּֽשְׁבוּ֙ בָּאָ֣רֶץ הַזֹּ֔את וּבָנִ֤יתִי אֶתְכֶם֙ וְלֹ֣א אֶהֱרֹ֔ס וְנָטַעְתִּ֥י אֶתְכֶ֖ם וְלֹ֣א אֶתּ֑וֹשׁ כִּ֤י נִחַ֙מְתִּי֙ אֶל־הָ֣רָעָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר עָשִׂ֖יתִי לָכֶֽם׃


If you will still abide in this land, then I will build you, and not pull you down, and I will plant you, and not pluck you up: for I repent of the evil that I have done to you.


###### Jeremiah 42:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:11)


אַל־תִּֽירְא֗וּ מִפְּנֵי֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ בָּבֶ֔ל אֲשֶׁר־אַתֶּ֥ם יְרֵאִ֖ים מִפָּנָ֑יו אַל־תִּֽירְא֤וּ מִמֶּ֙נּוּ֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה כִּֽי־אִתְּכֶ֣ם אָ֔נִי לְהוֹשִׁ֧יעַ אֶתְכֶ֛ם וּלְהַצִּ֥יל אֶתְכֶ֖ם מִיָּדֽוֹ׃


Be not afraid of the king of Bavel, of whom you are afraid; be not afraid of him, says the Lord: for I am with you to save you, and to deliver you from his hand.


###### Jeremiah 42:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:12)


וְאֶתֵּ֥ן לָכֶ֛ם רַחֲמִ֖ים וְרִחַ֣ם אֶתְכֶ֑ם וְהֵשִׁ֥יב אֶתְכֶ֖ם אֶל־אַדְמַתְכֶֽם׃


And I will show mercies to you, that he may have mercy upon you, and let you return to your own land.


###### Jeremiah 42:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:13)


וְאִם־אֹמְרִ֣ים אַתֶּ֔ם לֹ֥א נֵשֵׁ֖ב בָּאָ֣רֶץ הַזֹּ֑את לְבִלְתִּ֣י שְׁמֹ֔עַ בְּק֖וֹל יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃


But if you say, We will not dwell in this land, neither obey the voice of the Lord your God,


###### Jeremiah 42:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:14)


לֵאמֹ֗ר לֹ֚א כִּ֣י אֶ֤רֶץ מִצְרַ֙יִם֙ נָב֔וֹא אֲשֶׁ֤ר לֹֽא־נִרְאֶה֙ מִלְחָמָ֔ה וְק֥וֹל שׁוֹפָ֖ר לֹ֣א נִשְׁמָ֑ע וְלַלֶּ֥חֶם לֹֽא־נִרְעָ֖ב וְשָׁ֥ם נֵשֵֽׁב׃


saying, No; but we will go into the land of Miżrayim, where we shall see no war, nor hear the sound of the shofar, nor have hunger for bread; and there will we dwell:


###### Jeremiah 42:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:15)


וְעַתָּ֕ה לָכֵ֛ן שִׁמְע֥וּ דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה שְׁאֵרִ֣ית יְהוּדָ֑ה כֹּֽה־אָמַר֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה צְבָא֜וֹת אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל אִם־אַ֠תֶּ֠ם שׂ֣וֹם תְּשִׂמ֤וּן פְּנֵיכֶם֙ לָבֹ֣א מִצְרַ֔יִם וּבָאתֶ֖ם לָג֥וּר שָֽׁם׃


and now therefore hear the word of the Lord, you remnant of Yehuda; thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Yisra᾽el; If indeed you set your faces to enter into Miżrayim and go to sojourn there;


###### Jeremiah 42:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:16)


וְהָיְתָ֣ה הַחֶ֗רֶב אֲשֶׁ֤ר אַתֶּם֙ יְרֵאִ֣ים מִמֶּ֔נָּה שָׁ֛ם תַּשִּׂ֥יג אֶתְכֶ֖ם בְּאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרָ֑יִם וְהָרָעָ֞ב אֲשֶׁר־אַתֶּ֣ם**׀** דֹּאֲגִ֣ים מִמֶּ֗נּוּ שָׁ֣ם יִדְבַּ֧ק אַחֲרֵיכֶ֛ם מִצְרַ֖יִם וְשָׁ֥ם תָּמֻֽתוּ׃


then it shall come to pass, that the sword, which you feared, shall overtake you there in the land of Miżrayim, and the famine, of which you were afraid, shall follow close after you there in Miżrayim; and there you shall die.


###### Jeremiah 42:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:17)


וְיִֽהְי֣וּ כׇל־הָאֲנָשִׁ֗ים אֲשֶׁר־שָׂ֨מוּ אֶת־פְּנֵיהֶ֜ם לָב֤וֹא מִצְרַ֙יִם֙ לָג֣וּר שָׁ֔ם יָמ֕וּתוּ בַּחֶ֖רֶב בָּרָעָ֣ב וּבַדָּ֑בֶר וְלֹֽא־יִהְיֶ֤ה לָהֶם֙ שָׂרִ֣יד וּפָלִ֔יט מִפְּנֵי֙ הָרָעָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר אֲנִ֖י מֵבִ֥יא עֲלֵיהֶֽם׃


And all the men that set their faces to go into Miżrayim to sojourn there shall die by the sword, by the famine, and by the pestilence: and none of them shall remain or escape from the evil that I will bring upon them.


###### Jeremiah 42:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:18)


כִּי֩ כֹ֨ה אָמַ֜ר יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָאוֹת֮ אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵל֒ כַּאֲשֶׁר֩ נִתַּ֨ךְ אַפִּ֜י וַחֲמָתִ֗י עַל־יֹֽשְׁבֵי֙ יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם כֵּ֣ן תִּתַּ֤ךְ חֲמָתִי֙ עֲלֵיכֶ֔ם בְּבֹאֲכֶ֖ם מִצְרָ֑יִם וִהְיִיתֶ֞ם לְאָלָ֤ה וּלְשַׁמָּה֙ וְלִקְלָלָ֣ה וּלְחֶרְפָּ֔ה וְלֹא־תִרְא֣וּ ע֔וֹד אֶת־הַמָּק֖וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃


For thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Yisra᾽el; As my anger and my fury has been poured out upon the inhabitants of Yerushalayim; so shall my fury be poured out upon you, when you shall enter into Miżrayim: and you shall be an oath, and an astonishment, and a curse, and a reproach; and you shall see this place no more.


###### Jeremiah 42:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:19)


דִּבֶּ֨ר יְהֹוָ֤ה עֲלֵיכֶם֙ שְׁאֵרִ֣ית יְהוּדָ֔ה אַל־תָּבֹ֖אוּ מִצְרָ֑יִם יָדֹ֙עַ֙ תֵּדְע֔וּ כִּֽי־הַעִידֹ֥תִי בָכֶ֖ם הַיּֽוֹם׃


The Lord has said concerning you, O remnant of Yehuda; Do not go into Miżrayim: know certainly that I have forewarned you this day.


###### Jeremiah 42:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:20)


כִּ֣י (התעתים) [הִתְעֵיתֶם֮] בְּנַפְשׁוֹתֵיכֶם֒ כִּֽי־אַתֶּ֞ם שְׁלַחְתֶּ֣ם אֹתִ֗י אֶל־יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹֽהֵיכֶם֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר הִתְפַּלֵּ֣ל בַּעֲדֵ֔נוּ אֶל־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵ֑ינוּ וּכְכֹל֩ אֲשֶׁ֨ר יֹאמַ֜ר יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהֵ֛ינוּ כֵּ֥ן הַגֶּד־לָ֖נוּ וְעָשִֽׂינוּ׃


For you have gone astray at peril of your lives, when you sent me to the Lord your God, saying, Pray for us to the Lord our God; and according to all that the Lord our God shall say, so declare to us, and we will do it.


###### Jeremiah 42:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:21)


וָאַגִּ֥ד לָכֶ֖ם הַיּ֑וֹם וְלֹ֣א שְׁמַעְתֶּ֗ם בְּקוֹל֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֔ם וּלְכֹ֖ל אֲשֶׁר־שְׁלָחַ֥נִי אֲלֵיכֶֽם׃


And now I have this day declared it to you; but you have not obeyed the voice of the Lord your God, nor anything for which he has sent me to you.


###### Jeremiah 42:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/42:22)


וְעַתָּה֙ יָדֹ֣עַ תֵּדְע֔וּ כִּ֗י בַּחֶ֛רֶב בָּרָעָ֥ב וּבַדֶּ֖בֶר תָּמ֑וּתוּ בַּמָּקוֹם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר חֲפַצְתֶּ֔ם לָב֖וֹא לָג֥וּר שָֽׁם׃{ס}


Now therefore know certainly that you shall die by the sword, by the famine, and by the pestilence, in the place where you desire to go and to sojourn.



## Chapter 43

###### Jeremiah 43:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:1)


וַיְהִי֩ כְּכַלּ֨וֹת יִרְמְיָ֜הוּ לְדַבֵּ֣ר אֶל־כׇּל־הָעָ֗ם אֶת־כׇּל־דִּבְרֵי֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵיהֶ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֧ר שְׁלָח֛וֹ יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיהֶ֖ם אֲלֵיהֶ֑ם אֵ֥ת כׇּל־הַדְּבָרִ֖ים הָאֵֽלֶּה׃{ס}


And it came to pass, that when Yirmeyahu had made an end of speaking to all the people all the words of the Lord their God, for which the Lord their God had sent him to them, all these words,


###### Jeremiah 43:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:2)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר עֲזַרְיָ֤ה בֶן־הוֹשַֽׁעְיָה֙ וְיוֹחָנָ֣ן בֶּן־קָרֵ֔חַ וְכׇל־הָאֲנָשִׁ֖ים הַזֵּדִ֑ים אֹמְרִ֣ים אֶֽל־יִרְמְיָ֗הוּ שֶׁ֚קֶר אַתָּ֣ה מְדַבֵּ֔ר לֹ֣א שְׁלָחֲךָ֞ יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהֵ֙ינוּ֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר לֹא־תָבֹ֥אוּ מִצְרַ֖יִם לָג֥וּר שָֽׁם׃


then ῾Azarya the son of Hosha῾ya and Yoĥanan the son of Qareaĥ, and all the proud men, spoke, saying to Yirmeyahu, Thou speakest falsely: the Lord our God has not sent thee to say, Do not go into Miżrayim to sojourn there:


###### Jeremiah 43:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:3)


כִּ֗י בָּרוּךְ֙ בֶּן־נֵ֣רִיָּ֔ה מַסִּ֥ית אֹתְךָ֖ בָּ֑נוּ לְמַ֩עַן֩ תֵּ֨ת אֹתָ֤נוּ בְיַֽד־הַכַּשְׂדִּים֙ לְהָמִ֣ית אֹתָ֔נוּ וּלְהַגְל֥וֹת אֹתָ֖נוּ בָּבֶֽל׃


but Barukh the son of Neriyya sets thee on against us, to deliver us into the hand of the Kasdim, that they might put us into Bavel.


###### Jeremiah 43:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:4)


וְלֹא־שָׁמַע֩ יוֹחָנָ֨ן בֶּן־קָרֵ֜חַ וְכׇל־שָׂרֵ֧י הַחֲיָלִ֛ים וְכׇל־הָעָ֖ם בְּק֣וֹל יְהֹוָ֑ה לָשֶׁ֖בֶת בְּאֶ֥רֶץ יְהוּדָֽה׃


So Yoĥanan the son of Qareaĥ, and all the captains of the forces, and all the people, did not obey the voice of the Lord, to dwell in the land of Yehuda.


###### Jeremiah 43:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:5)


וַיִּקַּ֞ח יוֹחָנָ֤ן בֶּן־קָרֵ֙חַ֙ וְכׇל־שָׂרֵ֣י הַחֲיָלִ֔ים אֵ֖ת כׇּל־שְׁאֵרִ֣ית יְהוּדָ֑ה אֲשֶׁר־שָׁ֗בוּ מִכׇּל־הַגּוֹיִם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר נִדְּחוּ־שָׁ֔ם לָג֖וּר בְּאֶ֥רֶץ יְהוּדָֽה׃


But Yoĥanan the son of Qareaĥ, and all the captains of the forces, took all the remnant of Yehuda, that were returned from all nations, into which they had been driven, to dwell in the land of Yehuda;


###### Jeremiah 43:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:6)


אֶֽת־הַ֠גְּבָרִ֠ים וְאֶת־הַנָּשִׁ֣ים וְאֶת־הַטַּף֮ וְאֶת־בְּנ֣וֹת הַמֶּ֒לֶךְ֒ וְאֵ֣ת כׇּל־הַנֶּ֗פֶשׁ אֲשֶׁ֤ר הִנִּ֙יחַ֙ נְבוּזַרְאֲדָ֣ן רַב־טַבָּחִ֔ים אֶת־גְּדַלְיָ֖הוּ בֶּן־אֲחִיקָ֣ם בֶּן־שָׁפָ֑ן וְאֵת֙ יִרְמְיָ֣הוּ הַנָּבִ֔יא וְאֶת־בָּר֖וּךְ בֶּן־נֵרִיָּֽהוּ׃


men, and women, and children, and the king’s daughters, and every person that Nevuzar᾽adan the captain of the guard had left with Gedalyahu the son of Aĥiqam the son of Shafan, together with Yirmeyahu the prophet, and Barukh the son of Neriyya.


###### Jeremiah 43:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:7)


וַיָּבֹ֙אוּ֙ אֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרַ֔יִם כִּ֛י לֹ֥א שָׁמְע֖וּ בְּק֣וֹל יְהֹוָ֑ה וַיָּבֹ֖אוּ עַד־תַּחְפַּנְחֵֽס׃{ס}


And they came into the land of Miżrayim: for they did not obey the voice of the Lord: thus they came to Taĥpanĥes.


###### Jeremiah 43:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:8)


וַיְהִ֤י דְבַר־יְהֹוָה֙ אֶֽל־יִרְמְיָ֔הוּ בְּתַחְפַּנְחֵ֖ס לֵאמֹֽר׃


Then the word of the Lord came to Yirmeyahu in Taĥpanĥes, saying,


###### Jeremiah 43:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:9)


קַ֣ח בְּיָדְךָ֞ אֲבָנִ֣ים גְּדֹל֗וֹת וּטְמַנְתָּ֤ם בַּמֶּ֙לֶט֙ בַּמַּלְבֵּ֔ן אֲשֶׁ֛ר בְּפֶ֥תַח בֵּית־פַּרְעֹ֖ה בְּתַחְפַּנְחֵ֑ס לְעֵינֵ֖י אֲנָשִׁ֥ים יְהוּדִֽים׃


Take great stones in thy hand, and hide them in the mortar in the brick work, which is at the entrance of Par῾o’s house in Taĥpanĥes, in the sight of the men of Yehuda;


###### Jeremiah 43:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:10)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֲלֵיהֶ֡ם כֹּה־אָמַר֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה צְבָא֜וֹת אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל הִנְנִ֤י שֹׁלֵ֙חַ֙ וְ֠לָקַחְתִּ֠י אֶת־נְבוּכַדְרֶאצַּ֤ר מֶֽלֶךְ־בָּבֶל֙ עַבְדִּ֔י וְשַׂמְתִּ֣י כִסְא֔וֹ מִמַּ֛עַל לָאֲבָנִ֥ים הָאֵ֖לֶּה אֲשֶׁ֣ר טָמָ֑נְתִּי וְנָטָ֥ה אֶת־[שַׁפְרִיר֖וֹ] (שפרורו) עֲלֵיהֶֽם׃


and say to them, Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Yisra᾽el; Behold, I will send and take Nevukhadreżżar the king of Bavel, my servant, and will set his throne upon these stones that I have hid; and he shall spread his royal pavilion over them.


###### Jeremiah 43:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:11)


(ובאה) [וּבָ֕א] וְהִכָּ֖ה אֶת־אֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרָ֑יִם אֲשֶׁ֧ר לַמָּ֣וֶת לַמָּ֗וֶת וַאֲשֶׁ֤ר לַשְּׁבִי֙ לַשֶּׁ֔בִי וַאֲשֶׁ֥ר לַחֶ֖רֶב לֶחָֽרֶב׃


And when he comes, he shall smite the land of Miżrayim, and deliver such as are for death to death; and such as are for captivity to captivity; and such as are for the sword to the sword.


###### Jeremiah 43:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:12)


וְהִצַּ֣תִּי אֵ֗שׁ בְּבָתֵּי֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י מִצְרַ֔יִם וּשְׂרָפָ֖ם וְשָׁבָ֑ם וְעָטָה֩ אֶת־אֶ֨רֶץ מִצְרַ֜יִם כַּֽאֲשֶׁר־יַעְטֶ֤ה הָרֹעֶה֙ אֶת־בִּגְד֔וֹ וְיָצָ֥א מִשָּׁ֖ם בְּשָׁלֽוֹם׃


And I will kindle a fire in the houses of the gods of Miżrayim, and he shall burn them, and carry them away captives: and he shall fold up the land of Miżrayim, as a shepherd folds up his garment; and he shall go out from there in peace.


###### Jeremiah 43:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/43:13)


וְשִׁבַּ֗ר אֶֽת־מַצְּבוֹת֙ בֵּ֣ית שֶׁ֔מֶשׁ אֲשֶׁ֖ר בְּאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרָ֑יִם וְאֶת־בָּתֵּ֥י אֱלֹהֵֽי־מִצְרַ֖יִם יִשְׂרֹ֥ף בָּאֵֽשׁ׃{פ}


And he shall break up the pillars of Bet-Shemesh, that are in the land of Miż­rayim; and he shall burn with fire the houses of the gods of Miżrayim.



## Chapter 44

###### Jeremiah 44:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/44:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/44:1)


הַדָּבָר֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הָיָ֣ה אֶֽל־יִרְמְיָ֔הוּ אֶ֚ל כׇּל־הַיְּהוּדִ֔ים הַיֹּשְׁבִ֖ים בְּאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרָ֑יִם הַיֹּשְׁבִ֤ים בְּמִגְדֹּל֙ וּבְתַחְפַּנְחֵ֣ס וּבְנֹ֔ף וּבְאֶ֥רֶץ פַּתְר֖וֹס לֵאמֹֽר׃


The word that came to Yirmeyahu concerning all the men of Yehuda who dwelt in the land of Miżrayim, who dwelt at Migdol, and at Taĥpanĥes, and at Nof, and in the country of Patros, saying,


###### Jeremiah 44:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/44:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/44:2)


כֹּה־אָמַ֞ר יְהֹוָ֤ה צְבָאוֹת֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אַתֶּ֣ם רְאִיתֶ֗ם אֵ֤ת כׇּל־הָֽרָעָה֙ אֲשֶׁ֤ר הֵבֵ֙אתִי֙ עַל־יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם וְעַ֖ל כׇּל־עָרֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֑ה וְהִנָּ֤ם חׇרְבָּה֙ הַיּ֣וֹם הַזֶּ֔ה וְאֵ֥ין בָּהֶ֖ם יוֹשֵֽׁב׃


Thus says the Lord of hosts the God of Yisra᾽el; You have seen all the evil that I have brought upon Yerushalayim, and upon all the cities of Yehuda; and, behold, this day they are a ruin, and no one dwells in them,


###### Jeremiah 44:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/44:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/44:3)


מִפְּנֵ֣י רָעָתָ֗ם אֲשֶׁ֤ר עָשׂוּ֙ לְהַכְעִסֵ֔נִי לָלֶ֣כֶת לְקַטֵּ֔ר לַעֲבֹ֖ד לֵאלֹהִ֣ים אֲחֵרִ֑ים אֲשֶׁר֙ לֹ֣א יְדָע֔וּם הֵ֖מָּה אַתֶּ֥ם וַאֲבֹתֵיכֶֽם׃


because of their wickedness which they have committed to provoke me to anger, in that they went to burn incense, and to serve other gods, whom they knew not, neither they, you, nor your fathers.


###### Jeremiah 44:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/44:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/44:4)


וָאֶשְׁלַ֤ח אֲלֵיכֶם֙ אֶת־כׇּל־עֲבָדַ֣י הַנְּבִיאִ֔ים הַשְׁכֵּ֥ים וְשָׁלֹ֖חַ לֵאמֹ֑ר אַל־נָ֣א תַעֲשׂ֗וּ אֵ֛ת דְּבַֽר־הַתֹּעֵבָ֥ה הַזֹּ֖את אֲשֶׁ֥ר שָׂנֵֽאתִי׃


Yet I sent to you all my servants the prophets, sending them from morning till night, saying, Oh, do not do this abominable thing that I hate.


###### Jeremiah 44:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/44:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/44:5)


וְלֹ֤א שָֽׁמְעוּ֙ וְלֹא־הִטּ֣וּ אֶת־אׇזְנָ֔ם לָשׁ֖וּב מֵרָעָתָ֑ם לְבִלְתִּ֥י קַטֵּ֖ר לֵאלֹהִ֥ים אֲחֵרִֽים׃


But they hearkened not, nor inclined their ear to turn from their wickedness, to burn no incense to other gods.


###### Jeremiah 44:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/44:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/44:6)


וַתִּתַּ֤ךְ חֲמָתִי֙ וְאַפִּ֔י וַתִּבְעַר֙ בְּעָרֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֔ה וּבְחֻצ֖וֹת יְרוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וַתִּהְיֶ֛ינָה לְחׇרְבָּ֥ה לִשְׁמָמָ֖ה כַּיּ֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃{ס}


So that my fury and my anger was poured forth, and was kindled in the cities of Yehuda and in the streets of Yerushalayim; and they have become a waste and a desolation, as at this day.


###### Jeremiah 44:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/44:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/44:7)


וְעַתָּ֡ה כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָה֩ אֱלֹהֵ֨י צְבָא֜וֹת אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל לָמָה֩ אַתֶּ֨ם עֹשִׂ֜ים רָעָ֤ה גְדוֹלָה֙ אֶל־נַפְשֹׁ֣תֵכֶ֔ם לְהַכְרִ֨ית לָכֶ֧ם אִישׁ־וְאִשָּׁ֛ה עוֹלֵ֥ל וְיוֹנֵ֖ק מִתּ֣וֹךְ יְהוּדָ֑ה לְבִלְתִּ֛י הוֹתִ֥יר לָכֶ֖ם שְׁאֵרִֽית׃


Therefore now thus says the Lord, the God of hosts, the God of Yisra᾽el; Why do you commit this great evil against your souls, to cut off from you man and woman, child and suckling, from the midst of Yehuda, leaving you no remnant;

לְהַכְעִסֵ֙נִי֙ בְּמַעֲשֵׂ֣י יְדֵיכֶ֔ם לְקַטֵּ֞ר לֵאלֹהִ֤ים אֲחֵרִים֙ בְּאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרַ֔יִם אֲשֶׁר־אַתֶּ֥ם בָּאִ֖ים לָג֣וּר שָׁ֑ם לְמַ֙עַן֙ הַכְרִ֣ית לָכֶ֔ם וּלְמַ֤עַן הֱיֽוֹתְכֶם֙ לִקְלָלָ֣ה וּלְחֶרְפָּ֔ה בְּכֹ֖ל גּוֹיֵ֥ הָאָֽרֶץ׃


in that you provoke me to anger with the works of your hands, burning incense to other gods in the land of Miżrayim into which you are gone to dwell, that you might cut yourselves off, and that you might be a curse and a reproach among all the nations of the earth?

Have you forgotten the wickedness of your fathers, and the wickedness of the kings of Yehuda, and the wickedness of their wives, and your own wickedness, and the wickedness of your wives which they have committed in the land of Yehuda, and in the streets of Yerushalayim?

וְלָקַחְתִּ֞י אֶת־שְׁאֵרִ֣ית יְהוּדָ֗ה אֲשֶׁר־שָׂ֨מוּ פְנֵיהֶ֜ם לָב֣וֹא אֶרֶץ־מִצְרַ֘יִם֮ לָג֣וּר שָׁם֒ וְתַ֨מּוּ כֹ֜ל בְּאֶ֧רֶץ מִצְרַ֣יִם יִפֹּ֗לוּ בַּחֶ֤רֶב בָּרָעָב֙ יִתַּ֔מּוּ מִקָּטֹן֙ וְעַד־גָּד֔וֹל בַּחֶ֥רֶב וּבָרָעָ֖ב יָמֻ֑תוּ וְהָיוּ֙ לְאָלָ֣ה לְשַׁמָּ֔ה וְלִקְלָלָ֖ה וּלְחֶרְפָּֽה׃


and I will take the remnant of Yehuda, that have set their faces to go into the land of Miżrayim to sojourn there, and they shall all be consumed, and fall in the land of Miżrayim; they shall be consumed, and by the sword and by the famine: they shall die, from the least even to the greatest, by the sword and by the famine: and they shall become an oath, an astonishment, and a curse, and a reproach.

Therefore hear the word of the Lord, all those of Yehuda who dwell in the land of Miżrayim; Behold, I have sworn by my great name, says the Lord, that my name shall no more be named in the mouth of any man of Yehuda in all the land of Miżrayim, saying, the Lord God lives.


###### Jeremiah 44:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/44:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/44:27)


הִנְנִ֨י שֹׁקֵ֧ד עֲלֵיהֶ֛ם לְרָעָ֖ה וְלֹ֣א לְטוֹבָ֑ה וְתַ֩מּוּ֩ כׇל־אִ֨ישׁ יְהוּדָ֜ה אֲשֶׁ֧ר בְּאֶֽרֶץ־מִצְרַ֛יִם בַּחֶ֥רֶב וּבָרָעָ֖ב עַד־כְּלוֹתָֽם׃


Behold, I will watch over them for evil, and not for good: and all the men of Yehuda that are in the land of Miżrayim shall be consumed by the sword and by the famine, until there be an end of them.


###### Jeremiah 44:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/44:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/44:28)


וּפְלִיטֵ֨י חֶ֜רֶב יְשֻׁב֨וּן מִן־אֶ֧רֶץ מִצְרַ֛יִם אֶ֥רֶץ יְהוּדָ֖ה מְתֵ֣י מִסְפָּ֑ר וְֽיָדְע֞וּ כׇּל־שְׁאֵרִ֣ית יְהוּדָ֗ה הַבָּאִ֤ים לְאֶֽרֶץ־מִצְרַ֙יִם֙ לָג֣וּר שָׁ֔ם דְּבַר־מִ֥י יָק֖וּם מִמֶּ֥נִּי וּמֵהֶֽם׃


Yet a small number that escape the sword shall return out of the land of Miżrayim into the land of Yehuda, and all the remnant of Yehuda, who are gone into the land of Miż­rayim to sojourn there, shall know whose words shall stand, mine, or theirs.


###### Jeremiah 44:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/44:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/44:29)


וְזֹאת־לָכֶ֤ם הָאוֹת֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה כִּי־פֹקֵ֥ד אֲנִ֛י עֲלֵיכֶ֖ם בַּמָּק֣וֹם הַזֶּ֑ה לְמַ֙עַן֙ תֵּֽדְע֔וּ כִּי֩ ק֨וֹם יָק֧וּמוּ דְבָרַ֛י עֲלֵיכֶ֖ם לְרָעָֽה׃{פ}


And this shall be a sign to you, says the Lord, that I will punish you in this place, that you may know that my words shall surely stand against you for evil:


###### Jeremiah 44:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/44:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/44:30)


כֹּ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה הִנְנִ֣י נֹ֠תֵ֠ן אֶת־פַּרְעֹ֨ה חׇפְרַ֤ע מֶֽלֶךְ־מִצְרַ֙יִם֙ בְּיַ֣ד אֹֽיְבָ֔יו וּבְיַ֖ד מְבַקְשֵׁ֣י נַפְשׁ֑וֹ כַּאֲשֶׁ֨ר נָתַ֜תִּי אֶת־צִדְקִיָּ֣הוּ מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֗ה בְּיַ֨ד נְבוּכַדְרֶאצַּ֧ר מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֛ל אֹיְב֖וֹ וּמְבַקֵּ֥שׁ נַפְשֽׁוֹ׃{ס}


thus says the Lord; Behold, I will give Par῾o Ḥofra king of Miżrayim into the hand of his enemies, and into the hand of them that seek his life; as I gave Żidqiyyahu king of Yehuda into the hand of Nevukhadreżżar king of Bavel, who was his enemy, and sought his life.



## Chapter 45

###### Jeremiah 45:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/45:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/45:1)


הַדָּבָ֗ר אֲשֶׁ֤ר דִּבֶּר֙ יִרְמְיָ֣הוּ הַנָּבִ֔יא אֶל־בָּר֖וּךְ בֶּן־נֵרִיָּ֑ה בְּכׇתְבוֹ֩ אֶת־הַדְּבָרִ֨ים הָאֵ֤לֶּה עַל־סֵ֙פֶר֙ מִפִּ֣י יִרְמְיָ֔הוּ בַּשָּׁנָה֙ הָרְבִעִ֔ית לִיהוֹיָקִ֧ים בֶּן־יֹאשִׁיָּ֛הוּ מֶ֥לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֖ה לֵאמֹֽר׃


The word that Yirmeyahu the prophet spoke to Barukh the son of Neriyya when he had written these words in a book at the mouth of Yirmeyahu in the fourth year of Yehoyaqim the son of Yoshiyyahu king of Yehuda, saying,


###### Jeremiah 45:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/45:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/45:2)


כֹּה־אָמַ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל עָלֶ֖יךָ בָּרֽוּךְ׃


Thus says the Lord, the God of Yisra᾽el to thee, O Barukh;


###### Jeremiah 45:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/45:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/45:3)


אָמַ֙רְתָּ֙ אֽוֹי־נָ֣א לִ֔י כִּֽי־יָסַ֧ף יְהֹוָ֛ה יָג֖וֹן עַל־מַכְאֹבִ֑י יָגַ֙עְתִּי֙ בְּאַנְחָתִ֔י וּמְנוּחָ֖ה לֹ֥א מָצָֽאתִי׃


Thou didst say, Woe is me now! for the Lord has added grief to my pain; I am weary in my sighing, and I find no rest.


###### Jeremiah 45:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/45:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/45:4)


כֹּ֣ה**׀** תֹּאמַ֣ר אֵלָ֗יו כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה הִנֵּ֤ה אֲשֶׁר־בָּנִ֙יתִי֙ אֲנִ֣י הֹרֵ֔ס וְאֵ֥ת אֲשֶׁר־נָטַ֖עְתִּי אֲנִ֣י נֹתֵ֑שׁ וְאֶת־כׇּל־הָאָ֖רֶץ הִֽיא׃


Thus shalt thou say to him, The Lord says thus; Behold, that which I have built I break down, and that which I have planted I pluck up, namely this whole land.


###### Jeremiah 45:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/45:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/45:5)


וְאַתָּ֛ה תְּבַקֶּשׁ־לְךָ֥ גְדֹל֖וֹת אַל־תְּבַקֵּ֑שׁ כִּ֡י הִנְנִי֩ מֵבִ֨יא רָעָ֤ה עַל־כׇּל־בָּשָׂר֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה וְנָתַתִּ֨י לְךָ֤ אֶֽת־נַפְשְׁךָ֙ לְשָׁלָ֔ל עַ֥ל כׇּל־הַמְּקֹמ֖וֹת אֲשֶׁ֥ר תֵּלֶךְ־שָֽׁם׃{פ}


And seekest thou great things for thyself? seek them not: for, behold, I will bring evil upon all flesh, says the Lord: but thy life I will give to thee for a prey in all the places to which thou goest.

against Miżrayim, against the army of Par῾o Nekho, king of Miżrayim, which was by the river Perat in Karkemish, whom Nevukhadreżżar king of Bavel smote in the fourth year of Yehoyaqim the son of Yoshiyyahu king of Yehuda.

אִסְר֣וּ הַסּוּסִ֗ים וַֽעֲלוּ֙ הַפָּ֣רָשִׁ֔ים וְהִֽתְיַצְּב֖וּ בְּכוֹבָעִ֑ים מִרְקוּ֙ הָרְמָחִ֔ים לִבְשׁ֖וּ הַסִּרְיֹנֹֽת׃

###### Jeremiah 46:5

###### Jeremiah 46:6

Who is this that rises like the River, and like streams of turbulent waters?

For this is the day of the Lord God of hosts, a day of vengeance, that he may avenge him of his adversaries: and the sword shall devour, and it shall be sated, and drink its fill of their blood: for the Lord God of hosts has a sacrifice in the north country by the river Perat.

###### Jeremiah 46:14

###### Jeremiah 46:15

###### Jeremiah 46:16

###### Jeremiah 46:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/46:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/46:18)


חַי־אָ֙נִי֙ נְאֻם־הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת שְׁמ֑וֹ כִּ֚י כְּתָב֣וֹר בֶּהָרִ֔ים וּכְכַרְמֶ֖ל בַּיָּ֥ם יָבֽוֹא׃


As I live, says the King, whose name is the Lord of hosts, Surely as Tavor is among the mountains, and as Karmel by the sea, so shall he come.


###### Jeremiah 46:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/46:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/46:19)


כְּלֵ֤י גוֹלָה֙ עֲשִׂ֣י לָ֔ךְ יוֹשֶׁ֖בֶת בַּת־מִצְרָ֑יִם כִּי־נֹף֙ לְשַׁמָּ֣ה תִֽהְיֶ֔ה וְנִצְּתָ֖ה מֵאֵ֥ין יוֹשֵֽׁב׃{ס}


O thou daughter dwelling in Miżrayim, furnish thyself with the baggage of Exile: for Nof shall be waste and desolate without inhabitant.


###### Jeremiah 46:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/46:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/46:20)


עֶגְלָ֥ה יְפֵה־פִיָּ֖ה מִצְרָ֑יִם קֶ֥רֶץ מִצָּפ֖וֹן בָּ֥א בָֽא׃


Miżrayim is like a very fair heifer, but destruction comes; it comes out of the north.


###### Jeremiah 46:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/46:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/46:21)


גַּם־שְׂכִרֶ֤יהָ בְקִרְבָּהּ֙ כְּעֶגְלֵ֣י מַרְבֵּ֔ק כִּֽי־גַם־הֵ֧מָּה הִפְנ֛וּ נָ֥סוּ יַחְדָּ֖יו לֹ֣א עָמָ֑דוּ כִּ֣י י֥וֹם אֵידָ֛ם בָּ֥א עֲלֵיהֶ֖ם עֵ֥ת פְּקֻדָּתָֽם׃


Also her hired men in the midst of her are like fatted bullocks; for they also are turned back, and are fled away together: they did not stand, because the day of their calamity was come upon them, and the time of their punishment.


###### Jeremiah 46:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/46:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/46:22)


קוֹלָ֖הּ כַּנָּחָ֣שׁ יֵלֵ֑ךְ כִּֽי־בְחַ֣יִל יֵלֵ֔כוּ וּבְקַרְדֻּמּוֹת֙ בָּ֣אוּ לָ֔הּ כְּחֹטְבֵ֖י עֵצִֽים׃


Her sound is like that of a snake on the move; for they shall march with force and come against her with axes, like hewers of wood.


###### Jeremiah 46:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/46:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/46:23)


כָּרְת֤וּ יַעְרָהּ֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה כִּ֖י לֹ֣א יֵחָקֵ֑ר כִּ֤י רַבּוּ֙ מֵֽאַרְבֶּ֔ה וְאֵ֥ין לָהֶ֖ם מִסְפָּֽר׃


They shall cut down her forest, says the Lord, though it cannot be searched; because they are more numerous than locusts, and are innumerable.


###### Jeremiah 46:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/46:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/46:24)


הֹבִ֖ישָׁה בַּת־מִצְרָ֑יִם נִתְּנָ֖ה בְּיַ֥ד עַם־צָפֽוֹן׃


The daughter of Miżrayim shall be confounded; she shall be delivered into the hand of the people of the north.


###### Jeremiah 46:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/46:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/46:25)


אָמַר֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה צְבָא֜וֹת אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל הִנְנִ֤י פוֹקֵד֙ אֶל־אָמ֣וֹן מִנֹּ֔א וְעַל־פַּרְעֹה֙ וְעַל־מִצְרַ֔יִם וְעַל־אֱלֹהֶ֖יהָ וְעַל־מְלָכֶ֑יהָ וְעַ֨ל־פַּרְעֹ֔ה וְעַ֥ל הַבֹּטְחִ֖ים בּֽוֹ׃


The Lord of hosts, the God of Yisra᾽el, says; Behold, I will punish Amon of No, and Par῾o and Miżrayim, with their gods, and their kings; even Par῾o, and all those who trust in him:


###### Jeremiah 46:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/46:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/46:26)


וּנְתַתִּ֗ים בְּיַד֙ מְבַקְשֵׁ֣י נַפְשָׁ֔ם וּבְיַ֛ד נְבוּכַדְרֶאצַּ֥ר מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֖ל וּבְיַד־עֲבָדָ֑יו וְאַחֲרֵי־כֵ֛ן תִּשְׁכֹּ֥ן כִּֽימֵי־קֶ֖דֶם נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


and I will deliver them into the hand of those who seek their lives, and into the hand of Nevukhadreżżar king of Bavel, and into the hand of his servants: and afterwards it shall be inhabited, as in the days of old, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 46:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/46:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/46:27)


וְ֠אַתָּ֠ה אַל־תִּירָ֞א עַבְדִּ֤י יַֽעֲקֹב֙ וְאַל־תֵּחַ֣ת יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל כִּ֠י הִנְנִ֤י מוֹשִֽׁעֲךָ֙ מֵרָח֔וֹק וְאֶֽת־זַרְעֲךָ֖ מֵאֶ֣רֶץ שִׁבְיָ֑ם וְשָׁ֧ב יַעֲק֛וֹב וְשָׁקַ֥ט וְשַׁאֲנַ֖ן וְאֵ֥ין מַחֲרִֽיד׃


But fear not thou, O my servant Ya῾aqov, and be not dismayed, O Yisra᾽el: for, behold, I will save thee from afar, and thy seed from the land of their captivity; and Ya῾aqov shall return, and be quiet and at ease, and none shall make him afraid.


###### Jeremiah 46:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/46:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/46:28)


אַ֠תָּ֠ה אַל־תִּירָ֞א עַבְדִּ֤י יַֽעֲקֹב֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה כִּ֥י אִתְּךָ֖ אָ֑נִי כִּי֩ אֶעֱשֶׂ֨ה כָלָ֜ה בְּכׇֽל־הַגּוֹיִ֣ם**׀** אֲשֶׁ֧ר הִדַּחְתִּ֣יךָ שָּׁ֗מָּה וְאֹֽתְךָ֙ לֹא־אֶעֱשֶׂ֣ה כָלָ֔ה וְיִסַּרְתִּ֙יךָ֙ לַמִּשְׁפָּ֔ט וְנַקֵּ֖ה לֹ֥א אֲנַקֶּֽךָּ׃{פ}


Fear thou not, O Ya῾aqov my servant, says the Lord: for I am with thee; for I will make a full end of all the nations where I have driven thee: but I will not make a full end of thee, but correct thee in due measure; yet will I not utterly destroy thee.



## Chapter 47

###### Jeremiah 47:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/47:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/47:1)

The word of the Lord that came to Yirmeyahu the prophet against the Pelishtim, before Par῾o smote ῾Azza.


###### Jeremiah 47:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/47:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/47:2)


כֹּ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה הִנֵּה־מַ֜יִם עֹלִ֤ים מִצָּפוֹן֙ וְהָיוּ֙ לְנַ֣חַל שׁוֹטֵ֔ף וְיִשְׁטְפוּ֙ אֶ֣רֶץ וּמְלוֹאָ֔הּ עִ֖יר וְיֹ֣שְׁבֵי בָ֑הּ וְזָעֲקוּ֙ הָאָדָ֔ם וְהֵילִ֕ל כֹּ֖ל יוֹשֵׁ֥ב הָאָֽרֶץ׃


Thus says the Lord; Behold, waters rise up out of the north, and shall be an overflowing flood, and shall overflow the land, and all its fullness; the city, and those who dwell in it; then the men shall cry, and all the inhabitants of the land shall howl.


###### Jeremiah 47:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/47:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/47:3)


מִקּ֗וֹל שַֽׁעֲטַת֙ פַּרְס֣וֹת אַבִּירָ֔יו מֵרַ֣עַשׁ לְרִכְבּ֔וֹ הֲמ֖וֹן גַּלְגִּלָּ֑יו לֹא־הִפְנ֤וּ אָבוֹת֙ אֶל־בָּנִ֔ים מֵרִפְי֖וֹן יָדָֽיִם׃


At the noise of the stamping of the hoofs of his stallions, at the rushing of his chariots, at the rumbling of his wheels, the fathers do not look back to their children for feebleness of hands;


###### Jeremiah 47:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/47:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/47:4)

because of the day that comes to spoil all the Pelishtim, and to cut off from Żor and Żidon every helper that remains: for the Lord will spoil the Pelishtim, the remnant of the country of Kaftor.


###### Jeremiah 47:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/47:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/47:5)

Baldness is come upon ῾Azza; Ashqelon is cut off; O remnant of their valley, how long wilt thou gash thyself?


###### Jeremiah 47:6

O thou sword of the Lord, how long will it be ere thou be quiet? put up thyself into thy scabbard, rest, and be still.


###### Jeremiah 47:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/47:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/47:7)


אֵ֣יךְ תִּשְׁקֹ֔טִי וַיהֹוָ֖ה צִוָּה־לָ֑הּ אֶֽל־אַשְׁקְל֛וֹן וְאֶל־ח֥וֹף הַיָּ֖ם שָׁ֥ם יְעָדָֽהּ׃{פ}

## Chapter 48

Mo᾽av is destroyed; her little ones have raised a cry.


###### Jeremiah 48:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/48:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/48:5)


כִּ֚י מַעֲלֵ֣ה (הלחות) [הַלֻּחִ֔ית] בִּבְכִ֖י יַעֲלֶה־בֶּ֑כִי כִּ֚י בְּמוֹרַ֣ד חוֹרֹנַ֔יִם צָרֵ֥י צַעֲקַת־שֶׁ֖בֶר שָׁמֵֽעוּ׃

###### Jeremiah 48:6

Flee, save your lives, and be like the juniper tree in the wilderness.


###### Jeremiah 48:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/48:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/48:7)


כִּ֠י יַ֣עַן בִּטְחֵ֤ךְ בְּמַעֲשַׂ֙יִךְ֙ וּבְא֣וֹצְרוֹתַ֔יִךְ גַּם־אַ֖תְּ תִּלָּכֵ֑דִי וְיָצָ֤א (כמיש) [כְמוֹשׁ֙] בַּגּוֹלָ֔ה כֹּהֲנָ֥יו וְשָׂרָ֖יו (יחד) [יַחְדָּֽיו]׃


For because thou hast trusted in thy works and in thy treasures, thou shalt also be taken: and Kemosh shall go out into exile, his priests and his princes together.


###### Jeremiah 48:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/48:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/48:8)


וְיָבֹ֨א שֹׁדֵ֜ד אֶל־כׇּל־עִ֗יר וְעִיר֙ לֹ֣א תִמָּלֵ֔ט וְאָבַ֥ד הָעֵ֖מֶק וְנִשְׁמַ֣ד הַמִּישֹׁ֑ר אֲשֶׁ֖ר אָמַ֥ר יְהֹוָֽה׃

###### Jeremiah 48:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/48:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/48:9)

###### Jeremiah 48:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/48:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/48:10)


אָר֗וּר עֹשֶׂ֛ה מְלֶ֥אכֶת יְהֹוָ֖ה רְמִיָּ֑ה וְאָר֕וּר מֹנֵ֥עַ חַרְבּ֖וֹ מִדָּֽם׃
