Terror, and pit, and snare, shall be upon thee, O inhabitant of Mo᾽av, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 48:44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/48:44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/48:44)


(הניס) [הַנָּ֞ס] מִפְּנֵ֤י הַפַּ֙חַד֙ יִפֹּ֣ל אֶל־הַפַּ֔חַת וְהָֽעֹלֶה֙ מִן־הַפַּ֔חַת יִלָּכֵ֖ד בַּפָּ֑ח כִּֽי־אָבִ֨יא אֵלֶ֧יהָ אֶל־מוֹאָ֛ב שְׁנַ֥ת פְּקֻדָּתָ֖ם נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


He that flees from the Terror shall fall into the pit; and he that gets up out of the pit shall be taken in the snare: for I will bring upon it, upon Mo᾽av, the year of their punishment, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 48:45

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/48:45](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/48:45)


בְּצֵ֥ל חֶשְׁבּ֛וֹן עָמְד֖וּ מִכֹּ֣חַ נָסִ֑ים כִּי־אֵ֞שׁ יָצָ֣א מֵחֶשְׁבּ֗וֹן וְלֶהָבָה֙ מִבֵּ֣ין סִיח֔וֹן וַתֹּ֙אכַל֙ פְּאַ֣ת מוֹאָ֔ב וְקׇדְקֹ֖ד בְּנֵ֥י שָׁאֽוֹן׃


In the shadow of Ḥeshbon the fugitives stood without strength: for a fire has gone out of Ḥeshbon and a flame from the midst of Siĥon, and has devoured the corner of Mo᾽av, and the crown of the head of the roaring people.


###### Jeremiah 48:46

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/48:46](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/48:46)


אוֹי־לְךָ֣ מוֹאָ֔ב אָבַ֖ד עַם־כְּמ֑וֹשׁ כִּֽי־לֻקְּח֤וּ בָנֶ֙יךָ֙ בַּשֶּׁ֔בִי וּבְנֹתֶ֖יךָ בַּשִּׁבְיָֽה׃


Woe to these, O Mo᾽av! The people of Kemosh is perishing: for thy sons are taken into captivity, and thy daughters into thraldom.


###### Jeremiah 48:47

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/48:47](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/48:47)


וְשַׁבְתִּ֧י שְׁבוּת־מוֹאָ֛ב בְּאַחֲרִ֥ית הַיָּמִ֖ים נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה עַד־הֵ֖נָּה מִשְׁפַּ֥ט מוֹאָֽב׃{ס}


Yet I will bring back the captivity of Mo᾽av in the latter days, says the Lord. Thus for the judgment of Mo᾽av.



## Chapter 49

###### Jeremiah 49:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:1)


לִבְנֵ֣י עַמּ֗וֹן כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה הֲבָנִ֥ים אֵין֙ לְיִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אִם־יוֹרֵ֖שׁ אֵ֣ין ל֑וֹ מַדּ֗וּעַ יָרַ֤שׁ מַלְכָּם֙ אֶת־גָּ֔ד וְעַמּ֖וֹ בְּעָרָ֥יו יָשָֽׁב׃


Concerning the children of ῾Ammon, thus says the Lord; Has Yisra᾽el no sons? has he no heir? why then does Malkam inherit Gad, and his people dwell in his cities?


###### Jeremiah 49:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:2)


לָכֵ֡ן הִנֵּה֩ יָמִ֨ים בָּאִ֜ים נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֗ה וְ֠הִשְׁמַעְתִּ֠י אֶל־רַבַּ֨ת בְּנֵי־עַמּ֜וֹן תְּרוּעַ֣ת מִלְחָמָ֗ה וְהָֽיְתָה֙ לְתֵ֣ל שְׁמָמָ֔ה וּבְנֹתֶ֖יהָ בָּאֵ֣שׁ תִּצַּ֑תְנָה וְיָרַ֧שׁ יִשְׂרָאֵ֛ל אֶת־יֹרְשָׁ֖יו אָמַ֥ר יְהֹוָֽה׃


Therefore, behold, days are coming, says the Lord, when I will cause an alarm of war to be heard in Rabba of the children of ῾Ammon; and it shall become a desolate heap, and her daughters shall be burned with fire: then shall Yisra᾽el dispossess those who dispossessed him, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 49:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:3)


הֵילִ֨ילִי חֶשְׁבּ֜וֹן כִּ֣י שֻׁדְּדָה־עַ֗י צְעַ֘קְנָה֮ בְּנ֣וֹת רַבָּה֒ חֲגֹ֣רְנָה שַׂקִּ֔ים סְפֹ֕דְנָה וְהִתְשׁוֹטַ֖טְנָה בַּגְּדֵר֑וֹת כִּ֤י מַלְכָּם֙ בַּגּוֹלָ֣ה יֵלֵ֔ךְ כֹּהֲנָ֥יו וְשָׂרָ֖יו יַחְדָּֽיו׃


Howl, O Ḥeshbon, for ῾Ay is plundered: cry, O daughters of Rabba, gird yourselves with sackcloth; lament, and run to and fro by the hedges; for Malkam shall go into exile, and his priests and his princes together.


###### Jeremiah 49:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:4)


מַה־תִּתְהַֽלְלִי֙ בָּעֲמָקִ֔ים זָ֣ב עִמְקֵ֔ךְ הַבַּ֖ת הַשּׁוֹבֵבָ֑ה הַבֹּֽטְחָה֙ בְּאֹ֣צְרֹתֶ֔יהָ מִ֖י יָב֥וֹא אֵלָֽי׃


Why dost thou glory in the valleys? thy valley flows with blood, O backsliding daughter, that trusts in her treasures, saying, Who shall come against me?


###### Jeremiah 49:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:5)


הִנְנִי֩ מֵבִ֨יא עָלַ֜יִךְ פַּ֗חַד נְאֻם־אֲדֹנָ֧י יֱהֹוִ֛ה צְבָא֖וֹת מִכׇּל־סְבִיבָ֑יִךְ וְנִדַּחְתֶּם֙ אִ֣ישׁ לְפָנָ֔יו וְאֵ֥ין מְקַבֵּ֖ץ לַנֹּדֵֽד׃


Behold, I will bring terror upon thee, says the Lord God of hosts, from all those who are round about thee; and you shall be driven out every man straight before him; and there shall be none to take in the wanderer.


###### Jeremiah 49:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:6)


וְאַחֲרֵי־כֵ֗ן אָשִׁ֛יב אֶת־שְׁב֥וּת בְּנֵֽי־עַמּ֖וֹן נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


And afterwards I will bring back the captivity of the children of ῾Ammon, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 49:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:7)


לֶאֱד֗וֹם כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֔וֹת הַאֵ֥ין ע֛וֹד חׇכְמָ֖ה בְּתֵימָ֑ן אָבְדָ֤ה עֵצָה֙ מִבָּנִ֔ים נִסְרְחָ֖ה חׇכְמָתָֽם׃


Concerning Edom, thus says the Lord of hosts; Is wisdom no more in Teman? has counsel perished from the prudent? has their wisdom vanished?


###### Jeremiah 49:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:8)


נֻ֤סוּ הׇפְנוּ֙ הֶעְמִ֣יקוּ לָשֶׁ֔בֶת יֹשְׁבֵ֖י דְּדָ֑ן כִּ֣י אֵ֥יד עֵשָׂ֛ו הֵבֵ֥אתִי עָלָ֖יו עֵ֥ת פְּקַדְתִּֽיו׃


Flee, turn back, dwell in the deeps, O inhabitants of Dedan; for I will bring the calamity of ῾Esav upon him, the time when I punish him.


###### Jeremiah 49:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:9)


אִם־בֹּֽצְרִים֙ בָּ֣אוּ לָ֔ךְ לֹ֥א יַשְׁאִ֖רוּ עוֹלֵל֑וֹת אִם־גַּנָּבִ֥ים בַּלַּ֖יְלָה הִשְׁחִ֥יתוּ דַיָּֽם׃


If grape gatherers come to thee, would they not leave some gleanings? if thieves by night, they will destroy only till they have enough.


###### Jeremiah 49:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:10)


כִּֽי־אֲנִ֞י חָשַׂ֣פְתִּי אֶת־עֵשָׂ֗ו גִּלֵּ֙יתִי֙ אֶת־מִסְתָּרָ֔יו וְנֶחְבָּ֖ה לֹ֣א יוּכָ֑ל שֻׁדַּ֥ד זַרְע֛וֹ וְאֶחָ֥יו וּשְׁכֵנָ֖יו וְאֵינֶֽנּוּ׃


But I have stripped ῾Esav bare, I have uncovered his secret places, and he shall not be able to hide himself: his seed is plundered, and his brethren, and his neighbours, and he himself is no more.


###### Jeremiah 49:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:11)


עׇזְבָ֥ה יְתֹמֶ֖יךָ אֲנִ֣י אֲחַיֶּ֑ה וְאַלְמְנוֹתֶ֖יךָ עָלַ֥י תִּבְטָֽחוּ׃{ס}


Leave thy fatherless children, I will preserve them alive; and let thy widows trust in me.


###### Jeremiah 49:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:12)


כִּי־כֹ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה הִ֠נֵּ֠ה אֲשֶׁר־אֵ֨ין מִשְׁפָּטָ֜ם לִשְׁתּ֤וֹת הַכּוֹס֙ שָׁת֣וֹ יִשְׁתּ֔וּ וְאַתָּ֣ה ה֔וּא נָקֹ֖ה תִּנָּקֶ֑ה לֹ֣א תִנָּקֶ֔ה כִּ֥י שָׁתֹ֖ה תִּשְׁתֶּֽה׃


For thus says the Lord; Behold, they to whom it did not appertain to drink of the cup shall surely drink; and art thou one who shall go scot free? thou shalt not go unpunished, but thou shalt surely drink.


###### Jeremiah 49:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:13)


כִּ֣י בִ֤י נִשְׁבַּ֙עְתִּי֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה כִּֽי־לְשַׁמָּ֧ה לְחֶרְפָּ֛ה לְחֹ֥רֶב וְלִקְלָלָ֖ה תִּהְיֶ֣ה בׇצְרָ֑ה וְכׇל־עָרֶ֥יהָ תִהְיֶ֖ינָה לְחׇרְב֥וֹת עוֹלָֽם׃


For I have sworn by myself, says the Lord, that Bożra shall become a desolation, a reproach, a waste, and a curse; and all its cities shall be perpetual ruins.


###### Jeremiah 49:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:14)


שְׁמוּעָ֤ה שָׁמַ֙עְתִּי֙ מֵאֵ֣ת יְהֹוָ֔ה וְצִ֖יר בַּגּוֹיִ֣ם שָׁל֑וּחַ הִֽתְקַבְּצוּ֙ וּבֹ֣אוּ עָלֶ֔יהָ וְק֖וּמוּ לַמִּלְחָמָֽה׃


I have heard a rumour from the Lord, and an ambassador is sent to the heathen, saying, Gather yourselves together, and come against her, and rise up to the battle.


###### Jeremiah 49:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:15)


כִּֽי־הִנֵּ֥ה קָטֹ֛ן נְתַתִּ֖יךָ בַּגּוֹיִ֑ם בָּז֖וּי בָּאָדָֽם׃


For, lo, I have made thee small among the nations, despised among men.


###### Jeremiah 49:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:16)


תִּֽפְלַצְתְּךָ֞ הִשִּׁ֤יא אֹתָךְ֙ זְד֣וֹן לִבֶּ֔ךָ שֹֽׁכְנִי֙ בְּחַגְוֵ֣י הַסֶּ֔לַע תֹּפְשִׂ֖י מְר֣וֹם גִּבְעָ֑ה כִּֽי־תַגְבִּ֤יהַּ כַּנֶּ֙שֶׁר֙ קִנֶּ֔ךָ מִשָּׁ֥ם אוֹרִֽידְךָ֖ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


Thy terribleness has deceived thee, and the pride of thy heart, O thou that dwellest in the clefts of the rock, that holdest the height of the hill: though thou shouldst make thy nest as high as the eagle, I will bring thee down from there, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 49:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:17)


וְהָיְתָ֥ה אֱד֖וֹם לְשַׁמָּ֑ה כֹּ֚ל עֹבֵ֣ר עָלֶ֔יהָ יִשֹּׁ֥ם וְיִשְׁרֹ֖ק עַל־כׇּל־מַכּוֹתֶֽהָ׃


Also Edom shall be a desolation: everyone that goes by it shall be horrified, and shall hiss at all her disasters.


###### Jeremiah 49:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:18)


כְּֽמַהְפֵּכַ֞ת סְדֹ֧ם וַעֲמֹרָ֛ה וּשְׁכֵנֶ֖יהָ אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֑ה לֹא־יֵשֵׁ֥ב שָׁם֙ אִ֔ישׁ וְלֹא־יָג֥וּר בָּ֖הּ בֶּן־אָדָֽם׃


As in the overthrow of Sedom and ῾Amora and its neighbour cities, says the Lord, no man shall abide there, neither shall a son of man dwell in it.


###### Jeremiah 49:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:19)


הִ֠נֵּ֠ה כְּאַרְיֵ֞ה יַעֲלֶ֨ה מִגְּא֣וֹן הַיַּרְדֵּן֮ אֶל־נְוֵ֣ה אֵיתָן֒ כִּֽי־אַרְגִּ֤יעָה אֲרִיצֶ֙נּוּ֙ מֵעָלֶ֔יהָ וּמִ֥י בָח֖וּר אֵלֶ֣יהָ אֶפְקֹ֑ד כִּ֣י מִ֤י כָמ֙וֹנִי֙ וּמִ֣י יֹעִידֶ֔נִּי וּמִי־זֶ֣ה רֹעֶ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר יַעֲמֹ֖ד לְפָנָֽי׃{ס}


Behold, he shall come up like a lion from the wild country of the Yarden against a strong sheepfold: for in a moment I will make him run over her: and whoever is chosen, I will appoint him over her; for who is like me? and who will set me a time? and who is that shepherd that will stand before me?


###### Jeremiah 49:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:20)


לָכֵ֞ן שִׁמְע֣וּ עֲצַת־יְהֹוָ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֤ר יָעַץ֙ אֶל־אֱד֔וֹם וּמַ֨חְשְׁבוֹתָ֔יו אֲשֶׁ֥ר חָשַׁ֖ב אֶל־יֹשְׁבֵ֣י תֵימָ֑ן אִם־ל֤וֹא יִסְחָבוּם֙ צְעִירֵ֣י הַצֹּ֔אן אִם־לֹ֥א יַשִּׁ֛ים עֲלֵיהֶ֖ם נְוֵהֶֽם׃


Therefore hear the counsel of the Lord, that he has taken against Edom; and his purposes, that he has purposed against the inhabitants of Teman: Surely the least of the flock shall drag them away: surely their fold shall be appalled at them.


###### Jeremiah 49:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:21)


מִקּ֣וֹל נִפְלָ֔ם רָעֲשָׁ֖ה הָאָ֑רֶץ צְעָקָ֕ה בְּיַם־ס֖וּף נִשְׁמַ֥ע קוֹלָֽהּ׃


The earth is moved at the noise of their fall; the sound of her cry is heard at the Sea of Suf.


###### Jeremiah 49:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:22)


הִנֵּ֤ה כַנֶּ֙שֶׁר֙ יַעֲלֶ֣ה וְיִדְאֶ֔ה וְיִפְרֹ֥שׂ כְּנָפָ֖יו עַל־בׇּצְרָ֑ה וְֽ֠הָיָ֠ה לֵ֞ב גִּבּוֹרֵ֤י אֱדוֹם֙ בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֔וּא כְּלֵ֖ב אִשָּׁ֥ה מְצֵרָֽה׃{פ}


Behold, he shall come up and fly like the eagle, and spread his wings over Bożra: and at that day shall the heart of the mighty men of Edom be as the heart of a woman in her pangs.


###### Jeremiah 49:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:23)


לְדַמֶּ֗שֶׂק בּ֤וֹשָֽׁה חֲמָת֙ וְאַרְפָּ֔ד כִּֽי־שְׁמֻעָ֥ה רָעָ֛ה שָׁמְע֖וּ נָמֹ֑גוּ בַּיָּ֣ם דְּאָגָ֔ה הַשְׁקֵ֖ט לֹ֥א יוּכָֽל׃


Concerning Dammeseq. Ḥamat is confounded, and Arpad: for they have heard evil tidings: they are fainthearted; there is anxiety on the sea; it cannot be quiet.


###### Jeremiah 49:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:24)


רָפְתָ֥ה דַמֶּ֛שֶׂק הִפְנְתָ֥ה לָנ֖וּס וְרֶ֣טֶט׀הֶחֱזִ֑יקָה צָרָ֧ה וַחֲבָלִ֛ים אֲחָזַ֖תָּה כַּיּוֹלֵדָֽה׃


Dammeseq is grown feeble, and turns herself to flee, and fear has gripped her: anguish and sorrows have seized on her, like a woman in travail.


###### Jeremiah 49:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:25)


אֵ֥יךְ לֹֽא־עֻזְּבָ֖ה עִ֣יר (תהלה) [תְּהִלָּ֑ת] קִרְיַ֖ת מְשׂוֹשִֽׂי׃


How was the city of praise left without succour, the city of my joy!


###### Jeremiah 49:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:26)


לָכֵ֛ן יִפְּל֥וּ בַחוּרֶ֖יהָ בִּרְחֹבֹתֶ֑יהָ וְכׇל־אַנְשֵׁ֨י הַמִּלְחָמָ֤ה יִדַּ֙מּוּ֙ בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֔וּא נְאֻ֖ם יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָאֽוֹת׃


Therefore her young men shall fall in her streets, and all the men of war shall be cut off in that day, says the Lord of hosts.


###### Jeremiah 49:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:27)


וְהִצַּ֥תִּי אֵ֖שׁ בְּחוֹמַ֣ת דַּמָּ֑שֶׂק וְאָכְלָ֖ה אַרְמְנ֥וֹת בֶּן־הֲדָֽד׃{פ}


And I will kindle a fire in the wall of Dammeseq, and it shall consume the palaces of Ben-hadad.


###### Jeremiah 49:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:28)


לְקֵדָ֣ר**׀** וּֽלְמַמְלְכ֣וֹת חָצ֗וֹר אֲשֶׁ֤ר הִכָּה֙ (נבוכדראצור) [נְבוּכַדְרֶאצַּ֣ר] מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֔ל כֹּ֖ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֑ה ק֚וּמוּ עֲל֣וּ אֶל־קֵדָ֔ר וְשׇׁדְד֖וּ אֶת־בְּנֵי־קֶֽדֶם׃


Concerning Qedar, and concerning the kingdoms of Ḥażor, which Nevukhadreżżar king of Bavel shall smite, thus says the Lord; Arise, go up to Qedar, and plunder the men of the east.


###### Jeremiah 49:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:29)


אׇהֳלֵיהֶ֤ם וְצֹאנָם֙ יִקָּ֔חוּ יְרִיעוֹתֵיהֶ֧ם וְכׇל־כְּלֵיהֶ֛ם וּגְמַלֵּיהֶ֖ם יִשְׂא֣וּ לָהֶ֑ם וְקָרְא֧וּ עֲלֵיהֶ֛ם מָג֖וֹר מִסָּבִֽיב׃


Their tents and their flocks shall be taken away: they shall take to themselves their curtains, and all their vessels, and their camels; and they shall cry to them, Terror on every side!


###### Jeremiah 49:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:30)


נֻ֩סוּ֩ נֻּ֨דוּ מְאֹ֜ד הֶעְמִ֧יקוּ לָשֶׁ֛בֶת יֹשְׁבֵ֥י חָצ֖וֹר נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה כִּֽי־יָעַ֨ץ עֲלֵיכֶ֜ם נְבוּכַדְרֶאצַּ֤ר מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶל֙ עֵצָ֔ה וְחָשַׁ֥ב (עליהם) [עֲלֵיכֶ֖ם] מַחֲשָׁבָֽה׃


Flee, get you far off, dwell in the deeps, O inhabitants of Ḥażor, says the Lord; for Nevukhadreżżar king of Bavel has taken counsel against you, and has conceived a purpose against you.


###### Jeremiah 49:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:31)


ק֣וּמֽוּ עֲל֗וּ אֶל־גּ֥וֹי שְׁלֵ֛יו יוֹשֵׁ֥ב לָבֶ֖טַח נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה לֹא־דְלָתַ֧יִם וְלֹֽא־בְרִ֛יחַ ל֖וֹ בָּדָ֥ד יִשְׁכֹּֽנוּ׃


Arise, get you up to a nation at ease, that dwells in security, says the Lord, who have neither gates nor bars, dwelling alone.


###### Jeremiah 49:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:32)


וְהָי֨וּ גְמַלֵּיהֶ֜ם לָבַ֗ז וַהֲמ֤וֹן מִקְנֵיהֶם֙ לְשָׁלָ֔ל וְזֵרִתִ֥ים לְכׇל־ר֖וּחַ קְצוּצֵ֣י פֵאָ֑ה וּמִכׇּל־עֲבָרָ֛יו אָבִ֥יא אֶת־אֵידָ֖ם נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


And their camels shall be a booty, and the multitude of their cattle a spoil: and I will scatter to every wind those who have the corners of their hair cut off; and I will bring their calamity from every side says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 49:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:33)


וְהָיְתָ֨ה חָצ֜וֹר לִמְע֥וֹן תַּנִּ֛ים שְׁמָמָ֖ה עַד־עוֹלָ֑ם לֹא־יֵשֵׁ֥ב שָׁם֙ אִ֔ישׁ וְלֹא־יָג֥וּר בָּ֖הּ בֶּן־אָדָֽם׃{ס}


And Ḥażor shall be a den of jackals, and a desolation for ever: there shall no man abide there, nor any son of man dwell in it.


###### Jeremiah 49:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:34)


אֲשֶׁ֨ר הָיָ֧ה דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֛ה אֶל־יִרְמְיָ֥הוּ הַנָּבִ֖יא אֶל־עֵילָ֑ם בְּרֵאשִׁ֗ית מַלְכ֛וּת צִדְקִיָּ֥ה מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֖ה לֵאמֹֽר׃


The word of the Lord that came to Yirmeyahu the prophet against ῾Elam in the beginning of the reign of Żidqiyyahu king of Yehuda, saying,


###### Jeremiah 49:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:35)


כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֔וֹת הִנְנִ֥י שֹׁבֵ֖ר אֶת־קֶ֣שֶׁת עֵילָ֑ם רֵאשִׁ֖ית גְּבוּרָתָֽם׃


Thus says the Lord of hosts; Behold, I will break the bow of ῾Elam, the chief of their might.


###### Jeremiah 49:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:36)


וְהֵבֵאתִ֨י אֶל־עֵילָ֜ם אַרְבַּ֣ע רוּח֗וֹת מֵֽאַרְבַּע֙ קְצ֣וֹת הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם וְזֵ֣רִתִ֔ים לְכֹ֖ל הָרֻח֣וֹת הָאֵ֑לֶּה וְלֹא־יִהְיֶ֣ה הַגּ֔וֹי אֲשֶׁ֛ר לֹא־יָב֥וֹא שָׁ֖ם נִדְּחֵ֥י (עולם) [עֵילָֽם]׃


And upon ῾Elam will I bring the four winds from the four quarters of heaven, and will scatter them to all those winds; and there shall be no nation to which the outcasts of ῾Elam shall not come.


###### Jeremiah 49:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:37)


וְהַחְתַּתִּ֣י אֶת־עֵ֠ילָ֠ם לִפְנֵ֨י אֹיְבֵיהֶ֜ם וְלִפְנֵ֣י**׀** מְבַקְשֵׁ֣י נַפְשָׁ֗ם וְהֵבֵאתִ֨י עֲלֵיהֶ֧ם׀רָעָ֛ה אֶת־חֲר֥וֹן אַפִּ֖י נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה וְשִׁלַּחְתִּ֤י אַחֲרֵיהֶם֙ אֶת־הַחֶ֔רֶב עַ֥ד כַּלּוֹתִ֖י אוֹתָֽם׃


For I will cause ῾Elam to lose heart before their enemies, and before them that seek their life: and I will bring evil upon them, my fierce anger, says the Lord; and I will send the sword after them, till I have consumed them:


###### Jeremiah 49:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:38)


וְשַׂמְתִּ֥י כִסְאִ֖י בְּעֵילָ֑ם וְהַאֲבַדְתִּ֥י מִשָּׁ֛ם מֶ֥לֶךְ וְשָׂרִ֖ים נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


and I will set my throne in ῾Elam, and will destroy from there the king and the princes, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 49:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/49:39)


וְהָיָ֣ה**׀** בְּאַחֲרִ֣ית הַיָּמִ֗ים (אשוב) [אָשִׁ֛יב] אֶת־[שְׁב֥וּת] (שבית) עֵילָ֖ם נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


But it shall come to pass in the latter days, that I will bring back the captivity of ῾Elam, says the Lord.



## Chapter 50

###### Jeremiah 50:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:1)


הַדָּבָ֗ר אֲשֶׁ֨ר דִּבֶּ֧ר יְהֹוָ֛ה אֶל־בָּבֶ֖ל אֶל־אֶ֣רֶץ כַּשְׂדִּ֑ים בְּיַ֖ד יִרְמְיָ֥הוּ הַנָּבִֽיא׃


The word that the Lord spoke against Bavel and against the land of the Kasdim by Yirmeyahu the prophet.


###### Jeremiah 50:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:2)


הַגִּ֨ידוּ בַגּוֹיִ֤ם וְהַשְׁמִ֙יעוּ֙ וּֽשְׂאוּ־נֵ֔ס הַשְׁמִ֖יעוּ אַל־תְּכַחֵ֑דוּ אִמְרוּ֩ נִלְכְּדָ֨ה בָבֶ֜ל הֹבִ֥ישׁ בֵּל֙ חַ֣ת מְרֹדָ֔ךְ הֹבִ֣ישׁוּ עֲצַבֶּ֔יהָ חַ֖תּוּ גִּלּוּלֶֽיהָ׃


Declare among the nations, and publish, and set up a banner; proclaim, and conceal not: say, Bavel is taken, Bel is confounded, Merodakh is broken in pieces; her idols are put to shame, her images are shattered.


###### Jeremiah 50:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:3)


כִּ֣י עָלָה֩ עָלֶ֨יהָ גּ֜וֹי מִצָּפ֗וֹן הֽוּא־יָשִׁ֤ית אֶת־אַרְצָהּ֙ לְשַׁמָּ֔ה וְלֹא־יִהְיֶ֥ה יוֹשֵׁ֖ב בָּ֑הּ מֵאָדָ֥ם וְעַד־בְּהֵמָ֖ה נָ֥דוּ הָלָֽכוּ׃


For out of the north comes up a nation against her, who shall make her land desolate, and none shall dwell in it: they shall remove, they shall depart, both man and beast.


###### Jeremiah 50:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:4)


בַּיָּמִ֨ים הָהֵ֜מָּה וּבָעֵ֤ת הַהִיא֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה יָבֹ֧אוּ בְנֵי־יִשְׂרָאֵ֛ל הֵ֥מָּה וּבְנֵי־יְהוּדָ֖ה יַחְדָּ֑ו הָל֤וֹךְ וּבָכוֹ֙ יֵלֵ֔כוּ וְאֶת־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיהֶ֖ם יְבַקֵּֽשׁוּ׃


In those days, and in that time, says the Lord, the children of Yisra᾽el shall come, they and the children of Yehuda together, going and weeping: they shall go, and seek the Lord their God.


###### Jeremiah 50:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:5)


צִיּ֣וֹן יִשְׁאָ֔לוּ דֶּ֖רֶךְ הֵ֣נָּה פְנֵיהֶ֑ם בֹּ֚אוּ וְנִלְו֣וּ אֶל־יְהֹוָ֔ה בְּרִ֥ית עוֹלָ֖ם לֹ֥א תִשָּׁכֵֽחַ׃


They shall ask the way to Żiyyon with their faces towards it, saying, Come, and let us join ourselves to the Lord in a perpetual covenant that shall not be forgotten.


###### Jeremiah 50:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:6)


צֹ֤אן אֹֽבְדוֹת֙ (היה) [הָי֣וּ] עַמִּ֔י רֹעֵיהֶ֣ם הִתְע֔וּם הָרִ֖ים (שובבים) [שׁוֹבְב֑וּם] מֵהַ֤ר אֶל־גִּבְעָה֙ הָלָ֔כוּ שָׁכְח֖וּ רִבְצָֽם׃


My people has been lost sheep: their shepherds have caused them to go astray, they have turned them away on the mountains: they have gone from mountain to hill, they have forgotten their resting place.


###### Jeremiah 50:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:7)


כׇּל־מוֹצְאֵיהֶ֣ם אֲכָל֔וּם וְצָרֵיהֶ֥ם אָמְר֖וּ לֹ֣א נֶאְשָׁ֑ם תַּ֗חַת אֲשֶׁ֨ר חָטְא֤וּ לַֽיהֹוָה֙ נְוֵה־צֶ֔דֶק וּמִקְוֵ֥ה אֲבוֹתֵיהֶ֖ם יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


All that found them have devoured them: and their adversaries have said, We are not guilty, because they have sinned against the Lord, the habitation of justice, even the Lord, the hope of their fathers.


###### Jeremiah 50:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:8)


נֻ֚דוּ מִתּ֣וֹךְ בָּבֶ֔ל וּמֵאֶ֥רֶץ כַּשְׂדִּ֖ים (יצאו) [צֵ֑אוּ] וִֽהְי֕וּ כְּעַתּוּדִ֖ים לִפְנֵי־צֹֽאן׃


Flee out of the midst of Bavel, and depart from the land of the Kasdim, and be as the he goats before the flocks.


###### Jeremiah 50:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:9)


כִּ֣י הִנֵּ֣ה אָנֹכִ֡י מֵעִיר֩ וּמַעֲלֶ֨ה עַל־בָּבֶ֜ל קְהַל־גּוֹיִ֤ם גְּדֹלִים֙ מֵאֶ֣רֶץ צָפ֔וֹן וְעָ֣רְכוּ לָ֔הּ מִשָּׁ֖ם תִּלָּכֵ֑ד חִצָּיו֙ כְּגִבּ֣וֹר מַשְׁכִּ֔יל לֹ֥א יָשׁ֖וּב רֵיקָֽם׃


For, lo, I will raise and cause to come up against Bavel an assembly of great nations from the north country: and they shall array themselves against her; from there she shall be taken: their arrows shall be like those of a death dealing warrior; none shall return in vain.


###### Jeremiah 50:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:10)


וְהָיְתָ֥ה כַשְׂדִּ֖ים לְשָׁלָ֑ל כׇּל־שֹׁלְלֶ֥יהָ יִשְׂבָּ֖עוּ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


And the land of the Kasdim shall be a spoil: all that plunder her shall be satisfied, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 50:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:11)


כִּ֤י (תשמחי) [תִשְׂמְחוּ֙] כִּ֣י (תעלזי) [תַעַלְז֔וּ] שֹׁסֵ֖י נַחֲלָתִ֑י כִּ֤י (תפושי) [תָפ֙וּשׁוּ֙] כְּעֶגְלָ֣ה דָשָׁ֔ה (ותצהלי) [וְתִצְהֲל֖וּ] כָּאַבִּרִֽים׃

your mother shall be greatly ashamed; she that bore you shall be disgraced: behold, shall be the last of the nations, a wilderness, a dry land, and a desert.

Array yourselves against Bavel round about: all you that bend the bow, shoot at her, spare no arrows: for she has sinned against the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 50:15

###### Jeremiah 50:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:16)

בַּיָּמִ֣ים הָהֵם֩ וּבָעֵ֨ת הַהִ֜יא נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֗ה יְבֻקַּ֞שׁ אֶת־עֲוֺ֤ן יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ וְאֵינֶ֔נּוּ וְאֶת־חַטֹּ֥את יְהוּדָ֖ה וְלֹ֣א תִמָּצֶ֑אינָה כִּ֥י אֶסְלַ֖ח לַאֲשֶׁ֥ר אַשְׁאִֽיר׃{פ}


In those days, and in that time, says the Lord, the iniquity of Yisra᾽el shall be sought for, and there shall be none; and the sins of Yehuda, but they shall not be found: for I will pardon them whom I will leave as a remnant.

How is the hammer of the whole earth cut asunder and broken! how is Bavel become a desolation among the nations!


###### Jeremiah 50:24

Come against her from the utmost border, open her stables: cast her up like heaps of grain, and destroy her utterly! let nothing of her be left!

###### Jeremiah 50:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:33)

###### Jeremiah 50:34

###### Jeremiah 50:36

###### Jeremiah 50:37

###### Jeremiah 50:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:39)


לָכֵ֗ן יֵשְׁב֤וּ צִיִּים֙ אֶת־אִיִּ֔ים וְיָ֥שְׁבוּ בָ֖הּ בְּנ֣וֹת יַעֲנָ֑ה וְלֹא־תֵשֵׁ֥ב עוֹד֙ לָנֶ֔צַח וְלֹ֥א תִשְׁכּ֖וֹן עַד־דּ֥וֹר וָדֹֽר׃


Therefore the wild beasts of the desert shall dwell there, with jackals and desert owls: and it shall be no more inhabited forever; neither shall it be dwelt in from generation to generation.


###### Jeremiah 50:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:40)


כְּמַהְפֵּכַ֨ת אֱלֹהִ֜ים אֶת־סְדֹ֧ם וְאֶת־עֲמֹרָ֛ה וְאֶת־שְׁכֵנֶ֖יהָ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה לֹא־יֵשֵׁ֥ב שָׁם֙ אִ֔ישׁ וְלֹא־יָג֥וּר בָּ֖הּ בֶּן־אָדָֽם׃


As God overthrew Sedom and ῾Amora and its neighbour cities, says the Lord; so shall no man abide there, neither shall any son of man dwell in it.


###### Jeremiah 50:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:41)


הִנֵּ֛ה עַ֥ם בָּ֖א מִצָּפ֑וֹן וְג֤וֹי גָּדוֹל֙ וּמְלָכִ֣ים רַבִּ֔ים יֵעֹ֖רוּ מִיַּרְכְּתֵי־אָֽרֶץ׃


Behold, a people shall come from the north, and a great nation, and many kings shall be raised up from the farthest parts of the earth.


###### Jeremiah 50:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:42)


קֶ֣שֶׁת וְכִידֹ֞ן יַחֲזִ֗יקוּ אַכְזָרִ֥י הֵ֙מָּה֙ וְלֹ֣א יְרַחֵ֔מוּ קוֹלָם֙ כַּיָּ֣ם יֶהֱמֶ֔ה וְעַל־סוּסִ֖ים יִרְכָּ֑בוּ עָר֗וּךְ כְּאִישׁ֙ לַמִּלְחָמָ֔ה עָלַ֖יִךְ בַּת־בָּבֶֽל׃


They shall hold the bow and javelin: they are cruel, and will not show mercy: their voice shall roar like the sea, and they shall ride upon horses, everyone lined up, like a man to the battle, against thee, O daughter of Bavel.


###### Jeremiah 50:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:43)


שָׁמַ֧ע מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֛ל אֶת־שִׁמְעָ֖ם וְרָפ֣וּ יָדָ֑יו צָרָה֙ הֶחֱזִיקַ֔תְהוּ חִ֖יל כַּיּוֹלֵדָֽה׃


The king of Bavel has heard the report of them, and his hands have grown feeble: anguish has taken hold of him, and pangs as of a woman in travail.


###### Jeremiah 50:44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:44)


הִ֠נֵּ֠ה כְּאַרְיֵ֞ה יַעֲלֶ֨ה מִגְּא֣וֹן הַיַּרְדֵּן֮ אֶל־נְוֵ֣ה אֵיתָן֒ כִּֽי־אַרְגִּ֤עָה (ארוצם) [אֲרִיצֵם֙] מֵעָלֶ֔יהָ וּמִ֥י בָח֖וּר אֵלֶ֣יהָ אֶפְקֹ֑ד כִּ֣י מִ֤י כָמ֙וֹנִי֙ וּמִ֣י יוֹעִדֶ֔נִּי וּמִי־זֶ֣ה רֹעֶ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר יַעֲמֹ֖ד לְפָנָֽי׃


Behold, he shall come up like a lion from the wild country of the Yarden against a strong sheepfold: for in a moment I will make them run over her: and whoever is chosen I will appoint him over her; for who is like me? and who will set me a time? and who is that shepherd that will stand before me?


###### Jeremiah 50:45

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:45](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:45)


לָכֵ֞ן שִׁמְע֣וּ עֲצַת־יְהֹוָ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֤ר יָעַץ֙ אֶל־בָּבֶ֔ל וּמַ֨חְשְׁבוֹתָ֔יו אֲשֶׁ֥ר חָשַׁ֖ב אֶל־אֶ֣רֶץ כַּשְׂדִּ֑ים אִם־לֹ֤א יִסְחָבוּם֙ צְעִירֵ֣י הַצֹּ֔אן אִם־לֹ֥א יַשִּׁ֛ים עֲלֵיהֶ֖ם נָוֶֽה׃


Therefore hear the counsel of the Lord, that he has taken against Bavel; and his purposes, that he has purposed against the land of Kasdim: Surely the least of the flock shall drag them away: surely the fold shall be appalled at them!


###### Jeremiah 50:46

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:46](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/50:46)


מִקּוֹל֙ נִתְפְּשָׂ֣ה בָבֶ֔ל נִרְעֲשָׁ֖ה הָאָ֑רֶץ וּזְעָקָ֖ה בַּגּוֹיִ֥ם נִשְׁמָֽע׃{ס}


At the noise of the taking of Bavel the earth is moved, and the cry is heard among the nations.



## Chapter 51

###### Jeremiah 51:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:1)


כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה הִנְנִי֙ מֵעִ֣יר עַל־בָּבֶ֔ל וְאֶל־יֹשְׁבֵ֖י לֵ֣ב קָמָ֑י ר֖וּחַ מַשְׁחִֽית׃


Thus says the Lord; Behold, I will raise up against Bavel, and against them that dwell in Lev-qamay, a destroying wind;


###### Jeremiah 51:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:2)


וְשִׁלַּחְתִּ֨י לְבָבֶ֤ל׀זָרִים֙ וְזֵר֔וּהָ וִיבֹקְק֖וּ אֶת־אַרְצָ֑הּ כִּי־הָי֥וּ עָלֶ֛יהָ מִסָּבִ֖יב בְּי֥וֹם רָעָֽה׃


and will send to Bavel winnowers, and they shall winnow her, and shall empty her land: for in the day of trouble they shall be against her round about.


###### Jeremiah 51:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:3)


אֶֽל־יִדְרֹ֤ךְ (ידרך) הַדֹּרֵךְ֙ קַשְׁתּ֔וֹ וְאֶֽל־יִתְעַ֖ל בְּסִרְיֹנ֑וֹ וְאַֽל־תַּחְמְלוּ֙ אֶל־בַּ֣חֻרֶ֔יהָ הַחֲרִ֖ימוּ כׇּל־צְבָאָֽהּ׃


Against him that bends let the archer bend his bow, and against him that lifts himself up in his coat of mail: and spare not her young men; destroy utterly all her host.


###### Jeremiah 51:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:4)


וְנָפְל֥וּ חֲלָלִ֖ים בְּאֶ֣רֶץ כַּשְׂדִּ֑ים וּמְדֻקָּרִ֖ים בְּחוּצוֹתֶֽיהָ׃


Thus the slain shall fall in the land of the Kasdim, and they that are thrust through in her streets.


###### Jeremiah 51:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:5)


כִּ֠י לֹא־אַלְמָ֨ן יִשְׂרָאֵ֤ל וִֽיהוּדָה֙ מֵאֱלֹהָ֔יו מֵיְהֹוָ֖ה צְבָא֑וֹת כִּ֤י אַרְצָם֙ מָלְאָ֣ה אָשָׁ֔ם מִקְּד֖וֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


For Yisra᾽el has not been widowed, nor Yehuda of his God, of the Lord of hosts; though their land was filled with sin against the Holy One of Yisra᾽el.


###### Jeremiah 51:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:6)


נֻ֣סוּ**׀** מִתּ֣וֹךְ בָּבֶ֗ל וּמַלְּטוּ֙ אִ֣ישׁ נַפְשׁ֔וֹ אַל־תִּדַּ֖מּוּ בַּעֲוֺנָ֑הּ כִּי֩ עֵ֨ת נְקָמָ֥ה הִיא֙ לַיהֹוָ֔ה גְּמ֕וּל ה֥וּא מְשַׁלֵּ֖ם לָֽהּ׃


Flee out of the midst of Bavel, and let every man save his life: be not cut off in her iniquity; for this is the time of the Lord’s vengeance; he will render to her a recompense.


###### Jeremiah 51:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:7)


כּוֹס־זָהָ֤ב בָּבֶל֙ בְּיַד־יְהֹוָ֔ה מְשַׁכֶּ֖רֶת כׇּל־הָאָ֑רֶץ מִיֵּינָהּ֙ שָׁת֣וּ גוֹיִ֔ם עַל־כֵּ֖ן יִתְהֹלְל֥וּ גוֹיִֽם׃


Bavel was a golden cup in the Lord’s hand, that made all the earth drunk: the nations have drunk of her wine; therefore the nations are mad.


###### Jeremiah 51:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:8)


פִּתְאֹ֛ם נָפְלָ֥ה בָבֶ֖ל וַתִּשָּׁבֵ֑ר הֵילִ֣ילוּ עָלֶ֗יהָ קְח֤וּ צֳרִי֙ לְמַכְאוֹבָ֔הּ אוּלַ֖י תֵּרָפֵֽא׃


Bavel is suddenly fallen and destroyed: wail for her; take balm for her pain, perhaps she may be healed.


###### Jeremiah 51:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:9)


רִפִּ֤אנוּ אֶת־בָּבֶל֙ וְלֹ֣א נִרְפָּ֔תָה עִזְב֕וּהָ וְנֵלֵ֖ךְ אִ֣ישׁ לְאַרְצ֑וֹ כִּֽי־נָגַ֤ע אֶל־הַשָּׁמַ֙יִם֙ מִשְׁפָּטָ֔הּ וְנִשָּׂ֖א עַד־שְׁחָקִֽים׃


We would have healed Bavel, but she was not healed: forsake her, and let us go every one to his own country: for her judgment reaches to heaven, and is lifted up to the skies.


###### Jeremiah 51:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:10)


הוֹצִ֥יא יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶת־צִדְקֹתֵ֑ינוּ בֹּ֚אוּ וּנְסַפְּרָ֣ה בְצִיּ֔וֹן אֶֽת־מַעֲשֵׂ֖ה יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ׃{ס}


The Lord has brought forth our vindication: come, and let us declare in Żiyyon the work of the Lord our God.


###### Jeremiah 51:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:11)


הָבֵ֣רוּ הַחִצִּים֮ מִלְא֣וּ הַשְּׁלָטִים֒ הֵעִ֣יר יְהֹוָ֗ה אֶת־ר֙וּחַ֙ מַלְכֵ֣י מָדַ֔י כִּֽי־עַל־בָּבֶ֥ל מְזִמָּת֖וֹ לְהַשְׁחִיתָ֑הּ כִּֽי־נִקְמַ֤ת יְהֹוָה֙ הִ֔יא נִקְמַ֖ת הֵיכָלֽוֹ׃


Select the arrows; fill up the quivers: the Lord has raised up the spirit of the kings of the Maday: for his purpose against Bavel is to destroy it; because it is the vengeance of the Lord, the vengeance for his temple.


###### Jeremiah 51:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:12)


אֶל־חוֹמֹ֨ת בָּבֶ֜ל שְׂאוּ־נֵ֗ס הַחֲזִ֙יקוּ֙ הַמִּשְׁמָ֔ר הָקִ֙ימוּ֙ שֹֽׁמְרִ֔ים הָכִ֖ינוּ הָאֹֽרְבִ֑ים כִּ֚י גַּם־זָמַ֣ם יְהֹוָ֔ה גַּם־עָשָׂ֕ה אֵ֥ת אֲשֶׁר־דִּבֶּ֖ר אֶל־יֹשְׁבֵ֥י בָבֶֽל׃


Set up a banner upon the walls of Bavel, make the watch strong, set up the watchmen, prepare the ambushes: for the Lord has both devised and done that which he spoke against the inhabitants of Bavel.


###### Jeremiah 51:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:13)


(שכנתי) [שֹׁכַנְתְּ֙] עַל־מַ֣יִם רַבִּ֔ים רַבַּ֖ת אוֹצָרֹ֑ת בָּ֥א קִצֵּ֖ךְ אַמַּ֥ת בִּצְעֵֽךְ׃


O thou that dwellest upon many waters, abundant in treasures, thy end is come, and the measure of thy greed.


###### Jeremiah 51:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:14)


נִשְׁבַּ֛ע יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת בְּנַפְשׁ֑וֹ כִּ֣י אִם־מִלֵּאתִ֤יךְ אָדָם֙ כַּיֶּ֔לֶק וְעָנ֥וּ עָלַ֖יִךְ הֵידָֽד׃{ס}


The Lord of hosts has sworn by himself, saying, Surely I will fill thee with men as with locusts; and they shall raise shout against thee.


###### Jeremiah 51:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:15)


עֹשֵׂ֥ה אֶ֙רֶץ֙ בְּכֹח֔וֹ מֵכִ֥ין תֵּבֵ֖ל בְּחׇכְמָת֑וֹ וּבִתְבוּנָת֖וֹ נָטָ֥ה שָׁמָֽיִם׃


He has made the earth by his power, he has established the world by his wisdom, and hath stretched out the heaven by his understanding.


###### Jeremiah 51:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:16)


לְק֨וֹל תִּתּ֜וֹ הֲמ֥וֹן מַ֙יִם֙ בַּשָּׁמַ֔יִם וַיַּ֥עַל נְשִׂאִ֖ים מִקְצֵה־אָ֑רֶץ בְּרָקִ֤ים לַמָּטָר֙ עָשָׂ֔ה וַיּ֥וֹצֵא ר֖וּחַ מֵאֹצְרֹתָֽיו׃


When his voice resounds with great mass of water in the heavens, and he raises vapours from the ends of the Earth; when he makes lightning flashes among the rain, and brings forth the wind out of his storehouses:


###### Jeremiah 51:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:17)


נִבְעַ֤ר כׇּל־אָדָם֙ מִדַּ֔עַת הֹבִ֥ישׁ כׇּל־צֹרֵ֖ף מִפָּ֑סֶל כִּ֛י שֶׁ֥קֶר נִסְכּ֖וֹ וְלֹא־ר֥וּחַ בָּֽם׃


every man becomes stupid, without knowledge: every founder is put to shame by his carved idol: for his molten image is false, and there is no breath in them.


###### Jeremiah 51:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:18)


הֶ֣בֶל הֵ֔מָּה מַעֲשֵׂ֖ה תַּעְתֻּעִ֑ים בְּעֵ֥ת פְּקֻדָּתָ֖ם יֹאבֵֽדוּ׃


They are vanity, the work of delusion: in the time of their punishment they shall perish.


###### Jeremiah 51:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:19)


לֹֽא־כְאֵ֜לֶּה חֵ֣לֶק יַעֲק֗וֹב כִּֽי־יוֹצֵ֤ר הַכֹּל֙ ה֔וּא וְשֵׁ֖בֶט נַחֲלָת֑וֹ יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת שְׁמֽוֹ׃{פ}


The portion of Ya῾aqov is not like them; for he is the fashioner of all things, and (Yisra᾽el) is the tribe of his inheritance: the Lord of hosts is his name.


###### Jeremiah 51:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:20)


מַפֵּץ־אַתָּ֣ה לִ֔י כְּלֵ֖י מִלְחָמָ֑ה וְנִפַּצְתִּ֤י בְךָ֙ גּוֹיִ֔ם וְהִשְׁחַתִּ֥י בְךָ֖ מַמְלָכֽוֹת׃


Thou art my chief and weapons of war: for with thee will I break in pieces the nations, and with thee will destroy kingdoms;


###### Jeremiah 51:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:21)


וְנִפַּצְתִּ֣י בְךָ֔ ס֖וּס וְרֹכְב֑וֹ וְנִפַּצְתִּ֣י בְךָ֔ רֶ֖כֶב וְרֹכְבֽוֹ׃


and with thee will I break in pieces the horse and his rider; and with thee will I break in pieces the chariot and its rider;


###### Jeremiah 51:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:22)


וְנִפַּצְתִּ֤י בְךָ֙ אִ֣ישׁ וְאִשָּׁ֔ה וְנִפַּצְתִּ֥י בְךָ֖ זָקֵ֣ן וָנָ֑עַר וְנִפַּצְתִּ֣י בְךָ֔ בָּח֖וּר וּבְתוּלָֽה׃


with thee also will I break in pieces man and woman; and with thee will I shatter old and young; and with thee will I shatter the young man and the maid;


###### Jeremiah 51:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:23)


וְנִפַּצְתִּ֤י בְךָ֙ רֹעֶ֣ה וְעֶדְר֔וֹ וְנִפַּצְתִּ֥י בְךָ֖ אִכָּ֣ר וְצִמְדּ֑וֹ וְנִפַּצְתִּ֣י בְךָ֔ פַּח֖וֹת וּסְגָנִֽים׃


I will also break in pieces with thee the shepherd and his flock; and with thee will I break in pieces the farmer and his yoke of oxen; and with thee will I break in pieces governors and deputies.

וְשִׁלַּמְתִּ֨י לְבָבֶ֜ל וּלְכֹ֣ל**׀** יוֹשְׁבֵ֣י כַשְׂדִּ֗ים אֵ֧ת כׇּל־רָעָתָ֛ם אֲשֶׁר־עָשׂ֥וּ בְצִיּ֖וֹן לְעֵינֵיכֶ֑ם נְאֻ֖ם יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


And I will pay back to Bavel and to all the inhabitants of Kasdim all their evil that they have done in Żiyyon in your sight, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 51:25

הִנְנִ֨י אֵלֶ֜יךָ הַ֤ר הַמַּשְׁחִית֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה הַמַּשְׁחִ֖ית אֶת־כׇּל־הָאָ֑רֶץ וְנָטִ֨יתִי אֶת־יָדִ֜י עָלֶ֗יךָ וְגִלְגַּלְתִּ֙יךָ֙ מִן־הַסְּלָעִ֔ים וּנְתַתִּ֖יךָ לְהַ֥ר שְׂרֵפָֽה׃


Behold, I am against thee, O destroying mountain, says the Lord, which destroyest all the earth: and I will stretch out my hand upon thee, and roll thee down from the rocks, and will make thee a burnt mountain.

שְׂאוּ־נֵ֣ס בָּאָ֗רֶץ תִּקְע֨וּ שׁוֹפָ֤ר בַּגּוֹיִם֙ קַדְּשׁ֤וּ עָלֶ֙יהָ֙ גּוֹיִ֔ם הַשְׁמִ֧יעוּ עָלֶ֛יהָ מַמְלְכ֥וֹת אֲרָרַ֖ט מִנִּ֣י וְאַשְׁכְּנָ֑ז פִּקְד֤וּ עָלֶ֙יהָ֙ טִפְסָ֔ר הַעֲלוּ־ס֖וּס כְּיֶ֥לֶק סָמָֽר׃


Set up a banner in the land, blow a shofar among the nations, prepare the nations against her, call together against her the kingdoms of Ararat, Minni, and Ashkenaz; appoint a captain against her; cause the horses to come up like bristling locusts.

###### Jeremiah 51:51

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:51](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:51)


בֹּ֚שְׁנוּ כִּֽי־שָׁמַ֣עְנוּ חֶרְפָּ֔ה כִּסְּתָ֥ה כְלִמָּ֖ה פָּנֵ֑ינוּ כִּ֚י בָּ֣אוּ זָרִ֔ים עַֽל־מִקְדְּשֵׁ֖י בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


We are ashamed, because we have heard reproach: shame has covered our faces: for strangers are come into the sanctuaries of the Lord’s house.


###### Jeremiah 51:52

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:52](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:52)


לָכֵ֞ן הִנֵּֽה־יָמִ֤ים בָּאִים֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה וּפָקַדְתִּ֖י עַל־פְּסִילֶ֑יהָ וּבְכׇל־אַרְצָ֖הּ יֶאֱנֹ֥ק חָלָֽל׃


Therefore, behold, days are coming, says the Lord, when I will do judgment upon her carved idols: and through all her land the wounded shall groan.


###### Jeremiah 51:53

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:53](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:53)


כִּֽי־תַעֲלֶ֤ה בָבֶל֙ הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם וְכִ֥י תְבַצֵּ֖ר מְר֣וֹם עֻזָּ֑הּ מֵאִתִּ֗י יָבֹ֧אוּ שֹׁדְדִ֛ים לָ֖הּ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


Though Bavel should mount up to heaven, and though she should fortify the height of her strength, yet from Me shall spoilers come to her, says the Lord.


###### Jeremiah 51:54

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:54](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:54)


ק֥וֹל זְעָקָ֖ה מִבָּבֶ֑ל וְשֶׁ֥בֶר גָּד֖וֹל מֵאֶ֥רֶץ כַּשְׂדִּֽים׃


A sound of a cry comes from Bavel, and great destruction from the land of the Kasdim:


###### Jeremiah 51:55

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:55](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:55)


כִּֽי־שֹׁדֵ֤ד יְהֹוָה֙ אֶת־בָּבֶ֔ל וְאִבַּ֥ד מִמֶּ֖נָּה ק֣וֹל גָּד֑וֹל וְהָמ֤וּ גַלֵּיהֶם֙ כְּמַ֣יִם רַבִּ֔ים נִתַּ֥ן שְׁא֖וֹן קוֹלָֽם׃


because the Lord has spoiled Bavel, and has quelled from her midst the great clamour; when her waves roar like great waters, noise of their voice is sounded:


###### Jeremiah 51:56

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:56](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:56)


כִּי֩ בָ֨א עָלֶ֤יהָ עַל־בָּבֶל֙ שׁוֹדֵ֔ד וְנִלְכְּדוּ֙ גִּבּוֹרֶ֔יהָ חִתְּתָ֖ה קַשְּׁתוֹתָ֑ם כִּ֣י אֵ֧ל גְּמֻל֛וֹת יְהֹוָ֖ה שַׁלֵּ֥ם יְשַׁלֵּֽם׃


because the spoiler is come upon her, upon Bavel, and her mighty men are taken, every one of their bows is broken: for the Lord God of recompenses shall surely pay back.


###### Jeremiah 51:57

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:57](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:57)


וְ֠הִשְׁכַּרְתִּ֠י שָׂרֶ֨יהָ וַחֲכָמֶ֜יהָ פַּחוֹתֶ֤יהָ וּסְגָנֶ֙יהָ֙ וְגִבּוֹרֶ֔יהָ וְיָשְׁנ֥וּ שְׁנַת־עוֹלָ֖ם וְלֹ֣א יָקִ֑יצוּ נְאֻ֨ם־הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת שְׁמֽוֹ׃{ס}


And I will make drunk her princes, and her wise men, her governors, and her deputies, and her mighty men: and they shall sleep a perpetual sleep, and not awake, says the King, whose name is the Lord of hosts.


###### Jeremiah 51:58

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:58](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:58)


כֹּֽה־אָמַ֞ר יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֗וֹת חֹ֠מ֠וֹת בָּבֶ֤ל הָרְחָבָה֙ עַרְעֵ֣ר תִּתְעַרְעָ֔ר וּשְׁעָרֶ֥יהָ הַגְּבֹהִ֖ים בָּאֵ֣שׁ יִצַּ֑תּוּ וְיִֽגְע֨וּ עַמִּ֧ים בְּדֵי־רִ֛יק וּלְאֻמִּ֥ים בְּדֵי־אֵ֖שׁ וְיָעֵֽפוּ׃{ס}


Thus says the Lord of hosts; The broad walls of Bavel shall be utterly broken, and her high gates shall be burned with fire; and the people shall labour in vain, and the nations for the fire; and they shall be weary.


###### Jeremiah 51:59

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:59](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:59)


הַדָּבָ֞ר אֲשֶׁר־צִוָּ֣ה**׀** יִרְמְיָ֣הוּ הַנָּבִ֗יא אֶת־שְׂרָיָ֣ה בֶן־נֵרִיָּה֮ בֶּן־מַחְסֵיָה֒ בְּלֶכְתּ֞וֹ אֶת־צִדְקִיָּ֤הוּ מֶֽלֶךְ־יְהוּדָה֙ בָּבֶ֔ל בִּשְׁנַ֥ת הָרְבִעִ֖ית לְמׇלְכ֑וֹ וּשְׂרָיָ֖ה שַׂ֥ר מְנוּחָֽה׃


The word which Yirmeyahu the prophet commanded Seraya the son of Neriyya, son of Maĥseya, when he went with Żidqiyyahu, the king of Yehuda into Bavel in the fourth year of his reign. And this Seraya was marshal of the camp.


###### Jeremiah 51:60

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:60](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:60)


וַיִּכְתֹּ֣ב יִרְמְיָ֗הוּ אֵ֧ת כׇּל־הָרָעָ֛ה אֲשֶׁר־תָּב֥וֹא אֶל־בָּבֶ֖ל אֶל־סֵ֣פֶר אֶחָ֑ד אֵ֚ת כׇּל־הַדְּבָרִ֣ים הָאֵ֔לֶּה הַכְּתֻבִ֖ים אֶל־בָּבֶֽל׃


So Yirmeyahu wrote in a book all the evil that should come upon Bavel, namely all these words that are written against Bavel.


###### Jeremiah 51:61

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:61](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:61)


וַיֹּ֥אמֶר יִרְמְיָ֖הוּ אֶל־שְׂרָיָ֑ה כְּבֹאֲךָ֣ בָבֶ֔ל וְֽרָאִ֔יתָ וְֽקָרָ֔אתָ אֵ֥ת כׇּל־הַדְּבָרִ֖ים הָאֵֽלֶּה׃


And Yirmeyahu said to Seraya, When thou comest to Bavel, and shalt see, and shalt read all these words;


###### Jeremiah 51:62

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:62](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:62)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֗ יְהֹוָה֙ אַתָּ֨ה דִבַּ֜רְתָּ אֶל־הַמָּק֤וֹם הַזֶּה֙ לְהַכְרִית֔וֹ לְבִלְתִּ֤י הֱיֽוֹת־בּוֹ֙ יוֹשֵׁ֔ב לְמֵאָדָ֖ם וְעַד־בְּהֵמָ֑ה כִּֽי־שִׁמְמ֥וֹת עוֹלָ֖ם תִּהְיֶֽה׃


then shalt thou say, O Lord, Thou hast spoken against this place, to cut it off, that none shall remain in it, neither man nor beast, but that it shall be desolate forever.


###### Jeremiah 51:63

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:63](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:63)


וְהָיָה֙ כְּכַלֹּ֣תְךָ֔ לִקְרֹ֖א אֶת־הַסֵּ֣פֶר הַזֶּ֑ה תִּקְשֹׁ֤ר עָלָיו֙ אֶ֔בֶן וְהִשְׁלַכְתּ֖וֹ אֶל־תּ֥וֹךְ פְּרָֽת׃


And it shall be, when thou hast made an end of reading this book, that thou shalt bind a stone to it, and cast it into the midst of Perat:


###### Jeremiah 51:64

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:64](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/51:64)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֗ כָּ֠כָה תִּשְׁקַ֨ע בָּבֶ֤ל וְלֹֽא־תָקוּם֙ מִפְּנֵ֣י הָרָעָ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֧ר אָנֹכִ֛י מֵבִ֥יא עָלֶ֖יהָ וְיָעֵ֑פוּ עַד־הֵ֖נָּה דִּבְרֵ֥י יִרְמְיָֽהוּ׃{פ}


and thou shalt say, Thus shall Bavel sink, and shall not rise, because of the evil that I will bring upon her: and they shall be weary. Thus far the words of Yirmeyahu.



## Chapter 52

###### Jeremiah 52:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/52:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/52:1)


בֶּן־עֶשְׂרִ֨ים וְאַחַ֤ת שָׁנָה֙ צִדְקִיָּ֣הוּ בְמׇלְכ֔וֹ וְאַחַ֤ת עֶשְׂרֵה֙ שָׁנָ֔ה מָלַ֖ךְ בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם וְשֵׁ֣ם אִמּ֔וֹ (חמיטל) [חֲמוּטַ֥ל] בַּֽת־יִרְמְיָ֖הוּ מִלִּבְנָֽה׃


Żidqiyyahu was twenty one years old when he began to reign, and he reigned eleven years in Yerushalayim. And his mother’s name was Ḥamutal the daughter of Yirmeyahu of Livna.


###### Jeremiah 52:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/52:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/52:2)


וַיַּ֥עַשׂ הָרַ֖ע בְּעֵינֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה כְּכֹ֥ל אֲשֶׁר־עָשָׂ֖ה יְהוֹיָקִֽם׃


And he did that which was evil in the eyes of the Lord, according to all that Yehoyaqim had done.


###### Jeremiah 52:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/52:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/52:3)


כִּ֣י**׀** עַל־אַ֣ף יְהֹוָ֗ה הָֽיְתָה֙ בִּירוּשָׁלַ֣͏ִם וִֽיהוּדָ֔ה עַד־הִשְׁלִיכ֥וֹ אוֹתָ֖ם מֵעַ֣ל פָּנָ֑יו וַיִּמְרֹ֥ד צִדְקִיָּ֖הוּ בְּמֶ֥לֶךְ בָּבֶֽל׃


For through the anger of the Lord it came to pass in Yerushalayim and Yehuda, till he cast them out from his presence. Then Żidqiyyahu rebelled against the king of Bavel.


###### Jeremiah 52:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/52:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/52:4)


וַיְהִי֩ בַשָּׁנָ֨ה הַתְּשִׁעִ֜ית לְמׇלְכ֗וֹ בַּחֹ֣דֶשׁ הָעֲשִׂירִי֮ בֶּעָשׂ֣וֹר לַחֹ֒דֶשׁ֒ בָּ֠א נְבוּכַדְרֶאצַּ֨ר מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֜ל ה֤וּא וְכׇל־חֵילוֹ֙ עַל־יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם וַֽיַּחֲנ֖וּ עָלֶ֑יהָ וַיִּבְנ֥וּ עָלֶ֛יהָ דָּיֵ֖ק סָבִֽיב׃


And it came to pass in the ninth year of his reign, in the tenth month, on the tenth day of the month, that Nevukhadreżżar king of Bavel came, he and all his army, against Yerushalayim, and encamped against it, and built a siege work against it round about.


###### Jeremiah 52:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/52:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/52:5)


וַתָּבֹ֥א הָעִ֖יר בַּמָּצ֑וֹר עַ֚ד עַשְׁתֵּ֣י עֶשְׂרֵ֣ה שָׁנָ֔ה לַמֶּ֖לֶךְ צִדְקִיָּֽהוּ׃


So the city was besieged until the eleventh year of king Żidqiyyahu.


###### Jeremiah 52:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/52:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/52:6)


בַּחֹ֤דֶשׁ הָרְבִיעִי֙ בְּתִשְׁעָ֣ה לַחֹ֔דֶשׁ וַיֶּחֱזַ֥ק הָרָעָ֖ב בָּעִ֑יר וְלֹא־הָיָ֥ה לֶ֖חֶם לְעַ֥ם הָאָֽרֶץ׃


And in the fourth month, on the ninth day of the month, the famine was severe in the city, so that there was no bread for the people of the land.


###### Jeremiah 52:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/52:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/52:7)


וַתִּבָּקַ֣ע הָעִ֗יר וְכׇל־אַנְשֵׁ֣י הַמִּלְחָמָ֡ה יִבְרְחוּ֩ וַיֵּצְא֨וּ מֵהָעִ֜יר לַ֗יְלָה דֶּ֜רֶךְ שַׁ֤עַר בֵּין־הַחֹמֹתַ֙יִם֙ אֲשֶׁר֙ עַל־גַּ֣ן הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ וְכַשְׂדִּ֥ים עַל־הָעִ֖יר סָבִ֑יב וַיֵּלְכ֖וּ דֶּ֥רֶךְ הָעֲרָבָֽה׃


Then the city was breached, and all the men of war fled, and went out of the city by night by the way of the gate between the two walls, which was by the king’s garden; (now the Kasdim were by the city round about:) and they went by the way of the plain.


###### Jeremiah 52:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/52:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/52:8)


וַיִּרְדְּפ֤וּ חֵיל־כַּשְׂדִּים֙ אַחֲרֵ֣י הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ וַיַּשִּׂ֥יגוּ אֶת־צִדְקִיָּ֖הוּ בְּעַֽרְבֹ֣ת יְרֵח֑וֹ וְכׇ֨ל־חֵיל֔וֹ נָפֹ֖צוּ מֵעָלָֽיו׃


But the army of the Kasdim pursued after the king, and overtook Żidqiyyahu in the plains of Yereĥo; and all his army was scattered from him.


###### Jeremiah 52:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/52:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/52:9)


וַֽיִּתְפְּשׂוּ֙ אֶת־הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ וַיַּעֲל֨וּ אֹת֜וֹ אֶל־מֶ֧לֶךְ בָּבֶ֛ל רִבְלָ֖תָה בְּאֶ֣רֶץ חֲמָ֑ת וַיְדַבֵּ֥ר אִתּ֖וֹ מִשְׁפָּטִֽים׃


Then they took the king, and carried him up to the king of Bavel to Rivla in the land of Ḥamat; where he gave judgment upon him.


###### Jeremiah 52:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/52:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/52:10)


וַיִּשְׁחַ֧ט מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֛ל אֶת־בְּנֵ֥י צִדְקִיָּ֖הוּ לְעֵינָ֑יו וְגַ֛ם אֶת־כׇּל־שָׂרֵ֥י יְהוּדָ֖ה שָׁחַ֥ט בְּרִבְלָֽתָה׃


And the king of Bavel slew the sons of Żidqiyyahu before his eyes: he slew also all the princes of Yehuda in Rivla.


###### Jeremiah 52:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/52:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/52:11)


וְאֶת־עֵינֵ֥י צִדְקִיָּ֖הוּ עִוֵּ֑ר וַיַּאַסְרֵ֣הוּ בַֽנְחֻשְׁתַּ֗יִם וַיְבִאֵ֤הוּ מֶֽלֶךְ־בָּבֶל֙ בָּבֶ֔לָה וַיִּתְּנֵ֥הוּ (בבית) [בֵֽית־]הַפְּקֻדֹּ֖ת עַד־י֥וֹם מוֹתֽוֹ׃


Then he put out the eyes of Żidqiyyahu; and the king of Bavel bound him in fetters, and carried him to Bavel, and put him in prison till the day of his death.


###### Jeremiah 52:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/52:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Jeremiah/r/52:12)


וּבַחֹ֤דֶשׁ הַֽחֲמִישִׁי֙ בֶּעָשׂ֣וֹר לַחֹ֔דֶשׁ הִ֗יא שְׁנַת֙ תְּשַֽׁע־עֶשְׂרֵ֣ה שָׁנָ֔ה לַמֶּ֖לֶךְ נְבוּכַדְרֶאצַּ֣ר מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֑ל בָּ֗א נְבֽוּזַרְאֲדָן֙ רַב־טַבָּחִ֔ים עָמַ֛ד לִפְנֵ֥י מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֖ל בִּירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


Now in the fifth month, on the tenth day of the month, which was the nineteenth year of Nevukhadreżżar king of Bavel, Nevuzar᾽adan, captain of the guard, who served the king of Bavel, came to Yerushalayim.

וַיִּשְׂרֹ֥ף אֶת־בֵּית־יְהֹוָ֖ה וְאֶת־בֵּ֣ית הַמֶּ֑לֶךְ וְאֵ֨ת כׇּל־בָּתֵּ֧י יְרוּשָׁלַ֛͏ִם וְאֶת־כׇּל־בֵּ֥ית הַגָּד֖וֹל שָׂרַ֥ף בָּאֵֽשׁ׃

and all the army of the Kasdim, that were with the captain of the guard, broke down all the walls of Yerushalayim round about.
